Apotheosis – Ascension to Godhood
Vol.15

Chapter 1401
Who was this old man …?
After returning from the Illusory Battlefield, Luo Zheng's strength had increased, but he could clearly feel that his surroundings had changed.
It was as if he had been completely kept in the dark.
Xun's expression said it all. She seemed to have expected this, but she didn't say anything.
The old man circled around Chen Huangyi's corpse and strolled through the air.
One couldn't sense a strong aura from him, but this old man's temperament was very special, as if he had a very special affinity.
After slowly walking in front of Bi Feiyu, the old man gently stretched out his hand, and the broken sword fell into his hand. "Catch it …"
With that, he tossed the broken sword to Luo Zheng.
"How do I catch it?" Luo Zheng endured the pain and raised his hands. His hands had already turned into a pair of skeletal hands. Luo Zheng's current endurance was astonishing. If it had been someone else, they would have already fainted from the pain.
Luo Zheng had even endured the torture of the chaotic energy. This bit of pain wasn't enough to make him lose his composure.
"Buzz."
As Luo Zheng's voice fell, the broken sword hung in the air.
The old man sized up Luo Zheng's hands and sighed slightly. "From the beginning, you should have thought of a way to come to my Demi-Immortal Clan. Running around by yourself and causing so much trouble, you're too good at causing trouble."
The old man sighed. In this universe, there was no Divine Sea Realm martial artist who could attract dozens of Celestial Venerables at the same time.
Luo Zheng ascending to the Human Alliance wasn't a big problem, but running around was a big problem. He had completely become an uncontrollable factor, making it difficult for the Demi-Immortal Clan to make arrangements.
Before the Illusory Battlefield opened, Luo Zheng should have made a trip to the Demi-Immortal Clan, but in the end, he didn't go.
This caused some problems with the Demi-Immortal Clan's arrangements. And the Demi-Immortal Clan's Luo Xi Xuan, who had been eliminated by Hua Tianming, was also eliminated by Hua Tianming because of Luo Zheng.
"Young?" Luo Zheng said with a faint smile. He had already guessed who this old man was. Back when Luo Zheng had been trapped in the Immortal mansion, someone had sent word to him that he should pay a visit to the Demi-Immortal Clan. The original message had been about the former Clan Chief of the Demi-Immortal Clan, one of the most powerful figures in the universe.
The old man stared at Luo Zheng, then nodded. "You're the last of the younger generation …"
"What do you mean?" Luo Zheng stared blankly.
The old man smiled faintly. "You will know in the future. Let me take a look at your hand."
Luo Zheng raised his hands and presented them in front of the old man.
The old man's gaze fell on Luo Zheng's skeletal hands. "Your bones can actually resist the corrosion of the Grand Clarity Water. How surprising. However, your flesh has been corroded. I'm afraid it'll be a bit troublesome. Even if you regrow the flesh on your hands, I'm afraid it won't be as good as before. But I'll think of something. For now … that's all."
With that, the old man reached out and flicked his fingers. Waves of blue light spread out from his hand.
The blue light was a ball of viscous green liquid. It seemed to have a life of its own. It flowed through Luo Zheng's skeletal hands, spreading along his five fingers and transforming into two pairs of hands. At first glance, it looked like Luo Zheng was wearing a pair of gloves.
As the green liquid flowed, blood-red threads appeared within it and connected to Luo Zheng's bones.
Not long after, Luo Zheng's hands, which had lost all feeling except for the pain coming from the wound, started to feel numb and itchy. Very soon, he regained feeling in his hands ….
Luo Zheng squeezed his hands. The green liquid was translucent, so he could still see his bones. It could temporarily replace his hands. However, the green hands were too conspicuous and felt very strange.
After helping Luo Zheng to treat his wounds, the old man reached out and pointed his finger. The broken sword flew back to Luo Zheng's hand.
This time, Luo Zheng grasped the broken sword and asked, "What on earth is this used for?"
The old man replied faintly, "This sword is a key thing left by Chen Huang Yijian. You keep it safe. Now I'll take you out."
Luo Zheng nodded. He had a lot of questions, but now was not the time to ask questions. At least, he had to deal with Bi Feiyu first.
"How should we deal with this guy?"
Luo Zheng and Bi Feiyu exchanged glances. Luo Zheng's eyes flashed with a sharp light. If the old man hadn't appeared, Luo Zheng could almost imagine what would have happened to him. He and Xun might have died here.
The old man glanced at Bi Feiyu indifferently, "He's just a clown. Let him stay here." After saying that, he unlocked the hexagonal seal on Xun. With a light stroke of his finger, a space crack appeared out of thin air in front of them.
Xun's complexion was very bad. Although it couldn't be seen from the Sword Spirit's body, Luo Zheng could feel Xun's exhaustion.
After being defeated many times, the Sword Spirit would also disintegrate, not to mention Xun's soul.
At this moment, Xun didn't want to say anything. She just greeted the old man and turned into a red light, entering Luo Zheng's body.
Seeing that the old man was about to enter the space crack, Luo Zheng pointed to Chen Huangyi's corpse behind him, "How should we deal with this?"
The old man glanced at it, "It's just Old Chen's skin. He should have foreseen this day. Since he didn't say anything, let's just let him sit there …"
Luo Zheng didn't think much of this sentence, but Bi Feiyu's face suddenly turned pale. According to the old man's words, the Old Heightslord had foreseen what he would do.
Watching Luo Zheng and the old man leave, Bi Feiyu still couldn't move.
Bi Feiyu didn't regain his freedom until the Hidden Thousand-Feet Spider closed the bronze door again.
But he couldn't break the bronze door to come in, and now it was even more impossible for him to go out. Looking at the Old Heightslord's corpse, the fear in his eyes became more and more intense. He suddenly felt his hair stand on end. In a trance, he seemed to see the corpse move and slowly get up …
After taking a few steps through the space crack, Luo Zheng saw the old man open another space crack and come to the gate of the Killing Sword Mountain.
At the gate of the bronze door, the Elder of the Sword-Holding Hall and Mu Mingxue were waiting outside.
When Luo Zheng saw Mu Mingxue, he finally felt relieved. Although Luo Zheng deduced that Bi Feiyu was lying, Mu Mingxue was still in a dangerous situation.
"Young Master, did you succeed?" Mu Mingxue saw Luo Zheng and quickly asked.
A helpless smile appeared on Luo Zheng's face as he shook his head.
At the same time, he asked the old man in confusion, "Chen Huangyi told me that the Killing Sword Mountain could help me hone my Sword Essence and step into the Emotion-Severing Divine Dao. Why didn't I gain anything in the Killing Sword Mountain?"
Honing his Sword Essence was Luo Zheng's first goal in coming to the Sword Manor, but now he got a broken sword of unknown use. This was too different from what Luo Zheng had expected.
"Emotion-Severing Divine Dao, hehe, Chen Huangyi is indeed a genius. He has already taught you the method," the old man said with a smile.
"What method?" Luo Zheng was confused again.
The old man pointed at Luo Zheng, "Evil Qi. Didn't you feel any changes after the evil Qi filled your body?"
Luo Zheng thought for a moment, "Yes, but can this evil Qi help me step into the Emotion-Severing Divine Dao?"
Just now, the countless red spiders all turned into evil Qi and poured into Luo Zheng's body. Luo Zheng didn't know that this was the last part of the Sword-fighting Technique. Using evil Qi to change one's mind, it would make one more impulsive and more decisive. Only then would one have an indomitable momentum.
The old man nodded, "When you can freely suppress these evil Qi, and even use these evil Qi, you can officially step into the Emotion-Severing Divine Dao. Slowly comprehend it. For you, this is still a small matter."
"Suppress these evil Qi?"
Luo Zheng frowned. He really didn't have a way to control the evil Qi. How could he completely suppress them?
"Don't make a fuss. There are originally many Cultivation Methods in this universe that use evil Qi as the core. When you reach that step, you will naturally understand." The old man said with a matter-of-fact expression.
It seems that I can only figure it out myself, Luo Zheng thought.
＝＝＝＝＝

Chapter 1402
The old man wanted to leave with Luo Zheng, and the people from Sword Wielding Hall followed.
This group of people were confused, why did Luo Zheng go in alone, and an old man come out?
The old man's strength was also unfathomable, who was he? Why did he appear in their forbidden grounds …
"Luo Zheng, did you see manor lord Bi?" Someone asked.
"Manor lord Bi?" Luo Zheng shook his head, "I didn't see him."
"That's weird, after manor lord Bi left, I didn't see him."
"He couldn't have entered Killing Sword Mountain right?"
"Killing Sword Mountain is a forbidden ground, why would manor lord Bi knowingly enter?"
Hearing these words, Luo Zheng laughed coldly, your manor lord Bi has already been sentenced to life imprisonment.
Seems like Bi Feiyu entered Killing Sword Mountain, and the people from Sword Villa didn't know.
Everyone discussed, and the two sector lords from Sword Villa stared at the old man and asked, "May I ask, who are you, and why did you appear in our Sword Villa's sacred grounds?"
These two sector lords were ranked second and third in Sword Villa, and their position was only second to Bi Feiyu.
According to the order Chen Huang Yijian left behind, other than the person who had the Wen Qing sword heart, no one else could enter Killing Sword Mountain. This person coming out of Killing Sword Mountain could be considered breaking the rules.
But the two sector lords had some self-awareness.
After all, they already understood that this old man should be some sky sovereign in the universe … no matter which sky sovereign, they were all existences they couldn't afford to offend, so they could only ask. Even if he didn't answer, they couldn't do anything.
If they weren't afraid of this old man's strength, the two sector lords would have already made their move.
Facing the questioning of the two sector lords, the old man didn't mind, and smiled faintly, "Some things in this universe have already been triggered, so I have no choice but to come here and take Luo Zheng away."
"Take Luo Zheng away?"
"Absolutely not!"
The two sector lords panicked, and the elders of Sword Wielding Hall also had ugly expressions.
After discussing for an entire night, the result was already very clear!
Manor lord had personally said, no matter if Luo Zheng inherited Yi Jian Tianzun's inheritance, he would still be the manor lord of Sword Villa, no matter if this brat agreed or not, even if he had to force him to stay in Sword Villa.
Everyone had reached a consensus.
Naturally, they did not know that Bi Feiyu did not plan to let Luo Zheng come out alive. If Luo Zheng died inside, Bi Feiyu would hypocritically tell everyone that Luo Zheng might have met with an accident, and then take the opportunity to swallow the inheritance of the Sword-fighting Empyrean.
"Swish …"
The two Sector Lords took the lead to block in front, while the group of Sword Wielding Hall elders also blocked in front of Luo Zheng and the old man.
Even if it was an Empyrean, they probably had no choice but to stop it.
The sector lord clasped his hands at the old man and said, "Luo Zheng has the Wen Qing sword heart and is the successor chosen by our old village lord. It's unreasonable for you to take him away like this!"
The old man glanced at the Sector Lord, "Luo Zheng is in charge of the Sword Manor?"
The Sector Lord nodded with a calm expression. They knew that they were not a match for him, but they would not retreat.
"Heh." A helpless smile appeared on the old man's face. At the same time, he shook his head and stared at the Sector Lord. "Let me ask you, is the fate of the entire universe more important, or is your small Sword Manor more important?"
The two Sector Lords' faces froze, and the elders of the Sword Wielding Hall were also stunned. Following that, someone from the crowd said, "The fate of the universe is naturally up to you Empyreans to resist. Our Sword Manor no longer has an Empyrean …"
"That's the problem." The old man said coldly, "The Sword Manor is no longer important. What's important is what Chen Huang left behind. There's not much time left, Luo Zheng doesn't have the time to waste here."
After that, he took Luo Zheng and was about to leave. Mu Mingxue followed closely behind. Seeing that the two Sector Lords were about to stop them, the old man's eyes flashed. Everyone was unable to move at all, as if they were trapped by some kind of extremely strong power. The two Fiend Demon Tengu not far away also revealed a terrified expression, curling up their huge bodies.
"Let's go." The old man pulled Luo Zheng. With one step, there was a trail of footprints formed by white light on the ground. These footprints became denser and denser. Luo Zheng was originally walking in the cave, but as he walked, the surrounding scenery became more and more blurry. It was as if the walls of the cave were plated with a layer of white light. Finally, the walls disappeared. He had already entered a white tunnel …
After walking out of this tunnel, Luo Zheng discovered that he had entered an unknown starfield.
"Space teleportation." Luo Zheng's eyes flashed.
Space teleportation could allow one to cross a relatively long distance in a short period of time.
The higher the cultivation, the further the distance one could cross. The distance a Celestial Venerable could cross was much further than a Sector Lord …
However, this method of crossing distances was completely incomparable to Luo Zheng's teleportation token. Using the teleportation token, he could reach any place in the universe in a short period of time. As soon as they appeared in this starfield, the old man immediately continued to teleport with Luo Zheng. After repeating this more than ten times and spending more than half a day, they finally arrived at their destination.
Compared to other powers, the Demi-Immortal Clan's territory was much smaller. In total, they only had three Great Worlds. It was even smaller than the territory controlled by a second-rate power.
However, no one dared to underestimate the Demi-Immortal Clan. Everyone knew that in the face of this crisis, the Demi-Immortal Clan would play an indispensable role.
Along the way, Luo Zheng asked all the questions in his heart, but the old man did not answer. He only told Luo Zheng one thing. According to previous calculations, the one who obtained the Wen Qing Sword Heart should be Xuanyuan Chenfeng. The one who won the Illusory Battlefield should also be Xuanyuan Chenfeng.
However, there was an accident in this calculation. Luo Zheng took away everything that belonged to Xuanyuan Chenfeng …
Hearing this, Luo Zheng was a little unhappy. Why did it have to be that guy? That guy talked nonsense on the chessboard, and now the old patriarch was doing the same thing?
"Last time, I asked you to go to the Demi-Immortal Clan in the hope that you would be excluded and become the eleventh in the Illusory Battlefield," the old man continued.
"Why?" Luo Zheng was even more unhappy. In a battle of martial arts, he had no reason to show weakness to others.
The old man said expressionlessly, "It's not because of any reason, but because it's good for you and the whole universe. In the end, we replaced you with Luo Xi Xuan, so she was eliminated at the eleventh place, losing to Hua Tian Ming."
"What do you mean? You mean Luo Xi Xuan lost to Hua Tianming on her own initiative? She deliberately didn't let herself enter the top ten? "
Luo Zheng had a deep impression of Luo Xi Xuan. That woman had a Sanskrit page that was exactly the same as his.
In fact, after entering the top ten, Luo Zheng was very strange. Logically speaking, the Demi-Immortal Clan, which had produced many geniuses, should not only have Jiang Zhengyi. Moreover, Jiang Zhengyi was eliminated in the bottom five. The performance of the Demi-Immortal Clan was completely inconsistent with their reputation!
Seeing Luo Zheng making a fuss about nothing, the old man's face did not change, as if everything was as it should be. He just said lightly, "You young generation are indeed talented, but in the end, you are still too young. Many things are not as they seem on the surface. The ultimate truth of this world …"
"What is the ultimate truth?"
Luo Zheng's face was full of despair. After the end of the Dream Battlefield, Luo Zheng felt that everything had changed. It was as if everyone was hiding something from him, and he was the only one who didn't know anything.
"I will disappoint you if I tell you, but you will know in the future," the old man replied, "Now that you have returned to the Demi-Immortal Clan, I will first help you restore your hands, then change your blood, and at the same time restore your soul. If you need, we can provide you with a body for free. Of course, it is a woman's body."
"Actually, I want to see the truth of this world first," Luo Zheng said gloomily.
However, the old man did not care about Luo Zheng's request at all.
After joining the Demi-Immortal Clan, the first thing to do was to change one's blood.
However, Luo Zheng's hands were seriously injured, so the matter of changing his blood was naturally pushed to the back.
It was not difficult to help Luo Zheng regrow his flesh and blood, but to restore him to his previous level, this was a very troublesome matter.
Luo Zheng was arranged to stay in the heart of the Demi-Immortal Clan. His ice-blue palace was built next to a mirror-like lake, surrounded by tall fir trees that grew in picturesque disorder. It was a peaceful place.
"The scenery here is really good," Mu Mingxue said with a smile, looking into the distance with her hands behind her back.
However, Luo Zheng was not interested in the scenery at all. He was thinking about what he would face next. The Demi-Immortal Clan seemed to be planning something. According to the old patriarch, Xuanyuan Chenfeng was also part of the Demi-Immortal Clan's plan, but his appearance had ruined the Demi-Immortal Clan's plan.
Then, Luo Zheng could also assume that the Demi-Immortal Clan had joined forces with Gods Without Thought, or even with many other forces … This was not difficult to speculate. This crisis would sweep across the entire universe, and no one would be able to escape. Then, the previous disputes were meaningless, and everyone could only rely on each other for warmth.
Just as he was thinking this, the scene in front of him proved Luo Zheng's guess.

Chapter 1403
Seeing Hua Tianming, Luo Zheng smiled faintly and gently floated down to the lakeside.
Immediately, Mu Mingxue followed behind and landed behind Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng originally wanted to reach out and pat Hua Tianming's shoulder, but Mu Mingxue immediately said nervously, "Young Master …"
Luo Zheng also noticed that although the green thing on his hand had temporarily replaced his flesh, it was still very fragile. If he was not careful, he would be the one to feel pain.
The moment he patted it, he raised his hands, as if surrendering.
Seeing Luo Zheng's appearance, Luo Xi Xuan stared at his hands and giggled, revealing two cute dimples, "Did you get too used to the Illusory Battlefield and forget to protect yourself? To end up like this … "
There was no death in the Illusory Battlefield, and many cultivators recklessly squandered their lives. In any case, they would eventually be resurrected.
After returning to the real world, this kind of illusion would not disappear, and there would be certain repercussions. This kind of situation would cause cultivators to become indifferent to life and death and not panic in times of crisis, but sometimes it would harm some cultivators. After all, being seriously injured in the real world was not a projection of the Illusory Battlefield. If one was not careful, they would pay the price of their life.
"I heard you were injured, but I didn't expect your hands to end up like this," Hua Tianming also sighed.
"That's not important," Luo Zheng curled his lips, "I have a lot of things I want to ask …"
Hua Tianming's face revealed a trace of slyness, "Brother Luo Zheng, if you ask first, this little brother will naturally tell you everything!"
This kind of expression was rarely seen on Hua Tianming's face, and Hua Tianming did not know how to fake it. Luo Zheng's heart skipped a beat, probably understanding that he would not be able to get the things he wanted to know the most from this guy's mouth.
But this was also good, knowing a little was still a little bit. Luo Zheng calculated in his heart …
However, Luo Zheng's first question was to face Luo Xi Xuan.
"First question, I want to know where you obtained that Sanskrit page?" Luo Zheng asked.
Luo Xi Xuan was slightly surprised. She did not expect Luo Zheng's first question to be directed at her. She blinked and smiled, "Didn't you want to ask about Tianming? I didn't promise to answer you."
Luo Xi Xuan was not one of those old foxes. Her eyes flashing, a trace of guilt. Luo Zheng had already guessed the answer.
He stared at Luo Xi Xuan, and then shifted to Hua Tianming, "It seems you already know that I also have a Sanskrit page."
These Sanskrit characters.
When he was burned by the flames, all the Sanskrit words would appear on his body.
More than ten years ago, when Luo Zheng was still in the Blue Cloud Sect Grand Competition, he was exposed once.
With Hua Tianming's eyesight, he would never forget those Sanskrit characters, just like how Luo Zheng would never forget Hua Tianming's sword moves.
In this Illusory Battlefield, Hua Tianming had exposed Luo Xi Xuan's Sanskrit page, so it was easy to connect the two. After deducing, the two naturally knew the truth about Luo Zheng …
Seeing the flicker in Luo Xi Xuan's eyes, Luo Zheng was even more certain that there was a connection between the Sanskrit pages, or that there was some kind of hidden destiny.
Luo Zheng disliked destiny more and more. Although destiny treated Luo Zheng very well, and he also responded to it by never giving up on his efforts, it now seemed that this destiny was very deceptive. It gave Luo Zheng a goal and made him go straight for it, not willing to present the things behind it to Luo Zheng.
This kind of imaginary goal was beyond Luo Zheng's tolerance, just like how Luo Yan was also one of the imaginary goals.
In order to guide him, destiny played like this. He didn't want to live in a lie.
After thinking it through, Luo Zheng began to bargain with fate. He didn't want to be a donkey that kept staring at the carrot. He wanted to know where the end of his journey in this life was, and what was his purpose for setting off!
"Tell me! What is the connection between these pages … "
Luo Zheng's expression was very serious, even a little ferocious. Although it wasn't the first time he and Luo Xi Xuan had met, it was also the first time they had talked. Facing such a lovely beauty with such an attitude, it was indeed very inappropriate. However, Luo Zheng was very anxious and couldn't care less about his demeanor.
Luo Xi Xuan took two steps back and leaned against Hua Tianming's back, her delicate little face showing some grievance. "He won't let me say it."
"Who?" Luo Zheng asked.
"Of course it's old man Feng!"
The old patriarch of the Demi-Immortal Clan was surnamed Feng, but in the entire Demi-Immortal Clan, only Luo Xi Xuan dared to call him old man Feng.
Luo Xi Xuan was also a little angry at Luo Zheng's attitude. If this guy wasn't Hua Tianming's brother and if he wasn't injured, she really would have taught Luo Zheng a lesson, so she said fiercely, "Besides, you can't blame old man Feng! I asked you to come, but you didn't come, and then you fought your way into the top ten, while I didn't even get a single Divine Spark Plate! Instead, I took your place … I'm the one who feels wronged! "
Luo Xi Xuan entered the Illusory Battlefield to fight for the top ten, or even the top three!
If Luo Zheng didn't take a spot, then Luo Xi Xuan and Hua Tianming's battle would be a double round-robin battle for the top ten, and she wouldn't have been eliminated. Of course, she probably wouldn't have lost to Hua Tianming either.
Hua Tianming sighed, "Brother Luo Zheng, don't blame Little Xuan. If you're really confused, I can tell you something."
"Go ahead," Luo Zheng calmed down a little.
"Brother Luo Zheng, you should know about the Great Tribulation of the Universe, right?" Hua Tianming asked.
"En," Luo Zheng nodded.
The history of the universe was not continuous. Over a long period of time, life was born, development, and growth. It was like a tree blooming and bearing fruit, then growing old and finally withering, ashes to ashes, dust to dust, dust to dust.
This process took 3.6 billion years.
It was an unimaginable amount of time, because it was too long.
But even if it was long, this time had a beginning and an end.
When a martial artist reached the limit of life, such as after becoming a True God and escaping the restrictions of the Five Decay of the Celestial Being, 3.6 billion years wasn't very long. This was because eternal life represented infinite time, and eternal life was greater than everything else. It could be 36 billion years of life, or 360 billion years of life, or even longer, an unimaginable length of time.
"This rule is the embodiment of the Heavenly Dao. Even Heavenly Venerables can't jump over it," Hua Tianming said. "But why must we be destroyed once every 3.6 billion years, and for no reason at all?"
Hua Tianming asked again.
Mu Mingxue, who was behind Luo Zheng, blinked. She had no right to speak here. Mu Mingxue cleverly remained silent, but she was also thinking about Hua Tianming's question.
Why …
No living creature liked to be destroyed.
Even ants wanted to live, not to mention these people, who would become the top existences in the universe in the future.
Hua Tianming said "we," and Luo Zheng also knew that he was referring to all living creatures in the universe. Luo Zheng smiled and said, "Do you have the determination to be a savior?"
Who knew that Hua Tianming would shake his head, stare at Luo Zheng, and say, "No."
The gaze in his eyes became deep. It was a gaze that could only be revealed after experiencing the baptism of time. After all, Hua Tianming had cultivated alone for 300 years. His situation was completely different from Luo Zheng's.
"The crisis of the universe this time is different from the past. This crisis is related to you and me, and the root is involved in the Realm of the Gods … If the universe can't survive this crisis, there won't be another Yan Era," Hua Tianming said lightly.
"There won't be another Yan Era?" Luo Zheng frowned. He remembered Old Man Feng's words. There wouldn't be another younger generation. They were the last of the younger generation.
Hua Tianming nodded and sat down in front of Mirror Lake. Luo Xi Xuan also sat down beside him, and Luo Zheng and Mu Mingxue also sat up.
It seemed that Hua Tianming knew many things that Luo Zheng didn't, but Luo Zheng was also puzzled. Where did he find out?
Hua Tianming noticed Luo Zheng's expression. "At first, I didn't know either. At first, I knew much less than you." He smiled. "But when I entered the Wild Continent and saw the sixteen scratches on the stone mountain, I learned many things."
"Sixteen scratches?" Luo Zheng asked, puzzled.
"En, under the first scratch were the words' Vast Heaven ', under the second scratch were the words' Seal Heaven '… under the fifteenth scratch were the words' Clear Heaven ', and under the sixteenth scratch were the words' Firmament '…"
"This universe has experienced sixteen Yan Eras?" Luo Zheng's eyes flashed.
The fragment of the Heavenly Dao in Luo Zheng's hand was a fragment of Clear Heaven …
Who made those scratches? ,
It actually recorded all the Heavenly Dao since the creation of the universe.
If one experienced the Heavenly Dao and left a scratch, wouldn't that person have lived for a full 57.6 billion years?

Chapter 1404
In that unimaginably long period of time, it was enough for the sea to turn into mulberry fields, and then from mulberry fields to sea thousands of times.
The words engraved on the mountain would quickly be eroded by time and couldn't possibly stand for that long.
Luo Zheng's face was full of doubt and he quickly overturned his own conclusion …
Hua Tianming also shook his head. "No, the words on the mountain wall aren't very old. There are vines called bluestones growing around the mountain wall. Although the words look very simple and unadorned, the bluestones and vines didn't cover them up. In other words, the words were carved less than half a year ago!"
Half a year …
Someone had carved the words on the mountain as a clue half a year ago.
These clues must have been waiting for this person, and there was no civilization on the Wasteland Continent. According to the Heavenly Sword Diagram's index, only Hua Tianming had been drawn into it.
In other words, someone had carved the words on the mountain wall half a year ago and then waited for Hua Tianming to discover them.
Realizing that Luo Zheng had sensed something was wrong, Hua Tianming's face revealed a helpless smile. "They've always been by our side … including you."
Always by our side …
Luo Zheng suddenly felt a chill.
"From the moment you stepped out of the Luo Family's door, or even from the moment you were born, they've always been by our side," Hua Tianming added, emphasizing to Luo Zheng again.
This kind of conspiracy theory, coming from Hua Tianming's mouth, made one's hair stand on end.
Luo Zheng also found it hard to believe that there had always been people by his side. This didn't make sense.
Seeing Luo Zheng's incredulous expression, Hua Tianming continued, "Don't you think it's strange? There are so many coincidences in fate, and every time you narrowly escape death … some people will solemnly swear that it's your destiny! "
"Destiny does exist." Luo Zheng was even more confused. Hua Tianming seemed to be overturning everything Luo Zheng had previously believed.
Hua Tianming suddenly smiled. With a light shake of his sword, he instantly stabbed out dozens of times, each sword piercing into the mirror lake …
The Mirror Lake was originally very quiet, and the surface of the lake was like a mirror. After being disturbed by the dozens of swords, the ripples spread to both sides.
"A total of fifty stabs." Even though Hua Tianming's speed of attack was extremely fast, and most people would only think that he was shaking in place, Luo Zheng was able to count it clearly.
"With a one in fifty probability, which sword has the deepest mark under the lake?" Hua Tianming asked again.
How could Luo Zheng explain clearly when those streaks of sword light went deep into the lake and into the mud? This was a difficult question.
"With your luck, a one in fifty chance is too easy," Hua Tianming said with a smile. "In fact, the things you've experienced are countless times less likely than this. You can take a guess …"
Luo Zheng was silent for a moment. "The sixteenth sword."
"Wrong." Hua Tianming shook his head. "The depth of each sword is exactly the same. There's no difference. Your destiny … has lost its effect."
Hua Tianming only used a small example to directly negate Luo Zheng's destiny.
"They're all liars. You can continue to think. Who told you about destiny?" Hua Tianming asked again.
When it came to destiny …
From the earliest, it was Yun Luo, then Daoist Tian Miao, then Xun, and most recently the mysterious girl who presided over the Illusory Battlefield!
"Think about what characteristics they have?" Hua Tianming said with a smile.
Luo Zheng frowned and kept thinking. There seemed to be an additional truth in his heart, but that truth was always covered by a layer of gauze, preventing him from seeing it clearly …
These people had all suddenly appeared by his side!
Thinking about it carefully, even if his talent was outstanding, and he had been promoted from the Blue Cloud Sect to the Cloud Hall, these people were all special people. They had suddenly appeared, but they didn't have to appear!
Just like Ning Yudie, as the Hall Master of the Cloud Hall, she had to appear. Because if he entered the Cloud Hall, he would eventually meet Ning Yudie!
But these people were different!
Xun!
Luo Zheng's eyes suddenly flashed. At this moment, Xun was still sleeping in Luo Zheng's body.
Hua Tianming seemed to see through Luo Zheng's thoughts, but said, "Xun is different. She does know part of the truth, but most of it is just a ride."
"Is that so …"
But according to Hua Tianming's logic, everything he had experienced was a pretense!
At this moment, Luo Zheng even had a hallucination. It was as if everything in this world was fake and could not be trusted.
When his memories began to rewind, his previous encounters seemed to have become a trap.
How had he found that Immortal Mansion?
Back then, when he had obtained the Weapon Refining Ancestral Arts, he had strangely obtained information about Nine Yin Mountain and Jade Dragon Valley. At that time, before he had returned to Guanghan County, someone had very appropriately mentioned Nine Yin Mountain and Jade Dragon Valley to him!
Someone wanted to control Immortal Mansion.
They had placed a clue in the Weapon Refining Ancestral Technique so that he would "accidentally" discover it. Then, they would take the opportunity to let him know, and he would be able to easily enter Immortal Mansion …
In fact, the traces of this deliberate arrangement were quite obvious, but at that time, Luo Zheng could not have been vigilant to this extent.
Such a huge price was just to give him Immortal Mansion. What was their goal?
Then what about Luo Yan?
To lure him to find Luo Yan, there must be an even bigger conspiracy. Was he really just a chess piece?
Seeing Luo Zheng's agitated appearance, a trace of helplessness appeared on Hua Tianming's face. He had known that Luo Zheng would have a very intense reaction, and Luo Zheng had to adapt to this process by himself.
Hua Tianming did not say the following words to Luo Zheng. He would wait for him to adjust before speaking.
"Do you want to drink?" Hua Tianming asked.
"It's no use." Luo Zheng shook his head. What kind of wine could get him drunk?
Luo Xi Xuan turned her hand and a few jade bottles appeared in her hand. They were only the size of a palm. She smiled slightly and said, "It's guaranteed to be useful."
Luo Zheng glanced at Luo Xi Xuan indifferently and took a jade bottle from her hand. After unscrewing it with one hand, he smelled a strong wine fragrance. The fragrance was very sweet, as if it was just ordinary sweet wine.
Not to mention this kind of sweet wine, even the strongest wine was not much different from water in Luo Zheng's mouth.
However, when Luo Zheng took a sip, he found that the wine was following a strange path and spreading to his mind. He felt his soul burning …
"Cloudy Soul Wine," Luo Xi Xuan said lightly, "Only the Demi-Immortal Clan has it. This is a good wine that can cause your soul to fall into a drunken state, but it's not good to drink too much."
"He's too agitated," Hua Tianming shook his head and said as he also took a bottle of Cloudy Soul Wine, made a toast, and drank it in one gulp.
Hua Tianming had borne no less than Luo Zheng over the years, and he gradually discovered that his fate was even more unbearable and helpless than Luo Zheng's.
Strictly speaking, this Cloudy Soul Wine was not a kind of wine, but was made with a kind of poison that targeted the soul. However, it could cause a cultivator to experience drunkenness. If the poison was directly consumed, the soul would immediately be corroded. The Demi-Immortal Clan used special methods to moderate the nature of the poison.
Some cultivators were very addicted to wine, but after their cultivation reached a certain level, they would no longer need any wine. This kind of wine was the kind that could only be found by chance.
Luo Zheng felt the world around him begin to spin, and he fell into an uncontrollable state. He didn't know how many years it had been since he had been drunk, but soon, he lay on his back by the lake and fell into a deep sleep.
"Young Lord is drunk," Mu Mingxue said obediently. She got up and wanted to help Luo Zheng to rest.
Hua Tianming also drank a bottle of Cloudy Soul Wine, but his eyes were clear because he had just drunk water …
At this moment, Hua Tianming's eyes were staring at Mu Mingxue, as if he wanted to see through her, "Which Great Thousand World did you ascend to?"
Luo Xi Xuan also stood beside Mu Mingxue, tiny bolts of lightning constantly jumping around her fingertips, as if to prevent Mu Mingxue from escaping.
Mu Mingxue's face was completely innocent, "I don't know … the Great Thousand World doesn't have its own name."
Because there were too many Great Thousand Worlds, they often didn't have their own name, only a number. It was normal that Mu Mingxue didn't know.
Hua Tianming gently crushed the jade bottle in his hand and said to Mu Mingxue, "Tell me your purpose. I don't want strange people to accompany Luo Zheng. If you don't want to tell me, then you can only die."
＝＝＝＝＝

Chapter 1405
Hua Tianming seemed to be looking for Luo Zheng to reminisce about the past.
But his real purpose was to make things difficult for Mu Mingxue.
Mu Mingxue's appearance seemed to be purely accidental. She left the Well of Ascension on the same day and left behind some clues. Then, very "coincidentally", she was found by Luo Zheng …
Hua Tianming couldn't predict where they were, but he could find a sudden clue.
He really didn't have any evidence to prove that Mu Mingxue belonged to "them," but this sudden appearance proved that Mu Mingxue was unreliable.
"You have no other choice, believe me," Hua Tianming said calmly.
Mu Mingxue took a few steps back, her face still full of grievance. "I have no ulterior motives towards Luo Zheng. I just want to be by his side. Why are you doing this to me!"
Hua Tianming stared at Mu Mingxue, trying to find a flaw in her expression. But Mu Mingxue's performance was perfect, as if she really did appear by Luo Zheng's side by chance …
"I'm very grateful to Luo Zheng for saving me. As long as I'm by his side, I'm willing to be his slave or servant," Mu Mingxue continued.
The lightning in Luo Xi Xuan's hand became more and more intense. She was waiting for Hua Tianming's next move.
Indeed, there were no flaws. Hua Tianming's gaze was calm, but he raised the Sword of Tengshe in his hand and slowly handed it to Mu Mingxue.
The sharp tip of the sword was already pressed against Mu Mingxue's forehead. The extreme sword intent emitted a faint silver glow. His sword intent was relatively moderate and extreme, so it wasn't as boundless as Luo Zheng's sword intent. If Luo Zheng's sword was pointed at someone, the sword intent would have already killed them.
"There won't be a second chance for you. What is your purpose?" Hua Tianming said coldly …
No matter how amazing an actor was, there would always be a compromise between life and death, unless the other party really didn't want to live. Hua Tianming wanted to drive Mu Mingxue to the brink of death and make her admit it herself.
Actually, he didn't dare to kill Mu Mingxue before he had absolute evidence.
Before this, he had a perfect evaluation of Luo Zheng's personality. Luo Zheng was more talented than him, his personality was relatively simple, and he trusted the people around him.
This woman had been by Luo Zheng's side for so long. If he casually killed her now, the impact on Luo Zheng would be immeasurable. He needed absolute proof, so when he said that he would kill Mu Mingxue, it was just a threat.
However, Mu Mingxue's performance was much better than Hua Tianming had expected.
Even in the face of death, she still didn't panic, and her tone was very resolute. "If you kill me, I'll die with a grievance. Why? I'm sure Luo Zheng will ask you for me! "
Luo Xi Xuan frowned and shook her head slightly, "In fact, a Soul Search should be enough. The Demi-Immortal Clan has a way to perform a Soul Search without damaging your soul. It will take a long time, but it will eventually uncover the truth."
"Okay," Mu Mingxue replied indifferently. "I can cooperate with you, but you have to prove my innocence! I don't want Luo Zheng to be suspicious of me. "
Just as she finished speaking, Hua Tianming smiled.
He slowly put away Tengshe, stared at Mu Mingxue, and said, "It's confirmed."
"Confirmed what?" Mu Mingxue asked.
"You're one of them," Hua Tianming said with conviction.
"What do you mean?" Mu Mingxue's face darkened.
"You really don't have any flaws, but …" Hua Tianming continued, "But the biggest flaw is that you don't have any flaws. You're too confident and too calm. An ascended Divine Sea Realm martial artist without any foundation or backing, you can still be so calm in the face of pressure. You're really something."
"Nonsense. You can say whatever you want," Mu Mingxue said coldly.
Hua Tianming continued to shake his head. "But I won't kill you, and I won't expose you to Luo Zheng. No matter what reason you have for staying by Luo Zheng's side, as long as I find a flaw, I'll definitely kill you first!"
Mu Mingxue coldly snorted, then carefully helped Luo Zheng up and carried Luo Zheng back to the building.
Hua Tianming and Luo Xi Xuan were still standing by the Mirror Lake. Luo Xi Xuan said, "I still think it's better to kill her directly."
"In the end, Luo Zheng will rebel greatly …" Hua Tianming shook his head. "She's so sure because she thinks I don't dare to kill her. I really don't dare."
"But if she stays by Luo Zheng's side, it'll be even more troublesome." Luo Xi Xuan sighed.
Hua Tianming smiled faintly. "There'll be trouble in the end. We'll talk about it when the next step comes."
… …
Luo Zheng had a strange and long dream.
This dream was as long as a person's life. He dreamt of his growth, and he dreamt that he accidentally fell into a big black hole. That hole was connected to the other side of the world, and he saw the patriarch of the Ancient Witch Tribe. Suddenly, a bolt of lightning struck the patriarch's face, and he saw Ning Yudie's face in an instant!
Ning Yudie … was she also someone he couldn't trust?
A formless sadness shrouded Luo Zheng's body. It made him feel like he was being pitied. He didn't like this feeling. He didn't like feeling pity for himself.
Living a lifetime in a trap was indeed a tragedy.
His once indomitable aura collapsed overnight.
That kind of indomitable momentum was built on one's confidence in oneself, on one's martial heart, and also on one's luck.
He was a Heaven's Chosen, a Dao Child. He was the darling of the Heavenly Dao. Along the way, demons and monsters had to give way to him and pave the way for him.
However, all of this was fake. He wasn't the one destined by the heavens. Someone was carefully guiding him, setting up so many traps for him. He walked up step by step for an ultimate goal.
"Boom!"
An explosion suddenly sounded in his mind. After all, the Corrupted Soul Wine had a certain level of toxicity. Even if Luo Zheng's soul was so powerful, there were still some very strong aftereffects after getting drunk.
After waking up, Luo Zheng found himself lying on a stone bed.
Mu Mingxue saw that Luo Zheng had woken up. She stepped on her small cotton shoes and quickly poured out some spirit tea from the teapot. This spirit tea could calm one's soul. After drinking it, it had the effect of relieving the effects of alcohol. However, it was relieving the poison of the Corrupted Soul Wine.
"Young Master has woken up," Mu Mingxue said with a smile. That smile was as gentle as ever, making people feel that it was just right. This was a unique style of Mu Mingxue, just like a cup of fresh green tea.
Luo Zheng was still a little confused. He seemed to have forgotten something. Before he fell asleep, he really wanted to ask …
After seeing Mu Mingxue, he thought harder. It seemed that before he fell asleep, he heard Mu Mingxue and Hua Tianming arguing.
This was simply impossible!
"What did you say to Hua Tianming?" Luo Zheng asked.
Mu Mingxue's expression was still calm. "You can ask him, but I don't want to say," There was a trace of grievance in her expression.
"What's wrong?" Seeing Mu Mingxue's expression, Luo Zheng's face sank. It couldn't be that Hua Tianming bullied her, right? But he knew that Hua Tianming was a very upright person. He definitely wouldn't be so bored as to bully Mu Mingxue, not to mention that Luo Xi Xuan was still by Hua Tianming's side.
"It's nothing, Young Master. Can I not say?" Mu Mingxue said pitifully.
Seeing her like this, Luo Zheng was very confused, but he didn't ask anymore. He should ask Hua Tianming about this matter.
However, that old man Feng had brought him here and told him to wait here. He didn't know where Hua Tianming had gone, so he could only wait for that brat to appear again.
The scenery here was very pleasant. Although Luo Zheng wasn't in a good mood, it was still very pleasant. Sometimes, he would completely empty his mind and admire the flowers by the lake outside with Mu Mingxue.
Three days later, people came one after another.
They were all black-clothed men from the Demi-Immortal Clan. These people didn't speak, leaving behind a few things and then leaving.
These things were all very rare medicinal herbs. Any one of them could be taken out to be the highlight of an auction.
Even Luo Zheng, who was used to seeing rare treasures, couldn't help but click his tongue when he saw these herbs. The Demi-Immortal Clan's foundation was really deep beyond his imagination.
When these things were almost all delivered, three black-clothed leaders appeared. They were all Realm Lords.
The three of them introduced themselves. They could be considered relatively important figures in the Demi-Immortal Clan. The three of them were all skilled in healing martial artists. In the universe, their methods were definitely top notch.

Chapter 1406
It wasn't difficult to treat Luo Zheng's hands.
Luo Zheng also had some pills that could regenerate flesh, but the difference between the regenerated flesh and blood would be quite big …
Originally, Luo Zheng thought that they would use some kind of elixir or something like that.
But they only came to do one thing, and that was to make extremely accurate measurements.
From the fracture of Luo Zheng's muscles to the thickness and width of his bones, they were accurate to the point of a hair's breadth.
After working for four to six hours, the three of them left.
Mu Mingxue and Luo Zheng were also very confused. Were these people here to help him treat his hands?
One day later, the leader of the group came back. He took Luo Zheng outside, measured the distance, and walked ten steps away from the door. "It's here. Come here."
Luo Zheng stood in the direction pointed by the man, somewhat confused.
Although he passed the test of the Demi-Immortal Clan, he didn't know much about the Demi-Immortal Clan, so he didn't know what they were going to do.
The man grabbed Luo Zheng's arm, adjusted his position, and then nodded, took two steps back, and made a gesture towards the sky …
The originally clear sky was suddenly filled with dark clouds. Amidst the layers of dark clouds, two green lightning bolts streaked across the sky, like two green dragons that were faintly discernible in the clouds!
"Crackle …"
Luo Zheng stared, and almost before he could react, the two green lightning bolts had already descended and hit Luo Zheng's hands!
The powerful force of the lightning made Luo Zheng's hair stand on end. His mind almost stopped, and he almost couldn't think.
Luo Zheng knew that some lightning actually contained a powerful life force. These lightning bolts could give birth to life in the Spirit Sea, but he didn't expect that the Demi-Immortal Clan would use this method to treat his hands …
Meridians continued to extend from his white bony hands, crawling all the way from his arms, like small worms, circling around the back of his hands, and then along the bones of his fingers, all the way to his fingertips.
Next were the muscles, tendons, skin, and so on.
Luo Zheng felt waves of numbness in his hands. He couldn't exert any strength at all. In addition, he also felt an invisible force twisting his hands, preventing him from falling to the ground.
When the green lightning dissipated, the power suddenly disappeared. The remaining power of the lightning suddenly lashed out, and he rolled on the ground, unable to move for a long time.
The man walked out and grabbed Luo Zheng's hands. He turned them over and looked at them. Then he nodded expressionlessly. "There should be no problem, but it's still not perfect. Someone will help you later. Try not to use too much strength in your hands these days."
After that, the man flew away without looking back, leaving Luo Zheng and Mu Mingxue here. They looked at each other in dismay.
The Demi-Immortals really were eccentric, and each one was more bizarre than the last.
Luo Zheng moved his hands. There was a conspicuous dividing line between his hands and arms. The upper half of his arms was black, while the skin that had just grown was white. It was like a pair of well-maintained women's hands.
It seemed that it would take some time to get used to it. Luo Zheng felt somewhat helpless.
Not long after, the Demi-Immortals sent another person. This time it was Old Man Feng, the patriarch of the Demi-Immortals.
Seeing Old Man Feng, Luo Zheng had a bellyful of questions. For example, why did Hua Tianming and the others come to the Demi-Immortals? Were the top ten of the Illusory Battlefield here? What was the purpose of inviting him to the Demi-Immortals?
Old Man Feng was a sly old fox. He didn't directly answer Luo Zheng's questions. "This time I came to help you heal that little girl's damaged soul." As soon as his voice fell, Old Man Feng pointed his finger at Luo Zheng's chest.
Luo Zheng felt his body suddenly shake. The red light that had condensed in his body quickly rushed out.
These light spots were the most primitive form of the Sword Spirit. As they continued to condense, the red light dissipated and finally condensed into Xun's body.
Xun fell into a deep sleep. She hugged her knees and closed her eyes, hugging herself like a baby.
Old Man Feng then took out a small incense burner. After lighting it, he placed it in a corner of the room. He didn't know what kind of incense was burning inside, but it seemed to have an extremely strong soul nourishing effect. From the incense burner, a faint purple smoke slowly drifted out. As the smoke drifted, Luo Zheng inadvertently inhaled a bit. He immediately felt refreshed. Old Man Feng didn't say anything, but Luo Zheng knew that this was an extraordinary item. It was probably priceless.
Most of the purple smoke rushed into Xun's body.
"During this time, don't take her back into your body. Let her stay in this room," Old Man Feng advised.
Luo Zheng nodded. He couldn't be too far away from Xun, but he could still move around. This wasn't a problem. "I want to ask …"
Just as he opened his mouth, Old Man Feng gestured for him to keep quiet. He smiled faintly. "All problems will be solved. In ten days, I will take you to exchange your blood. At that time, I will talk to you about those dragons of yours …"
Seeing that Old Man Feng was about to leave, Luo Zheng didn't seem to have any means to keep this Celestial Master. In a moment of desperation, Luo Zheng said calmly, "If you don't explain these things clearly, I refuse to join the Celestial Clan!"
The doubts in his heart all began when he met this old man. Although nothing major had happened during this time, Luo Zheng's world view had been overturned by the constant guessing.
In this strange environment, Luo Zheng felt like he was going crazy!
Old Man Feng smiled faintly. His figure became more and more blurred. When his figure completely disappeared in front of Luo Zheng, he only left a sentence. "We are proving our sincerity. The final choice is in your hands."
Luo Zheng curled his lips, but his eyes were fixed on Xun. His face was expressionless as he thought about that sentence in his mind.
Indeed, the Celestial Clan wasn't completely trustworthy. What they were doing now was completely proving their sincerity to him.
What was their purpose?
To ensure that he would be loyal to them in the future?
No …
Luo Zheng would only walk his own path. As long as it didn't violate his own principles, he wouldn't do anything to betray the Celestial Clan. This was Luo Zheng's character.
Unless some difficult choices in the future were to be made, then the "sincerity" shown by the Celestial Clan would influence his choice.
Luo Zheng kept analyzing in his heart.
After thinking for a while, Luo Zheng finally calmed down. No matter what choice he made, it was in his hands. Perhaps his fate would be controlled by the secret forces, but he would eventually unveil this layer of veil.
His gaze finally fell on Xun.
Even though she was curled up into a ball, it couldn't hide Xun's beautiful figure. Her eyes were tightly shut, and her thick eyelashes were like a pair of brushes trembling slightly.
Xun's personality was relatively straightforward. Before this, Xun's behavior hadn't been strange. They had traveled together and supported each other. She wanted to help him advance in the martial path, and he would help her return to the throne.
Now, it seemed that things weren't as simple as they seemed.
There were some details in Xun's words that were worth thinking about. The biggest problem was Yao's motive for killing Xun.
From Xun's description, Yao had made her preparations and directly attacked Xun. But what was her motive? If she really wanted to control the entire Fiend Night Clan, then Yao would have to kill Ying. However, Ying and Yao lived together peacefully.
Moreover, Ying clearly knew that Yao wanted to monopolize the Fiend Night Clan. Yet, she still dared to reincarnate and become Qing Yu? She didn't seem to be afraid of Yao's methods.
This was very contradictory.
After Xun woke up, he would have a good talk with her. She wouldn't hide anything from him, right …
"Sir, here's some spirit tea." Mu Mingxue handed Luo Zheng a cup of green tea at an appropriate time.

Chapter 1407
Little by little, the purple smoke from the incense burner seeped into Xun's body. Xun's aura grew stronger and stronger.
Luo Zheng tried to study what was burning in the incense burner. When he looked down, he saw a purple crystal being burned by a thin wisp of flame.
"This is the Shining Dragon Crystal. It's a crystal that's formed by boiling dragon oil. It's very precious and has an excellent effect on healing the soul." The Azure Dragon's voice sounded in his mind again. "The value of this small piece of Shining Dragon Crystal is almost equivalent to a supreme artifact."
"You're finally willing to talk," Luo Zheng said.
In the past, Qinglong had been like a guiding light to Luo Zheng, but now he was silent.
Qinglong knew that Luo Zheng was dissatisfied, but he smiled faintly and said, "An ant faces a mountain. If it doesn't look up, it will climb over the mountain one day. But if the little ant knows the height of the mountain, I think it will lose the courage to climb over it."
The little ant that Qinglong was talking about was Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng understood this. Things hadn't reached that point yet. Knowing would only do him harm.
But Luo Zheng was still curious ….
"It's a lot earlier than I expected," Qinglong sighed faintly. "But it's good. You're about to change your blood. Once you become a member of the Demi-Immortal Clan, many truths will be revealed."
"Why do I have to change my blood? Because I want to join the Demi-Immortal Clan? "Luo Zheng frowned." Can I refuse? "
Although the Demi-Immortal Clan had helped Luo Zheng, he didn't like the feeling of being kept in the dark. It was as if everyone was hiding a great conspiracy.
"Yes, you have to change your blood. But it has nothing to do with joining the Demi-Immortal Clan," Qinglong said.
"Can you give me a clear explanation?" Luo Zheng didn't want to beat around the bush. He only wanted one thing, and that was the truth.
"Because the blood of the Demi-Immortal Clan is very special. Increasing your strength is only one aspect. It has another important function, and that is to prevent certain people from discovering you," Qinglong said.
Those people were probably people who couldn't be revealed. Luo Zheng rolled his eyes. He didn't want to know who those people were. Qinglong wouldn't tell him anyway ….
Qinglong also sensed Luo Zheng's emotions. He said, "Some people have attacked you once. They must have thought that you were a seedling …. In fact, you really shouldn't have competed for the first place in the Demi-Immortal Clan, you were too conspicuous......"
"You fought once?" Luo Zheng asked, puzzled.
"When you were in the Divine Kingdom Continent," Qinglong said.
Luo Zheng's eyes flashed again. He remembered that terrifying strike. That very regular black line had almost unimaginable power. It was also that strike that had destroyed all the mountains in the Vast Thousand Domains.
"That should be their negligence. After all, there are thousands of seedlings like you in the Vast Thousand Domains …. But most of them haven't grown up yet. Even if they did, they would be discovered during the Heavenly Tribulation and would be killed. But you survived, so now you have the upper hand," Qinglong said.
Thousands of seedlings?
Luo Zheng felt that he had gained another piece of information.
There was more than one person like him? Why did they pay such a high price?
It couldn't be to kill a True God, right?
Even if it was to kill a True God, it didn't need to pay such a high price ….
Through inference, Luo Zheng vaguely saw the tip of the iceberg. He realized that the mountain in front of him was indeed unimaginably high.
"So you have to change the blood of the Demi-Immortal Clan. This is a very important part," Qinglong said.
Luo Zheng nodded and didn't refute.
There were some people he had to trust. Qinglong was one of them, and so was Xun. Perhaps Xun didn't explain something clearly, but even if he was deceived, Luo Zheng would stand firmly behind Xun.
Three days later, the candle dragon crystal in the incense burner was almost burned out, and the purple smoke gradually dissipated.
Xun still hadn't woken up. She was still sleeping, and her expression was very peaceful.
Luo Zheng didn't know if it was the effect of the candle dragon crystal or if she deliberately refused to wake up, but there was nothing he could do about it. He could only put her into his body.
After being put into Luo Zheng's body, Xun opened her eyes slightly, pursed her thin lips, and closed her eyes again.
At this time, the people of the Demi-Immortal Clan finally came. As old man Feng said, today was the day he changed his blood.
Along with the people of the Demi-Immortal Clan, they traveled millions of miles through the world passage. After a day and a half, they finally arrived at their destination.
In front of Luo Zheng's eyes, there was a huge waterfall, as if the flow of the sea had been cut off, rushing down from a mountain thousands of feet high!
With such a large amount of water falling from above, there should be a violent roar, but he didn't know what method the Demi-Immortal Clan used, but the sound of the waterfall was not loud.
"Let's go in." The leader led the way. The moment he entered the waterfall, the water curtain naturally split into two sides, as if a door had opened automatically.
Luo Zheng and Mu Mingxue looked at each other, and then followed him into the waterfall.
Unexpectedly, as soon as Luo Zheng entered, he heard a fierce quarrel …
Hidden in the waterfall was a majestic hall, magnificent in gold and jade everywhere. On both sides of the hall were carvings of various qilins, dragons, snakes, tigers, leopards, and all kinds of fierce legendary beasts. Among them, Luo Zheng found a statue of a "fierce scorpion".
However, Luo Zheng's attention was attracted by the quarrel.
"I will never agree to let Luo Zheng use such precious blood!"
"Humph, can he bear it? The Heavenly Origin Blood has been guarded by the Demi-Immortal Clan for so many years! "
"Patriarch, if you aren't afraid he will explode and die, then let him change it! If he dies, we will take the Heavenly God's Blood! "
"In fact, I have begun to suspect the purpose of the Demi-Immortal Clan. Patriarch, what are you planning?"
Rather than a quarrel, it was more like a siege.
In the center of the hall, there were seven people gathered together, fiercely pointing at old man Feng …
Among these people, four were Great Realm Lords, and the other three were Celestial Venerables. Luo Zheng had seen one of them before. When Luo Zheng was besieged by many Celestial Venerables, it was one of them who passed on the message.
In fact, Luo Zheng was also a little puzzled. Why didn't old man Feng directly use the Roaring Command? Perhaps there were other hidden reasons. Perhaps the Demi-Immortal Clan was not allowed to use the Roaring Command?
Seeing how Old Man Feng was at a loss for words, Luo Zheng felt a wave of satisfaction in his heart. Who told you to act all high and mighty in front of me? So, as one of the most powerful existences in the universe, there are times when you are in trouble!
"Luo Zheng is here!"
It was unknown who said this, but the seven people all turned their heads to look at him.
These seven people were either Great Realm Lords or Celestial Venerables. How shocking was their aura?
Not to mention the seven of them, even one of the Celestial Venerables could damage Luo Zheng's soul with just their soul pressure alone, or even destroy it.
However, in the end, these seven people did not really use their soul pressure to suppress Luo Zheng. That pressure was just the release of their emotions.
Even so, their aura was enough to topple mountains and overturn seas. Luo Zheng's face instantly turned pale, and Mu Mingxue's legs went soft, and she fainted on the ground …
Seeing Mu Mingxue faint, Luo Zheng's eyebrows immediately furrowed.
"You old fellows don't have any magnanimity. How can you treat a junior like this?"
Old man Feng waved his hand, and a warm breeze brushed past his face.
Immediately, Luo Zheng felt the pressure on him lessen, and he squatted down to help Mu Mingxue up.
Although he knew that these people were the top figures of the Demi-Immortal Clan, their actions just now left a very bad impression on Luo Zheng.
These people all stared at Luo Zheng with cold expressions.
Just now, Luo Zheng had heard that the blood he wanted to exchange was not ordinary, which was why these people objected.
However, these people could not persuade old man Feng, so they naturally did not have good expressions towards Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng's analysis was probably correct. The Demi-Immortal Clan's golden blood was divided into three types: Heavenly Origin Blood, Earthly Origin Blood, and Human Origin Blood.
Most of the Demi-Immortal Clan martial artists exchanged blood for Human Origin Blood. Only a small number of martial artists exchanged for Earthly Origin Blood.
As for Heavenly Origin Blood, the Demi-Immortal Clan had only inherited two portions. Many years ago, they had used one portion. Now, they wanted to exchange the last portion of Heavenly Origin Blood for Luo Zheng. How could these Celestial Venerables and Great Realm Lords be willing? Naturally, they argued with reason to stop the old patriarch.
＝＝＝＝＝

Chapter 1408
Luo Zheng originally had some thoughts about exchanging blood.
But now, seeing these people's expressions, Luo Zheng wanted to immediately take action.
"Old patriarch, today is the day of exchanging blood. Can we begin now?" Luo Zheng had a faint smile on his face.
Old man Feng was having a headache under the questioning of these people. He probably still had to convince Luo Zheng. However, he did not expect Luo Zheng's attitude to take a 180-degree turn. This was out of his expectations.
"Kid, have you really thought it through? Let me tell you, this Heavenly Origin Blood is very special and abnormally overbearing. If you don't handle it well, you might die from blood loss, "one of the Empyreans said coldly.
Another person beside him immediately chimed in, "Ordinary warriors can't withstand it at all. I suggest you think it through carefully!"
Luo Zheng stepped forward and entered the center of the hall. His gaze swept over these experts.
The younger generation of warriors would find it difficult to even stand under the gaze of these people. Luo Zheng's expression was indifferent. He had seen more than one or two Empyreans. He had even encountered dozens of Empyreans besieging him.
Luo Zheng suddenly smiled, "Sirs, do you think I'm an ordinary warrior?"
As soon as these words came out, the seven people's expressions froze. How could someone who could obtain first place in the Illusory Battlefield be ordinary?
Old man Feng also snorted coldly, "At this juncture, you're still worried about this Heavenly Origin Blood! Your vision can only see one step ahead. It's too narrow … "After saying this, he turned to Luo Zheng and smiled," Since you're ready, follow me! "
Old man Feng then turned and walked into the hall. Luo Zheng nodded and called out to Mu Mingxue, who also followed old man Feng into the inner hall. The Empyreans and Great Realm Lords looked at each other, then followed them into the inner hall.
The structure of the inner hall was very strange. The center of the ground was deeply sunken, forming a spiral staircase.
"You stand in the center," old man Feng instructed.
Luo Zheng nodded slightly and stepped down along the spiral on the ground, standing in the center.
Those several Celestial Venerables clearly knew that they couldn't stop them, so they could only watch from the side. Mu Mingxue leaned against a corner and stared at Luo Zheng below.
"The Heavenly Origin Blood is indeed very tyrannical. Whether or not you can withstand it will depend on yourself." Although old man Feng said this, he did not sound worried at all. He knew Luo Zheng very well, even better than Luo Zheng himself!
After he finished speaking, old man Feng lightly patted the ground, and two martial artists brought out a glass jar that was as tall as a person. This glass jar was as tall as a person, and what was inside was none other than a jar of golden blood!
If it was an ordinary blood exchange, old man Feng wouldn't have had to do it himself, but the Heavenly Origin Blood was too precious, not to mention that Luo Zheng's identity was very special. This time, old man Feng had to do it himself.
"Sit down, cross your legs, close your eyes, and calm your mind," old man Feng instructed.
Luo Zheng did as he was told. After sitting down cross-legged, he entered a state of cultivation, and at the same time calmed his mind. For a martial artist, this was as smooth as eating and drinking.
But just as he calmed down, he suddenly felt a sharp pain in his chest. There was a small hole the size of a little finger in his chest!
His heart had been pierced!
But the angle of the hole was very clever, and the position of the hole was also very precise. A column of blood spurted out in front of him!
At this moment, Luo Zheng was still a little flustered. Although a martial artist's blood was powerful, the blood in his body had almost flowed out. He probably wouldn't be able to survive …
When the scarlet blood spurted out, it flowed along a spiral in front of him. There seemed to be a strange gravitational force in the groove of the spiral that drew his blood away!
At the same time, old man Feng opened the glass jar, and the jar of Heavenly Origin Blood suddenly rushed out!
When it was in the jar, the golden Heavenly Origin Blood looked normal. But when old man Feng opened the jar, it seemed to release a powerful vicious beast. The large mass of Heavenly Origin Blood flowed on the ground, constantly rolling, darting left and right in an attempt to escape …
This mass of blood had been left there for a long time, and had already formed a blood spirit with its own consciousness. It understood that it was going to be devoured by someone, and was naturally unwilling.
Old man Feng coldly snorted. He reached out and grabbed at the air, instantly erasing the blood spirit. Then, the mass of golden Heavenly Origin Blood flowed along a groove in the spiral.
There were two parallel grooves in the spiral. One was where Luo Zheng's blood flowed out, while the other was where the golden Heavenly Origin Blood flowed in. As a result, the spiral turned into two parallel lines, one gold and one red.
When the golden Heavenly Origin Blood flowed down the groove and arrived in front of Luo Zheng, old man Feng pointed a finger at Luo Zheng's chest. At the same time, the golden Heavenly Origin Blood leaped up like a small snake and burrowed into the small hole!
Two types of blood, one entering and one exiting. This was the entire blood exchange process.
Although Luo Zheng's eyes were tightly shut, he could still sense what was happening in front of him. He sighed in his heart. He wondered who came up with this blood exchange method, but it was very ingenious.
Soon, Luo Zheng no longer had the leisure to let his imagination run wild.
He soon felt the tyrannical power of the Heavenly Origin Blood!
After the Heavenly Origin Blood entered Luo Zheng's body, it flowed along his arteries, from the main arteries to the branches, and then dispersed into the capillaries. It began to spread throughout his body, and at the same time, began to circulate in his body!
After all, this was not Luo Zheng's blood. Luo Zheng suddenly felt his whole body swelling up, as if his entire body was about to burst open, and some monster was about to rush out.
But this was only an illusion.
In fact, it was Luo Zheng's meridians that were swelling up, and the meridians spread throughout almost every part of his body. That was why Luo Zheng had such an illusion.
In the eyes of others, the meridians on Luo Zheng's forehead, neck, and arms were all swelling up. It was as if there were countless small snakes under his skin, and they were even slowly wriggling. It was a rather frightening sight.
The Heavenly Venerables continued to stare coldly at Luo Zheng. They knew how important Luo Zheng was. It was necessary for Luo Zheng to change his blood, but changing his blood for her would make it easier for him to hide. For this effect, the Earth Origin Blood was enough. There was no need to use the Heavenly Origin Blood at all!
As for their previous threat to Luo Zheng, saying that he would explode and die if he absorbed the Heavenly Origin Blood.
These people had already seen Luo Zheng's abnormal physique through the Illusory Battlefield. It would not be so easy to make Luo Zheng explode and die …
Now that Luo Zheng was absorbing the Heavenly Origin Blood so smoothly, they were somewhat puzzled.
The last person to absorb the Heavenly Origin Blood was the old patriarch himself. At the time, he had already been a Realm Lord and had exchanged his Earth Origin Blood for the Heavenly Origin Blood. Even so, the old patriarch had suffered greatly!
Old Man Feng, who could not bear it, had almost destroyed the entire hall.
When had it become so easy to absorb the Heavenly Origin Blood?
The Heavenly Venerables glanced at each other, their eyes filled with astonishment.
"Don't you find it strange?"
"Mm, it seems like this kid's original bloodline should be the Heavenly Origin Blood …"
"Ha ha, that's impossible."
Their discussion did not reach Luo Zheng's ears. At this moment, Luo Zheng really felt like he was about to explode.
Every part of his body was releasing an endless pressure that was squeezing his entire body outwards! His body was like a jar that was restraining this immense pressure within his body.
Under this immense pressure, Luo Zheng was miserable beyond words. He wished he could split himself in half and drive this pressure away!
However, Luo Zheng's body was ultimately different from a human's!
At this moment, the uniqueness of his body was also revealed. Under the intense pressure, it once again turned into little warm currents. These were the Primordial Astral Stars …

Chapter 1409
Luo Zheng felt his scalp go numb when he saw the densely packed little spiders that looked like a sea of blood spreading towards him.
Luo Zheng had encountered many dangers before, but he had never been so passive. He had fallen into this Realm Lord's trap while being unprepared. This indeed gave Luo Zheng a headache.
The only thing that comforted Luo Zheng was Xun.
Although Xun didn't reveal any information to Luo Zheng, from Xun's attitude, she didn't seem to like Bi Feiyu's scheme.
Bi Feiyu seemed to be very wary of these little spiders. When they spread over, he lightly pointed his finger and a circle of dark red flames spread out, enveloping his entire body.
The sea of spiders was like a tide. They climbed up the silk thread and quickly reached Luo Zheng's side.
Luo Zheng looked at these fist-sized red spiders with a puzzled expression. The possibility of these spiders hurting him wasn't high, and Luo Zheng himself wasn't afraid of strong poison. Why were they gathering around him?
While he was thinking, the little spiders climbed onto Luo Zheng's body. They extended their sharp mouthparts and fiercely nailed onto Luo Zheng's skin.
What surprised Luo Zheng was that the mouthparts of these little spiders were so powerful that Luo Zheng's divine artifact body couldn't resist them at all. The mouthparts sunk deeply into Luo Zheng's skin!
Immediately, the originally swollen body of the little spiders deflated in an instant. They tilted to one side and rolled off Luo Zheng's body. They were actually dead!
All the little red spiders were doing the same thing. After nailing themselves onto Luo Zheng's body, they seemed to have injected something from their abdomens into Luo Zheng's body and rolled to one side.
Luo Zheng didn't feel anything, but he felt even more puzzled. If these little spiders injected poison, they shouldn't have died immediately. Why did they do this?
This process was quite long, lasting a full four to six hours.
The small spiders, which had been surging like seawater, had become sparse. Below Luo Zheng, the corpses of the small red spiders had been piled up like a mountain.
Seeing that it was about time, Bi Feiyu sneered. He flew up and grabbed Luo Zheng, pulling him down from the golden spider web.
At this moment, Luo Zheng could only let Bi Feiyu do whatever he wanted. Not to mention that he couldn't move because of the Golden Spider Silk, even if he didn't have any restraints on his body, he still wouldn't be Bi Feiyu's opponent.
He carried Luo Zheng and walked to the other side. After a few steps, he smiled and waved his hand. The hexagonal seal also floated down and followed behind him.
"Be patient, it's going to end soon," Bi Feiyu sneered.
He carried Luo Zheng to a corner of the space, where another bronze door appeared in front of him. This bronze door was a lot smaller than the one outside the Massacre Sword Mountain, but it was almost the same!
"The gate of the Massacre Sword Mountain is easy to deal with, but this door is a big trouble for me. I wasted a lot of time here. Fortunately, you came, hehe …"
After that, he dragged Luo Zheng and threw him toward the bronze door!
Luo Zheng was like a thrown stone, smashing hard on the bronze door. His head directly hit the door, making a loud bang!
The bronze door's hardness was amazing. Luo Zheng's head could be considered as a divine tool, but it didn't suffer any damage under this impact.
Seeing this, Bi Feiyu grabbed the air and pulled Luo Zheng back into his hand. Then he grabbed Luo Zheng and threw him toward the bronze door again!
"Bang!"
After the second time, the bronze door still didn't have any reaction.
Bi Feiyu frowned, "Is that thing asleep?"
Thinking of this, he grabbed Luo Zheng and threw him at the bronze door again …
"Hong!"
This time, the force was strong enough. After being thrown by a sector lord, even Luo Zheng felt dizzy!
At this moment, the bronze door reacted again. The statue on it began to move, and the thousand legged spider on the bronze door actually climbed down!
"This spider, is it alive?" Luo Zheng was stunned.
On the bronze door outside the Massacre Sword Mountain, there was a statue of a thousand legged spider. There was also one on this bronze door, so Luo Zheng didn't look at it carefully.
Now, Luo Zheng realized that this thousand legged spider wasn't a statue, but a living giant spider. This spider had been on the door for who knew how many years …
Such a spider on the door was able to stop a sector lord like Bi Feiyu from entering. It wasn't hard for Luo Zheng to imagine how strong this spider was.
In the past, Luo Zheng's thoughts were simple. Above the three transformations was the sector lord, and above that was the sky sovereign.
Now it seemed that there were still many things in this universe that he didn't understand. Even Qinglong and Xun were hiding things from him.
This made Luo Zheng doubt himself.
In that case, was there another reason for guiding him upwards and even leading him to look for his sister?
He didn't know if there was a chance to solve these questions. Luo Zheng laughed bitterly in his heart.
"Zi …"
The spider slowly climbed down from the bronze door and extended its slender limbs. The tip of the limb was like a blade. With a light flick, the golden threads binding Luo Zheng's body immediately broke.
The moment Luo Zheng regained his freedom, he wanted to jump up and escape!
However, just as he wanted to do so, a depressed expression appeared on his face. He couldn't move at all.
The huge spider used its ten limbs to 'lift' Luo Zheng up and entered the bronze door.
Bi Feiyu had a faint smile on his face as he followed closely behind. The black panther he raised also followed closely behind. Xun was also brought into the bronze door.
Countless limbs slowly crawled on the ground. Luo Zheng was lifted into the air, right below the spider's abdomen. He sized up his surroundings.
In the cave behind the bronze door, Luo Zheng finally saw swords …
There were many long swords, and they were scattered all over the cave. There were all kinds of weapons, including Immortal Artifacts, Sacred Artifacts, and Divine Artifacts …
"These are the swords on the killing sword mountain? Or is it just an illusion? " Luo Zheng curiously sized up his surroundings. However, not long after, Luo Zheng saw an extremely shocking scene.
He saw Yi Jian Tianzun, Chen Huang Yi Jian!
"How is this possible …"
Luo Zheng's eyes revealed a look of disbelief.
Luo Zheng couldn't feel any vitality from this person. His entire body was completely withered. He should have died here after falling into the Five Decay of Heaven. Even though the Sky Sovereign had died, he still emitted an aura that was difficult to approach.
