Luo Zheng had already confirmed that Chen Huang Yi Jian had become a True God. Therefore, when he saw Chen Huang Yi Jian's corpse, he fell into a daze.
It could be seen that Bi Feiyu was also very confused.
The Wen Qing sword heart on Luo Zheng's body was definitely passed down to him by the Old Manor Lord.
Therefore, when Luo Zheng said that the Old Manor Lord had become a True God, Bi Feiyu himself believed seventy to eighty percent of it. At this moment, when he suddenly saw the Old Manor Lord's corpse, he immediately panicked.
For this bronze door, Bi Feiyu could be said to have racked his brains, but in the end, he was unable to break through it.
He never thought that Yi Jian Tianzun would actually die behind this door!
This door required a person who possessed the Wen Qing sword heart to obtain the approval of the Thousand Legged Spider and open it …
As for the Wen Qing sword heart, it required the Yi Jian Tianzun to bestow it. But now, the Yi Jian Tianzun had died behind this door.
This formed a paradox. Then who was the person who gave Luo Zheng the Wen Qing sword heart?
He and Luo Zheng were also in a state of great confusion. At the same time, they were also extremely vigilant!
Xun sat upright within the hexagonal seal and looked at the scene not far away. A faint smile appeared on her face.
In her opinion, Bi Feiyu's actions were extremely laughable.
The first place in the Illusory Battlefield was not as simple as a symbol. It involved the future direction of the entire universe.
It was not an easy task to kill Luo Zheng.
If one were to talk about death, Luo Zheng might die in the upcoming great calamity, but he definitely wouldn't die here.

Chapter 1410
The information in this blood was very vague and hard to understand.
Every time Luo Zheng immersed himself in it, someone seemed to whisper something in Luo Zheng's ear.
Luo Zheng tried to listen to the content, but no matter how hard he tried, he couldn't hear it clearly.
Several days later, Dugu Jian Xiaoxiao was also sent to this place. This brat's complexion was very bad, completely deathly pale. After seeing everyone, he began to complain, "What kind of lousy blood is this? You almost killed me!"
Dugu Jian Xiaoxiao wasn't born in a big family, so he didn't have many scruples and fear. He only believed in the sword next to him. Coming to the Sky Immortal Tribe wasn't his own idea, so it was normal for him to speak rudely.
The warriors who entered this place all had a deep expression on their faces. The process of changing blood wasn't that wonderful.
Dugu Jian Xiaoxiao was also here. Now, besides Qing Yu, only Xi Youqin was missing.
Seeing Xi Youqin again in the universe, Luo Zheng was looking forward to it, but at the same time, he was a little worried. Through the memory of the Boundless World in his mind, he found that Ning Yudie had also ascended. However, he didn't know which big world she had ascended to. After finishing this phase, Luo Zheng should go to find Ning Yudie.
However, with Luo Zheng's current prestige, no matter which big world Ning Yudie had ascended to, as long as she belonged to the three big forces of the Human Tribe, it shouldn't be a big problem. At least, they wouldn't make things difficult for Ning Yudie.
What Luo Zheng could do next was to wait. Wait for Xi Youqin to arrive, and wait for Old Man Feng's plan.
However, the days of waiting were inevitably boring …
Xuanyuan Chenfeng was bored to death, so he walked around. He liked to find a big rock and show off his tall and sturdy back. Then, he would show off there for the whole day.
Lie Qianhan, on the other hand, was a little neurotic. He would often find Xuanyuan Chenfeng to tell him some lame jokes, but he would be chased away by Xuanyuan Chenfeng after a few sentences.
Other than cultivating every day, Hua Tianming would chat with Luo Zheng. This guy should be the one who knew the most of the truth among the younger generation present, but he still kept his mouth shut. Every time they talked about something important, he would very frankly tell Luo Zheng that he couldn't reveal it. Luo Zheng couldn't do anything about it.
The most carefree person was Ji Luoxue.
Every day, she would find a comfortable corner and assume a lazy posture. She would open a novel in her hands and immerse herself in the colorful stories. If she was tired of reading, she would find Luo Zheng and ask Luo Zheng to tell her some stories.
Luo Zheng didn't refuse. In any case, in the boundless universe, there were countless stories like this. He just needed to find those that were more famous and more widely spread.
Unexpectedly, after Luo Zheng spoke a few times, more and more people gathered around, including Hua Tianming, Dugu Jian Xiaoxiao and others. Even Ku Deng occasionally came to listen. Luo Zheng joked, "If this continues, it'll be time to charge …"
However, Xuanyuan Chenfeng and Lie Qianhan both had disdainful looks on their faces every time they passed by.
These two originally hated Luo Zheng's previous actions, and now they were even more so …
On this day, when Luo Zheng had just reached the key point, he saw that everyone was looking at him with an incomparably frightened expression.
Luo Zheng's face was full of bewilderment. He wasn't telling a horror story. Moreover, this group of people wasn't afraid of anything. Why were they looking at him with such expressions?
"Luo Zheng … your skin," Ji Luoxue pointed at Luo Zheng himself.
Luo Zheng looked at his hands. His entire skin suddenly turned crimson red. At the same time, the restlessness that had disappeared long ago reappeared …
The Evil Qi erupted!
When Luo Zheng entered the bottom of the Killing Sword Mountain, countless blood-red spiders exploded. The Evil Qi frantically rushed into Luo Zheng's body, but it didn't erupt.
Old Feng said that the Evil Qi hid the secret of the Severing Emotions Divine Path, but Luo Zheng didn't see it.
Naturally, Luo Zheng didn't know that the Evil Qi contained a special kind of poison. This kind of poison wasn't an ordinary kind of poison. It had a deep impact on the martial artist's soul, and the key to cultivating the Severing Emotions Divine Path was this poison.
At this moment, when the Evil Qi erupted, the Evil Qi mixed with the poison rushed from the bottom to the top, rushing towards Luo Zheng's mind!
"Buzz!"
At this moment, Luo Zheng's eyes turned red, and the turbulent Evil Qi erupted!
"Swish swish swish swish swish …"
Ji Luoxue and the others, who had originally gathered around Luo Zheng, distanced themselves from Luo Zheng in an instant.
There was no other reason. Strong Evil Qi could easily be mistaken as killing intent. In fact, people who cultivated Evil Qi were indeed difficult to approach. They looked like dangerous people …
How fast were these Dao Disciples to react?
After distancing themselves, everyone stared at Luo Zheng vigilantly, constantly on guard against Luo Zheng's sudden attack.
Although Luo Zheng's strength was the strongest among these ten people, no matter how strong Luo Zheng was, it was impossible for him to face several people alone. Xuanyuan Chenfeng and Lie Qianhan, who were further away, stared coldly at Luo Zheng. They couldn't wait for something to happen to Luo Zheng. With such turbulent Evil Qi, it would be best if he suffered a backlash.
"What's wrong with Luo Zheng?" Ji Luoxue frowned and asked.
"Should we inform the people of the Demi-Immortal Tribe?" Dugu Jian Xiaoxiao asked.
"Amitabha. Donor Luo is surrounded by Evil Qi. It seems that the sin of killing is too heavy …" Ku Deng put his palms together and started to recite the Rebirth Mantra to purify Luo Zheng's Evil Qi.
It would be better if he didn't purify it. As soon as he was about to purify it, Luo Zheng pounced on Ku Deng like a fierce leopard!
Facing Luo Zheng's bare-handed attack, ordinary warriors would have run away. Ku Deng didn't show any fear on his face. He reached out his hand to Luo Zheng, and a Buddha's hand appeared beside Luo Zheng, firmly holding him in place!
However, this move only managed to slow Luo Zheng down slightly. As Luo Zheng struggled violently, the Buddha's hand was torn apart like a burlap sack. It was instantly torn apart!
At this moment, Ku Deng showed a cautious look. "Donor Luo seems to be crazy. Guys, please help him …"
Ji Luoxue moved lightly, and with a flick of her slender fingers, the Five Elements Law connected to each other and formed a net, covering Luo Zheng in place. Jiang Zhengyi, who didn't get along well with the others, suddenly clapped his hands, and a big golden bell fell down on Luo Zheng!
The others also took action. They didn't hurt Luo Zheng, but just wanted to bind Luo Zheng in place.
In the end, Luo Zheng wasn't able to break free from the joint forces of these Daoists. Even though he was venting his strength in a frenzy, it was still futile …
After a long time, the manic feeling in Luo Zheng's heart gradually disappeared, and his eyes gradually became clear. Seeing that everyone was looking at him as if he was facing a great enemy, he was puzzled.
"You seem to be controlled by your own Evil Spirit." Ji Luoxue showed a strange look. "In my impression, you don't seem to be a bloodthirsty person. Where did you collect so much Evil Spirit?"
"When Luo Xi Xuan comes, let her report this matter to her superiors," Jiang Zhengyi said coldly. He was paranoid about justice, so he didn't have a good impression of Luo Zheng. It was as if he wanted to kill Luo Zheng.
"No need." Hua Tianming smiled. "This is Luo Zheng's cultivation method …"
"Cultivation method?" Dugu Jian Xiaoxiao was puzzled. "Luo Zheng's main cultivation method is Evil Spirit?"
The main cultivation method of Evil Spirit was all dangerous and evil. It could be achieved in a short period of time, but it could never be achieved. Sooner or later, it would collapse. People who cultivated this kind of cultivation method were destined to not go far, and they couldn't have Luo Zheng's current achievements. Dugu Jian Xiaoxiao naturally didn't believe it.
"It's just a cultivation method," Hua Tianming replied indifferently.
Luo Zheng was also puzzled. He remembered the feeling of agitation he felt just now. Could he cultivate the Emotion Severing Divine Dao with this feeling? Could he open the last lotus leaf? This was impossible, but Hua Tianming said so, and so did Old Man Feng. He didn't have a choice. He couldn't expel the Evil Spirit.
After a day, Luo Zheng's Evil Spirit erupted again. This time, it was even more ferocious. Everyone had no choice but to subdue him again.
With this disturbance, everyone had something to do. While listening to Luo Zheng's story, they also had to be on guard against Luo Zheng's sudden attack. This situation was very strange …
This lasted for six or seven days. The Demi-Immortal clan finally brought Xi Youqin here.
Xi Youqin also went through the blood exchange. Her face was very pale. She was deceived into coming here. If she knew that the blood exchange would be so painful, she wouldn't have agreed. In the end, in order to truly reunite with Luo Zheng, she accepted it.
As soon as she arrived in the valley, she saw everyone besieging Luo Zheng. She screamed, and a touch of anger appeared on her pale face. She rushed down.
＝＝＝＝＝

Chapter 1411
Over the past few days, Luo Zheng had become more and more irritable with each eruption of the baleful aura that contained poison.
But gradually, he began to understand Chen Huang Yijian's intentions.
The Emotion Severing Divine Dao was to completely eliminate one's own emotions. However, even Buddha had times when he was angry. As a living being, it was unrealistic for a martial artist to completely eliminate all emotions!
Unless Luo Zheng was a hard rock, then he was truly emotionless.
The complete Emotion Severing Divine Dao was almost non-existent. Even though Luo Zheng's talent was top-notch, and he was gradually approaching the Emotion Severing Divine Dao by relying on his own feelings, he was still lacking a bit …
Chen Huang's Yijian used a very special method.
That special baleful aura should have been created by Chen Huang Yijian himself. He used the baleful aura to fill his body, completely turning himself into a person who only knew how to kill.
In this state, Luo Zheng had completely blocked off his own emotions. His perception of external objects was infinitely reduced.
At this moment, he didn't even know whether he was a human, a ghost, or something else.
So in his agitation, he attacked everyone. Whether it was Hua Tianming, Ji Luoxue, or even Lie Qianhan, whom he hated very much, he treated them all equally and attacked them indiscriminately.
When there were no fluctuations in one's emotions, it was called emotion severing. To put it bluntly, Luo Zheng had become a stone that only knew how to kill. He didn't know the meaning of his killing, nor did he understand the meaning of killing his target.
Old Man Feng should have long expected that he would have this kind of reaction. He threw him here and let him torment himself. In any case, the other guys weren't so easy to provoke, so nothing bad would happen.
However, at this stage, Luo Zheng wasn't familiar with the method of severing emotions with baleful aura. When he became irritable, he could indeed sever emotions, but he couldn't control himself.
As he went berserk again and again, Luo Zheng's consciousness slowly became independent.
For example, at the beginning, he was like the most primitive beast. After losing consciousness, he completely relied on instinct to charge at Hua Tianming and the others.
But now, after Luo Zheng's eyes turned red, he could control his actions to a certain extent.
By the time Luo Zheng could control himself with ease, he would have stepped into the Emotion Severing Divine Dao.
Today, Luo Zheng had already learned how to draw his sword. Of course, he was still unable to use the Godly Yi Sword. Hua Tianming and the others had already gotten used to it. They seemed to treat it as their own training. Seeing that the situation was not right, they all used their own methods to restrain Luo Zheng.
However, something unexpected happened today.
Xi Youqin wasn't good at thinking too much. Many of the ideas in her mind often didn't turn around, especially when she was facing Luo Zheng.
At first glance, Xi Youqin thought that these guys were bullying Luo Zheng by relying on their numbers.
How could she look at such a scene?
As his figure surged, he forcefully endured the side effects of Blood Exchange. His light pink sleeves fluttered as four swords shot out!
"Whoosh! Whoosh!
Whoosh! "Xi Youqin's strength hadn't been fully released in the Illusory Battlefield. In a hurry, she didn't know when to relax. The four swords instantly fell around Luo Zheng and formed a defensive sword array, immediately isolating Hua Tianming and the others!
This sword array was so fierce that even Hua Tianming and the others were very afraid of it. They could only retreat first.
Then, Xi Youqin stood firmly in front of Luo Zheng. Facing the others, she frowned and said angrily, "You have so many people, and you're bullying Luo Zheng! Do you want to die? "Her words were very rude. She had never taken these Heavenly Legends and Dao Disciples seriously.
Ji Luoxue curled the corners of her mouth, and a trace of playfulness and mockery appeared on her cold and beautiful face.
Ku Deng and Jiang Zhengyi didn't say anything.
Hua Tianming knew Xi Youqin's character. His eyes flashed, and he helplessly reminded her, "Be careful of your back..."
"What?"
Xi Youqin was stunned. Then, she very sluggishly sensed the surging murderous aura behind her.
When he turned his head to look, he saw a blue sword coming straight at his face!
Xi Youqin would never have imagined that Luo Zheng's sword would stab at her. In fact, she had already formed four swords. She could block Luo Zheng's sword with a raise of her hand.
After all, Luo Zheng hadn't completely controlled himself in this state, so he couldn't be considered very powerful.
However, Xi Youqin's way of thinking was very different from ordinary people. Or rather, it was very easy for her to fall into emotions, especially when facing Luo Zheng.
She didn't dodge or block. Instead, she thought very sadly about why Luo Zheng wanted to assassinate her.
I wonder if he will tell me the truth before he dies, she thought to herself.
The sword wasn't slow. In an instant, it was only a few inches away from Xi Youqin. Just as it was about to pierce through Xi Youqin without hesitation, Luo Zheng suddenly roared. His left hand clenched into a fist, and he abruptly punched his right hand that was holding the sword!
The strength was so great that the Thunder and Wind Divine Sword flew out of his hand. The tip of the sword also moved away from Xi Youqin's face. However, as the sword flew out, the blade circled back and cut off a section of hair by Xi Youqin's ear. The hair fluttered in the wind. It was a very close call.
"Clang!"
The sword fell to the ground...
Luo Zheng gritted his teeth and panted heavily. He cursed angrily, "Are you an idiot?!"
Xi Youqin stared blankly at Luo Zheng. Her eyes were filled with a strange color. At the same time, she felt very wronged. She was almost killed by Luo Zheng just now, and now he was calling her an idiot...
In the Illusory Battlefield, she didn't have many opportunities to interact with Luo Zheng. Perhaps in this man's heart, she was no longer important?
"You... what do you mean?" Xi Youqin's eyes suddenly turned red. She and her master had come from afar and endured the pain of blood exchange. When they finally saw Luo Zheng, he actually had such a reaction.
Every time the baleful aura's poison broke out, it would last for a considerable period of time. Luo Zheng had gradually been able to control it in the past few days. However, it was very strenuous for him to desperately suppress the baleful aura and not let himself get lost.
After all, Luo Zheng was trying to control himself while adapting to the baleful aura in order to comprehend the Emotion Severing Divine Dao. But now, for the sake of Xi Youqin's safety, he was suppressing the baleful aura. This was the opposite of the Dao.
Luo Zheng waved his hand. He resisted the restlessness in his heart. He gritted his teeth and said, "Can someone please take her away first..."
"Whoosh!"
Ji Luoxue's figure flashed and made a "please" gesture. She had also widened her horizons. She had never seen such a stupid woman. It seemed that she couldn't see Luo Zheng's baleful aura at all. She didn't even bother to explain to Xi Youqin.
At this time, no matter how stupid Xi Youqin was, she understood something. Her face alternated between red and white, which made her look very cute.
After a long time, the baleful aura in Luo Zheng's body settled down again. He finally returned to normal. With a trace of anger on his face, he strode towards Xi Youqin. "Don't you know that what you did just now was very dangerous?"
Just now, he had almost killed Xi Youqin with his sword. Luo Zheng himself was scared.
Xi Youqin's face revealed an aggrieved expression, like a child who had done something wrong. She also knew that it was her fault. She didn't dare to be stubborn in front of Luo Zheng. "I didn't see clearly..."
Seeing Xi Youqin's pale face, Luo Zheng also knew that she had gone through a blood change. A trace of pity appeared in his eyes. After all, she had good intentions. However, in this world, there were too many people who did bad things with good intentions. They even put themselves in danger.
Luo Zheng originally wanted to reprimand her, but he swallowed it back. Instead, he reached out and touched her hair. "Don't be like this next time."
"Oh..." Xi Youqin curled her lips and nodded. Then, a bright smile appeared on her face. She rushed up on tiptoe and hugged Luo Zheng's waist. She gently sniffed Luo Zheng's scent.
Luo Zheng also knew that Xi Youqin's promise was completely unreliable. She didn't have a deep memory of being taught a lesson. In the end, she could only sigh helplessly...
As for Xuanyuan Chenfeng and Lie Qianhan, who were not far away, when they saw this scene, the two of them snorted coldly at the same time.
Now, apart from Qing Yu, the other nine people in the top ten of the Illusory Battlefield had all arrived.
However, there was still no movement from the Demi-Immortal Clan.
Xi Youqin couldn't wait for someone to disturb her. She felt that the way Luo Zheng cultivated the Emotion Severing Divine Dao was strange. The next day, she also joined in. However, when she made a move, she often couldn't control her strength. Soon, she was driven to the side to watch the show.
Luo Zheng's control of this state was becoming clearer and clearer. He was becoming more and more skillful. It was only a matter of time before he completely became a Dao Child.
At this time, the Demi-Immortal Clan finally sent someone over. It was Old Man Feng who summoned the nine people to discuss matters.

Chapter 1412
Everyone had been stuck here for quite some time.
When they heard Old Man Feng's summons, they realized that they could finally leave this damned place.
Under the guidance of the Demi-Immortal Clan warrior, they met up with Luo Xi Xuan and continued to move forward.
This trip was quite secretive. Luo Zheng and the others also felt that it was strange, as if someone was following them. Luo Xi Xuan led everyone into and out of the space tunnel, repeating this process dozens of times.
Then, they entered a big city and continued to dive into the cellar of a residence. After going through thousands of twists and turns, they finally reached their destination.
In this huge underground cave, there were traces of man-made carvings everywhere. Many Demi-Immortal Clan warriors were shuttling back and forth. They looked very busy, as if something was about to be completed.
At the end of the cave, there were copper pillars that were more than ten feet wide. These copper pillars did not support the ceiling of the cave and did not seem to have the function of bearing weight.
Xuanyuan Chenfeng and Lie Qianhan were already impatient.
"Luo Xi Xuan, what do you want to do by bringing us so deep underground?" Xuanyuan Chenfeng asked.
Luo Xi Xuan turned her head and glanced at Xuanyuan Chenfeng indifferently. "We'll be there soon. You will know soon."
When everyone passed through the copper pillars, Xuanyuan Chenfeng's eyes lit up and he immediately rushed out!
At the end of the copper pillars, there were layers of oval-shaped stairs, and on the stairs stood more than twenty people.
These twenty people were the strongest existences in the universe. These people could almost change the entire universe. These twenty people were all Heavenly Venerables.
Xuanyuan Chenfeng was excited because he saw his family's ancestor. "Grandpa!"
Heavenly Venerables were also divided into ranks. The strength of these twenty Heavenly Venerables varied, and their ranks were also different …
The one standing at the highest point was the Patriarch of the Xuanyuan Clan, Xuanyuan Chenfeng's father, the leader of Gods Without Thought, Original Sin Heavenly Venerate!
Of course, other than Xuanyuan Chenfeng, Ji Luoxue and Lie Qianhan's fathers were also there.
Original Sin Heavenly Venerate just nodded at Xuanyuan Chenfeng indifferently. "Chenfeng is here."
When Xuanyuan Chenfeng saw so many Celestial Venerables standing together, he didn't go over. Instead, he stood with Hua Tianming and the others.
Lie Qianhan and Ji Luoxue also greeted their own Patriarchs. Now that so many Celestial Venerables had gathered here, it looked like this was going to be an extremely important meeting. As juniors, it was naturally better for them to behave themselves. However, with so many Celestial Venerables present and the atmosphere extremely solemn, these ten juniors were still a little nervous.
Among the ten juniors present, perhaps only Luo Zheng and Xi Youqin looked the most indifferent.
After all, Luo Zheng had seen such a scene before. Back when he was besieged, his complexion was not much better. Now, he was indifferent. Moreover, this group of Celestial Venerables should not make things difficult for him. It was just that Xi Youqin's actions made him feel rather helpless.
"This!"
A small golden plate appeared in Xi Youqin's hand. On the plate was a strangely shaped purple fruit. It was a specialty of the Extreme Purple Realm, the Fragrant Caltrop Fruit … She had brought a lot of food from the Extreme Purple Realm this time.
Along the way, Xi Youqin had taken out no less than 40 to 50 different kinds of food from her Sumeru Ring. While they were hurrying along, she had constantly fed Luo Zheng. However, feeding him now was somewhat inappropriate.
Seeing Luo Zheng wave his hand, Xi Youqin didn't seem to care about her question at all. "Is it not tasty?" Therefore, in an instant, she took out a small brocade pouch and fished out a fruit with thorns all over. "This is the Golden Chestnut. It's tastier than the Fragrant Caltrop Fruit. I'll give it to you …"
Luo Zheng was speechless. He could only wave his hand to stop Xi Youqin. Seeing that Xi Youqin didn't have the slightest comprehension and was about to change to another type of food, Luo Zheng could only stop her with a true essence sound transmission …
After Luo Zheng's reminder, Xi Youqin looked around and found that there were many powerful figures staring at her. She stuck out her tongue and hid behind Luo Zheng with an apologetic expression.
Not long after, Old Man Feng's figure also appeared.
The Demi-Immortals were a unique race in the universe, and because they didn't have too many conflicts with other races, they maintained good relations with other races and organizations. Because of that, Patriarch Limitless had gathered all of the races in the universe together, even those who normally wouldn't compromise with each other.
Old Man Feng had his back to the group of juniors. Facing the many Empyreans, he said, "I believe that everyone has already understood this Old Man Feng's prophecy. This prophecy has come true … Ai, this Old Man Feng is also very reluctant!"
"This great calamity has already begun several epochs ago. You can't be blamed for it." Empyrean Original Sin was the first to speak.
"Humph, this is nothing but some foreshadowing that was buried in the ancient sorcerer race. Those dregs that live in the dark side of the world, besides running all over the place, what else can they do? If we join forces, wouldn't it be easy to exterminate them? "The demon race's Empyrean Demondawn loudly said.
Empyrean Demondawn was a guest that the Demi-Immortal race didn't welcome.
The Demi-Immortal race had good relations with most other races, but they didn't like the demon race. They had even had conflicts with the demon race before. However, now that the universe was facing a great examination, the Demi-Immortal race still invited the demon race.
Now, a small crisis had erupted in the universe.
Countless martial artists had already spread the word of this crisis, making it seem extremely strange and evil. But, many Empyreans were well aware that this was the doing of the ancient sorcerer race.
This was an ancient race. They carried a powerful curse on their backs and wandered the dark side of the world.
Because they couldn't appear in the true world, this race was able to escape the great calamity 3.6 billion years ago. But, the powerhouses of the Heavenly Dao didn't take them seriously …
Just like Empyrean Demondawn had said, the most troublesome aspect of the ancient sorcerer race was that they traveled through the dark side of space. Moreover, their influence was sparsely distributed and their life form was completely different from that of humans. If they destroyed one, they would quickly expand in another.
However, if the entire universe joined forces, wanting to clean up the ancient sorcerer race wouldn't be too difficult.
It was nearly impossible for all the races of the universe to join forces. But now that they were facing a similar crisis, they could consider doing so.
"Don't underestimate the ancient sorcerer race." The one who spoke was a beautiful woman.
The Celestial Venerables present had lived for at least tens of millions of years. Most of them looked like middle-aged uncles or old men. Most of them looked like old men. Only this woman looked very young. Her entire body seemed to be made of water. Her figure was hidden in a black robe. It was hard to tell if her figure was good or bad, but it shouldn't be bad.
"After the ancient sorcerer race was cursed, they weren't considered a powerful force. We've always overlooked them. But, there's one thing we can't compare with them," the woman continued.
"What do you mean, Empyrean Oracle?" The Ji Family Ancestor asked.
So this was Empyrean Oracle. Luo Zheng's eyes flashed.
Of the three great human forces, the leader of the Human Alliance was Empyrean Oracle. Who would have thought it would be a woman?
Empyrean Oracle's eyes flashed. "Although they're neither human nor ghost, there's no doubt that they're the race that understands the universe the most. No one in the universe can compare with them, not even the Demi-Immortal race!"
Old Man Feng nodded. "What Empyrean Oracle says is true! Although the ancient sorcerer race has been cursed, they've inherited so many eras. Their understanding of the universe is far greater than ours. If they really want to stir up trouble, it'll be a headache! "
"Even so, it'll be a headache at most. But each and every one of you is like the universe is about to be destroyed!" Empyrean Demondawn coldly said.
Empyrean Original Sin lightly said, "Our opponent isn't just the ancient sorcerer race."
"Then who else is there!?" Empyrean Demondawn was somewhat annoyed. His demon race seemed to be the slowest, as if they didn't know anything …
"It's a god … a True God." Old Man Feng replied.
Hearing Old Man Feng's words, Empyrean Demondawn turned mute.
If a True God wanted to destroy the universe, then they would be in great trouble …

Chapter 1413
From the boundless universe to the entire universe, and then to the Divine Realm, this was an orderly and orderly world.
Generally speaking, there were very few True Divinities that would descend to the Divine Realm; this was not permitted.
There were legends of True Divinities that ascended to the Divine Realm from their own universe, but in the end, those were only legends. Up until now, no True Divinity had ever appeared in the universe.
The True Divinity marks that Luo Zheng saw in the Karma Hall were left behind by True Divinities before they ascended. As for whether or not True Divinities had ever returned to their own universe, Luo Zheng wasn't sure. His understanding of this world wasn't deep enough and he could only learn a thing or two from the debates of these Empyreans.
"Because those True Divinities did not return from the Divine Realm." Old Man Feng continued to say, "They came from another layer of heaven, another universe."
"What?"
Empyrean Demondawn's eyes widened.
Some of the Empyreans already knew of this and had indifferent expressions. But, there were also some Empyreans that were hearing this for the first time and couldn't hide their surprise.
They didn't have a clear enough understanding of the essence of this world, thus they couldn't predict where their future enemies would be.
Old Man Feng reached out a hand and a small dot appeared in the center of the cave.
The moment the dot appeared, it began to expand rapidly. It expanded rapidly and turned into a sphere that was about ten feet in size.
"I don't know the true appearance of the Divine Realm." Old Man Feng said. "Some information is inherited from my blood, but this information isn't complete. I can only tell everyone what I know. As for this sphere in front of you, let's assume that it is the shape of the Divine Realm …"
"The Divine Realm is spherical?" Someone asked.
Old Man Feng shook his head. "It's likely an irregular shape."
That person nodded. Everyone else also listened attentively. Although these Empyreans didn't have the possibility of becoming a True Divinity, they still yearned for the Divine Realm. That was the world of gods.
"In the Divine Realm there are many True Divinities, and there are existences even more formidable than True Divinities …" Old Man Feng continued to say, "They are the same as us. They have a perfect inner world that can circulate within their bodies. Moreover, they have a divine spark!"
As Old Man Feng spoke, his eyes turned towards Luo Zheng and the other youths.
Of these ten people, besides Luo Xixuan who had missed out on a divine spark plate, the other nine all possessed a divine spark plate. Only a divine spark plate could carry a divine spark and become a True Divinity.
"The greatest difference between us and True Divinities is that they possess a divine spark Divinity..................... this so-called True Divinity....................... The power of a True God comes from the power of faith of countless living beings in their inner worlds!
After hearing this, Luo Zheng's face showed an expression of sudden enlightenment.
He was no stranger to the power of faith. In truth, the Empyreans present were also no stranger to it. Some races had already perfectly developed the power of faith and were even extremely proficient in using it. For example, the Yao Night Clan's Pond of Faith was an example. By relying on the three kings of the Pond of Faith, a World Lord could kill an Empyrean!
However, it wasn't that easy to gather faith from the universe. Only the Yao Night Clan was able to gather so much faith power through the piety of their clansmen. For example, forces like Deities Without Thought had only been able to use the power of faith they had collected for many years to forge divine weapons.
But the inner world was different. In the inner world, he was the absolute ruler, the absolute god!
True Gods could condense their Godhood in their inner world and spread it out endlessly, creating more living beings of a higher level and collecting more faith.
"In the god domain, besides the numerous true gods, there are even more powerful existences. In theory, the internal worlds of these people aren't in their bodies." Old man Feng continued. He had deciphered this information from his Heavenly Origin Blood, and also understood his mission.
There were only two portions of Heavenly Origin Blood. One belonged to him, and the other should have belonged to Luo Zheng. This was his blood of destiny!
The golden bloodlines of the Demi-Immortals contained different information, regardless of whether it was heavenly origin blood, earthly origin blood, or even human origin blood. However, there was no doubt that the higher the grade of the blood, the more important the information would be.
"The Inner World isn't in the body? Where is it? "asked an Empyrean.
Old man Feng pointed a finger, and small dots of light appeared on the huge sphere. The dots of light changed shape, transforming into tiny people. Before long, there were about seventy to eighty tiny people standing on the sphere.
"These people represent the supreme existences of the god domain. They are far more powerful than true gods. I don't know how many of them there are," explained old man Feng.
"Buzz buzz buzz …"
At this moment, small red balls began to appear around the ball. These small balls were much smaller than the "Divine Domain", and they all surrounded the "Divine Domain".
"The big sphere represents the god domain, and these small spheres represent the universe." Old man Feng continued.
Some of the sharp-eyed Empyreans had already understood. The number of tiny people on the huge sphere was the same as the ones around the god domain. The Empyrean revealed an expression of sudden realization. "Strange old man Feng, you mean to say that our universe is the internal world of some great character?"
"What do you mean?"
"I understand too! This is a real world, but it is also the internal world of some super expert! "
"We are actually part of the internal world of some great character?"
Some of the Empyreans who didn't know the truth were stunned. Their internal worlds were extremely perfect. Not only did they have thunder and lightning, but they also had sunlight, snow, pastures, countless gods, and even half-step World Lords. Countless martial artists formed from true qi worked hard in their internal worlds. They got married and had funerals, challenging themselves. But in the end, they couldn't escape the constraints of their internal worlds. This was because these Empyreans were the law makers of their internal worlds. They were the masters of their internal worlds!
Now, these Empyreans finally understood. This universe was most likely the internal world of some powerhouse!
"A powerhouse that uses the universe as their internal world is an existence even more powerful than a true god. Their influence covers the entire god domain, and they also possess the ability to be undying and indestructible. This is because their life forms are firmly connected to the universe. They are Saints!" Old man Feng continued.
"Saints!"
"We are the life forms of some Saint's internal world?"
"Heavens …"
These Empyreans were far too familiar with their internal worlds. As they heard Old man Feng's words, they found it hard to believe. So they were also formed from a wisp of true essence!
The Realm of the Gods Empyreans, Original Sin Empyrean, Thunder Law, and the others didn't have any change in their complexions. But, from deep within their pupils, a trace of unwillingness could be seen …
They had indeed become the peak existences of this world, but in the end they couldn't escape this circle. According to this logic, even if there was a channel to leave the universe, to leave this massive 'internal world', they would still disintegrate into bits of true essence and dissipate. The possibility of this happening was extremely high. This was a difficult truth to accept, but one that they had no choice but to accept!
For instance, the powerhouses of these Empyreans' internal worlds, no matter what their cultivation was, as long as they left their internal worlds, they would revert back to true essence. This was the restriction brought about by the rules. This sort of erasure was impossible to resist, no matter how strong one was!
In the universe, only a Dao Child had the qualifications to cross the Heavenly Dao and become a True Divinity. This was because the moment they comprehended the divine dao, they possessed this ability.
Thus, being a Dao Child wasn't a type of strength, but a type of qualification!
And now, the one person in the universe who possessed this qualification was standing in front of these Empyreans.
"This is extremely contradictory." An Empyrean continued to say, "Since we belong to the internal world of some Great Saint, how could this Great Saint allow people from other Heavenly Daos to come here?"
Old man Feng nodded, his expression incomparably cautious. "You're right. In normal circumstances, this sort of situation wouldn't occur. I didn't understand it before, but little friend Hua Tianming has found the answer."
As his voice fell, all eyes fell on Hua Tianming.
With so many Empyreans looking at him, even Hua Tianming felt a great pressure. He nodded to everyone and said, "What if that Saint … is already on the verge of death?"
＝＝＝＝＝

Chapter 1414
Hearing Hua Tianming's words, the many Empyreans on the stage fell silent for a long time.
To them, the Divine Realm was nothing more than a fantasy world. They knew that only by becoming a True Divinity could they enter the Divine Realm.
But, they didn't know that this universe was actually the inner world of a Saint, and that this Saint was already in a dying state.
If a True Divinity could escape the five declines of the Celestial Being, their lifespan would no longer be limited. Then, who could cause this Saint to die?
Even if they were Empyreans, they still needed time to digest this news.
"Because the Saint is dying, some flaws have appeared in this universe." Hua Tianming paused for a moment and then continued, "I believe that everyone here has seen Softness's methods. She broke through the highest level of the universe's rules and grasped the strength of the tenth level Laws. But, she is not an Empyrean."
Many Empyreans subconsciously nodded.
When Softness fought Luo Zheng, she had indeed displayed this method.
These Empyreans were puzzled. Why was Softness able to accomplish this? Why was she able to grasp the strength of the tenth level Laws?
"The reason is that Softness found this flaw, and this flaw was also discovered by someone from another universe. They are currently trying to find a way to pass through the Good Fortune Wall …" Hua Tianming said.
As he spoke to here, the situation became a bit clearer.
In truth, these Empyreans still had many questions. Who were these invaders? Were they also humans? Or perhaps some other race … if a True Divinity existed, how could they resist?
"Since the other side has a True Divinity helping them, how can we resist?" Empyrean Demondawn sneered.
"Humph, don't you demons always regard dying in battle as glory? The dignified Empyrean Demondawn is afraid now? "An Empyrean taunted.
"You say it so easily. Then, let me ask you, what will we use to resist a True Divinity?" Empyrean Demondawn asked.
Beneath this questioning, everyone remained silent.
Although the universe was vast and there were countless geniuses and even many Empyreans, a True Divinity was still an existence that stood above the rules. They were powerhouses that stood above the Heavenly Dao. It was indeed difficult for them to resist.
"We still have a chance." Hua Tianming blinked his eyes, and a warm smile appeared on his face. He was also puzzled. These Celestial Venerables were, after all, awesome people in the universe. He didn't expect that they would all panic in the face of danger. At this moment, he had to take control of the situation.
Original Sin Heavenly Master, Oracle Heavenly Master, and Old Man Feng looked at Hua Tianming with admiration. Original Sin Heavenly Master was the first to speak. "Yes, we do have a chance. We still have them …"
As he spoke, he pointed at Luo Zheng's group of ten.
"Them? Were they going to rely on these little fellows to fight against a True God? Original sin … Have you become muddle-headed? It's true that they have potential, but forget about the other side's True Gods and Empyreans, any random Sector Lord can pinch these little fellows to death with a single hand! "An Empyrean said.
Old Man Feng, however, shook his head. "We still have time. They can still grow. Furthermore, although that Saint is in a dying state, his inner world will still reject those True Gods. Their powers won't be too strong."
"Yes, time is too important," Oracle Heavenly Master also said.
These nine people, including Luo Xi Xuan, were the most talented Dao Children in the universe. They were the lucky ones that were born from the Heavenly Dao's sense of crisis. And this time, the crisis was a matter of life and death. The Dao Children that were born were exceptionally talented, and the speed of their growth was also quite astonishing!
For some fighters to reach the sector lord level, the time they spent was measured in millions of years.
However, these ten people had completely broken through the speed of their cultivation. Therefore, the "God-level talent" that was once used to measure geniuses was no longer applicable to them! Their cultivation speed was tens of thousands, even hundreds of thousands of times faster than ordinary martial artists!
In fact, this was not only reflected in Luo Zheng's group, but also in every warrior. The God-level geniuses of the past were no longer considered "geniuses" because there were too many of them …
The speed of the entire universe had increased by a lot. This was also a sense of crisis that erupted from that "Saint" before he died. He sped up the evolution of his inner world and released more resources!
Seeing the worried looks on the faces of the ten Heavenly Masters below, Old Man Feng continued, "Not only do we have time … we also have the help of a True God."
"We also have the help of a True God?"
"A True God that came from this universe?"
"Who is he?"
Old Man Feng smiled faintly. "Everyone underestimates our Demi-Immortal race too much. Our Demi-Immortal race was created by the disciples of the Saint. Our mission in this universe is to maintain it. There are some methods that are not suitable to be revealed to everyone now. I only hope that everyone will not lose confidence and fight together with us!"
The Demi-Immortal race was indeed a rather special race in this universe. In the eyes of other races, the Demi-Immortal race appeared inexplicably and had no interest in expanding their power. On the contrary, they often did strange things …
Old Man Feng's explanation made sense.
Just as he finished speaking, a black crack suddenly appeared in the air in the cave!
"Ka ka ka …"
A piece of space was suddenly torn apart!
The entire space in the cave began to fluctuate. Everyone was fluttering along with the fluctuations in space, their bodies twisting and turning …
From that huge crack, a sharp claw covered in cracks suddenly emerged!
From the moment the space was torn to the moment the sharp claw appeared, the entire process was extremely rushed!
"Retreat!"
"Get out of the way!"
Luo Zheng's body floated and he conveniently pulled Xi Youqin up. The sharp claw also slid past Xi Youqin's feet. If he was slower by a blink of an eye, Xi Youqin would not have been able to escape.
"Ultimate Flash!"
Hua Tianming's feet glowed and he also floated away!
"Pa!"
Luo Xi Xuan was even more straightforward, directly turning into a bolt of lightning and landing on the top of the cave. The top of the cave was scorched black, but she stood upside down like a bat, staring at the sharp claws with a look of fear in her eyes.
Xuanyuan Chenfeng, Lie Qianhan, Ji Luo Xue, and the others all used their own methods …
Only Jiang Zhengyi was unable to dodge in time!
Jiang Zhengyi's forte was not speed. What he excelled in was absolute defense and destructive power.
However, his cultivation was still shallow, and he was caught by the sharp claw!
"Kacha!"
The golden light on Jiang Zhengyi's body shattered. That sharp claw was probably from an extremely powerful beast. Its strength was probably above that of a Sector Lord. Not only did it crush Jiang Zhengyi's protective golden light, but it also crushed his body!
Seeing this scene, Luo Zheng and the others all sucked in a deep breath of cold air.
Just now, they were all caught in a dangerous atmosphere. It was so heavy that it made it hard for them to breathe, but …
Didn't they say there was still time? How did the opponent kill his way to the Demi-Immortal Tribe's nest in an instant?
"Boom!"
After the sharp claw tore Jiang Zhengyi apart, it did not stop for a moment. It directly slammed towards Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng's eyes darkened. The strength in his body surged as he wanted to cross space and escape. However, the sharp claw was extremely fast. In the blink of an eye, it was in front of Luo Zheng and was about to crush him.
There were more than twenty Celestial Venerables present.
Just now, they did not expect that someone would dare to make a move here. That was why the claw succeeded!
"Evil creature!"
Heavenly Venerate Oracle was the first to react.
He unsheathed his longsword, and a sharp sword light flashed!
"Shua!"
The strength of this sword light was very subtle. After a thin line appeared on the wrist of the claw, the entire claw fell off. The cut was so bright that it could be seen clearly.
"Ancient Sorcerer Tribe, how dare you!" Heavenly Venerate Original Sin's face was filled with anger. He turned around and escaped to the other side of the space!
Old Man Feng laughed coldly. His eyes were filled with rage. Jiang Zhengyi was not as important as Luo Xi Xuan, but he was still one of the ten Dao Children. He did not expect that he would be crushed to death here. Old Man Feng's hands turned into claws and grabbed at the crack, pulling it back!
The wrist that had been cut off by Heavenly Venerate Oracle wanted to retract back into the crack.
An invisible pulling force forcibly pulled the broken wrist and the owner of the broken wrist out from the crack.
"Boom!"

Chapter 1416
Luo Zheng's heart skipped a beat.
Luo Zheng hadn't told anyone about the nine divine dragons and the Cauldron of Divine Flame.
Old Man Feng had mentioned it once, but Luo Zheng had thought it was more likely to be the Ancestral Dragon. Now, Luo Zheng understood that these people seemed to know him better than he knew himself.
He was a little depressed, but it wasn't a bad thing for him.
Besides, if these celestial masters wanted to harm him, they wouldn't have taken so many detours.

Old Man Feng nodded, appearing very patient.
However, before Luo Zheng could ask the Azure Dragon, his voice had already been transmitted over.

"How did the Demi-Immortal Tribe know of your existence?" Luo Zheng asked curiously.
Qinglong was silent for a while, then said, "They're pawns set up by the Sage's disciple, and we True Dragons are the Sage's contracted beasts. It's not strange that they know of our existence."
The True Dragons were the Sage's contracted beasts?
Then wasn't the entire True Dragon Realm a giant "Spirit Beast Pouch"?
This was Luo Zheng's own speculation.
Although this speculation was somewhat unreliable, the most unreliable thing had happened. Such a vast universe was only the Sage's inner world, so it was understandable that the True Dragon Realm, as an independent great world, was regarded as a Spirit Beast Pouch.
What kind of creature was this Sage? Luo Zheng couldn't help but sigh inwardly.
The more he learned, the stranger this world became. Nothing was like what he saw on the surface.
Under everyone's watchful eyes, Luo Zheng walked toward the crystal coffins.
Old Man Feng was also very cautious. The soul essences in these crystal coffins were all living creatures. There were ferocious beasts, and there were also the soul essences of various divine beasts. They were all hunted by the Demi-Immortals over the years. If they were released, they could easily devour Luo Zheng's soul.
The value of these soul essences was difficult to estimate. Even the leaders of the major factions, such as Oracle and Original Sin, couldn't help but be moved at the sight of so many soul essences. Over the years, the Demi-Immortals had hunted down so many soul essences in the universe that even the three major factions of the human race paled in comparison.
"Relax, I will erase the souls in these soul essences …"
As soon as Old Man Feng's voice fell, he extended two oddly long fingers and lightly flicked one of the crystal coffins open.
The moment the crystal coffin was opened, all kinds of ghostly wails and howls could be heard. A turbulent aura surged out from the coffin and rushed toward Luo Zheng!
If the souls in the soul essences were erased, the soul essences would gradually dry up and turn into soul crystals. Although soul crystals were very beneficial to the soul, their effects were greatly reduced. After these soul essences were captured, they were nurtured by the Demi-Immortals.
The moment the soul essences surged out, Old Man Feng's eyes flashed and a jade ruler suddenly appeared between his eyebrows.
The Pure Glass Jade Ruler was also the Old Patriarch's life treasure.
After the jade ruler appeared, it suddenly swung toward the soul essences!
Every time it swung, it was accompanied by a sharp howl. Even if there was only one soul essence left, these souls were unwilling to be completely erased. However, in the face of the Old Patriarch, these souls had no room to resist!
Under Old Man Feng's control, the erased soul essences turned into a gentle orange light and shot toward Luo Zheng's head. In an instant, they entered Luo Zheng's mind.
Pouring soul essences into the body was originally a method of cultivating the soul.
However, this method of cultivation was too extravagant for many martial artists.
Even a single Empyrean would find it difficult to collect them. This was the accumulation of countless years of the Demi-Immortals' accumulation of soul essences. Just the consumption of these soul essences was enormous.
After the soul essences entered Luo Zheng's body, they turned into a stream of spring water and spread toward the depths of his soul.
"Hua …"

These nine true dragons had a great demand for soul essences!

As the soul essences continued to pour in, the nine true dragons circling above the Divine Flame Cauldron began to reveal their colors!
Green, crimson, blue, white, purple, black, yellow …
For a moment, the true dragons on the surface of the Divine Flame Cauldron emitted dazzling colors!
The most dazzling of them all was the golden true dragon in the center!

This dragon roar contained a boundless emotion. It spread out from Luo Zheng's mind and filled the entire cave!

As Empyrean Demondawn spoke, the Empyreans of the other races also had deep expressions.
The reason the Demi-Immortal Clan had summoned the leaders of the other races was to deal with the greatest crisis in the universe.

If this universe collapsed, they would not be able to cross the Heavenly Dao and escape the shackles of the Heavenly Dao. That was a high-level technique of the Space Law. In the same space, two types of energy vibrated together, causing terrifying damage!
"Pa pa …"
The black figures quickly exploded and turned into black powder that scattered on the ground.
However, the black powder instantly danced and turned into a few fist-sized skulls that bit towards the Ninth Palace Lord!
The Ninth Palace Lord slapped out again. Although the vibration was powerful, it couldn't hurt the small skulls at all!
The small skulls rushed up and bit the Ninth Palace Lord's back. Their white teeth constantly gnawed at the Ninth Palace Lord's Protective True Yuan. This was the curse of the Ancient Shaman Race!
"Let's go!"
The Ninth Palace Lord had no time to care about anything else. The Sacred Lord had ordered him to protect Ning Yudie, so he had to bring her to a safe place.
The two shuttled through the Sacred Land and finally entered an underground palace. Along with them were the core disciples of the Jade Pure Sacred Land.
This underground palace was a refuge built by the Jade Pure Sacred Land in order to deal with unexpected situations.
At this moment, thousands of core disciples had already poured into the palace!
Beautiful divine runes appeared one after another, forming a beautiful cross above the palace. This place had the largest divine rune array in the Jade Pure Sacred Ground.
"Boom, boom …"
Accompanied by a few black lightning bolts, more than a dozen giant skulls suddenly smashed down. Countless black figures rushed towards the underground palace.
At this moment, two slender energy tentacles extended from the divine rune array!
These two energy tentacles waved around like two whips. The power was extremely terrifying. With a casual sweep, they killed hundreds of Ancient Shamans. However, after these Shamans died, they immediately turned into a curse. Small skulls flew up and gnawed at the divine rune array!
These small skulls were very special. Back then, Luo Zheng had encountered them in the Illusory War. He had tried everything to get rid of them, but he couldn't. In the end, after entering the second stage, the pile of small skulls disappeared on their own.
Although the two slender energy tentacles were extremely powerful and constantly sent the small skulls flying, they flew back in an instant and continued to gnaw at the divine rune array.
In the underground palace …
The faces of the core disciples turned deathly pale.
Most of these disciples had participated in the Illusory War before, but this was not the Illusory War. If they couldn't escape today, they would really die.
The Ninth Palace Lord leaned against a huge pillar. The small skulls behind him were still gnawing at his Protective True Yuan.
"Are you okay?" Ning Yudie looked at the Ninth Palace Lord's back.
The Ninth Palace Lord shook his head. "Pa!" He pinched open a Zhen Yuanyu and began to absorb the True Yuan within.
Under the crazy gnawing of the small skulls, the Ninth Palace Lord's Protective True Yuan was quickly consumed. After breaking through the Protective True Yuan, the small skulls began to gnaw on his flesh!
Even with the replenishment of the Zhen Yuanyu, it couldn't make up for the consumption of the small skulls.
Soon, blood spurted out from the Ninth Palace Lord's back. The small skulls had bitten open Ninth Palace Lord's back.
Seeing this scene, Ning Yudie's brows furrowed. Cold air shot out from her hand and condensed a thick layer of ice on the surface of the small skulls, completely freezing them. However, the ice didn't last more than a breath of time.
"Kacha …"
Cracks quickly appeared on the thick layer of ice. Soon, the small skulls completely devoured it and continued to gnaw at the Ninth Palace Lord's body.
However, the Ninth Palace Lord's Protective True Yuan had recovered by 30%. He could hold on for a while …
"Ahhh! Ahhh! "
At this moment, not too far away, a martial artist let out a pitiful scream. A martial artist tumbled around and fell into a pool of blood.
Ning Yudie raised her head and looked over. This cultivator was also an Extreme Divine Realm cultivator, but there were two small skulls constantly gnawing at his back. His body was a bloody mess, and his blood was splattered all over the ground. It was a ghastly sight!
The Ninth Palace Lord was a Divine Transformation Realm martial artist, yet he was unable to resist these small skulls. There was no need to even mention the Divine Realm martial artist. Not every martial artist had a body as abnormal as Luo Zheng's, allowing these small skulls to gnaw on his body.
In the blink of an eye, the martial artist's entire back had been bitten open. The smell of blood filled the underground palace …
"Get out of my way!"
A nearby martial artist couldn't bear to watch any longer and smashed the two small skulls away. However, after circling around in the air, the small skulls still persevered and turned around, once again gnawing at the Divine Realm martial artist's back!
This sort of situation could be seen everywhere in the underground palace. In the battle just now, many martial artists had tried to kill members of the Ancient Sorcerer Race. However, no one thought that killing a member of the Sorcerer Race would have such serious consequences. If this continued, they would be gnawed to pieces by these small skulls!
"What should we do …"
Ning Yudie's face revealed a trace of panic. She almost reflexively thought of Luo Zheng.

Chapter 1420

Chapter 1421

The Celestial Venerables of the universe all possessed powerful divination abilities.

For example, there were only 108 Crowns of Heaven's Will. When one Crown of Heaven's Will appeared in the universe, that Crown of Heaven's Will would be unique. If the Celestial Venerables were to calculate any martial artist without any unique characteristics, the probability of that martial artist being the case was almost infinitely small …

Ordinary soul searching techniques would cause irreversible damage to the soul. In fact, Luo Zheng had thought of letting Old Man Feng directly search his soul. Even if his soul was damaged, he wanted Old Man Feng to find Ning Yudie's location.

There were some people in the universe who had the ability to read souls. For example, the Holy Master of the Jade Pure Holy Land could verify the truth of Ning Yudie's words without harming Ning Yudie.
Luo Xi Xuan also had this ability, and her ability was one level higher than that of the Holy Master.

Chapter 1422

After flying on the surface of the sea for a day and a night, they encountered some ferocious beasts along the way.
However, these ferocious beasts sensed the powerful auras of the ten or so people above them and hid in all directions, sinking into the sea …
Lie Qianhan and Xuanyuan Chenfeng, on the other hand, were bored. Along the way, they casually killed some ferocious beasts, causing a huge commotion.
When the sun rose the next day, a white line appeared in front of Luo Zheng. That white line continued to enlarge in everyone's eyes. It was a snowy mountain standing on the surface of the sea.
Seeing these snowy mountains, Luo Zheng's face revealed a trace of a smile. That was the great snowy mountain west of the Central Domain. They had arrived at their destination.
After crossing the snowy mountain, the group did not stop at all. They continued to head east and found the first big city, Xuandao City …
This big city was built by the Xuandao Mansion. Although the Xuandao Mansion's influence was not as strong as before, this big city had a space passage that could be used.
"Swoosh swoosh …"
Ten or so figures descended rapidly from the sky and landed in front of the space passage.
The few guards in front of the space passage were instantly stunned when they saw this scene …
They completely did not know the origins of the ten or so people in front of them. The key was that each of their auras was shockingly powerful, and their cultivations were unfathomable.
Fortunately, these people didn't have any hostility and had restrained their auras. Otherwise, these guards would have fainted on the spot.
"What … what … do you want to do?" The guard in the lead was slightly braver, and he stuttered as he asked.
"We're borrowing the space passage to Ocean of Clouds City." Luo Zheng glanced indifferently at them, and then he flicked his hand, causing two top-grade True Origin Stones to descend before the guard. After that, he looked straight ahead and stepped into the space passage.
Luo Xi Xuan revealed a trace of a kind smile to those guards, and then they followed Luo Zheng into the space passage.
After these people disappeared, the guards heaved a sigh of relief.
At this moment, Ocean of Clouds City was immersed in silence.
Since Luo Zheng ascended, the situation in the Central Domain had been firmly controlled by the Cloud Hall and the World Business Alliance. Even though other powers were showing signs of rising, like the Void Spirit Sect which had been rebuilt, they were left far behind by these two powers.
In a short period of a few years, two people had ascended from the Cloud Hall. One was Luo Zheng, and the other was the master of the Cloud Hall, Ning Yudie. This was absolutely unprecedented in the history of the Central Domain, and the story of the two of them had become legends that were being spread by countless people.
At this moment, a strand of fluctuation suddenly arose from the space passage between Ocean of Clouds City and Xuandao City. Luo Zheng took a step forward from it, and his gaze descended onto the pure white clouds in the sky. The Cloud Hall was still floating above Ocean of Clouds City.
Actually, in these past few years, Luo Zheng had frequently used the Will of the Vast Thousand Worlds to search through its memories.
He knew everything that had happened in the Central Domain, and he had a strange feeling when he looked at the Cloud Hall.
After all, Ning Yudie had given birth to Lin while he was away, and he was already a father.

Chapter 1423
The teleportation formations in Cloud Sea City had undergone an expansion two years ago.
Cloud Sea City was now considered the center of the Central Domain. The spatial passageways ran from east to west, north to south, and there were as many as 13 large cities. They could be considered the main transportation routes of the entire Central Domain.
Perhaps you didn't need to go to Cloud Sea City, but if you wanted to go to some relatively remote large cities, you had to transit through the spatial passageways in Cloud Sea City.
The sudden appearance of Luo Zheng's group instantly attracted countless gazes!
The guards stationed in the Cloud Hall also surrounded them at the same time. According to the rules, they had to interrogate them in such a situation.
Because of the Cloud Hall, these guards could be considered to have met many important figures here. However, Luo Zheng's group exuded an indescribable aura as soon as they stepped out of the spatial passageway.
This kind of aura was formed by many people mixed together, and it was formed by many experts.
For example, Hua Tianming gave off a feeling of being bathed in the spring breeze just by standing there. It made people feel very comfortable and comfortable, but they also understood that this person couldn't be ignored.
On the other hand, Luo Zheng gave off a feeling of sharpness, like a sword that was always in its sheath. Although its sharpness was hidden, it was still eye-catching!
Ji Luoxue was like a winter plum, cold but not difficult to get close to …
The leader of the guards also braced himself and approached with his subordinates. The leader muttered in his heart. The Central Domain had been peaceful for seven or eight years. Today, another group of existences that we can't afford to offend seemed to have appeared. I'm afraid that something is going to happen again.
If cannon fodder like us offend these experts, we'll probably be annihilated in an instant.

Many years ago, he had met Luo Zheng several times. Moreover, every time Luo Zheng appeared, his cultivation would be much higher.
When he got closer, he immediately saw Luo Zheng. This time, Luo Zheng's cultivation was so strong that he couldn't distinguish it at all …
However, with the experience from a few years ago, this leader tried his best to control his expression. However, his tone was still a little excited and trembling as he said, "Is Lord Luo back?"

"Lord Luo …"
"What Lord Luo?"
"Who is this person?"
The others were still puzzled.
Luo Zheng had ascended for seven or eight years, and he was already a legendary figure in their hearts. They found it very difficult to believe that Luo Zheng had returned.....
However, at this moment, several figures stepped out from another teleportation channel.
It was the members of the Zhuo Clan, and it was Zhuo Bufan who led the group.
... Luo Zheng Zheng as Luo Zheng's........

He had just left the teleportation channel after completing a mission at the Void Spirit Sect when he felt an aura that terrified him.
Zhuo Zheng's expression changed drastically. He saw the appearance of the person in the lead clearly. It was his father's murderer, Luo Zheng!
However, Zhuo Bufan's mental state had been smoothed out over the years. He also knew that Master Zhuo's rebellion had almost destroyed the entire Zhuo family.
No matter if it was forbearance or helplessness, he knew that he was only an insignificant insect to Luo Zheng in the end. So when he saw Luo Zheng again, he wasn't so infuriated anymore, but he was still extremely surprised.
"Luo Zheng?" Zhuo Zheng stood on the spot in a daze.
The Zhuo family cultivators behind him were also stunned when they heard this.
Then, more people recognized Luo Zheng.
"It's really him!"
"He's actually back …"
"Luo Zheng is back!"
Luo Zheng wasn't from Ocean of Clouds City, but it didn't stop him from becoming the glory of Ocean of Clouds City. It could be said that when the cultivators of Ocean of Clouds City mentioned Luo Zheng, they didn't just treat him as an object of reverence, they treated him as something to show off to outsiders.
In the beginning, they were still quite nervous. These people could easily destroy Ocean of Clouds City, so how could they dare to speak carelessly?
However, they weren't so nervous when they heard Luo Zheng. After all, they were from the same family.
Luo Zheng glanced indifferently at Zhuo Bufan. The Zhuo Clan had suffered the punishment it deserved when it rebelled all those years ago, so Luo Zheng wouldn't take it to heart. He nodded indifferently to Zhuo Bufan and said, "Let's go to the Cloud Hall …."
"I'll lead the way," Zhuo Bufan said immediately. He saw Xi Youqin and Hua Tianming in the crowd, and he was shocked once again.
He might not be familiar with Xi Youqin. After all, after entering the Cloud Hall, Xi Youqin only stayed by Luo Zheng's side and rarely went out. However, Hua Tianming … Although this guy was put in an important position by the Cloud Hall, how did his cultivation reach such a level?
It was a level that he couldn't understand even when he looked up ….
Although he was puzzled, Zhuo Bufan didn't speak carelessly. He felt that this group of people was too unusual.
"Haha … this is where you grew up, Luo Zheng?" Lie Qianhan couldn't hold it in any longer.
He and Xuanyuan Chenfeng came here to accompany Ji Luoxue to join in the fun.
These prodigies and Daoists of the four great clans had been isolated in their clans since they were young and rarely went out. Even if they left their clans, the lowest level they had seen was a tenth-rank Holy Land, and almost all the people they saw were above the Divine Sea Realm.
Now, however, they felt that they had come to a very special environment. There were many warriors in this environment, and they were all Xiantian lifeforms. They were unbelievably weak.
"A world of ants," Xuanyuan Chenfeng couldn't help but sneer.
The warriors of the Zhuo clan led the way in front. Hearing these two people's words, Zhuo Bufan and the others felt uneasy. Who were these people? Why did these two guys sound so unfriendly? Could it be that Luo Zheng was kidnapped and came here? These people should be from the Upper Realm!
However, they didn't dare to refute. With their strength, they were indeed not much stronger than ants in the eyes of these people.
"If the two of you continue to talk nonsense, get the hell back to the Upper Realm!"
Xi Youqin turned her head and raised her eyebrows as she reprimanded them.
No matter what, Xi Youqin also came from the Greater Thousand World. These two guys had inexplicably followed the crowd here.
She knew that Luo Zheng didn't like these two guys, but they were still guests from afar. Ning Yudie was being hunted down, and they were here to wait for her. When the time came, they might be able to help. After all, Xuanyuan Chenfeng's strength was not to be underestimated. However, it was inappropriate for him to say such sarcastic words.
Xi Youqin's words were very effective. Lie Qianhan only snorted coldly, and Xuanyuan Chenfeng was silent …
This scene immediately changed Zhuo Bufan's guess. At the same time, he had a whole new level of respect for Xi Youqin. However, they knew too little about the Upper Realm, and their guesses were also very different.
Luo Zheng's appearance spread like wildfire among the warriors.
Originally, there were only a dozen people in the group, but now, there was a mighty crowd following behind them …
Of course, flying in the air required at least the Divine Illumination Realm. Warriors below the Divine Illumination Realm could only look at the sky and sigh. They didn't have the chance to see the first person to ascend from the Central Domain.
Just as Luo Zheng entered the Cloud Hall, he saw a flame curling up not far away. Then, some people wailed, and at the same time, there was a childish laughter.
Luo Zheng had a strange look on his face and asked, "Who can cause trouble in the Cloud Hall?"
The current Cloud Hall could be said to be the best in the Central Domain. Who could easily enter the Sect-Protecting Formation that he had set up back then and cause trouble here?
Unexpectedly, Zhuo Bufan showed a bitter smile on his face. After a while, he said, "You'll know when you see it for yourself."
When Luo Zheng returned to the Cloud Hall this time, he planned to first meet with Granny Yu and settle this group of Daoists down. He would then closely monitor the entire Upper-Class World. No matter where Ning Yudie went against the flow, he would take action immediately.
However, at this moment, out of curiosity, he tiptoed and his figure floated over …
What entered his sight was a child of seven or eight years old. He had a delicate appearance and was dressed in pure gold brocade. There was a Divine Flame Sigil in his hand, and flames were constantly spewing out from the Divine Sigil, burning the house opposite.

Chapter 1425
The Celestial Venerables present did not look too good.

These fellows who lived on the reverse side of the world were best at witchcraft, curses, and poisons …

From his throat came a very unpleasant sound, as if countless pieces of metal were crazily grinding together. "Hahahaha … don't struggle! You're simply dreaming! "He pointed at Luo Zheng and the others.

It was said that long ago, the ancient witch race had angered the gods and were cursed by the gods. This was why they had become neither human nor ghost.
As for whether or not the real reason was the legendary reason, there was no way to verify this. Perhaps only the ancient witch race had records of this.
"Very good. I haven't used the soul searching technique in a long time," Old Man Feng said with an expressionless face.
This ancient witch race member had ridden a Cilie beast to ambush them. He had already been prepared to sacrifice himself. After killing someone in the Demi-Immortal Tribe, and in front of so many Empyreans, it was impossible for him to escape unscathed. He had already disregarded his own life.

Sou!
Almost like a conditioned reflex, this person wanted to flee.

This person was considered a "Great Witch" in the ancient witch race, and a Great Witch was equivalent to a Realm Lord in the universe. It was impossible for him to escape.
Old Man Feng's expression was cold as he walked in front of this Great Witch. When their gazes met, the Great Witch's pitch-black eyes suddenly emitted circles of milky white light. His gaze also became blank. This was a phenomenon that would only occur when one's soul was being searched.
Just as Old Man Feng was searching his soul, a strange smile suddenly appeared on the Great Witch's blank face!
Boom!
An invisible force suddenly slammed into the Great Shaman's chest, and a furious expression appeared on Old Man Feng's face.
The Empyreans also did not understand why Old Man Feng would kill this person after using the soul searching technique.
They saw Old Man Feng reach out a hand and press down on his forehead. With a light flick of his finger, a small black snake was pulled out from his forehead. This small snake continued to circle around Old Man Feng's hand, emitting a hissing sound from its mouth.
Seeing this small black snake, the Empyreans all paled.
"Ghost Weeping Soul Snake?"
This was one of the most insidious methods of the ancient witch race. This sort of Ghost Weeping Soul Snake was attached to the soul of a Great Witch. Normally, it wouldn't be of much use, but if someone used a soul searching technique or a soul attack, they would incite the Ghost Weeping Soul Snake to attack. And, this Ghost Weeping Soul Snake's poison was specifically targeted at the soul. This poison wasn't a potent poison, but once it seeped into the soul it would be impossible to expel. Year after year, it would accumulate in the soul until the day it erupted. Even an Empyrean would not be able to escape.
This Great Witch knew that he couldn't escape death and he also knew that Old Man Feng would search his soul. When he heard Old Man Feng's words, he revealed such an expression in order to lure Old Man Feng into searching his soul. But, he had nearly fallen into Old Man Feng's trap!
This was also because Old Man Feng's soul form was very special. If it were any other Empyrean, they would already be miserable.
As Empyrean Demondawn saw this, his complexion also paled.

Old Man Feng sighed. He ordered people to clean up the scene. After the Cilie Beast was taken away, his eyes finally fell on Luo Zheng. "This Great Witch's target should be Luo Zheng."

"These are the soul essences of various God Beasts." Old Man Feng said. "It's time to awaken the five-clawed golden dragon within your body."

Chapter 1434
