Chapter 1435
For the past few days, Luo Zheng had been sitting upright in the ice palace, closing his eyes and closely monitoring the movements of the entire Vast Thousand World.
After ascending to the universe, Luo Zheng still had a mysterious connection with the Vast Thousand World, but he could not use his power to transfer it.
After returning this time, he once again communicated with the will of the Vast Thousand World, but he found that there were some changes to this will.
The entire Vast Thousand World was diamond-shaped. If one looked down from a high place, it would look like the diamond-shaped scales of a fierce beast. However, this time, Luo Zheng noticed that there was something strange at the top of the diamond-shaped scales, as if a flaw had appeared at the edge of the Vast Thousand World …
What exactly was the will of the Vast Thousand World? Luo Zheng himself could not explain it clearly.
For martial artists, the will was a psychological state produced when they achieved a certain goal. Under normal circumstances, it was expressed through words and actions.
However, for the Vast Thousand World, words definitely did not exist. Otherwise, Luo Zheng would not be able to say anything to him because he was connected to the will of the Vast Thousand World. As for actions …
The only action it took should be when Luo Zheng went to the Divine Kingdom back then. The Vast Thousand World condensed an incarnation, hoping to use it to repair the ascension passage.
In the end, it was the complete opposite. The will of the Vast Thousand World was swallowed up by Luo Zheng, and the ascension passage was repaired by martial artists from the Upper Realm.
After that, the will of the Vast Thousand World did not take any action. It only helped Luo Zheng transfer power.
However, when he returned to the Lower Realm this time, Luo Zheng found a trace of movement.
He did not know if the barrier of this world was destroyed by the Vast Thousand World itself, or if it was a sudden situation.
The will of the Vast Thousand World had entered another neighboring Vast Thousand World, but the range of the will covering the other Vast Thousand World was not wide. Under Luo Zheng's investigation, he only sensed a small area of the vast sea, and there were some relatively common creatures in the sea.
Although Luo Zheng had not been to the other Great Wide World, he thought that it should be similar to the Great Wide World that he was in.
If he had time, Luo Zheng even wanted to take a look at the edge of the Vast Thousand World. Although the barrier had been broken, Luo Zheng seemed to be able to escape into it and cross this Vast Thousand World to enter another Vast Thousand World. This was something that had never happened before.
But at this stage, Luo Zheng naturally wasn't in the mood …
After the other Daoists settled down, they chose to walk around. After all, they had never been to the Lower Realm before. Although they couldn't compare to Huan Yu, they were actually curious about the local conditions and customs here.
As for Xi Youqin, after accompanying Luo Zheng for a day, she chose to go back to the Void Spirit Sect. After all, her family members were still in the Void Spirit Sect. Now that the Void Spirit Sect had been rebuilt, it seemed to be on the rise. If Xi Youqin could go back, she could help the Void Spirit Sect a lot. After all, Xi Youqin was already at the Divine Sea Realm. In terms of strength, no one in the Great Thousand World could be her opponent!
On this day, Luo Zheng was still closely monitoring the entire world …
At the entrance of the palace, a small head popped out. The eyes on the head were bright and sparkling, looking very much like a young Luo Zheng.
Luo Nian's impression of his father only existed in his imagination.
Although Luo Zheng had ascended, his mark had been deeply engraved in every corner of the Cloud Hall. At such a young age, Luo Nian had already heard too many legends about his father.
Once, he had hoped to see his father. When he was six years old, he had said that he would definitely ascend to find his father one day.
But this time, Luo Zheng's return greatly disappointed Luo Nian.
This was the first time his father had met him, but he had actually mercilessly reprimanded him. In his young mind, the heroic image of his father had collapsed, and he had suddenly become a terrifying monster.
It should be known that in the Cloud Hall, no one dared to restrain him.
Previously, when Ning Yudie was still here, she could still restrain Luo Nian. After Ning Yudie ascended, Granny Yu was only blindly arrogant and willful, and he displayed his naughtiness incisively and vividly.
In Luo Nian's young mind, there was only one word left for Luo Zheng: "annoying."
But in the end, Luo Zheng was his father, and he still had an irrepressible curiosity about Luo Zheng.
After sulking for two days, he finally sneaked to the top floor of the Cloud Hall, and secretly sized up Luo Zheng from a corner.
Although Luo Zheng's eyes didn't look over, with his eyes closed, his invisible perception had already enveloped Luo Zheng. Luo Nian's body shape, appearance, and height … were all drawn in Luo Zheng's mind.
Luo Zheng couldn't help but admit that although this kid was naughty, his foundation was quite solid!
Compared to Luo Zheng when he was young, he probably couldn't even catch up with Luo Nian.
After all, the Cloud Hall couldn't be compared to a small Luo family … But then again, although foundation was important, hard work was the decisive factor.
For example, Xuanyuan Chenfeng, Ji Luoxue, and the others. Their foundation was already unmatched in the universe, but this didn't mean that they could easily obtain first place. Their own hard work and hard work were also decisive factors.
Luo Zheng himself was the best example.
So in Luo Zheng's opinion, this kid's character had to be corrected …
But he had just seen Luo Nian, and he had acted so harshly, he was afraid that he had scared him.
In the end, it was the result of Grandma Yu's pampering, so he couldn't be blamed.
Thinking of this, Luo Zheng slightly lifted his knees, and his figure disappeared.
"Huh?"
Luo Nian's eyes were filled with curiosity. His reaction wasn't slow, and he immediately understood that Luo Zheng had noticed him. He turned around, wanting to slip away, but when he looked back, he found that his father was already standing behind him, with a kind smile on his face.
Although Luo Zheng had a smile on his face, Luo Nian still showed a timid expression.
"Luo Nian?" Luo Zheng called out.
"Hm?" Luo Nian subconsciously responded. His eyes turned, and then he timidly called out, "Dad …"
As soon as he called out, Luo Zheng took Luo Nian into his arms.
Luo Zheng had Luo Nian's childhood memories. As long as he wanted to, he could recall anything that happened to anyone in the Vast Thousand Realms at any time.
But these memories belonged to the Vast Thousand Realms, not to Luo Zheng. The feeling of unfamiliarity between him and his son couldn't be eliminated.
Hearing Luo Nian call him "dad," Luo Zheng felt a strange feeling in his heart. He grabbed Luo Nian and said, "I'll take you out!"
He put Luo Nian on his neck, and in a flash, he shot up into the sky!
Although the uncles in the Cloud Hall took care of Luo Nian in every way possible, they weren't his father. At this moment, in Luo Nian's little mind, he finally understood that "dad" could be played like this …
"Haha …"
Flying in the air, they quickly approached the "Light of Abundant Water" barrier.
"We're going to hit it!"
Luo Nian was very familiar with this barrier. It looked like a layer of water ripples, but if they really hit it, it would be terrible.
"Close your eyes," Luo Zheng instructed.
Seeing the blazing white light in front of him, and the power of the Law of Space wrapped around them, Luo Nian closed his eyes. The moment he opened his eyes, he only saw a trace of the remaining blazing white light. The power of the Law of Space had begun to dissipate.
At this moment, Luo Zheng and Luo Nian had already penetrated the barrier …
"Master said the Light of Abundant Water is very powerful. Even if one is proficient in the Law of Space, it's impossible to penetrate it!" Luo Nian said as if he was reciting a book.
"Master? Which master? "Luo Zheng asked curiously.
"My master's surname is Zong!" Luo Nian said.
Luo Zheng immediately knew who Luo Nian's master was. It was Zong Rui, the former chief runemaster of the Cloud Hall.
He didn't expect Luo Nian's Divine Rune Technique to be taught by Zong Rui …
"So high, so high!"
As Luo Zheng flew higher and higher, the clouds below were also left far behind. Those clouds were like a white ocean, slowly rolling below. Luo Nian couldn't help but shout in excitement, completely forgetting how terrifying Luo Zheng was.
"Dad, if we keep flying like this, can we fly to the Upper World?" Luo Nian suddenly asked.
Luo Zheng thought for a moment and answered with a smile, "Yes."
If they could withstand the destruction of the astral wind, they could indeed go all the way up from here and escape into the universe.
"Granny said mother went to the Upper World … Then if we keep flying like this, can we find mother?" Luo Nian asked again.
This kid's thinking …
Luo Zheng smiled slightly. If they didn't take the Ascension Passage and went all the way up along the sky of the universe, ordinary warriors of the Divine Sea Realm didn't know how long it would take to reach the nearest big world.
After all, the little guy was too young to explain this to Luo Nian. He just smiled and said, "Mother will come back."
"When will mother come back?" Luo Nian's eyes suddenly lit up.
Luo Zheng stared at the end of the sea of clouds and said firmly, "It shouldn't be too long. In half a month, Nian 'er will definitely see mother."
He only hoped that nothing would happen to Ning Yudie in the Ascension Passage.

Chapter 1436
When the entire universe was dissected, the space was divided into front and back.
The back of the space was not an independent world, but it complemented the space that people were familiar with, like a shadow following the body...
But the vast majority of creatures did not like to live on the back. It was a place where sunlight could not reach. There was no vital essence, no creatures, and everything was empty.
The Ancient Sorcerer Race was the only race that lived on the back of the world...
This race was too mysterious. Celestial Venerables had investigated their purpose, but in the end, they did not find anything. Not only that, but they also caused trouble, such as having skulls hanging all over their bodies...
Although these little skulls couldn't cause any harm to Celestial Venerables, they couldn't be destroyed or removed. They could only use incomparably troublesome methods to dispel the curse, or even directly banish it. It was still very annoying.
Over time, not many people were willing to provoke this trouble.
What did the Ancient Sorcerer Race rely on to survive, what exactly were they planning? This cultural secret became more and more mysterious with the precipitation of time...
Until recently, when the Demi-Immortal Race revealed the news, a small number of high-level people in the universe knew that this was a conspiracy that spanned more than ten eras, hundreds of billions of years.
In the face of such a time span, even Celestial Venerables were like moths flying for a moment, unable to make any changes.
This was a game that only True Gods and Saints could participate in...
It was a pity that the universe had only one era, the beginning and the end of one era, and had finally come to an end.
It was time to place a piece in this game. That was why the situation had become so intense and painful. At the same time, Huan Yu's own mechanism had become sensitive and powerful. Thus, geniuses appeared from every corner as if they didn't need money.
Among them, the Vast Thousand Domains where Luo Zheng lived was the most peculiar. Not only did it give birth to a first-grade Extreme Purple Yin Body, but it also gave birth to Hua Tianming and Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng thought this was a coincidence.
But the Ancient Sorcerer Race did not think so, and neither did Old Man Feng. In fact, after Luo Zheng left, Old Man Feng had already completed the first round of arrangements...
After all, Luo Zheng's blood flowed in Luo Nian's body. Although the father and son met for the first time, it only took a few days for them to become familiar with each other.
Although the little guy was naughty and mischievous, he was indeed extremely intelligent.
His mind seemed to be filled with countless whys. Staying by Luo Zheng's side, he threw out those whys one by one.
"Father, why do warriors need to cultivate …" Luo Nian asked.
This question sounded very simple, even a little idiotic, but it went straight to the point. Instead, it asked Luo Zheng.
He thought for a moment, then smiled and said, "In order to protect the people around you, if there are bad people who bully Nian 'er, I can help you …"
"But why do we have to ascend? Isn't this world good? "In Luo Nian's mind, the Central Domain was already a huge environment.
For mortals, the vast Central Domain was already boundless. Ascending was indeed not a good choice. The Upper Realm was not a fairyland. Instead, it was a world where competition was even more brutal.
"To become stronger," Luo Zheng continued.
Thus, Luo Nian's question became, "Why did you become stronger …"
This question fell into a loop in terms of logic. It was indeed an unsolvable answer.
If you wanted to find the most basic answer, you would probably have to trace it back to the first batch of creatures born in the chaos. Why was the Domain of the Gods born in the chaos? When did the Saints of the Domain of the Gods develop a sense of competition …
Then, what kind of scene was at the end of the Martial Path?
Luo Zheng would eventually search for the answer, but at the moment, he could not answer Luo Nian's question.
Thus, Luo Zheng very honestly told Luo Nian, "Father doesn't know either."
Luo Nian was a little curious. He felt that Luo Zheng was an omnipotent existence. He did not expect that there was something his father did not know.
"When I find out in the future, I'll tell you!" Luo Nian blinked and said seriously.
Hearing Luo Nian's words, Luo Zheng suddenly smiled. "Searching for the answer is not an easy task."
"Luo Nian is not afraid of difficulties." He was still very serious.
Luo Zheng felt very gratified to have such ambition at such a young age. Perhaps he himself did not understand what he wanted to pursue, but with this persistence, many difficult things would become very easy for him.
A few days passed in the blink of an eye.
On the twelfth day, Luo Zheng still did not detect any fluctuations in the boundless universe.
But he did not expect that Lie Qianhan and Xuanyuan Chenfeng would cause such trouble …
The two of them went to the Central Domain for a stroll. When they returned, their bodies were scratched.
Especially Lie Qianhan. His injuries were very serious. His entire body was covered with small cuts. It looked like he had been injured by a sharp weapon.
Xuanyuan Chenfeng was not much better. He looked very distressed …
"You … where did you run off to?" Luo Zheng was also stunned by this scene.
These two people had a cultivation of the Divine Realm. In addition to the strength of a Dao Child, even a Divine Transformation Realm powerhouse might not be their match. Who in the Central Domain could cause these two people to be so distressed?
Lie Qianhan only snorted coldly and then sat down cross-legged to heal his wounds. If this degree of injury was placed on an ordinary martial artist, it would be very bad. But which Dao Child did not have some sacred medicine on them?
Xuanyuan Chenfeng spoke the truth. "What kind of damned place is this boundless universe! We went all the way north. The storm was so strong that it almost killed us … "
"The storm of the Chaotic Astral Ocean." Luo Zheng immediately understood.
Since ancient times, there had been an extremely strong storm in the Chaotic Astral Ocean. But Luo Zheng himself did not understand the cause of the storm. He was very clear about how strong the storm was. In the past, Luo Zheng did not dare to approach the core of the storm. Even now, he was afraid that he still could not …
These two fellows were confident in their strength. Seeing such a strange scene in the lower realms, they naturally would not miss this opportunity.
Who would have thought that the deeper they went, the more terrifying the wind blades were. Some of the wind blades even contained wind laws up to the eighth level, ninth level … that was already equivalent to the full power attack of a sector lord. How could the two of them block it?
For this reason, Lie Qianhan had to use a defensive treasure passed down to him by the Lie Family to escape unscathed. Not only did he feel heartache, but he also had injuries all over his body …
These days, there were many people in the Central Domain who wanted to visit Luo Zheng.
People from the World Business Alliance, Void Spirit Sect, and even the main family of the Cloud Hall wanted to meet Luo Zheng. But Luo Zheng rejected them all.
All the problems would have to wait until Ning Yudie could return safely …
On the fourteenth day, there was still no movement in the boundless universe.
On the fifteenth day, it was the same …
In the blink of an eye, Luo Zheng returned to the Cloud Hall and waited. Half a month had already passed.
He was getting more and more anxious.
Thinking back to when he was in the Demi-Immortal Tribe, he seemed to be too anxious. He should have asked Old Man Feng to follow along the ascension passage instead of waiting at the other end of the passage.
However, being anxious was only one reason. On the other hand, Luo Zheng had doubts about his fate. That group of people had hidden a lot of important information from him. Even Xun didn't seem to be close to him anymore. At this stage, when Luo Zheng encountered a problem, the first thing he would consider was the motivation he could use.
If Ning Yudie was intercepted by the Ancient Sorcerer Tribe, then he would have waited in vain!
On the sixteenth day after Luo Zheng's return …
Divine Nation Continent …
Divine Nation of Yu the Great was holding a grand celebration at this moment.
The new Divine Nation Monarch was ascending the throne at this moment because the previous Divine Nation Monarch had already ascended.
At this moment, all the cultivators and commoners in the Divine Nation were participating.
The ascension passage of the boundless universe was constantly changing. When a cultivator ascended from the boundless universe, the ascension passage would change direction.
Half a year ago, Ning Yudie of the Central Domain ascended, and the world passage moved to the Central Domain, near Cloud Sea City.
And three days ago, the previous Divine Nation Monarch of Yu the Great ascended, and the ascension passage returned to the Divine Nation Continent …
The new Divine Nation Monarch of Yu the Great was only at the sixth level of the Life and Death Realm.
With the restoration of the ascension passage, the Divine Ocean Realm cultivators squeezed in the Divine Nation ascended to the Upper World. In fact, the strength of the four Divine Nations was not increased, but weakened. After all, the current Monarchs could only be at the Life and Death Realm. Once they reached the Divine Ocean Realm, they would be forced into the ascension passage by the boundless universe.

Chapter 1437
Suddenly, many small black spots appeared in the cracks!
These black dots were falling down at an extremely fast speed ….
"This is a natural phenomenon!"
"God bless the Monarch. This phenomenon coincides with the Monarch's ascension …"
"This is a good omen!"
"Shut up, all of you!" Liu Yang, the Monarch of the Great Yu Divine Kingdom, stared at the black spots, and his face suddenly changed. He had seen clearly that these black spots were many warriors!
Due to the distance, Liu Yang himself could not see the cultivation of these people clearly. But coming from the sky? These people were definitely not good people. How could this be a good omen?
Very quickly, some of the weaker martial artists also saw clearly that those black dots were actually people who were rapidly descending!
At this moment, Liu Yang had seen the leader clearly.
That person seemed to be seriously injured. His body was dripping with blood, and his momentum was waning.
But even so, the waning aura still frightened Liu Yang. This person's strength was not simply unfathomable. Liu Yang had a feeling that this person could kill him with just a flick of his finger!
As for the warriors behind, their cultivation seemed to be slightly weaker, but they were all in the Divine Sea Realm …
This was thousands of warriors in the Divine Sea Realm!
With such a huge army, they could destroy the four Divine Nations with their eyes closed!
They came from the Upper World!
Almost instantly, Liu Yang came to this conclusion in his heart.
But what were they doing here? What was so valuable about the Lower World, a desolate place, that they would visit?
"No, judging from their appearance, some of them are injured, and the leader is seriously injured. They should be hiding from something …" Liu Yang's mind was extremely agile, and he soon saw the key point.
The leader's strength was unfathomable, and he was leading such a large group of warriors in the Divine Sea Realm …
Who was chasing after them?
If they were implicated, let alone the Great Yu Divine Kingdom, the entire continent of the Divine Kingdom, and even the entire Vast Thousand World would be in trouble.
"Who the f * ck thought this was a good omen just now?"
Liu Yang angrily rebuked, and the civil and military officials in the ascension ceremony immediately fell silent.
Then, Liu Yang threw down the golden crown on his head and shot towards the sky. He knew very well that if he didn't handle it well, not to mention the throne, he wouldn't even be able to keep his life … Moreover, Liu Yang didn't care about the throne at all. He was already a sixth layer Life and Death realm martial artist, and could break through to the Divine Sea realm at any time. At that time, he would ascend to the higher realm just like his father and his grandfather. Because after the inter-world passage was repaired, the four Divine Nations' monarchs had changed one after another.
In the past few thousand years, the ruler of the country had not changed, but because they had all chosen to ascend, many rulers had left their country and ascended after less than two years of their reign.
High up in the sky …
"Cough … cough …" Nine Palace Master coughed violently.
The past half a month was perhaps the most difficult time for Nine Palace Master.
"There seems to be a town below, but I wonder which force it belongs to?" Nine Palace Master asked.
Ning Yudie focused her gaze and looked down from a high altitude. She could see the entire continent …
Seeing the shape of the continent, Ning Yudie immediately rejected the idea of the Central Domain. She quickly came to an answer, "This is the Divine Kingdom Continent, it belongs to the human race!"
Ning Yudie had never been to the Divine Kingdom Continent before, but she remembered Luo Zheng mentioning that the Divine Kingdom Continent's shape was very special. There was a circular inland sea in the middle, and the continent surrounded this inland sea. It was like a bracelet with nothing in the middle. This shape was very easy to recognize, so she quickly came to a conclusion.
"Divine Kingdom?" Nine Palace Master paused, a trace of disdain on his face.
Forces like divine kingdoms only existed in the lower realms. It was almost impossible for them to exist in the upper realms. Whether it was the Human Dao Alliance or Gods Without Thought, they developed to the point where countless tenth rank sacred lands formed an alliance to form a superpower.
Actually, the Xuanyuan, Lie, Ji, and Ji families were another form of divine kingdoms. However, under the alliance of these four families, Gods Without Thought had developed to such a level that there was no need for them to call themselves emperors.
Those who were truly powerful didn't need to add titles such as Battle Emperor or Battle Emperor behind their names.
This was because their names already represented the strongest in the universe.
Moreover, the strongest in the universe already had the title of Heavenly Venerate.
"Forget it … Those guys have been sealed by me with A Thought of Heaven and Earth. It will take some time to break them. Let's rest first before making any plans!"
There were a considerable number of core disciples in the Jade Pure Sacred Ground who had followed Nine Palace Master into the Immortal Ascension Passage. These people's talents might not be considered much in the universe, but they were definitely the future main force of the Jade Pure Sacred Ground. He had unintentionally protected so many core disciples, which was considered a great merit in the Jade Pure Sacred Ground.
If it was in the past, Nine Palace Master would be very happy. However, he was not happy at all now. The Jade Pure Sacred Ground would probably cease to exist in the future. So what if the merit was great?
Just as he finished speaking, a figure came up from below.
This person was wearing a yellow robe with a True Dragon coiled on his sleeves. Nine Palace Master and Ning Yudie both knew that this person should be the king of this divine kingdom.
"Almighties, I am Liu Yang. May I know why you have come to my Great Yu Divine Kingdom? If I can help you, I will do my best!"
Although Liu Yang had ascended to the position of king, he was very respectful. He was very clear about the strength of these people. It did not matter if he cooperated or not. They could destroy the Great Yu Divine Kingdom in an instant. It could even be said that these people were not interested in destroying the Great Yu Divine Kingdom …
Therefore, it was better to be polite first. If Liu Yang could help, he would help because he had no choice. If he could not help, he could send them away with good words.
Nine Palace Master did not say anything. Since Ning Yudie had ascended to this great world, she would be in charge of these matters.
"Great Yu Divine Kingdom? I am the Palace Master of the Central Domain's Cloud Cloud Hall, Ning Yudie, "Ning Yudie said lightly.
"The Central Domain's … Tianbei Domain's Ning Yudie?" Liu Yang was stunned.
Ever since Luo Zheng had gone to the Divine Kingdom Continent, the four Divine Kingdoms had gradually paid more attention to the Central Domain. After the Astral Ocean Storm had stopped, people from the two continents came and went.
Because of the storm, although it was not very convenient to come and go, it was still much more frequent than before.
There were even some rich merchants in the Divine Kingdoms who found some business opportunities and took great risks to cross the Chaotic Astral Ocean to trade. Moreover, the construction of a space passage between the two continents had been put on the agenda …
The Cloud Hall's current power could rival any of the four Divine Kingdoms.
Liu Yang naturally had a copy of the information about the Palace Master of the Cloud Hall, Ning Yudie!
He did not expect that the Palace Master of the Cloud Hall had also ascended. However, after ascending, she had brought back thousands of Divine Sea Realm warriors and an unfathomable powerhouse. What was this?
"To tell you the truth, there are strong enemies chasing after us in the Ascension Passage. We only want to rest here before we decide whether to stay or leave," Ning Yudie said.
As expected! Liu Yang's heart ached. His guesses were always close to the truth. They were really being chased here. According to Ning Yudie, they needed to rest first before deciding whether to stay or leave. What should he do? He could not wait for them to leave as soon as possible! However, Liu Yang knew that he had no choice.
Ning Yudie naturally had some selfish motives in her heart.
The Ancient Sorcerer Tribe was neither a human nor a ghost. If they were really lured into the Central Region, wouldn't Palace Yun be in trouble? Right now, they had no choice but to sacrifice the Divine Kingdom Continent.
"Everyone, please." Liu Yang greeted them respectfully with a bitter expression.
Powerhouse was also a title.
Just like the powerhouses on the Divine Kingdom Continent, the most powerful ones were at the Divine Sea Realm. Naturally, they had to be called Powerhouses of the Divine Sea Realm.
There were thousands of powerhouses in front of them …
…
On the top floor of the Cloud Hall, Luo Zheng suddenly opened his eyes at this moment.
When Luo Zheng sensed the vision of the Great Wide World, he quickly captured the memory of Ning Yudie.
Ning Yudie's memory was interrupted in the will of the Great Wide World less than a year ago because Ning Yudie had ascended at that time. She would not leave a memory imprint in this will. However, the moment she returned, Luo Zheng captured the memory of Ning Yudie again.
This memory was very short and only recorded the conversation between Ning Yudie and Liu Yang of the Great Yu Divine Kingdom after she entered the Great Wide World.

Chapter 1438
Luo Zheng had been worried that the ascension tunnel wouldn't lead to the distant seas, or even to the Sea God Continent.
Although Luo Zheng was a peak god realm cultivator, it would still take some time to cross the vast sea. If the pursuers caught up with him, he might not be able to save them in time.
On the Divine Kingdom Continent, the situation was much more optimistic.
"Let's go!"
Luo Zheng immediately notified Hua Tianming and the others.
Xi Youqin had returned early after making a trip to the Void Spirit Sect. Although she held a grudge against Ning Yudie, she was more concerned about Luo Zheng's mood. She knew that Luo Zheng needed help the most right now.
Hua Tianming, Xi Youqin, Ku Deng, Dugu Jian Xiaoxiao, and Ku Deng had been on standby, ready to depart at any time.
Now that Luo Zheng had called for them, they all responded in unison.
Lie Qianhan and Xuanyuan Chenfeng, on the other hand, looked hesitant. After a few days of recuperation, their injuries had completely healed, or even disappeared. The effects of the Thoughtless of the Gods were extraordinary.
The two of them didn't know why they'd come either. They seemed to have seen Ji Luoxue and the other Daoists come, so they followed …
But they didn't get along well with Luo Zheng, so how could they be willing to risk their lives for Luo Zheng?
Luo Zheng couldn't be bothered with them. He'd already ordered someone to open a gap in the Abundant Light, and was about to fly out.
"Father! Father! Nian 'er wants to go too! "
Luo Nian ran out. He couldn't fly, so he stood on his tiptoes and waved his hands continuously.
"Nian 'er, just wait at home. Granny Yu, I'll have to trouble you!" Luo Zheng said seriously.
In truth, Luo Zheng believed that there was no place in the universe that was absolutely safe. The only safest place was the Immortal Abode. But seeing Luo Nian like this, it wouldn't be easy to stuff him into the Immortal Abode. He could only leave it to Granny Yu.
Although Granny Yu blamed Luo Zheng a lot, she could only try her best to coax Luo Nian.
Then, Luo Zheng turned into a streak of light and flew away, followed by Hua Tianming, Xi Youqin, and Dugu Jian Xiaoxiao.
At this moment, Ji Luoxue had his hands behind his back and floated in front of Xuanyuan Chenfeng. He glanced at Lie Qianhan and Xuanyuan Chenfeng and then said indifferently, "Did you forget the orders of the Head Family?"
Xuanyuan Chenfeng knew what Ji Luoxue was referring to. He snorted coldly, "Hmph, you should have assisted me!"
Ji Luoxue shook his head. "Assist whoever takes first place. In the end, you still lost to him!"
"That's because I didn't use my full strength." Speaking of his defeat, Xuanyuan Chenfeng's face still revealed a resentful expression. It could be seen how unwilling he was to lose at that time.
"A loss is a loss. Luo Zheng may not have done his best." Ji Luoxue smiled.
Xuanyuan Chenfeng curled his lips and let out a long sigh before standing up.
Lie Qianhan shook his head and looked as if he had seen a ghost. The three people from Gods Without Thought then followed him …
In a corner of the Cloud Cloud Hall, Red Maple and Pei Tianyao squatted down and looked at the streaks of light passing by in the sky.
"Luo Zheng, that guy, he didn't even want to meet us!" Red Maple looked slightly angry.
"Didn't Tianming say that? The Palace Master hasn't returned and he doesn't have the heart to meet guests. Not only did he not meet you, there were too many people who wanted to visit Luo Zheng, but he rejected them all. I think the Palace Master must be in danger. "Pei Tianyao said indifferently.
"If the Palace Master is in trouble, at least let us know. We can also help!" Red Maple was still a little unhappy.
Pei Tianyao smiled faintly. "Look at the strength of those guys. They are one level higher than the Divine Ocean Realm. They are Divine Ocean Realm warriors. If they called you, would you be helping or causing trouble?"
Red Maple curled his lips. He wanted to refute, but Pei Tianyao was right. The difference in strength was too big. What could he do if he went? Show off the Flower Escape Technique?
Red Maple's eyes were staring at the sky. He saw a streak of light suddenly stop. It was a little monk. The little monk seemed to have sensed something and looked at them.
Red Maple and Pei Tianyao were in a very remote corner. Normal people wouldn't notice them.
Red Maple also felt strange. He patted Pei Tianyao's shoulder and said: "Hey, where is your colleague? Why is he looking at us?"
"What do you mean my colleague!" Pei Tianyao's face showed a depressed expression.
"Haha, aren't you a reincarnated monk? He is still a monk …" Red Maple laughed.
Pei Tianyao's personality was gentle. He was not like when Hua Tianming was still alive. If Hua Tianming was ridiculed like this, Red Maple would definitely be in trouble.
"I don't know, but I also think that little monk is very special." Pei Tianyao was also a little puzzled.
The Buddhist sects in the Central Region were not flourishing. The few Buddhist sects there were only Third Grade sects. However, there were very few cultivation techniques in the Cloud Hall that were suitable for Pei Tianyao to cultivate. He also did not know that there was a powerful superpower in the universe, the Ten Thousand Buddha Sacred Realm!
The little monk who stopped in the air was naturally Ku Deng.
Ku Deng was about to follow Luo Zheng, but suddenly, he felt his Buddha's heart beating. A sense of intimacy suddenly rose. He felt strange. Turning his head, he saw Pei Tianyao.
Ku Deng's face showed a thoughtful expression. Seeing Luo Zheng's figure in the distance, he thought that they would not leave this place for a while. He should help Luo Zheng solve the problem first …
In the clear sky of the Central Domain, eight figures turned into a thin line and fled forward quickly!
Under the lead of Luo Zheng, they went all the way north …
The speed of these eight people was not slow. It didn't take long for them to cross the Central Domain and enter the Chaotic Astral Sea.
Looking at the omnipresent storm in the Star Sea, Luo Zheng paused for a while.
Directly crossing the storm, for Luo Zheng and the others, there was a certain risk. At least, they had to avoid the core of the Chaotic Astral Sea.
"Don't tell me we have to cross it directly." Lie Qianhan's eyes showed a trace of fear. The storm here had left a very deep impression on him …
In order to avoid the storm in the Chaotic Astral Sea, the team of the Demi-Immortal Clan chose to go on the back side of the space.
But on the back side, they encountered the Shadow Clan, which was the Ancient Sorcerer Clan.
Luo Zheng closed his eyes and immersed himself in his mind.
He saw that Ning Yudie seemed to have settled down in the Divine Kingdom and was resting. She didn't mean to leave immediately.
There was plenty of time …
"Follow me!"
Luo Zheng turned into a light and moved toward the northwest.
Although the Chaotic Astral Sea was terrifying, as long as they didn't get close to the core, they could take a detour and avoid the most powerful storm in the center.
Although the journey was longer, it was much safer …
Divine Kingdom Continent.
The thousands of core disciples of the Jade Pure Holy Land were settled in the huge imperial palace.
Most of them were focused on healing their injuries …
The Ancient Sorcerer Clan's fighting strength was not necessarily strong.
The key was that after killing a life form of this clan, they would be cursed. Those damned little skulls were like nightmares, entangling the martial artist until they died. There was no way to deal with them at all.
"Bang!"
Ninth Palace Master punched those little skulls and sent them flying away!
With the terrifying strength of a Divine Transformation Realm powerhouse, she could send those little skulls flying hundreds of thousands of feet.
Only now was she the most relaxed. Ninth Palace Master instantly took out two Zhen Yuanyu pills. After crushing them, she quickly swallowed them. Then, she took out three emerald green pills and swallowed them. Her complexion immediately recovered a lot.
"After resting, where do we go?" Ninth Palace Master asked.
As a Divine Transformation Realm powerhouse, Ninth Palace Master should make her own decision.
But facing those unsolvable existences of the Ancient Sorcerer Clan, she was also hesitant. She actually asked Ning Yudie instead.
When Ning Yudie suggested returning to the lower realms, Ninth Palace Master thought it was a very wise decision. With the universe in chaos, perhaps only the lower realms weren't affected.
But what was strange was that the Ancient Sorcerer Clan was actually chasing after them.
Ninth Palace Master was also puzzled. There shouldn't be anyone too important in their group, so why would they chase after them?
Moreover, the Ancient Sorcerer Clan appeared at a very strange time and place. When they just arrived at the Ascension Well, the Ancient Sorcerer Clan appeared. It was as if their original goal was to guard the Ascension Well.
Ning Yudie also shook her head, a blank look on her face. "I didn't expect that the Ancient Sorcerer Clan would suddenly appear at the Ascension Well. They shouldn't be trying to stop us."
"I think so too." Ninth Palace Master agreed with Ning Yudie's speculation. "Let's think about it another way. This Ancient Sorcerer Clan was originally guarding the Ascension Well. Then, there should be something important at the bottom of the Ascension Well."
The bottom of the Ascension Well was connected to the Ascension Passage, and the Ascension Passage was connected to the Great Thousand World below.
In other words, there should be something in the Great Thousand World that the Ancient Sorcerer Clan cared about.

Chapter 1439
"I remember now. There seems to be an Ancient Witch Tribe in this Great Wide World," Ning Yudie said.

Originally, the Ninth Palace Master had fled with Ning Yudie. Now, it seemed that they were involved in even greater trouble.
However, at that time, choosing to escape to the lower realm should be the only right choice.
What was worse was, what about the pursuers behind them?
After this rest, the Ninth Palace Master had recovered 70% of his strength, but the little skull could not be dispelled. What awaited him was still a slow death.
Unless a Celestial Venerable made a move, he would probably be able to save his life.

Not to mention whether the Ancient Witch Tribe in the Jade Pure Holy Land had been dealt with, if they returned directly through the ascension passage, they would eventually encounter the pursuers of the Ancient Witch Tribe.
In their current situation, they were in a dilemma!

Ning Yudie's expression was sour. She really wanted to return to the Cloud Hall to see Granny Yu and Nian 'er, whom she had been missing day and night.

In fact, when she was going against the flow of the ascension passage and facing a group of pursuers behind her, Ning Yudie had thought about this. The Great Wide World was only so big. If she led these Ancient Witch Tribe down, would it affect the Central Domain?
She could choose to retreat or even die in the ascension passage, but the Ninth Palace Master and the thousands of core disciples would not make such a sacrifice.
Her mind was in a mess. Ning Yudie did not know how to deal with it. All kinds of chaotic emotions attacked her heart.
She sat on the ground with her slender arms wrapped around her knees. She buried her head in her hands and could not help but tear up. Once again, she fell into a state of helplessness. There was no one she could rely on beside her.
Luo Zheng should still be in the upper realm …
All kinds of resources from the Divine Nation of Yu the Great were sent into the Imperial Palace.
Many of the precious materials were accumulated by the Great Yu Divine Nation over countless years. Some of them were even transferred from the other three Divine Nations. The other three Divine Nations also knew about this. Since they were all on the same continent, they couldn't escape.
Liu Yang's heart was bleeding as he sent these resources into the palace!
Ten-thousand-year-old Heavenly Fleeceflower Root, hundred-thousand-year-old Blood Moon Crystal, bundles of Three Sheng … These were all priceless treasures!

Only Miao Miao and a few others casually picked up some materials. Most of them picked them up and looked at them before tossing them aside.

Ninth Palace Master sat on a high platform and looked down at the core disciples who were resting. His face suddenly flashed with a touch of determination.
After all, he was one of the top figures in the Jade Pure Holy Land. He would not sit and wait for death!
Ninth Palace Master suddenly stood up and shouted, "Everyone!"
All the core disciples focused their gazes on Ninth Palace Master.
To be able to become a core disciple of the Jade Pure Holy Land, one's talent and background were not too low. They could only see Ninth Palace Master as their guide.
"The Ancient Sorcerer Tribe is not as powerful as we thought," Ninth Palace Master said loudly.
Everyone looked puzzled. They all knew what Ninth Palace Master said. The Ancient Sorcerer Tribe was not very powerful in terms of strength. A Divine Sea Realm warrior could easily kill them.
They did not know what method the Ancient Sorcerer Tribe used. When a living creature was killed, it would only produce a murderous aura. The murderous aura was the curse of the living creature after its death.
However, the curse of the Ancient Sorcerer Tribe after they died was the little skull that could not be removed. That thing was like an incurable disease. As long as they were entangled by the little skull, there was no way they could survive.
"We need a group of men of sacrifice to kill the Ancient Sorcerer Tribe! Only then can the others survive! "Ninth Palace Master said again," Those who are willing to be men of sacrifice can stand up! "
The lush square in the imperial palace was suddenly silent.
The plan that Ninth Palace Master proposed had been discussed before.
These core disciples were generally younger. Previously, there were so many people in the underground palace who were swallowed alive by the little skull. The scene of them being bitten into pieces was still vivid in their minds.
They were all frightened ….
In their hearts, killing the Ancient Sorcerer Tribe was equivalent to suicide!

But now?
If no one chose to sacrifice themselves, everyone would have to die.
Perhaps Ninth Palace Master's attitude had infected some people, or perhaps they were really at their wits' end.
After a moment of silence, someone finally walked up to the high platform and stood beside Ninth Palace Master.

He was an elite of the Jade Pure Sacred Ground and was also a core disciple to be nurtured. But now, he was going to become a man of sacrifice …
The second …
The third …

One could imagine that most of the pressure for this counterattack would be on Ninth Palace Master. Ninth Palace Master would bear the majority of the curse of the ancient sorcerer race.
Then, the Ninth Palace Master told the core disciples about how to set up the Matrix and how to guard the Matrix's Eye. These people came from the Jade Pure Holy Land. The Matrix that the Ninth Palace Master had set up was of the same origin as the Jade Pure Holy Land's inheritance. These people understood it very quickly.
When Ning Yudie saw this aura, she had a whole new level of respect for these cultivators. Although these cultivators were core disciples, they were not considered dazzling in the universe. But in a desperate situation, the power that erupted was not ordinary. This was also the power of human nature.
If they could snipe the ancient sorcerer race here, it would be the best result.
At this moment, in the four Divine Kingdoms, everyone was also in a state of panic.
The upper echelons of the Divine Kingdoms all understood what had happened. This group of experts who came out of nowhere seemed to have attracted a lot of trouble.
But they did not have the slightest room to resist. They could not chase this group of people away, right? Who would dare to open their mouth?
Just like that, twelve hours passed!
Twelve hours later, another huge crack appeared in the sky!
"Swoosh swoosh swoosh swoosh swoosh …"
From that crack, many small black spots appeared again …
Those small black spots fell rapidly from the sky, and the black spots began to grow bigger and bigger.
Some citizens of the Divine Kingdoms also looked up at the sky curiously. Soon, they realized that the things that were falling were actually huge skulls!
"Boom!"
The huge skulls dragged a black lightning bolt and smashed into the ground, creating a deep pit in the ground!
Then, one by one, bony black shadows crawled out of the skull!
Around the skulls, there were many cultivators of the Divine Kingdoms. When the cultivators saw this scene, they did not even dare to breathe loudly. The terrifying appearance of these creatures had already made them feel like their guts were being torn apart!

Chapter 1440
Ning Yudie and the remaining 700 core disciples retreated to the depths of the palace.

Giant skulls were constantly smashing down near the Imperial City of the Great Yu Kingdom.
The Ancient Sorcerer Tribe was not very interested in the warriors of the Great Thousand World, so there was no large-scale massacre. Only a few unlucky ones were directly smashed to death by the giant skulls.

The Ninth Palace Master, who was sitting in the middle, suddenly shouted!
The seven blazing white cone-shaped crystals that were neatly placed in front of him suddenly exploded!
"Buzz buzz buzz …"
The entire Demon Purging Vast Sky Array suddenly emitted a blazing white light.
The white light flowed out according to the layout of the Imperial City's walls, decorating the entire Imperial City like a dream!
The 300 Divine Sea Realm warriors stood in their respective positions and released their Vital Essence at the same time. Their aura suddenly became stronger!
Facing the first wave of the Ancient Sorcerer Tribe, the Ninth Palace Master stretched out his hand and clapped!
"Kacha!"
A violent wave was released from the center of the Vast Sky Demon Purging Formation, which was in front of the Ninth Palace Master!
This fluctuation came from the folding of space!
The Vast Sky Clear Devil Array was an array that used spatial laws to set up. As the controller of the array, the Ninth Palace Master's current strength was comparable to a Sector Lord!
Spatial Folding was able to fold the space of ten feet into a space of one foot!
Under such pressure, everything within the space would be deformed and destroyed ….
From a visual point of view, it looked like a door that was constantly being squeezed forward. The door swept past the palace's courtyard wall, and the entire courtyard wall was instantly squeezed. Its volume also began to shrink rapidly. The originally half-meter wide wall was reduced to the thickness of a "pancake"!
This scene was very strange. Although it was a thin pancake, because it was extremely compressed, it became very sturdy. It was still a wall, but it had become thinner …
The walls of the courtyard were inanimate objects. Even if they were squeezed, they would only deform. However, it was different for living things.
The neatly arranged greenery in the palace was directly squeezed into a ball of green liquid that flowed all over the ground. The trees, on the other hand, were turned into pieces of strangely shaped planks that collapsed on the ground.
As for the ancient witch race …
Under the pressure, they were all torn into pieces. In the end, they became a black piece of paper that laid on the ground. It was as if a huge monster had stepped on them, and they were flattened very "evenly".
Since they had become like this, these ancient witch race beings were definitely dead.
However, the three hundred death warriors and the Ninth Palace Master didn't have any excitement on their faces. Killing the ancient witch race wasn't difficult. The troublesome part was their curse.
"Cough cough cough …"
As expected, small skulls began to appear on their flattened bodies. Their lower jaws were constantly vibrating as they charged towards the Ninth Palace Master!

"Scram!"
The Ninth Palace Master had already thrown caution to the wind. At this moment, he was full of vigor!
Borrowing the power of the Vast Sky Clear Devil Array, he sent out another palm strike. After all, after the Vast Sky Clear Devil Array was set up, his strength was comparable to a Sector Lord!
He wanted to see if he could borrow this array to get rid of the small skulls!
That space squeezing force once again covered the small skulls.
"Kacha, kacha …"
Under the pressure of space, the sound of explosions was very crisp.
Everyone saw that around the small skulls, there were many small cracks. Those were the cracks of space.
This space didn't crush the small skulls. Instead, cracks appeared in the space itself …
"No …"

The Ninth Palace Master waved his hand and sent out another two waves of folded space ripples!
This Vast Sky Clear Devil Array was a converging array. The 300 Divine Sea Realm warriors at the center of the array only needed to distribute their vital essence according to the Ninth Palace Master's instructions.
The biggest weakness of this Vast Sky Clear Devil Array was the Ninth Palace Master himself. As long as he didn't fall, then the entire array would be locked in space. The 300 Divine Sea Realm warriors who were spread out would be relatively safer!

Waves of cracking sounds rang out again!
The witch race people who dared to rush in were all compressed into a thin piece of black paper. They were stacked on the ground piece by piece, forming all kinds of strange shapes!

Although those small skulls were sent flying by the Ninth Palace Master's slap, no matter how far they flew, the small skulls would persevere and return.
The more witch race people died, the more small skulls there were!
In the first and second waves, there were already forty-five little skulls gathered together …
As the Ninth Palace Master killed more and more witch race people, the number of small skulls also began to increase rapidly!
Not long after, there were more than a hundred small skulls flying around in the air!
The Ninth Palace Master could still handle such a number.
However, after an hour, there were already thousands of small skulls gathered in the air!
These small skulls were like garbage in the sea. They gathered and intertwined with each other. "Cough, cough, cough" sounds were very dense. One look at it would make one's scalp tingle!
Most of the small skulls were biting at the Ninth Palace Master. There were also some small skulls that bit at the blazing white array pattern outside the Vast Demon Purging Array that was formed by the spatial laws …
Although the 300 Divine Sea Realm warriors knew that they were going to die, when they saw so many small skulls, their faces turned pale.
Some people's array cores were located closer to the outside. Those small skulls were very close to them. Although the Ninth Palace Master had said that as long as he did not fall, these small skulls would not be able to break the array, they still could not help but panic!
Fortunately, this array did not require them to do too much. They only needed to release their Vital Essence according to the set rhythm. If it was another array that required cooperation, it would have collapsed long ago and then been broken!
However, as these small skulls bit, they were also constantly consuming the Vital Essence in the Vast Demon Purging Array. Everyone felt that the Ninth Palace Master was crazily extracting the Vital Essence in their bodies! At this rate, it would not be long before their Vital Essence seas were dried up!

As the number of small skulls increased, their bites became more and more powerful. Their Vital Essence was being consumed faster and faster. This Vast Demon Purging Array would eventually be destroyed by those small skulls!
After an hour, there were tens of thousands of small skulls gathered in front of everyone. These small skulls danced in the air and covered the sky. They were slapped away by the Ninth Palace Master, but like maggots, they swooped down again and continued to bite at the Vast Demon Purging Array …

Every time a warrior fell, the burden on the other warriors would increase. The Vital Essence was being extracted at a faster rate. The Ninth Palace Master's face was getting uglier and uglier.
As long as the Vast Demon Purging Array collapsed, everyone would be bitten into a pile of meat by these small skulls in a short period of time.
"Is resistance futile in the end?"
Seeing the waves of skeletons, the Ninth Palace Master's heart sank. If this continued, then in less than an incense stick of time, everyone's Vital Essence would be sucked dry!
At this moment, the group of core disciples that had been hiding in the depths of the Imperial Palace appeared behind the Vast Demon Purging Array.
"Ninth Palace Master, there's still us!" Ning Yudie's voice suddenly sounded out. On that incomparably beautiful face, all the helplessness and sadness had disappeared. Instead, it was replaced with a resolute look.

Chapter 1441
Now, the entire Demon Purging Array was under immense pressure.
More and more warriors were running out of Vital Essence, but the Ninth Palace Master had no way out.

Any more resistance was futile.

However, the Ninth Palace Master had no idea where Ning Yudie could escape to.
Perhaps the purpose of the Ancient Sorcerer Tribe was to capture all the visitors from the Upper Realm in one fell swoop. After all, Ning Yudie had nowhere to run in this Great Thousand World.

She was thinking the same thing as the Ninth Palace Master. Since there was no way to escape, escaping now would only be a matter of time. Furthermore, Ning Yudie had another layer of worry. They were also paying close attention to the battle in the palace. They also had a lot of worries in their hearts. After all, this disaster happened on the Divine Kingdom Continent.

The True Essence Sea in her body began to drop at a visible speed.
Ning Yudie's strength wasn't considered strong. The vital essence in her Divine Ocean was less than that of the core disciples of the Jade Pure Holy Ground.
However, Ning Yudie's situation was terrible, but the other warriors weren't any better. After all, the number of small skulls had almost doubled, and the pressure on the Demon Purging Vast Sky Formation had doubled!
After only holding on for an hour, many warriors' vital essence had been depleted by more than half.
Some warriors tried to meditate on the spot and absorb the vital essence of heaven and earth for their own use. However, the vital essence of heaven and earth in the Lower Realm was far inferior to that of the Upper Realm. Moreover, even in the Upper Realm, this absorption was far from enough. Unless they all had an unlimited amount of Zhen Yuanyu in their hands, it was only possible to maintain the balance under the crazy replenishment.
Another hour had passed!
The number of small skulls piled up in front of everyone was no less than thirty to forty thousand!

Ning Yudie silently crushed a top grade true essence stone. If even Zhen Yuanyu couldn't replenish her vital essence, then there was no need to mention these true essence stones. Absorbing them into her body was only a drop in the ocean. Her vital essence sea had already been completely dried up.
It wasn't just Ning Yudie, but all the Divine Sea realm martial artists' Divine Seas had also dried up …
Everyone's eyes turned towards Ninth Palace Master.
Ninth Palace Master bitterly smiled …
As a Divine Transformation realm martial artist, his inner world had already produced a massive number of living beings. He even had a small Divine Kingdom, and within this Divine Kingdom were several third-grade Holy Lands with countless living beings.
Just now, a massive disaster had erupted within his inner world …
All living beings were cruelly torn apart by him, returning to their vital essence!
All the houses, buildings, roads, lakes, and all sorts of wolves, snakes, tigers, leopards, and vicious beasts within his inner world weren't spared.
Some False Tribulation realm and Life and Death realm martial artists were also returned to their vital essence.
