The short square tables in the palace were lined up in a row, with three to five people sitting at a table. They were sitting at the same table as Hua Tianming, sitting in the upper seats.
They were able to sit at this table because of Luo Zheng and Hua Tianming.
In terms of strength and status, they were not qualified to sit here.
Hearing the conversation between Luo Zheng and the others, Pei Tianyao and Red Maple were secretly shocked.
How big was the universe?
They had never seen it, but they heard from Hua Tianming that there were hundreds of millions of Spirit Sea Realm warriors!
One hundred million …
They were so shocked that they almost sat on the ground.
In contrast, in this world, Spirit Sea Realm warriors could do whatever they wanted. The power of the universe was indeed beyond their imagination.
From Luo Zheng, they learned that these people were actually the top ten Daoists in the universe?
They didn't know what Daoists meant, but standing out from hundreds of millions of Spirit Sea Realm warriors and ranking in the top ten, this talent was too terrifying …
This time, Pei Tianyao found that Ku Deng had been staring at him, and he was a little shocked.
When Luo Zheng left with this group of people, this monk also had the same look. Why did he come again today?
Ku Deng stared at Pei Tianyao and said such words. Everyone followed Ku Deng's gaze and finally their eyes fell on Pei Tianyao.
"Hey, hey, your peer is staring at you again." Red Maple nudged Pei Tianyao with his elbow.
"Fuck you! It's your peer! "Pei Tianyao was already uncomfortable, but at this moment, he burst out with a rare swear word.
Red Maple found it funny. He also saw that Pei Tianyao seemed to be a little nervous, so he didn't say anything.
"Brother Ku Deng, what do you mean?" Luo Zheng's face flashed with a trace of curiosity.
After ascending to the Upper World, Luo Zheng had never entered the Ten Thousand Buddha Sanctuary. Buddhism and Taoism were independent, and some things were quite mysterious. He faintly had a premonition that the meaning of Ku Deng's words was not simple.
Ku Deng smiled faintly, but nodded to Pei Tianyao. "How should I address you, benefactor?"
"Pei Tianyao." Pei Tianyao stood up and bowed.
Ku Deng looked like a novice monk and he was even younger than Pei Tian Yao. However, he was still a Peak God Realm warrior, so Pei Tian Yao had to be polite to him.
Who knew that after Pei Tianyao bowed, Ku Deng also put his palms together and said, "Amitabha, you are the reincarnation of the Acalanatha, but you don't have the fate to ascend. If you can enter my Ten Thousand Buddha Sanctuary, you will be regarded as a Daoist …" After saying that, Ku Deng looked at Luo Zheng and Ning Yudie and said, "I don't know if I, Ku Deng, can ask for such a person from the Cloud Hall?"
The other Daoists did not take the Cloud Hall seriously.
Not to mention Rank Six Sects, they would even disdain Rank Ten Holy Lands. After all, the forces they belonged to had thousands of Rank Ten Holy Lands under their control.
However, Ku Deng was very polite. He asked the Cloud Hall for the person.
"Daoist." Luo Zheng's eyebrows twitched slightly.
Ning Yudie's eyes also kept flickering …
After she ascended, she learned about the division of strength in the universe from the Ninth Palace Master. She also knew about the division of various geniuses.
The Daoist was the absolute Daoist in the universe. This kind of honor could be said to be "one in a million"!
For a moment, Ning Yudie did not know what to say. Her Cloud Hall was too powerful. It had three Daoists, and now it seemed that there were more. From this point of view, what kind of force was the Cloud Hall? Rank Eleven Holy Lands? Rank Twenty-One Holy Lands …
The warriors in the Central Domain did not know what a Daoist meant. They only knew that Pei Tianyao was chosen by the monk in the Divine Realm, so he should have a good talent. They estimated that Pei Tianyao should at least have a Divine Realm talent, right?
However, the expressions of Xuanyuan Chenfeng and the others were very interesting.
"Cultivate as a Daoist? Ku Deng, can you make this decision on behalf of the Hidden Spirit Temple? Are you kidding? "Xuanyuan Chenfeng laughed strangely.
They were Daoists themselves, so they knew the status of a Daoist. It was almost the symbol of a force's future! They thought that Ku Deng was teasing them when he found a Daoist in a corner of the Mortal World.
Ku Deng, however, smiled and asked, "Have monks ever lied?"
"Thunder Punishing Heavenly Master did," Lie Qianhan immediately said. Thunder Punishing Heavenly Master was the strongest Heavenly Master in the Ten Thousand Buddha Sacred Realm.
"Lord Heavenly Master lied, that's his business." Ku Deng still looked indifferent. He did not want to be entangled with these two people, so he continued to look at Ning Yudie. "Also, ask if the Cloud Hall can release this person."
In fact, since Ku Deng found Pei Tianyao here, he would definitely be taken away by the Ten Thousand Buddha Sacred Realm. Today, the Cloud Hall did not release him, so he would naturally report to the Hidden Spirit Temple. At that time, someone would take Pei Tianyao away. However, at this moment, he still asked for Ning Yudie's opinion.
Pei Tianyao still has a confused expression on his face … …
When he left the Eastern Domain, he also wanted to enter a temple. However, the incense in the Central Domain was not strong, and there were not many skills suitable for him to practice. In the end, he could only rely on his own understanding.
Until now, he had reached the False Tribulation Realm and became the backbone of the Cloud Hall. However, compared with the current Hua Tianming and Luo Zheng, there was a huge difference.
Ning Yudie smiled gently. "Little Master Ku Deng, you are too polite. Although Pei Tianyao is a disciple of the Cloud Hall, if he has a better place to go, I will not stop him. Let me ask him if he agrees."
Before Pei Tianyao could speak, Red Maple said first, "What is a Daoist? I will ask for him, haha!"
Ku Deng said lightly, "There are 3,000 Great Tao in the world, but that is just an imaginary number. The so-called Daoist is a person who has become a True God, who can cross the universe and surpass the Heavenly Master. I can't guarantee that you can reach this step, but the reincarnation of the Acalanatha, haha …"
Ku Deng did not want to say the rest of his words.
"What is a Heavenly Master?" Red Maple asked again.
Becoming a God or an Immortal was not a clear concept for the warriors in the Mortal World. Red Maple took the opportunity to satisfy his curiosity.
"A Heavenly Master is a person who carries the Mandate of Heaven, the most powerful existence in the universe." Ku Deng answered truthfully.
"What is below the Heavenly Master?" Red Maple asked again.
Even the good-tempered Ku Deng was speechless. As for Hua Tianming and Pei Tianyao, they had already rolled their eyes.
"Benefactor, if you want to know the division of the strength of the warriors in the universe, I can tell you in private." Ku Deng said.
"I have asked, haha." Since Ku Deng said so, Red Maple stopped making trouble.
Luo Zheng also looked at Pei Tianyao with a smile. Such an opportunity was so rare that it was hard for him to describe it, but he believed that Pei Tianyao would not refuse.
"I am willing to go with Little Master Ku Deng." Pei Tianyao cupped his hands. He knew that if he agreed to this matter, everything in the future would change dramatically, but he didn't know that the change would be so big that he couldn't imagine it.
Xuanyuan Chenfeng and Lie Qianhan were speechless again.
They believed that Ku Deng would not talk nonsense, but their minds were in a mess at the moment. What the hell was this big wide world? Why was it so magical?
When a big world came, only a dozen Daoists were born. Almost half of them appeared here?
From the perspective of probability, it did not make sense at all.
At the moment, people were too lazy to pay attention to the rude words of these two people. The cultivation of the warriors present was not high, but they were smart. They knew the origin of these strong warriors. At the same time, they were very envious of Pei Tianyao. He was only in the False Tribulation Realm, but he could enter the universe. It was a blessing that could not be accumulated for several lifetimes.
When the banquet was getting more and more lively, on the other side of the Cloud Sea City, a thin space crack suddenly appeared. When the space crack appeared, a faint green light bloomed from the crack!
Then a green shadow came out of the space crack. It was Lord Tianwu of the Ancient Sorcerer Tribe.
The moment Lord Tianwu left the space, he raised a jade bone staff in his hand. He gently waved it in the air, and a circle of green spell appeared. His movement was very fast.
He knew what the Eight Children of the Heavenly Way meant. If he could eliminate them here, it would be a great blessing for the Ancient Sorcerer Tribe!
But since these people were released by the Demi-Immortal Tribe, it was impossible that they did not have protection.

Chapter 1446
The moment the green runes appeared, they turned into dozens of green ghost faces. These ghost faces were sinister and terrifying, appearing and disappearing in the air as they drilled into the Cloud Hall.
The Water-Filled Light Barrier even blocked the Law of Space to a certain extent, but these ghost faces crossed it very easily.
The Ancient Sorcerer Clan was not good at frontal combat. Even though Lord Celestial Sorcerer's cultivation was comparable to a Celestial Venerable, he could still use powerful sorcery …
Lord Celestial Sorcerer floated in the air, his strange eyes only having the whites of his eyes.
At this moment, he was concentrating on controlling the dozens of ghost faces, silently shuttling through the Cloud Hall.
However, while he was concentrating, Lord Celestial Sorcerer was also vigilant of the surrounding movements.
He found it hard to believe that the Demi-Immortal Clan would let these little fellows come out alone and not take any precautions around them. If the Demi-Immortal Clan was really careless to this extent, then he would definitely take advantage of this!
Whoosh whoosh whoosh …
"Little fellows, I found you." Lord Celestial Sorcerer quickly sensed the location of the Eight Scions of the Heavenly Dao. All the ghost faces quietly floated in the same direction at the same time.
As the ghost faces shuttled back and forth, they were like giant fireflies in the dark night, emitting a green light as they rushed into the hall at an extremely fast speed.
"Awoo!"
These ghost faces did not have a fixed shape. The moment they rushed in, they went straight for Luo Zheng and the others.
Luo Zheng was the first to react. The moment he saw these ghost faces, his expression changed. He reached out and grabbed Ning Yudie's waist with one hand, then hurriedly retreated!
He did not know the strength of these ghost faces, but the evil aura emitted from them made Luo Zheng instinctively vigilant!
A ghostly face inadvertently swept past a Hollow Tribulation Realm cultivator. That Hollow Tribulation Realm cultivator suddenly froze and opened his mouth wide. An incomparably terrified expression appeared on his face. This expression instantly froze on his face. He no longer had the slightest bit of vitality. He just stood there and died …
The palace suddenly burst into an uproar.
"What is this!"
"Retreat!"
"What a domineering thing!"
Some warriors only hesitated for a moment before their vitality completely disappeared, and they couldn't be more dead …
In the blink of an eye, the martial artists in the palace fell one after another, and the entire hall was in chaos.
This scene made the hearts of Luo Zheng and the others sink. This speed and strange means were definitely not something they could resist, or even be unable to resist.
Just how strong was the other party? He could easily take their lives without even showing up.
"Return to the Immortal Abode!"
Luo Zheng suddenly had such a thought.
Ning Yudie was by his side. He could bring her in, but what about Luo Nian? He was still in the Cloud Hall …
While he was hesitating, one of the ghostly faces pounced towards Luo Zheng!
As for Ku Deng, Ji Luoxue, and Xuanyuan Chenfeng, they were in a similar situation. These ghost faces were clearly coming for the eight of them!
"You go in first!" At this moment, Luo Zheng held the Gen Token in his hand. He was thinking about how to put Ning Yudie and Xi Youqin in first. As for Mu Mingxue, she was sitting in a seat further away from Luo Zheng.
But in this way, Hua Tianming and the others would probably …
Luo Zheng was also very anxious. Although he had a premonition that this trip back to the mortal world would not be so simple, the crisis had erupted so suddenly. Luo Zheng had never heard of this kind of means, and he didn't know who was using it!
Soon, the expressions on everyone's faces froze.
The cups and plates on the square table froze in midair, and the wine in the cups remained in the state of sputtering water droplets. The flickering candlelight almost stopped fluctuating, and the flow of time became abnormally slow at this moment.
In the center of the hall, a middle-aged warrior in a gray robe appeared.
Luo Zheng had seen this warrior once before. When he was changing the blood of the Demi-Immortal Clan, this person was present. He was a Celestial Master of the Demi-Immortal Clan.
He raised his hand and flicked his fingers, shooting out bolts of lightning from his fingertips.
"Clap, clap, clap, clap, clap …"
The fingertip thunder didn't seem to be very powerful, especially for a Celestial Master who had the power to destroy the world.
But every time a fingertip thunder flashed out, those ghost faces were instantly killed by the thunder and lightning!
After all the ghost faces were destroyed, the flow of time in the hall returned to normal.
It was unknown to what extent this Celestial Master's control of the Law of Time was. The flow of time was so slow that it almost stopped. Even his thoughts were similarly slow.
Therefore, no one saw this Celestial Master make a move. They only felt that there was a blur in front of them, and all the ghost faces disappeared …
The Celestial Master turned his head and smiled at Luo Zheng and the others. "Little guys … it's about time to go back."
"Many thanks to Celestial Master Huan Miao for saving our lives." Hua Tianming cupped his hands.
Luo Zheng also cupped his hands and thanked Celestial Master Huan Miao. Now, Luo Zheng was facing a threat that he couldn't resist. Moreover, this was the Cloud Hall. There were too many people that Luo Zheng needed to protect. He didn't expect the Demi-Immortal Clan to be so worried about them that they actually arranged for a Celestial Master to protect them.
"I wonder who dares to attack us?" Xuanyuan Chenfeng's expression was very ugly. He felt that he had been implicated by Luo Zheng. He was really unlucky and shouldn't have followed him.
Celestial Master Huan Miao said indifferently, "It's just a certain Celestial Wizard. But he should be in trouble now."
…
…
At the same time, Celestial Wizard outside the Cloud Hall also sensed that something was wrong. The Soul-Devouring Ghost Shadows he released were destroyed at the same time. His expression darkened. Suddenly, a soft voice sounded in his ears.
"I've noticed you too." At this moment, a white figure suddenly appeared beside Lord Celestial Wizard.
An exquisite short sword was already pressed against Lord Celestial Wizard's neck.
"Die." That person didn't hesitate at all. As soon as he finished speaking, a strong light suddenly bloomed from the short sword and slashed toward Celestial Wizard!
"Pfft!"
At this critical moment, Celestial Wizard's jade-green body suddenly dispersed, turning into a pool of green light that flowed wildly!
In an instant, all the green light gathered above the clouds!
When the green light reformed Celestial Wizard's body, there was a wound on his neck that couldn't be healed. In the darkness of the night, blood that emitted green light spurted out from the wound. Clearly, this slash had caused him to be severely injured!
Just as his body materialized, the white figure appeared behind the Heaven Wizard like a shadow and slashed out with the short sword in his hand!
"Pfft!"
Celestial Wizard's expression changed drastically. He turned into countless green lights again and scattered in all directions. This time, he directly escaped into the other side of the space.
The white figure let out a cold laugh and followed almost at the same time. The moment he entered, he no longer had any qualms!
After all, this was a battle between Celestial Venerables. Even the aftershocks of their attacks were enough to destroy the world. Even a massive world couldn't withstand it, let alone a Greater Thousand Worlds!
The moment Celestial Wizard reformed again, the white figure continued to follow him!
"Whoosh …"
"Universe Will Kill!"
The white figure waved the short sword in his hand, and a sword light flashed! This sword light spiraled and turned into an Eight Trigrams wheel that shot toward Celestial Wizard!
Now that he had entered the other side of the world, the white figure no longer had any qualms. This sword light was full of power — a Celestial Venerable's full-powered attack!
"Pfft!"
The sword light directly penetrated Celestial Wizard's body and came out from his back. After extending for tens of millions of kilometers, it gradually dissipated …
From afar, this sword light was as resplendent as the Milky Way that streaked across the night sky.
This sword light was also unleashed in the void.
If this sword light was unleashed in the Central Domain, it could split the Greater Thousand Worlds open and reach the end of the Greater Thousand Worlds. It could even blast open the barriers of the Greater Thousand Worlds and pierce through the connected Greater Thousand Worlds.
Green blood spurted out. Celestial Wizard was clearly injured, but he didn't show any fear. He only looked at the white figure indifferently and said, "I was wondering who it was. So it's Celestial Wizard Qian Lan … Hehe."
"How can the Ancient Wizard Tribe kill a Dao Disciple protected by the Demi-Immortal Tribe?" Celestial Wizard Qian Lan said indifferently as the short sword in his hand flickered with a cold light.
The Demi-Immortal Tribe didn't care about the Ancient Wizard Tribe at all. Although the Ancient Wizard Tribe was a little troublesome, that was all there was to it.
To them, the true danger came from the enemy behind the Wall of Sighs.
"Is that so?" Celestial Wizard sneered. "Since Celestial Wizard Qian Lan is here, then the one who destroyed my Soul-Devouring Ghost Shadow in the main hall should be Celestial Wizard Huan Miao. I wonder if Celestial Wizard Huan Miao can defeat the Candle Dragon? Hehe! "
Celestial Wizard Qian Lan knew that the Demi-Immortal Tribe had a backup plan, but he still dared to kill a Dao Disciple under such circumstances. Naturally, he also had a backup plan.

Chapter 1447
At the same time, Heavenly Lord Huan Miao said to Luo Zheng, "That Heaven Wizard is in trouble …"
As soon as she finished speaking, a spatial crack exploded, and a black tail rolled out from it. It wrapped around Heavenly Lord Huan Miao and dragged her to the other side of the space …
Because of Heavenly Lord Huan Miao's appearance, the chaotic situation stabilized.
With a Heavenly Lord here, the Ancient Sorcerer Tribe would not dare to cause trouble. No one expected the situation to change so quickly.
Luo Zheng and the others were stunned by this sudden scene.
In fact, the Demi-Immortal Tribe had already expected this to happen, so they had sent Heavenly Lords to protect Luo Zheng.
The Ancient Sorcerer Tribe's influence in the universe was not particularly strong. This time, they suddenly invaded many big worlds in the universe in order to awaken several ancient fierce beasts.
These ancient beasts were hidden in the depths of some large worlds. This was their purpose in invading the large worlds.
Heavenly Lord Huan Miao and Heavenly Lord Qian Lan had been quietly protecting Luo Zheng and the others. They had launched a counterattack before the Heaven Wizard attacked. However, the two Heavenly Lords did not expect that the Ancient Sorcerer Tribe had also come prepared.
The Candle Dragon, also known as the Candle Dragon, was originally a variant of the True Dragon. It was the sacred beast of the Ancient Sorcerer Tribe. This sacred beast was inherited from the Upper Realm and had the strength of a True Dragon!
Two Heavenly Lords, a Heaven Wizard, and a Candle Dragon fought fiercely on the other side of the Great Thousand World!
The faces of the people from the Central Region in the hall were ghastly pale.
Of course, the eight Daoists did not look good either.
In a confrontation at the level of Empyreans, just being swept up by the aftershocks would cause them to be smashed apart like butterflies in a storm.
Fortunately, the Ancient Sorcerer Tribe liked to hide in the shadows of the world and brought the two Heavenly Lords to the other side of the space. If they had fought in the sky above the Cloud Hall, the entire Central Domain would have collapsed in an instant!
"Luo Zheng, take us back!" Xuanyuan Chenfeng jumped out and said sternly.
"Yes, take us back to the Demi-Immortal Tribe or the Lie Family!" Lie Qianhan added.
In their opinion, this place was already a dangerous place. Naturally, they wanted to escape at the first possible moment … As for the upheaval in the Great Thousand World, what did it have to do with them?
Ji Luoxue and the others seemed to be moved as well. To put it bluntly, the lives of the eight of them were countless times more important than the lives of the cultivators in the Boundless World …
In fact, they had another way to leave the Greater Thousand World, which was to remove the effect of the Moon-Concealing Stone and let the Greater Thousand World reject them. In other words, they could "ascend" to the Upper Realm. However, the passage to the Greater Thousand World was random. Who knew which big world they would be teleported to? What if the Great World was facing the invasion of the Ancient Shamans? Wouldn't it be a dead end if they went in?
Only Luo Zheng's magical teleportation token could allow them to return safely. This was the safest way.
Luo Zheng's eyes kept flickering …
Xuanyuan Chenfeng's words were not wrong. At this moment, the best choice was to leave first.
As the most important Dao Children in the universe, they carried the fate of saving the universe and also carried the ardent hopes of those Celestial Venerables.
However, Luo Zheng was not willing to see his homeland destroyed in a day.
"Whoosh!"
Seeing Luo Zheng so hesitant, Xuanyuan Chenfeng suddenly took out a long spear and pressed the tip of the spear against Luo Zheng's forehead. "Quick!"
Luo Zheng's expression did not change in the slightest. He looked around at the warriors from the various sects in the Central Domain. These people's faces were also bitter. They naturally knew that this disaster was caused by Luo Zheng.
However, they could only be regarded as small fish and shrimps here. Even if they begged Luo Zheng to stay, it would be meaningless. This group of Divine Realm warriors was afraid that they could not avoid it. Perhaps Luo Zheng alone would not be able to stop them.
Therefore, Luo Zheng flicked his finger and the teleportation token jumped out from the sumeru ring.
"Those of you who are willing to leave can enter on your own! I will stay! "
At this moment, Luo Zheng had a plan. The others could enter the teleportation token and leave. He would arrange for Ning Yudie, Luo Nian, and the others to stay in the Immortal Mansion.
However, before Luo Zheng could activate the teleportation token, another cold voice was transmitted.
"Heh heh heh … Don't even think about leaving!"
Everyone's expression changed. They raised their heads and saw a crack appear out of thin air. From that crack, a black figure appeared. His entire body was covered in rugged barbs.
"It's a Great Shaman!"
"His cultivation is equivalent to a Realm Master!"
"Luo Zheng, hurry up and activate that damn thing of yours! Take us away! "Lie Qianhan roared.
"I already said that none of you want to leave. You're still dreaming!" The Great Shaman waved the bone staff in his hand. Wisps of black mist surrounded the bone staff and shot directly toward Luo Zheng and the others. In an instant, the mist entered the hearts of the eight people. "Heh heh, you can try to escape … When the time comes, your hearts will explode in a very strange shape."
This was the Luo Yan Heart Locking Curse of the Ancient Shaman Clan. Unless the person who cast the curse was killed, they would not be able to break free from the black mist. From this point of view, it was similar to a life threatening battle.
However, this depended on the strength of the person who cast the curse. This Great Shaman obviously believed that he had the strength to crush the eight Dao Children.
"Let's kill him first! The others … leave the Cloud Hall! "With a flash of light under Hua Tianming's feet, he dashed forward rapidly. Sword Tengshe was already in his hand!
Many warriors who were guests in the Cloud Palace today were in a daze at this moment. They did not expect that they would be involved in a fight at such a level. For a moment, they were so flustered that they even forgot to run away.
At this moment, after Hua Tianming spoke, those warriors fled in all directions.
This Great Shaman did not seem to care about the ordinary warriors at all. He only focused on the eight Dao Children.
"Disperse the people in the Cloud Hall temporarily. Then take Nian 'er and leave the Cloud Hall first," Luo Zheng ordered.
The moment Hua Tianming rushed forward, Luo Zheng also followed him!
At the same time, Ji Luoxue, Ku Deng, and Dugu Jian Xiaoxiao used their own methods at the same time.
When Xuanyuan Chenfeng and Lie Qianhan saw this scene, they could only grit their teeth and fight!
Although the eight Dao Children were only at the Divine Realm, it was not impossible for them to defeat a Divine Transformation Realm warrior.
Although this Great Shaman had the cultivation of a Realm Master, if the eight of them joined forces, they might be able to defeat him!
A wave of light spread over the Great Shaman in a flash. At the same time, Hua Tianming's figure appeared at the end of the wave of light. With the speed of the Ultimate Step Flash, he stabbed out with Sword Tengshe in his hand!
"Ha! As expected of a Dao Child. "A cold smile appeared on the Great Shaman's face. He tapped the bone staff in his hand lightly. The white jade bone staff suddenly shook, and a dense black ghost claw was formed. It suddenly grabbed Tengshe's sword!
"Clang!"
No one knew what the black ghost claw was. Even Tengshe's sword couldn't cut it open. The ghost claw waved and forced Hua Tianming to retreat.
"Die." The Great Shaman spun the bone staff in a circle. The shadow of a black bug appeared on the tip of the staff and pointed at Hua Tianming. It was the Shaman Tribe's Straight Death Staff Strike. When the staff touched someone's life, it would immediately end. The Ancient Shaman Tribe's method of harvesting lives was extremely overbearing.
"In your dreams!"
Luo Zheng's entire body directly slammed over. All the draconic scales in his body were activated. At this moment, he was akin to a desolate ancient beast. Even world overlords wouldn't be able to use their fleshly bodies to block such a collision. Let alone the fact that the ancient shamans weren't proficient in using their fleshly bodies.
The Great Shaman could only give up on killing Hua Tianming. After waving the bone staff, more black ghost claws appeared from the black fog. Two of the ghost claws turned into fists and smashed toward Luo Zheng!
"Pu, pu!"
With two muffled sounds, the two ghost claws were directly hit by Luo Zheng and turned into black smoke. However, the power of the ghost claws was also beyond Luo Zheng's imagination. Under the huge impact, Luo Zheng was also sent flying!
Then, Ji Luoxue, Dugu Jian Xiaoxiao, Xuanyuan Chenfeng, and the others all used their own methods and launched the first round of attacks …
Although they wanted to leave this place as soon as possible, they were currently bound by the Luo Yan Heart Locking Curse. They could only kill the Great Shaman first!
"Boom …"
The Cloud Hall floating in the sky suddenly trembled violently!
Although they focused all their power on the Great Shaman, the power that exploded out was still too great. The Cloud Hall couldn't withstand such a terrifying power.
The spear that Xuanyuan Chenfeng stabbed at the Great Shaman missed. The spear light that shot out overturned the roof of the palace and directly shot toward the Watery Light Enchantment!
In the past, Cui Xie naturally couldn't do anything to the Watery Light Enchantment. However, when Xuanyuan Chenfeng's spear light shot out, the Watery Light Enchantment rippled. Before the power could spread to the entire enchantment, the entire Watery Light Enchantment cracked and shattered.
＝＝＝＝＝

Chapter 1448
This night, Cloud Sea City was destined to be sleepless.
Under Ning Yudie's organization, all the warriors were quickly dismissed.
High up in the sky, all the cultivators of the Cloud Hall were pouring out from all sorts of exits. If one looked up from within the city, it would seem like the Cloud Hall was like a sack of rice that was filled with holes, and countless cultivators were pouring out like grains of rice and scattering in all directions.
The shaking of the Cloud Hall continued to intensify.
In the eyes of the mortals of the Central Domain, the Cloud Hall might be a colossus. It was a palace in the sky that would never sink.
But Ning Yudie and the others knew very well that no matter if it was the Great Shaman or the eight Ultimate God Realm warriors, it usually only took a single move to completely destroy the Cloud Hall …
"Granny, where's dad? Why don't I see him … "Luo Nian crawled on Granny Yu's back.
Although he was still young, when he saw the panic on the faces of countless warriors in the Cloud Hall, he understood that the Cloud Hall seemed to have encountered great trouble.
"Nian 'er, just be good. Your dad will be fine!" Granny Yu's face didn't look too happy.
The Cloud Hall was her life's work. That damned Luo Zheng had caused such great trouble.
Although she said that, Granny Yu was still worried about Luo Zheng. After all, the Cloud Hall was able to reach this step and become the number one sect in the Central Domain mainly because of Luo Zheng.
But everything had its pros and cons. After Luo Zheng ascended, he would eventually encounter trouble. But the trouble he encountered at this stage was far from what the Cloud Hall could touch. In the eyes of those powerful beings, the Cloud Hall was like a small mosquito that could be overturned with a flip of the hand.
Besides the Cloud Hall, all the city gates of Cloud Sea City were opened. All the transportation formations lit up throughout the night.
All the Third Rank Clans, the forces of the World Business Alliance, and even the mortals were moving out of the city.
The battle between Luo Zheng and the others was like a ferocious beast in the forest, and these low-level martial artists and mortals were like an ant nest in the forest. If the ferocious beast was careless and stepped on it, the ant nest would collapse, causing countless deaths and injuries …
"Boom!"
Accompanied by a loud noise, a dazzling thunderbolt burst into the air. The Cloud Hall suddenly shook, and it kept tilting to one side!
Fortunately, the Cloud Hall was originally a huge floating rock. Even if such a huge building was flipped, it would still float in the air!
Seeing this scene, Ning Yudie, who was dismissing everyone, suddenly felt her heart tighten. She stretched her neck and stared at the top of the Cloud Hall, her face full of worry.
The Cloud Hall could be rebuilt!
As long as the foundation was still there, she didn't care even if she had to abandon this floating rock. But nothing could happen to Luo Zheng.
The more she thought about it, the more worried she became. She paused and wanted to return to the Cloud Hall.
"Xiao Die, what are you doing?!" Granny Yu shouted from below.
Ning Yudie's eyes flickered. "I-I want to take a look …"
