Luo Zheng's body was about the same height as the scales on the Candle Dragon's tail. With this collision, the scales were forcefully shattered …
This did not depend on Luo Zheng's strength. It was just that Luo Zheng's back was against the Great Wide World, and he was like a nail that was fixed in place. When the Candle Dragon swept over, all the power was absorbed by Luo Zheng, and under the rebound, he was injured!
Heavenly Lord Huan Miao was almost in a state of despair …
The ancient Sorcerer Tribe was plotting, and so was the Demi-Immortal Tribe. However, he did not expect that the Demi-Immortal Tribe, after working hard for so many years and betting all their treasures on the Eight Children of the Heavenly Dao, would fail in the end.
This might not be Heavenly Lord Huan Miao's fault, but he still felt hopeless.
However, this scene at this moment was a huge visual impact to this Heavenly Lord who had lived for 130 million years.
It was as if a praying mantis had really blocked the wheels from moving forward. Not only had it blocked, but the praying mantis had also raised its weak front limbs and flipped the entire car over.
This almost overturned everyone's understanding.
Hua Tianming, Ku Deng, Xuanyuan Chenfeng, and Lie Qianhan's pale faces gradually turned red, but they were also stunned.
Even though Hua Tianming thought that he knew Luo Zheng better, and even knew some secrets that Luo Zheng himself did not know, at this moment, he realized that he was still too naive … Perhaps Luo Zheng did not know many of the plots, but he did not know all the things that Luo Zheng controlled. However, he could not figure out how Luo Zheng had done it.
Previously, when Luo Zheng blocked the Great Sorcerer's staff, Hua Tianming speculated that Luo Zheng had a secret treasure that could resist the Great Sorcerer's magic. This was not impossible, and it could even be explained that Luo Zheng's life force was so strong that the Cicada of the Nether Spring could not completely suck his life force dry.
The second time, when Luo Zheng attacked and restrained the Great Sorcerer, it could also be seen that Luo Zheng was immune to poison. Although it was abnormal to be able to do this, it was still not beyond the scope of understanding.
However, the scene in front of them was too unbelievable. This was already a situation that they could not understand!
Lie Qianhan could not help but rub his eyes. He doubted what he had seen, but after rubbing for a long time, he still saw the same scene in front of him.
"What happened?" Blood flowed from Xuanyuan Chenfeng's face. He could not sense the Candle Dragon's huge silhouette with his senses. He could only vaguely feel a powerful aura sweeping over. Although he had lost his sight, Xuanyuan Chenfeng was not in a hurry. With the foundation of [Gods No Thoughts], even if he only had half of his body left, as long as he had a breath left, he could recover completely.
"Luo Zheng … blocked the tail of that strange snake," Ji Luoxue said softly.
"… Nonsense!" How could Xuanyuan Chenfeng believe it? However, Ji Luoxue would not lie, let alone in this situation.
However, if they did not see this scene with their own eyes, how could they believe it?
"Roar —"
At this moment, the Candle Dragon suddenly let out a long cry.
It was not a big deal to break a scale on its tail, but it could not accept the fact that an ant had broken its scale.
It wriggled its huge body and was about to sweep out again.
This time, the Heavenly Lord of Huan Miao was going to attack. He would not make such a mistake again.
If it was possible, the Heavenly Lord of Huan Miao was willing to sacrifice himself to save the lives of these Daoists … If the Heavenly Lords died, they could use the Crown of Destiny to create another Heavenly Lord.
If these Daoists died, where would they create another True God?
Just as the Heavenly Lord of Huan Miao was fighting with the Candle Dragon, there was a huge change in the Greater World.
The power of the tail was almost completely transferred to the Greater World by Luo Zheng.
A battle between Heavenly Lords could even destroy a big world, let alone a Greater World.

Chapter 1452
The Wasteland Continent was uninhabited, and there was almost no change for countless years.
Lush vegetation covered the land, and countless ferocious beasts and unknown creatures were hidden within it …
Even the smallest ant was extremely dangerous. Wherever the Jungle Army Ants passed, the land would be barren, and even ferocious beasts would avoid them. These fist-sized ants could gnaw a giant elephant into a skeleton within a few breaths of time.
Back then, almost all of the experts from the God Nation Continent had perished there. In the end, the God Nation Continent gave up on exploring this wasteland.
For countless years, only Hua Tianming had truly ventured deep into the Wasteland Continent and taken away Chiyou's treasure.
At this moment, the entire Wasteland Continent began to tremble.
A huge crack extended continuously from one end of the Wasteland Continent, forming a bottomless canyon. Countless ferocious beasts howled in anguish.
"Rumble …"
The huge force was like a pair of invisible scissors, constantly cutting this Wasteland Continent. This continent, which was twice as wide as the God Nation Continent, was constantly falling apart.
Torrential seawater formed a wall of water that was thousands of feet tall. It poured in through the huge crack and crazily scoured the exposed surface of the continent. Countless creatures ran wildly in all directions on this continent.
The seawater sank deep into the crack in the continent, forming a huge siphon effect. Finally, it surged out in the opposite direction of the crack and rushed toward the God Nation Continent!
Fortunately, the cities of the four God Nations were concentrated around the Heavenly Feather Sacred Ocean. There were few people at the edge of the continent, and the tsunami did not cause too much damage to the God Nation Continent. However, the waves from the sea still alerted the four God Nations.
The Candle Dragon's attack had been diverted by Luo Zheng, and it had forcefully caused the Wasteland Continent to collapse …
"Let's go!"
Luo Zheng would not explain anything to everyone here.
If the Candle Dragon's tail swept over again, how could he resist? Could it be that he had to sacrifice the Central Domain and the God Nation Continent?
As soon as he finished speaking, Luo Zheng tore open a crack in space and instantly went out.
The other Daoists also tore open cracks in space and returned to the front side of this world.
Under the dim moonlight, the faces of the Daoists were filled with shock and uncertainty … After all, they had all thought that they were finished a moment ago.
When the Dao Children looked at Luo Zheng again, their gazes also changed.
Taking the Candle Dragon's attack head-on … Even if they expanded their thoughts to the extreme, they would still find it hard to imagine how Luo Zheng had done it.
There were countless secret treasures in this universe, and each of them had different functions. However, even the top ten treasures on the Universal Myriad Spirit Stele couldn't display such an effect. It was too incredible.
"What do we do now?" Ji Luoxue asked.
"If that Candle Dragon chases after us, we'll be in big trouble," said Dugu Jian Xiaoxiao, looking at Luo Zheng.
No matter what, this place was extremely dangerous for the eight of them, and they absolutely couldn't stay here for long. The wisest choice was to leave at this moment.
"You guys … leave first. I won't leave." After Luo Zheng finished speaking, he activated the Teleportation Token again.
Ku Deng and the others nodded. They didn't need to take risks because of what was happening in front of them. Leaving at this moment was definitely the wisest choice.
However, the moment Luo Zheng activated the Teleportation Token, the minute space energy couldn't gather. The blazing white light turned into strands of oddly shaped fragments, scattering in all directions like colored glass.
"What's going on?" Hua Tianming frowned slightly.
"It seems that all the space energy can't gather together." Luo Zheng's face also showed a strange expression. He had never seen such a situation before.
Speaking of this, Luo Zheng's figure flashed as he tried to use the Space Shuttle. However, as soon as his figure disappeared, he seemed to hit an invisible wall and stumbled in the air.
"Space Imprisonment!" Ji Luoxue's eyes flashed as she said immediately.
Lie Qianhan's face changed suddenly. He looked up and said, "Someone has imprisoned this part of the world …"
"Who could it be?" Xuanyuan Chenfeng said with his eyes closed. At this moment, he had wiped away the golden blood on his face. Since he was blind, he was more anxious to leave this place. When he heard about the Space Imprisonment, he naturally became more nervous.
"I'm afraid only a Celestial Venerable would be able to confine the Greater Thousand Realms. Perhaps they only confine this region. Let's try another place." At this moment, Luo Zheng truly wanted to send them out.
They sank down. After a long distance, Luo Zheng activated the Teleportation Token again, but the result was the same. It seemed that all the Space Law had lost its effect here.
"It seems that this time, we're really going to die here." Xuanyuan Chenfeng smiled bitterly.
In contrast, Hua Tianming was more open-minded. He asked Luo Zheng with a smile, "Luo Zheng, how did you block the Candle Dragon's attack just now?"
Luo Zheng's eyes flashed, but he chuckled and said, "I can't tell you at this stage!"
In fact, Luo Zheng did not need to hide this matter. Although the fusion with the Will of the Great Thousand World was Luo Zheng's secret, this secret could not be taken away by others. However, Hua Tianming obviously knew a lot of things, but he had been keeping Luo Zheng in suspense. Now, he was returning the favor.
Hua Tianming sniffed and looked at the thick clouds in the sky that were moving rapidly. He did not pursue Luo Zheng and only said, "It seems that something big is going to happen in this Great Thousand World. However, I keep feeling that something is wrong. It seems that the Ancient Sorcerer Tribe has set a trap here …"
"A trap? Against us? "Dugu Jian Xiaoxiao asked.
Hua Tianming shook his head. "No, maybe it's a trap against the Heavenly Masters."
It was not hard to guess that the Eight Children of the Heavenly Dao were in danger, and the two Heavenly Masters who were supposed to protect them were in a life-and-death battle. People like Old Man Feng and the Heavenly Master of Original Sin would definitely not be able to sit still. They would definitely rush over as soon as possible.
With the speed of a Heavenly Master, even if the lower realm and the upper realm were far apart, they would still be able to rush over in a short time.
When that time came, the Candle Dragon and the Heavenly Sorcerer would definitely die.
There were not many Heavenly Sorcerers in the Ancient Sorcerer Tribe. At least, there were less than the number of Heavenly Masters. It was very likely that there were no more than ten of them.
At this stage, the Ancient Sorcerer Tribe would not foolishly fight the Heavenly Master of Original Sin and the others head-on.
The Candle Dragon did not leave and chose to fight the Heavenly Master of Mystery. This in itself showed that the Ancient Sorcerer Tribe was not afraid of the Great Thousand World's reinforcements.
"The Ancient Sorcerer Tribe's strength was not enough, but they were not afraid of reinforcements." Ji Luoxue frowned. "Then, the only possibility is that this is a trap.
"Ji Luoxue has already thought of it." Hua Tianming smiled slightly.
"What's the use of thinking it through?" Lie Qianhan said coldly. "We can't leave this damned place. I have two Roaring Tokens, but we can't use them to transmit sound out of this place …"
Luo Zheng drifted downwards for a distance. At this moment, he turned around and said, "We can't leave this place. Let's return to the Cloud Hall first."
Along the way, Luo Zheng's brows were tightly knitted.
He had returned to the lower realm to rescue Ning Yudie. This matter could not have been planned by the Ancient Sorcerer Tribe.
On the other hand, the Ancient Sorcerer Tribe was very concerned about Ning Yudie and the Ninth Palace Master returning to the lower realm. That was why they had chased after them.
There was no connection between the two.
Just as Hua Tianming had speculated, the Great Thousand World was a trap. However, the Ancient Sorcerer Tribe did not intend to activate this trap now. It should be a coincidence that they had entered this trap.
This trap should be related to the Starry Sea Storm in the Chaotic Astral Ocean!
However, Luo Zheng could not find the answer to what exactly this trap was.
With his current strength, he could not interfere in a battle between Heavenly Venerables. At this stage, Luo Zheng could only return to the Cloud Hall first.
On the way back to the Cloud Hall, Luo Zheng took out the Gen Token and tried to communicate with the Immortal Mansion …
This Immortal Mansion was Luo Zheng's final barrier. If he could not enter the Immortal Mansion, then it would be really troublesome.
Soon, Luo Zheng's expression became even more unsightly.
Luo Zheng could quickly return to the Immortal Mansion through the Gen Token, but this did not mean that he had brought the Immortal Mansion with him. From beginning to end, the Immortal Mansion had been located in the huge forest. The Gen Token had only built a special passage.

Chapter 1453
According to Luo Zheng's plan, he would bring all the important people around him into the Immortal Abode when he returned to the Lower Realm this time.
Under the protection of this Immortal Abode, they would be absolutely safe.
At this moment, this path had been completely sealed by the Spatial Shackles …
The seven people behind him followed him silently. Although they did not speak, Luo Zheng could sense their nervousness.
Although they were powerful, they were still too young.
Now that they were following him, their hearts were probably in a mess.
This atmosphere made Luo Zheng feel a trace of heaviness.
Hua Tianming told himself that fate was just a beautiful lie. However, he had led everyone to this point, so he had to bring them out eventually …
The Cloud Hall in the distance was faintly discernible in the clouds. When Luo Zheng approached, he discovered that the Cloud Hall was now empty. Almost all the martial artists had been evacuated.
In a battle between Heavenly Venerables, this kind of retreat was almost meaningless.
If the Candle Dragon and Heavenly Venerate Huan Miao wanted to fight in the Central Domain, they could destroy the entire Central Domain in a few moves.
In the face of absolute power, weak martial artists were like fish on a chopping board. They had no room to fight back.
Just like Luo Zheng, who had been cultivating for so long, the martial artists in the Central Domain could not compare to him. However, this still could not change anything …
Soon, Luo Zheng found Ning Yudie in the ice palace at the top of the Cloud Hall.
She was sitting cross-legged in the center of the ice palace, cultivating wholeheartedly.
However, the vital essence in the Lower Realm was too thin. The cultivation speed of a Divine Sea Realm martial artist in the Lower Realm was far slower than in the Upper Realm.
Luo Zheng descended from the sky well of the ice palace, and the seven Dao Disciples followed him in.
Ning Yudie heard the commotion and opened her eyes.
She had been busy the entire night, and her face was slightly tired. This was not physical fatigue, but mental fatigue.
There were too many things that made her afraid, and her nerves were almost always in a state of tension.
She was worried that Luo Zheng would never return. She was worried that those experts would destroy the Central Domain. She was also worried about Nian 'er's situation.
At this moment, seeing Luo Zheng appear, she squeezed out a smile on her tired face.
"Where is Nian 'er?" Luo Zheng asked.
"Master took him to the east. It should be safe," Ning Yudie said.
Upon hearing this, the Daoists retorted in their hearts. Safety … There was no such thing as safety in the Great Wide World. No matter where they hid, they might be affected by those Heavenly Venerables if they were not careful.
Luo Zheng also had the same idea. He had used his power to destroy a wild continent with his own hands, so he understood that hiding was meaningless.
However, Luo Zheng didn't intend to let the disciples of the Cloud Hall return.
Humans often had this mentality. Even if they knew that they couldn't escape, they would still stubbornly look for a relatively safe place to live.
As for the next step, Luo Zheng didn't give any specific suggestions.
Everyone sat down and meditated in the ice palace, waiting quietly.
Xi Youqin was too lazy to meditate. Instead, she leaned against Luo Zheng and fell asleep. Ning Yudie took a few glances and also admired this woman for being able to fall asleep in such a situation …
Dugu Jian Xiaoxiao, Ji Luoxue, and the others still didn't give up. After some discussion, the two of them each proposed a way to get out of this predicament.
Ji Luoxue's idea was to remove the effect of the Moon-Concealing Stone and use the repulsive force of the Great Wide World to push them out.
She quickly put her idea into action.
After Ji Luoxue released the Moon-Concealing Stone, the repulsive force of the Great Wide World exerted itself on her.
This repulsive force couldn't be resisted and would keep dragging her into the Ascension Passage. This scene made the Daoists look hopeful. Perhaps they could use the Ascension Passage to escape this space imprisonment?
However, Ji Luoxue flew up lightly and finally stopped in the air. The Ascension Passage never appeared.
In fact, when Ji Luoxue proposed this idea, everyone expected this to happen. The Ascension Passage was originally a manifestation of the space rules. Now that space was imprisoned, the Ascension Passage naturally couldn't appear.
As for Dugu Jian Xiaoxiao's idea, it was more direct.
Now that everyone was imprisoned in this world and couldn't use the space rules, they could directly pass through the astral wind layer.
Although the astral wind at the top of the Great Wide World was powerful, they still had a way to resist it.
This plan was very feasible. After Dugu Jian Xiaoxiao proposed it, Lie Qianhan agreed. Then, the two of them, together with Ku Deng, set off from the top of the Cloud Hall and went all the way up.
After about four hours, the three of them returned to the Cloud Hall unhappily.
Looking at their expressions, Luo Zheng knew that they must have failed.
"After passing through the astral wind layer, there's an impenetrable space. We've used all kinds of methods, but we still can't get through," Lie Qianhan said gloomily.
Luo Zheng comforted him. In this situation, they could only wait.
Unknowingly, a night had passed. The sky was already bright.
Hua Tianming quietly stood up. He took out the Sword Tengshe. With a light wave of his sharp sword, a small crack appeared in the void.
The back of the space was originally endless darkness. Normally, it was very difficult to see anything when a small crack appeared.
However, the situation was different when the Candle Dragon and the Heavenly Lord of Mirage fought. He just wanted to see a little.
Seeing this, Ji Luoxue asked, "How's the situation?"
Hua Tianming shook his head. "I can't see anything."
There was no obstruction on the back of the space. When the human and dragon fought, it was very easy for them to escape far away …
This answer made everyone feel a little better.
At least they were already far away from the Central Domain. However, for Heavenly Lords, this distance was just a step away.
However, at that moment, Luo Zheng and the rest suddenly felt an extremely powerful aura descend. He was not the only one who felt this aura. The few Dao Disciples, including Ning Yudie, and even all the living beings in the Central Domain subconsciously looked up at the sky.
That majestic aura was like a layer of the sky directly covering them. It made the hearts of billions of living beings palpitate.
The clouds at the top of the Cloud Hall kept surging. Like a huge reverse whirlpool, they quickly dissipated into the surroundings …
Then, seven figures rapidly descended. In the blink of an eye, the few Dao Disciples, who were originally looking up, started to look at these seven figures at the same level.
"Patriarch …"
Although Xuanyuan Chenfeng was blind, he could still feel the Heavenly Lord of Original Sin's aura.
From yesterday until now, Xuanyuan Chenfeng had been in a state of panic. However, when he felt the aura of the Xuanyuan family's Patriarch, he relaxed a lot.
For him, the Heavenly Lord of Original Sin represented the most powerful existence in the universe. With him here, he was naturally safe.
Not only Xuanyuan Chenfeng, but Ji Luoxue, Ku Deng, and the others also heaved a long sigh of relief. Whether this Great Thousand World was a trap or not, it was much safer to see their family's elders.
This time, it was not only the Heavenly Lord of Original Sin who came. Old Feng, the Heavenly Lord of Revelation, and the Heavenly Lord of Thunder Punishment also appeared!
From the looks of it, it seemed that the trouble caused by the Ancient Sorcerer Tribe in the universe had been solved. However, even the usually amiable Old Man Feng had a cold expression on their faces. It seemed that the trouble was bigger than they had imagined.
The Heavenly Lord of Original Sin glanced at the few Dao Disciples. After confirming the number, he said indifferently, "Take them away."
The Heavenly Lord of Thunder Punishment raised his long sleeve. A Cosmos Bag flew out from it and sucked Xuanyuan Chenfeng and the others in first.
When it was Luo Zheng's turn, he dodged to the side and said with a sullen face, "I won't leave."
＝＝＝＝＝

Chapter 1454
The Celestial Venerables had naturally received the summons of Huan Miao and Venerable Qian Lan.
The first step after arriving at the Cloud Hall was to protect the eight Daoists, but Luo Zheng was determined not to leave.
They didn't force him. They left Luo Zheng and Xi Youqin here and then escaped into the Void.
Since these Celestial Venerables were here, the Space Shackles must have been removed.
Since that was the case, Luo Zheng no longer hesitated.
No matter how the situation in the Vast Thousand World evolved, Luo Zheng first had to transfer Ning Yudie and the others to a safe place.
Next, Luo Zheng left the Cloud Hall with Ning Yudie and Xi Youqin. They found Granny Yu and Nian 'er 150 kilometers north of Cloud Sea City, and then they found Red Maple, Pei Tianyao, and the others.
Then, he activated the Gen token in his hand in front of everyone.
Luo Zheng didn't warn them. Although the Immortal Mansion itself was no small matter, and the secrets inside could not be leaked, there were restrictions everywhere in the Immortal Mansion. Without the token in Luo Zheng's hand, they couldn't move around at all, so the Immortal Mansion became their temporary refuge.
Luo Zheng himself didn't enter.
Since returning to the Vast Thousand World, he faintly felt that the Will of the Vast Thousand World had undergone some strange changes.
As soon as he returned, he already knew that one end of the Vast Thousand World had been damaged. In other words, the two Vast Thousand Worlds were connected to each other without any obstruction.
In the past few days, he suddenly discovered that the barrier on the other end of the Vast Thousand World seemed to have been opened!
Luo Zheng searched the memories of the Vast Thousand World but didn't get a definite answer. There were only two things that he was sure of. The barrier was definitely destroyed by someone, and the person who destroyed it was definitely not a creature of the Vast Thousand World. It was most likely the Ancient Shaman Tribe. As for their true purpose, Luo Zheng didn't know.
It was also because of this point that Luo Zheng decided to stay.
Everyone in the Central Domain was on tenterhooks about the Cloud Hall incident …
News of calamities always spread extraordinarily quickly. All the cultivators in the Cloud Hall had been dismissed, and news of countless refugees had appeared in Ocean of Clouds City had spread through the various teleportation channels at an extremely fast speed.
The Central Domain was the soil they relied on to survive. Where could they go after leaving the Central Domain? Not everyone can pass through the storm of the Chaotic Astral Ocean.
After settling everything, Luo Zheng went to the other side of the world.
The reverse side of the world was a void that was shrouded in pure darkness. After Old Man Feng and the other Heavenly Lords entered the reverse side of the world, they had already escaped far away.
After determining the direction, Luo Zheng quickly flew in the boundless darkness.
When Celestial Venerables fought, they could move thousands of miles in the blink of an eye. It was impossible for Luo Zheng to follow their footsteps.
While Luo Zheng was exploring the Void, somewhere tens of thousands of miles away, Luo Zheng's eyes lit up.
The Candle Dragon that stretched for thousands of miles was currently coiled up. The wisp of 'Heavenly Flame' on top of its huge 'snake head' was already extremely dim. Its pair of strange snake eyes were staring straight ahead.
The Candle Dragon's body was already covered in wounds.
With the Candle Dragon's strength, he could fight against a middle-level Heavenly Venerable without being defeated. Old Man Feng, Heavenly Venerable Original Sin, Heavenly Venerable Thunder Punishment, and Heavenly Venerable Oracle were all upper-level Heavenly Venerables in the universe.
In the face of such powerful opponents, the Holy Beast of the Ancient Sorcerer Tribe did not have the ability to fight back at all.
Heavenly Venerable Huan Miao, who had fought with the Candle Dragon previously, was already severely injured and had fled far away. As for Heavenly Venerable Qian Lan, he had also killed the Heaven Sorcerer and joined the ranks of the Ancient Sorcerer Tribe to hunt down the Candle Dragon …
The Heavenly Venerables did not have much of an expression on their faces.
This trip to the Lower Realm was just to eliminate the Ancient Sorcerer Tribe as a nuisance.
The Candle Dragon in front of them was a treasure after being killed. Whether it was the pair of huge snake eyes or the Mystical Fire on top of his head, they were all extremely precious materials. The Heavenly Venerables had already begun to think about how to distribute the loot after killing the Candle Dragon …
However, at this moment.
In the center of the storm in the Chaotic Astral Ocean, in front of the tower that was hidden at the core.
Countless Battle Sorcerers prostrated themselves in front of the tower. The Great Sorcerer in the lead held a bone staff in his hand. A wisp of Netherworld Fire rose from the end of the bone staff. Everyone began to chant a complicated incantation continuously.
As they continued to chant, the Battle Sorcerers began to disintegrate one after another.
From their physical bodies to their souls, they turned into wisps of green flames that rapidly gathered at the end of the bone staff in the Great Sorcerer's hand!
That wisp of flame became more and more vigorous!
Swoosh!
The Netherworld Fire rose into the air, and the fire was extremely fierce. Then, the Great Sorcerer carefully extended the bone staff and slowly placed it in front of the tower!
Whoosh …
The Netherworld's Fire quickly revolved around the tower.
"Wuyaka!"
After the Great Sorcerer recited the incantation, he turned into a wisp of Netherworld Fire and entered the tower …
At this moment, the tower also began to slowly rotate, and the rotation speed became faster and faster. At the same time, many small cracks began to appear on the surface of the tower.
"Crack, crack …"
The cracks continued to expand, as if something was trapped inside and wanted to rush out of the tower!
At the same time, the storm in the core of the Chaotic Astral Ocean became stronger and stronger. Under the crazy cutting of the wind blades, the space was also constantly collapsing!
Under the countless cutting of the wind blades, countless spatial cracks appeared in the space in this area. Some of the wind blades spread through the cracks.
"Dong …"
In the end, the tower still exploded.
From the tower, an invisible will began to spread out rapidly!
And this storm turned into the help of this will!
At the same time, outside of hundreds of neighboring worlds, a similar tower exploded. In this world, there was a place of storm. Warriors in this world called it the "Forbidden Area of the God of Wind". It was said that there was a God of Wind living in the center of the storm. Therefore, the gale in this place never dissipated. Even warriors of the Spirit Sea Realm would not be able to make it out alive!
After the pagoda exploded, there was also an invisible will that spread out!
These two towers were not the only ones that exploded at the same time. In fact, the towers that the Ancient Sorcerer Tribe had placed in the Greater World were also arranged according to a certain plan after precise calculations!
At this moment, as these pagodas continued to erupt, the willpower in the pagodas rapidly spread throughout the Upper-Class World. Almost all the barriers of these Upper-Class Worlds were broken through, and the invisible willpower spread from the opening of one Upper-Class World to the next!
Old Man Feng and the others confronted the Candle Dragon for a while before the Heavenly Lord of Original Sin made the first move.
Even in the face of the Candle Dragon's strong physical body, the Heavenly Lord of Original Sin was not afraid at all. He planned to fight force with force …
Hiss!
The Candle Dragon let out a sharp cry, and its huge body twisted in the void. It had chosen to retreat.
How could the Heavenly Lords let the Candle Dragon go? They all turned into streaks of light and surrounded it from different directions.
However, at this moment, the will had already spread out. When the Heavenly Lords sensed the will, their expressions changed. As if someone had cast a spell on them, they all froze on the spot.

Chapter 1455
After Luo Zheng merged with the will of the Vast Thousand Worlds, he himself became the child of the Vast Thousand Worlds.
In fact, he was the master of the Vast Thousand Worlds!
Therefore, he could naturally transfer his power to the Vast Thousand Worlds and even look up the memories of any time and place in the Vast Thousand Worlds!
However, that will spread over and gradually merged into the Great Wide World!
Luo Zheng could not stop this fusion. Because Luo Zheng's will had merged with the will of the Vast Thousand Worlds, three wills had merged with each other!
What surprised Luo Zheng even more was that this will was spreading at an extremely fast speed!
In less than ten breaths of time, this will had spread outside the Vast Thousand Worlds and merged with the wills of the other Vast Thousand Worlds again, continuing to spread rapidly!
"This …" Luo Zheng's face revealed a look of surprise.
Previously, after these worlds were damaged, Luo Zheng felt that he could detect more distant worlds, such as the sea area of the connected Vast Thousand Worlds.
However, at this moment, Luo Zheng also felt that the range of his perception was increasing at an incredible speed!
Luo Zheng was able to sense everything that was happening in the surrounding four great worlds with great detail.
For example, in the Greater Thousand World in the south, there was only one huge continent. This continent was almost twenty times the size of the Divine Kingdom's continent. The sea area in that Greater Thousand World was not that big, only occupying about a third of it.
The Greater Thousand World in the north was the complete opposite. The entire world was an ocean world with all kinds of islands. On these islands, there were all kinds of living beings, and the majority of these living beings were also from the Human Race …
After 100 breaths of time, Luo Zheng could perceive 16 Vast Thousand Worlds!
After the time it took for an incense stick to burn, Luo Zheng could already sense more than four hundred great worlds …
At this moment, the frantically spreading will gradually slowed down.
"Is the limit less than 500 Vast Thousand Worlds?" Luo Zheng kept perceiving in his mind and had a rough estimate in his heart.
However, he did not know if the transfer of power could target these Great Thousand Worlds. If it could be transferred to these Great Thousand Worlds, it would be a great boost for Luo Zheng. However, he hoped that this will could spread further!
However, at this moment, Luo Zheng suddenly realized that the number of worlds he could sense had doubled. It had directly increased from 500 worlds to over 1,000 worlds!
"What's going on?" Luo Zheng was slightly stunned.
He pondered for a while and soon understood that this will had spread out from the center of the Chaotic Astral Ocean and eventually spread to more than five hundred Great Thousand Worlds. In fact, this kind of extremely special will was not limited to only one. It also existed in other Great Thousand Worlds. As it continued to spread, the two wills converged and merged.
"Could it be that millions of Greater Worlds will be connected? If this was really set up by the Ancient Sorcerer Tribe, then what they are plotting is truly terrifying! "Luo Zheng was thoroughly shocked.
As soon as he came to this conclusion, the Great Wide World that Luo Zheng could perceive in his mind suddenly doubled.
3,000 Great Wide Worlds …
These wills continued to spread from their positions and began to merge with the wills scattered across millions of Great Thousand Worlds. The number of worlds Luo Zheng could perceive began to increase, and the speed of increase was getting faster and faster.
Fifteen thousand Great Thousand Worlds …
40,000 Great Wide Worlds …
100,000 Great Wide Worlds …
300,000 Great Wide Worlds …
800,000 Great Wide Worlds …
The further it went, the faster it spread. However, in the end, it still stopped.
Because in Luo Zheng's mind, he could already perceive millions of Great Thousand Worlds. To be exact, there were 1,000,000 and 33 Great Thousand Worlds.
This was a very wonderful feeling.
Everything in the 1,000,000 and 33 Great Thousand Worlds was connected into a whole!
Luo Zheng, on the other hand, was able to sense anything in this whole entity!
In each Great Thousand World, there were countless creatures living, cultivating, fighting, and reproducing. These Great Thousand Worlds hid many secrets, secret realms, and treasures, waiting for warriors to discover, excavate, and explore.
However, Luo Zheng only needed a thought, and these secrets would belong to him.
100,000 Great Thousand Worlds away from Luo Zheng, a war between different races broke out on a continent. All the demons were slaughtered by the Demon Night Clan, and the remaining few hundred demons launched a counterattack. In another Great Thousand World connected to this continent, a grand ceremony was being held. It seemed to be the wedding of the Monarch of a Divine Nation …
Luo Zheng could completely grasp the information of a war between sects, a mortal, or even a bug that spread its wings and flew high!
At this moment, Luo Zheng even had an illusion that he was the god of all the Great Thousand Worlds!
What made Luo Zheng even more speechless was that when this will spread to the edge of the Great Thousand Worlds, it seemed to have a faint tendency to go upward. Moreover, it spread through countless ascension passages into the universe. At the end of these ascension passages were 100,000 Great Worlds and countless stars!
Luo Zheng was speechless. This will wouldn't spread to the Upper World, would it? He didn't know if the power transfer would work.
Thinking of this, Luo Zheng flipped his hand and slapped his chest.
In a Great Thousand World tens of millions of miles away from Luo Zheng, a stone in the forest of a continent exploded without warning!
"This …" Luo Zheng's eyes flickered, but a smile appeared on his face.
In theory, Luo Zheng had merged all the Great Thousand Worlds. As long as he was in the Great Thousand Worlds, unless his opponent could destroy millions of Great Thousand Worlds, he was an invincible existence!
However, if he fought with a Celestial Venerable expert, he would still be in a very sorry state. After all, the Candle Dragon could destroy an entire continent with a sweep of his tail. If Luo Zheng continued to bear the attacks of a Celestial Venerable, the people in the Great Thousand Worlds would probably be plunged into misery and suffering.
Luo Zheng continued to sense the changes in the will of the Great Thousand Worlds.
At the same time …
Old Man Feng and the others felt the changes, and their expressions were as ugly as they could be.
"This is the real plan of the Ancient Sorcerer Tribe?" The Heavenly Venerable of Original Sin's expression was grave.
"They want to control the Way of the Heavens … This is impossible!" The Heavenly Venerable Shen Yu frowned and said.
As for the Candle Dragon in front of them, it was also undergoing a drastic change at that moment. Its gigantic body kept on twisting and shrinking. However, the Candle Dragon had chosen to transform at that moment!
Its huge body finally transformed into a petite lady. However, this lady had no hair, and there was only a wisp of Mystical Fire burning slowly above her head.
After transforming, the Candle Dragon was still covered in injuries. However, as it stared at the Heavenly Venerables, a cold smile appeared on its face. "I have disappointed all of you!"
After saying that, a rune that looked like a map appeared between its eyebrows. The rune flickered rapidly like a firefly, causing the Candle Dragon to enter a certain state.

Chapter 1456
Luo Zheng's soul had fused with all the wills of the Great Thousand Worlds.
The wills of the Great Thousand Worlds didn't have any thoughts, they would only react instinctively when the Great Thousand Worlds were damaged.
In a way, Luo Zheng's soul was able to control all the Great Thousand Worlds!
The will was extremely powerful, and it wasn't something Luo Zheng could fight against with his current strength.
However, Luo Zheng had taken the initiative, he didn't need to rely on his own soul to fight against this will.
With just a single thought, he could use the will of the Great Thousand Worlds to fight against it.
No matter how powerful one's soul was, it was impossible to fight against the will of the Great Thousand Worlds. Furthermore, this was a will formed from the union of a million and three Great Thousand Worlds. It had gone beyond the strength of all living beings in the universe.
Even Heavenly Sovereigns who were proficient in cultivating their souls couldn't fight against it.
"It seems like this will is using some kind of secret technique to fuse with the will of the Great Thousand Worlds." A strange expression emerged on Luo Zheng's face.
He had accidentally fused with the will of the Great Thousand Worlds, but it seemed like the other party was doing it on purpose!
With this thought in mind, Luo Zheng smiled. Following Luo Zheng's thought, the will of the Great Thousand Worlds erupted with killing intent!
"Swoosh …"
The invisible killing intent spread out in all directions!
All living beings in the million and three Great Thousand Worlds raised their heads and looked over at the same time.
Countless Xiantian living beings, Radiant God warriors, Divine Core warriors …
The lords of the Sacred Lands, the lords of the Divine Kingdoms, the ferocious beasts hiding under the ground, and the mighty warriors standing on top of the mountains, all of them could sense the killing intent.
"This … this is the killing intent of the Heavenly Dao!"
"The Heavenly Dao is enraged! Who is the one that has caused the Heavenly Dao to be enraged?!"
"Please calm down, Heavenly Dao!"
"This is a great opportunity, I'm going to comprehend the supreme True Intent from the Heavenly Dao!"
Countless living beings were trying to guess the meaning behind this killing intent. But, what they didn't know was that this killing intent was just triggered by Luo Zheng's thought.
How powerful was this killing intent?
In just a split second, the powerful will that invaded the Great Thousand Worlds was instantly extinguished by the will of the Great Thousand Worlds.
That powerful consciousness was like a candle flame standing in the middle of a torrential downpour. It was instantly extinguished!
At the same time, the Heavenly Venerables also felt the killing intent.
Old Man Feng, Heavenly Lord of Original Sin, Heavenly Lord of Oracle, and the others were also perplexed. Where did this murderous intent come from?
They naturally did not believe that the Great Wide World would really be enraged.
The Boundless World did have a certain level of self-consciousness, but that kind of consciousness would not transform into specific emotions. It could even be said that the Heavenly Dao was like a puppet that could move freely. It would only operate according to certain rules!
What was even more unexpected was that as soon as the killing intent appeared, the talisman that looked like a map on the Candle Dragon's forehead suddenly shattered. At the same time, the Candle Dragon let out a sharp shriek and could not help but cover her head with her hands!
Consciousness was an extension of the soul. Her consciousness had been directly destroyed by Luo Zheng. Naturally, she was in incomparable pain!
But compared to the shock in his heart, this pain was nothing!
When she combined with the will of the Great Wide World, she discovered that there was another consciousness in the will of the Great Wide World!
That strand of consciousness seemed to have been there for a long time. How was that possible?
The Ancient Shamans had spent countless years and countless amounts of blood to plan this grand scheme.
Their goal was to take control of the entire Heavenly Dao without anyone noticing.
Countless plans were proposed. After countless sleepless days and nights, they finally decided on this plan. They started from the bottom of the Great Wide World and gradually spread it to the entire Great Wide World. Finally, they reached the stars in the sky …
There were countless difficulties in this plan, but they were all overcome by the Ancient Shamans.
For example, a million Incarnation Comprehension Crystals …
This Incarnation Comprehension Crystal was a treasure for cultivating the soul.
It was widely used in the Divine Territory to cultivate the soul.
This Heavenly Dao had to be cultivated in the Great Wide World and crystallized in the depths of the Earth. After countless years of growth, it would grow.
This process was measured in hundreds of millions of years. The time span was four to five billion years.
The most troublesome thing was that when Yan Ji was destroyed, the Incarnation Comprehension Crystals that they had painstakingly cultivated were also destroyed once …
Billions of years of persistence were destroyed in a day. But the Ancient Shamans did not give up. They could only start all over again.
In fact, the plan of the Ancient Shamans was the same as that of the Demi-Immortal Tribe. All the plans were written in their blood and passed down from generation to generation. They continued to add bricks and tiles to the bricks and tiles. Only then could they complete an incredible feat with a long span of time.
All the storms spread at the same time and merged a false will with the Great Wide World. At that time, the Candle Dragon and other Sacred Beasts, as well as the Heaven Shamans of the Ancient Shamans, would be connected to the Great Wide World. They would become the actual controllers.
At that time, they would have the strength to fight against the Celestial Venerables of the Great Wide World. Only then would they have the qualifications to compete with the Demi-Immortal Tribe.
For this operation, they even pretended to attack the Great Wide World and launched a destructive attack on some of the Holy Lands in the Great Wide World. At the same time, they spread the news that they were awakening the Ferocious Beasts in the Great Wide World. The real plan was carried out together!
It was just that when they were pretending to attack the Huan Yu World, there was a small mistake, and they were forced into the Lower Realm by Ning Yudie and the others. It just so happened that the Ancient Sorcerer Tribe had set up a storm in that Upper Realm!
Coincidentally, because of Luo Zheng, Ning Yudie's whereabouts attracted Old Man Feng's divination.
The Ancient Shamans were worried that their plan would be exposed. In the end, they had no choice but to carry out their plan in advance. Even if they did it in front of the Celestial Venerables, they could be considered to have succeeded.
However, they did not expect that before the false will spread and merged, Luo Zheng had already merged with the will of one of the Great Wide Worlds. With the help of the diffusion of the false will, Luo Zheng became the actual controller of all the Great Wide Worlds!
Therefore, the Candle Dragon's merging failed. This was a failure that the Ancient Shamans could not tolerate.
After Luo Zheng destroyed the Candle Dragon's will, he quickly discovered that a few other strong wills were trying to merge with the will of the Great Wide World.
These wills were either the Sacred Beasts of the Ancient Shamans who were hiding in the Great Wide World or the powerful Heaven Shamans …
How could Luo Zheng tolerate the invasion of these wills?
With a thought, the will of the Great Wide World produced waves of murderous intent and wiped out all of these wills.
Therefore, besides the Candle Dragon, the Heaven Shamans also experienced extreme pain. At the same time, they discovered that there was a detestable soul hidden in the will of the Great Wide World!
"Who exactly is that fellow!"
All the Heaven Shamans of the Ancient Shamans roared crazily!
Indignation, anger, disappointment, powerlessness — these emotions reverberated continuously on the other side of the Great Wide World …
The strange expressions on the faces of Old Man Feng and the other Celestial Venerables slowly turned into relieved smiles.
They now knew about the Ancient Shamans' plan. A moment ago, they were still extremely worried. After all, once all the Great Wide Worlds were controlled, they would be in a passive position. However, at this moment, they saw the Candle Dragon's failure!
However, the Celestial Venerables were also baffled. Judging from the Candle Dragon's miserable cries, it seemed that its soul had been damaged. With the Candle Dragon's soul power, who else in this world could injure it severely besides the Celestial Venerables?
Thus, the Celestial Venerables launched an attack on the Candle Dragon in this baffling situation!
At this moment, the Candle Dragon had already transformed into its human form. Its combat power had already been greatly reduced. If it had merged with the will of the Great Wide World, the Celestial Venerables would not have been able to do anything to it. However, at this moment, it was completely disheartened. In the end, its head was chopped off by Celestial Venerable Shen Yu's sword!
The mystical fire on its head was still burning fiercely. The Candle Dragon's fire was considered a rare flame in the world. Naturally, the Celestial Venerables would not extinguish it. Even though the Candle Dragon had been dead for many years, the flames were still burning fiercely.
"The Ancient Shamans actually have the ability to seize the Heavenly Way. It's really amazing!" The Thunder Punishing Celestial Venerables sighed. Although the Ten Thousand Buddha Sacred Region that he led was powerful, it was not to this extent. "However, what exactly happened? Why did the Candle Dragon fail? "
Old Man Feng shook his head. He was also puzzled by the murderous intent that had appeared in the Great Wide World. After a while, he came up with a theory. "Perhaps … there was a traitor in the Ancient Shamans. One of them merged with the will of the Great Wide World and rejected the others."
Based on the current clues, Old Man Feng could only come up with this theory.
How could he have known that there was indeed someone who had merged with the will of the Great Wide World? However, it was not someone from the Ancient Shamans. That person was Luo Zheng.

Chapter 1457
Luo Zheng stayed in the Void for a long time.
Some powerful consciousnesses attacked the will of the Vast Thousand Worlds one after another, but they were all destroyed by him.
There were a total of forty-five attacks.
Some of the consciousnesses obviously came from the same person, and they seemed unwilling to be killed by Luo Zheng. They attacked again and again, trying to gain a place in the will of the Vast Thousand Worlds.
However, this kind of attack was destined to be futile.
The wills of 1,003 Vast Thousand Worlds were merged together for Luo Zheng's use. How powerful was this will?
Even if those consciousnesses were as powerful as Celestial Venerables, Luo Zheng could kill them as easily as crushing an ant...
Destroying consciousnesses caused great damage to the soul. After Luo Zheng killed them again and again, the unwilling souls finally stopped, and the Vast Thousand Worlds fell into silence.
"Those guys should be from the Ancient Witch Tribe," Luo Zheng analyzed indifferently.
He tried to find the creatures that attacked the will of the Vast Thousand Worlds from its memory, but it was a blank. These people were definitely not natives of the Vast Thousand Worlds, and at the same time, they could not be Old Man Feng and the others. The only thing he could be sure of was that they were from the Ancient Witch Tribe.
After the attacks stopped, Luo Zheng moved forward again in the Void, all the way to the Chaotic Astral Ocean. The back side of the Chaotic Astral Ocean was empty.
Luo Zheng originally thought that this was the lair of the Ancient Witch Tribe, but now he did not see any living creatures. Could it be that they had been wiped out by Old Man Feng and the others?
He naturally did not know that in order to complete the spread of the false will, all the Ancient Witch Tribe had sacrificed themselves, and even the tower that originally existed had been burned to ashes.
Thus, Luo Zheng tore open a crack and entered the front side of the world.
He did not expect that outside the crack, it was calm and peaceful...
"This... is this still the Chaotic Astral Ocean?" Luo Zheng was speechless.
The storm that had been hovering above the Chaotic Astral Ocean for countless years had disappeared.
The gentle waves slowly beat under Luo Zheng's feet. This part of the sea seemed to have completely recovered its tranquility.
Luo Zheng had vaguely guessed that the diffusion of the will of the Vast Thousand Worlds should have spread along with this storm. Now that all the wills of the Vast Thousand Worlds were continuously spreading, they had long been connected into one. This meant that the mission of the storm had ended.
It was unknown where Old Man Feng and the others had gone. Although Luo Zheng could not locate them through the will of the Great Wide World, the Great Wide World was peaceful. These Celestial Lords might have already taken control of the situation.
Thinking of this, Luo Zheng felt slightly relieved. The reason why he did not want to leave was that he was afraid that the entire Central Domain would be destroyed. If the Celestial Venerables could suppress the Ancient Sorcerer Tribe, Luo Zheng would feel much more at ease.
After circling around this part of the sea, as the storm stopped, some ferocious beasts that were hidden deep in the sea surfaced one after another. They seemed to be puzzled as to why the Chaotic Astral Ocean had suddenly become so peaceful.
Some powerful ferocious beasts in the sea discovered Luo Zheng, but after glancing at him and confirming Luo Zheng's cultivation, they eventually dove back into the sea. The power that Luo Zheng emitted made them understand that he was not an existence that they could provoke.
Not long after, Luo Zheng once again returned to the Central Domain.
Due to what had happened before, the entire Central Domain was in a state of panic. Moreover, when Luo Zheng killed the wills of the experts of the Ancient Sorcerer Tribe, a sense of crisis erupted, causing the people of the Central Domain to be extremely worried. At this moment, everyone was in danger, as if the end of the world was coming.
The people of the Vast Thousand Domains did not know the true face of the Heavenly Dao. They had all constructed different legends based on their own cultures. However, the invisible killing intent that came from the Heavenly Dao caused a considerable number of people to fall into a state of fear.
The Cloud Hall was still completely empty, and the entire city was like an empty city. Not to mention cultivators, even ordinary people had fled with their families. The news of danger spread from the upper echelons of society to the lower echelons of society, and it didn't take long for it to spread by everyone's ears and form a tide of fleeing ….
After returning to the Cloud Hall, Luo Zheng activated the Gen Token and released everyone who was temporarily hiding in the Divine Dwellings.
Seeing that the Cloud Hall was intact, Ning Yudie and the others heaved a sigh of relief. At least it was just a false alarm.
If the Celestial Venerables had really attacked here, even a hundred Central Domain would not be enough to destroy them.
However, Luo Zheng himself did not know that because he had merged with the Will of the Vast Thousand Domains, he had stopped the Candle Dragon from merging with the Will of the Vast Thousand Domains.
After all, after the Candle Dragon had merged with the Will of the Vast Thousand Domains, it would definitely fight with the many Celestial Venerables. Putting aside whether it would be able to win, since the Candle Dragon knew how to merge with the Will of the Vast Thousand Domains, it would definitely know how to transfer power. In fact, it might even be possible that its usage of the Will of the Vast Thousand Domains was much richer than Luo Zheng's!
If the Candle Dragon had to bear the attack of Old Man Feng and the others and transferred its power to the Vast Thousand Domains, it would not take long for the Vast Thousand Domains to be completely shattered.
After that, Ning Yudie gathered the disciples who had been dismissed from the Cloud Hall and reorganized the Cloud Hall.
Not long after, the Celestial Venerate of Huan Miao appeared in the Cloud Hall again and informed Luo Zheng that Old Man Feng and the others had returned to the universe. At the same time, it also informed Luo Zheng of the situation in the universe.
Currently, the entire universe was cleaning up the Ancient Sorcerer Tribe. Under the leadership of the Celestial Venerate of Original Sin, they had infiltrated various large domains to hunt down the Heaven Sorcerer Tribe. Now, the Ancient Sorcerer Tribe was basically in a state of disintegration …
The trouble caused by the Ancient Sorcerer Tribe had once caused the entire universe to be in extreme chaos. However, with the help of many Celestial Venerables, it had been suppressed.
"Actually, the most important thing is the battle with the Candle Dragon. These guys wanted to steal the Heavenly Dao, but it seems that an accident happened. The old patriarch is currently investigating the person who merged with the Will of the Vast Thousand Domains," the Celestial Venerate of Huan Miao said.
Hearing this, Luo Zheng's expression did not change, but his heart felt very strange.
Wasn't the person who merged with the Will of the Vast Thousand Domains himself?
The Celestial Venerate of Huan Miao did not see through Luo Zheng's thoughts. He had only come here because of the old patriarch's orders to inform Luo Zheng so that he could return to the Demi-Immortal Tribe in time and enter the Forbidden Land of God Refinement.
From the beginning, the Ancient Sorcerer Tribe was not the greatest threat. The greatest threat came from the other side of the Wall of Sighs, and the Wall of Sighs in this universe could be broken through at any time. These Dao Disciples had to increase their cultivation as soon as possible.
A few days later, Ning Yudie gradually gathered the disciples of the Cloud Hall and passed the position of Hall Master to Daoist Master Dameng.
After that, Luo Zheng invited Luo Nian and the girls into the Immortal Mansion. Once again, he activated the Teleportation Token and returned to the Great World where the Demi-Immortal Tribe was located.
The Teleportation Token was extremely convenient and could allow Luo Zheng to travel freely. However, there was a problem. The Great World where he was teleported, and even the Vast Thousand Domains, was uncertain of his position.
The Vast Thousand Domains were still fine. After all, it was only so big. With Luo Zheng's current speed, it would not take much time to cross over. However, if he were to teleport to any position in the Great World, he would still be very far from his destination.
This time, it was the same. Luo Zheng was teleported to the edge of the Great World.
If he could not find the Ascension Passage, with Luo Zheng's fastest speed, it would take him more than half a year to reach his destination.
In desperation, Luo Zheng could only use the Roaring Token to call for help.
After Luo Zheng returned, the ten Dao Disciples returned to their positions. Xuanyuan Chenfeng's eyes, which were originally blind, had now returned to normal.

Chapter 1458
The existence of these ten Dao Children was extremely important to the universe.
With their talents, they would eventually reach the realm of the Realm Lord and break through the limits of the Heavenly Dao. However, they didn't have much time left.
Although some Celestial Venerables judged that these people would need time to break through the Wall of Sighs.
This time span could be a hundred years or even a thousand years, but it could also be within these ten years.
After all, the Ancient Sorcerer Clan had already taken action, which meant that the other force was also ready …
The purpose of sending all the Dao Children to the Demi-Immortal Clan was to send them to the Forbidden Land of God Refinement.
It was said that the Forbidden Land of God Refinement wasn't in the universe, nor was it in the Realm of the Gods. It was in an extremely special region beneath the Realm of the Gods!
Even True Gods could only construct their own internal worlds.
To be able to construct a real world outside of the Divine Realm, only a Saint could do so.
It was said that the Saint who constructed the Forbidden Land of God Refinement was an extremely mysterious existence in the Realm of the Gods. No one knew why he constructed the Forbidden Land of God Refinement.
He didn't construct a world as large as the universe to stabilize himself. The Forbidden Land of God Refinement he constructed was a place for cultivation.
The Forbidden Land of God Refinement didn't only accept the Saint's internal worlds, which were the living beings of the universe, but also the newborn living beings of the Realm of the Gods!
True Gods also had descendants, and their descendants were called newborn living beings!
In a sense, the Realm of the Gods was the true primary world. Living beings like Luo Zheng, who were born from the Saint's internal worlds, belonged to the first level of living beings …
Most newborn living beings would enter the Forbidden Land of God Refinement to cultivate after they were born. This was because the Forbidden Land of God Refinement was the God Conferment Land.
Of course, although newborn living beings were almost all descendants of True Gods, it wasn't easy to become a True God. In fact, most newborn living beings would be eliminated. This cruel rule applied to the universe, and it also applied to the Realm of the Gods.
"The quota to enter the Forbidden Land of God Refinement is extremely precious to the warriors of the universe. I hope you can seize this opportunity …" Old Man Feng warned Luo Zheng and the others after he finished explaining the things they should pay attention to in the Forbidden Land of God Refinement.
"If we enter the Forbidden Land of Divine Refinement, won't we have to pass through the Wall of Sighs and leave the universe?" Ji Luoxue asked.
In everyone's eyes, the Wall of Sighs was the last layer of protection in this universe. If the Wall of Sighs could be penetrated so easily, then wouldn't it be very easy for the invaders from the other universe to enter?
Old Man Feng immediately understood Ji Luoxue's question. He simply said, "The Wall of Sighs isn't what you think it is. It's not a real wall. It's just a filtering method. The stronger someone is, the harder it is to pass through the Wall of Sighs."
Seeing Ji Luoxue's confused expression, old man Feng once again added, "Moreover, the quota for the Forbidden Land of Divine Refinement will directly send you to the Forbidden Land of Divine Refinement! All of you only need to cultivate carefully in the Forbidden Land of God Refinement. That place is not the Illusory Battlefield. After you die, you can't even reincarnate … "
There was no true reincarnation in this world. In other words, the reincarnation of the living beings in the universe was a gradual death, turning into bones, soil, a part of the universe, and a part of the world in the Saint's body.
However, even though the Demi-Immortal Clan had the quota to enter the Forbidden Land of Divine Refinement, they still needed some time to prepare.
Luo Zheng and the others were arranged to wait in the core sacred ground of the Demi-Immortal Clan. While waiting, they could also use the secret realms of the Demi-Immortal Clan to cultivate.
Naturally, the core disciples of the Demi-Immortal Clan gathered in the core sacred ground. These core disciples were the most outstanding existences in many other clans. After all, being recognized by the Demi-Immortal Clan meant that they were extraordinary.
Of course, they couldn't be compared to the ten Dao Children.
Ji Luoxue and the others were in high spirits. They were happy to break the records in the secret realms.
With their formidable talent and strength, in less than ten days, these fellows had consecutively broken all kinds of records in the core Holy Land. Moreover, they had left the previous records far behind, causing the other core disciples to be extremely depressed.
Luo Zheng didn't participate. It wasn't that he looked down on the secret realms built by the Demi-Immortal Clan. After all, many of the methods of the Demi-Immortal Clan came from the Domain of the Gods. Even though the resources in the universe couldn't be compared to those in the Domain of the Gods, it still had its own uniqueness.
However, at this stage, Luo Zheng was more concerned about the will of the Vast Thousand Worlds.
Luo Zheng now knew the ambition of the Ancient Sorcerer Clan. They wanted to completely control the entire Heavenly Way.
The will of the Great Wide World was still slowly spreading, following the countless ascension passages and slowly spreading into the universe. From the looks of it, the will would spread into the universe sooner or later. Was this the so-called control of the Way of the Heavens?
In this way, the will of the Vast Thousand Worlds should not only be used to transfer power. There should be a more powerful way to use it.
The Ancient Sorcerer Clan might have that way, but Luo Zheng could only explore it himself.
At the same time, Luo Zheng had another question in his mind.
For a long time, the mysterious girl, Jiang Yu 'er, was rumored to be the controller of the Heavenly Way. After all, she was very clear about the 128 Heavenly Wills.
However, when the will of the Vast Thousand Worlds spread, Jiang Yu 'er turned a blind eye to it. What role did Jiang Yu 'er play in the battle between the two universes?
At this stage, Luo Zheng couldn't figure it out. He also suppressed the idea of using the Roaring Token to communicate.
No matter what the situation was, Luo Zheng didn't want to expose the fact that he had merged with the will of the Vast Thousand Worlds for the time being.
During this time, Luo Zheng calmed down and began to cultivate.
On this day, Luo Zheng saw Xuanyuan Chenfeng and the others coming together, talking and laughing.
This group of guys was still too young. In fact, with their talent, cultivating in the Secret Territory of the Demi-Immortal Clan would not improve themselves much.
What they enjoyed was creating all kinds of records.
After each record was broken by them, the martial artists in the core sacred ground would howl in grief. These terrifying records might not be broken by anyone in this era.
"Luo Zheng, do you want to try?" Lie Qianhan asked, staring at Luo Zheng with the corners of his mouth raised.
Luo Zheng was sitting on a high pavilion, facing a green mountain scenery. When he heard this, he turned his head and asked curiously, "Try what?"
"Hehe, today we went to the Illusionary Tower! It is known as the most accurate talent test in the universe, "Lie Qianhan said with a smile.
"Oh, no need," Luo Zheng replied faintly.
The Illusionary Tower and the Exquisite Tower were side by side. Whether it was the structure or the function, they were almost identical.
Luo Zheng had already entered the Exquisite Tower, so he naturally knew that Lie Qianhan was referring to the Talent Stele in the tower …
At this time, Hua Tianming and the others also came over. They heard Luo Xi Xuan say to Hua Tianming, "Your Luosha title is ranked third, and it is gold. This is already very amazing! In the countless years of the Demi-Immortal Clan, there are only a few hundred people who have a gold title. Among them, there are only five who have a gold "Demi-Immortal," seven who have a gold "Asura," and nine who have a gold "Luosha." Chen Feng is really powerful, he actually got the gold Asura title! "
"I'm also very powerful, I'm a gold Luosha!" Xi Youqin said with a smile, her face faintly showing her excitement.
Among the ten Dao Children, Xuanyuan Chenfeng got the gold Asura title. In addition, Xi Youqin and the others were all gold Luosha titles. As for Luo Xi Xuan and Jiang Zhengyi, they both got their titles in the Illusionary Tower many years ago.
These days, Xi Youqin also followed them, trying to cultivate in the Secret Territory. She didn't stick to Luo Zheng all the time.

Hearing her words, Xuanyuan Chenfeng, Hua Tianming, and even Ku Deng all looked at Luo Zheng.

Chapter 1459

Under this inner vision, he noticed that the Chaos Sea was actually filled with living creatures. There were even many huge fish that slowly swam in the Chaos Sea.
According to the rules of cultivation, anyone who left their Inner World would immediately dissipate into a mass of True Essence. Even True Gods were no exception.
The only ones who could surpass this rule were the Sages.
Even the Sages couldn't bring any living beings out of their Inner World. Leaving the Inner World of the Sages was crossing the Heavenly Dao and leaving the universe. But at this moment, in Luo Zheng's hand, there really was a small, lively fish!

Chapter 1460
Luo Zheng stared blankly at the small fish in his hand.
Life forms formed from true essence would return to true essence once they were removed from the external world. According to this rule, this small fish should have turned into chaos energy after being removed from the external world by Luo Zheng …

It wasn't a product of the Heavenly Dao …
