This Maha's plan was far too perfect. The saints were originally far more powerful than the other races in the universe. If this race passed through the Wall of Sighs and established their own sect here, then with a solid backing, the final result could be imagined. They would swallow up the universe without a single drop of blood!
"I personally think that this plan is quite good." Maha continued to smile.
Empyrean Oracle's expression was cautious. He looked at the Old Patriarch.
Old Man Feng's expression was also solemn. These Empyreans were using true essence to communicate with each other.
In truth, the moment that Maha proposed this plan, they already understood the saints' plans.
It was absolutely impossible for the Sacred Race to give up the opportunity to invade the universe. However, at this moment, they didn't want an all-out war to break out.
If they agreed to Maha's request, there would be a benefit. They could buy time for Luo Zheng and the others!
Among the ten Daoists, as long as two or three of them became True Gods, they might have the possibility of changing the fate of the universe.
Although the Saint Clan was powerful, in the end, they came from another universe and not the Divine Realm.
Many Empyreans discussed among themselves for a long time, and many arguments arose.
At this moment, Maha was showing good patience. His hands were clasped behind his back as his gaze swept past the Empyreans and landed on Luo Zheng and the others, sizing them up …
After the saints invaded the universe, they massacred the Tang Chen clansmen on a massive scale. After constantly searching their souls, they obtained a considerable amount of information. The identities of the 10 Dao Children were publicly known throughout the universe, so Maha had a considerable understanding of these youths.
Maha knew that these little fellows were the most important hope of the universe.
But, Maha didn't take the risk to attack them and eliminate them …
Although Maha didn't have too many concerns about his own safety, if he were to attack now and these Empyreans truly attacked, his situation wouldn't be much better.
His life was far more precious than the lives of these Dao Children!
"Agree to their plan. This plan is beneficial to us!"
"As long as we can stabilize them first, my family's Morning Wind will eventually become a True Divinity …"
"What we lack is time!"
Some Empyreans were moved by Maha's request, including Empyrean Original Sin. He had poured too much effort into Xuanyuan Chenfeng, so he had considerable confidence in him!
But, some other Empyreans were opposed.
"Humph, this sort of compromise is even more useless!"
"We should immediately counterattack and seal them off at the edge of the great worlds, eliminating any possibility of them approaching the universe!"
"Placing your hopes on the charity of others is extremely stupid!"
These Empyreans were divided into two factions, both of them arguing endlessly!
But the Old Patriarch didn't say a single word from beginning to end. These Empyreans were unable to convince each other. Finally, they all looked towards the Old Patriarch.
In truth, in this place, Empyrean Oracle, Empyrean Original Sin, and Empyrean Thunder Punishment were all characters on par with the Old Patriarch. As upper Empyreans, the destiny of heaven they carried was far more formidable than an ordinary Empyrean's. But in terms of prestige, the Old Patriarch was more suitable. After all, he was the descendant of a disciple.
Seeing everyone looking at him, the Old Patriarch said with a true essence sound transmission, "I think … have you all missed something?"
Many Empyreans glanced at each other in dismay. Most of them didn't understand.
But, Empyrean Oracle blinked his beautiful eyes and said, "They aren't willing to attack."
Old Man Feng nodded. "Yes, why aren't they willing to attack?"
If the saints truly possessed absolute strength, then Maha wouldn't have brought these Empyreans here to negotiate.
The saints could gather True Divinities and push through great worlds one by one!
To negotiate meant that they lacked confidence.
"You mean that the saints don't dare to start a war?" Empyrean Original Sin asked.
"I am willing to take a gamble." The Old Patriarch nodded.
In this world, there was no absolute certainty. But since the other side was willing to negotiate, it meant that they didn't want to launch an all-out war in the near future. From this point alone, one could see some clues.
Many Empyreans also had complex complexions.
If they really agreed to Maha's request, the saints might not comply. Once the saints were ready, an all-out war would inevitably erupt.
The strong never needed to reason with the weak. At that time, it would be too late to regret.
But if the negotiations failed and the saints attacked now, how would they deal with this?
As the Old Patriarch said, he was willing to take a gamble!
He was willing to gamble that the saints wouldn't attack. He was willing to gamble that Luo Zheng and the others would return from the Forbidden Land of God Refinement. He was willing to gamble that their cultivations would rapidly rise in a short period of time, becoming a World Master, becoming a True Divinity …
As many Empyreans carefully analyzed the situation, they gradually realized that the Old Patriarch's thoughts were correct.
Rather than appeasing the saints for now, the final result would still be that they wouldn't be able to escape annihilation. Then, they might as well take a gamble and firmly seal these fellows at the edge of a great world, not allowing them to take even a single step forwards!
The many Empyreans quickly came to a consensus.
The Old Patriarch's eyes fell on Maha once more. Maha also knew that the Empyreans had reached a conclusion. He faintly smiled and said, "What do you think of my proposal?" In Maha's opinion, whether it was the human race or the demon race, in front of the saints, they were all inferior races.
These races didn't have a noble purpose in life. In order to survive, they would give in without a bottom line!
Old Man Feng lightly replied, "Although there are many races in the universe, there is no great world that is superfluous. I represent all the influences in the universe in rejecting your proposal."
Hearing Old Man Feng's words, Maha's frivolous smile finally vanished. He cursed the old fox in his heart, but he continued to threaten, "You will only have one chance!"
If Maha hadn't threatened then things would have been fine.
As he spoke, Old Man Feng and the other Empyreans laughed.
When one cultivated to the level of an Empyrean, their mental state was already comparable to fine iron. Once they decided on something, how could they easily change their minds?
Maha's threat exposed the lack of confidence of the saints even more!
It wasn't just the Empyreans, but even Luo Zheng and the others could see that the saints were bluffing!
"Haha, we don't need the saints to give us a chance! If you have the guts, then fight! "Lie Qianhan coldly sneered.
Among the ten Dao Children, Lie Qianhan's personality was the most unreasonable. It had to be said that his personality was somewhat similar to Maha's. Hearing Maha's rambling, he couldn't help but open his mouth to mock him.
The failure of this negotiation naturally made Maha extremely angry. When he heard Lie Qianhan's words, he flew into a rage out of humiliation. His purple pupils suddenly lit up with a flash of lightning!
"A mere pinnacle divine realm junior dares to carelessly interrupt? I will teach you a lesson on behalf of your elders!" As his voice fell, the lightning in Maha's eyes formed an arc in the air and shot towards Lie Qianhan with incomparable speed.
＝＝＝＝＝

Chapter 1463
That bolt of lightning was extremely strange. It didn't communicate with heaven and earth origin energy, nor did it use true essence.
It was as if it was formed from Maha's consciousness, and the laws of this lightning were slightly different from the laws of this universe!
Maha said that he would teach them a lesson, but the power of this bolt of lightning was not ordinary!
The many Empyreans in the universe were already very restrained this time.
The other party was from the Sacred Race, and Maha's status was extraordinary. It seemed that even Empyreans had to listen to his orders.
But Maha had gone too far by attacking the Daoists!
"You're courting death! You dare attack! "
The Lie Family Patriarch was the first to step forward. A powerful aura soared into the sky, and the Rising Heaven Holy Land immediately surged.
But that arc of lightning had already struck down, and it was too late to stop it!
"Boom!"
The arc of lightning instantly sank into Lie Qianhan's body, and then a squeal like a pig being slaughtered came from his body!
From the outside, this lightning didn't seem very powerful, and it was indeed in the category of "teaching a lesson". But Lie Qianhan was lying on the ground like a writhing water snake, crazily rolling around!
There weren't many methods that could cause Lie Qianhan such pain, so this lightning must be very torturous.
On one hand, the Lie Family Patriarch was worried about Lie Qianhan's injuries, and on the other hand, he was about to attack Maha!
A cold light flashed in the Lie Family Patriarch's hand, and a dragon head saber appeared. He raised the saber without any hesitation and slashed it out!
As for the nine Empyreans beside Maha, they all began to shift positions. Light green rings began to flash around their bodies. These rings quickly connected with each other, and the nine Empyreans formed an array formation!
Empyreans arranged an array!
Because Empyrean powerhouses carried different Wills of Heaven, it was very difficult for Empyreans to cultivate arrays with each other.
Of course, this scene only existed in the universe where Luo Zheng was.
In the universe occupied by the Sacred Race, almost all the great worlds were occupied by the Sacred Race. Like the Human Race, the Demon Race, and the Demon Race, these small races were all concentrated in two or three great worlds. They were like pigs raised in a pen by the Sacred Race.
Thus, the Sacred Race was a highly unified race.
In this universe, there were many Empyreans who had inherited the Mandate of Heaven, but they had no choice when it came to the Mandate of Heaven crown!
This was because the Heaven's Will crown would randomly appear after an Empyrean fell. It would belong to whoever managed to grab it. Whether the Heaven's Will was low, middle, or high, no one had a choice. Anyone with a Heaven's Will was an Empyrean. As for whether the Heaven's Will was good or not, who cared about that?
Only the Demi-Immortals, who relied on Lin Su's resurrection of the Will of Heaven, were able to obtain the Crown of Heaven's Will. Because of that, the other races had agreed not to fight over it. That was how they were able to select a Greater Will of Heaven!
But within the saints, after an Empyrean died, their race would quietly wait for the next Heaven's Will crown.
After the Heaven's Will crown appeared in the universe, all the races would rush forward to shoulder the Heaven's Will first.
The saints would quietly distinguish the Heaven's Will crown and analyze what sort of Heaven's Will of Heaven was contained within. If the Heaven's Will of Heaven wasn't good, then they would quietly wait for the Heaven's Will to slowly disappear. Before long, the Heaven's Will crown would disappear and then reappear somewhere else. Then, the Heaven's Will of Heaven would undergo a new change.
The saints used this method to replace the Heaven's Will of Heaven that wasn't too useful or wasn't strong enough. Only when they were able to sift out a powerful Heaven's Will would they appoint a saint World Lord to shoulder the Heaven's Will.
Thus, the saints' advantage didn't only lie in their True Divinities, but even the quality of their Empyreans was much higher than in the universe.
Because these Empyreans were familiar with each other, they were able to use their different abilities to lay down a powerful array formation!
The moment the Lie Family Old Ancestor chopped down with his saber, the other Empyreans wouldn't just sit back and watch!
Beneath the momentum of dozens of Empyreans, their momentum seemed to solidify. Even the air seemed to solidify!
A smile appeared on Maha's face. Facing the Lie Family Old Ancestor's saber strike, he didn't panic at all!
But in the middle of the nine saint Empyreans, space began to violently fluctuate. Just as the saber came slashing down, a giant hand suddenly appeared in that space!
As soon as the giant hand reached out, it forcefully grasped at the void and shattered the Lie Family Old Ancestor's saber light!
Kacha!
It wasn't just the saber light that shattered!
Even the dragon head saber in the Lie Family Old Ancestor's hand began to strangely crack. As the saber continued to twist, the dragon head saber turned into an iron rod!
The giant hand clearly hadn't come into contact with the dragon head saber, but it had produced such a strange effect. It was unknown just what degree of strength the giant hand had used!
The Lie Family Old Ancestor's complexion was extremely ugly. Even Empyrean Original Sin, Empyrean Oracle, and the others had incomparably gloomy expressions.
This dragon head saber was called Sea Burial. It was a treasure ranked twelfth on the Universe Myriad Spirit Stele. The material itself was refined from a dark star. This dragon head saber wasn't something that an ordinary Empyrean could use. Only the Lie Family Old Ancestor, who possessed boundless strength, could freely use it.
But such a saber hadn't even come into contact with the giant hand yet it had been crushed into an iron rod, a piece of scrap metal!
Just how strong was the owner of that giant hand?
"Humph!"
After the giant hand withdrew, a cold snort sounded out once more!
As this cold snort spread out, it caused the hearts of all the Empyreans to tremble! As for the Dao Children, their faces were as white as paper. They involuntarily retreated.
Only Luo Zheng stood in place, not moving at all.
Although a True Divinity hadn't descended, just a single hand was enough to give so many Empyreans such great pressure!
True Divinities were also divided into ranks.
The lowest level True Divinities, such as Empyrean Original Sin and Old Man Feng, had the strength to fight. After all, they were upper Empyreans, equivalent to half-step True Divinities.
But the owner of this hand didn't seem to be as simple as an ordinary True Divinity.
At this moment, Old Man Feng and the others were facing this giant hand, maintaining their restraint and silence. In fact, if several Empyreans joined forces, they might not necessarily be afraid of this giant hand. But now was not the time to fight to the death, so they could only endure for now.
Moreover, Lie Qianhan had already crawled up from the ground. Although his cries had been extremely tragic, he hadn't suffered any serious injuries.
"So this is a True Divinity …" Luo Zheng stared at the giant hand, his eyes narrowing slightly.
As he sized it up, Luo Zheng felt an extremely unfriendly gaze staring at him. It came from Maha.
Their gazes met, and their hearts skipped a beat.
Maha was originally disdainful of these Dao Children, but now that he noticed Luo Zheng, he instantly understood. This was the kid who had won first place in the Illusion War. Although his cultivation was only at the Extreme Divine Realm, this fellow's potential was extraordinarily strong.
Luo Zheng's gaze was calm and unperturbed. As he looked at Maha, there wasn't the slightest bit of anger or wariness in his eyes. It was as if this was only natural.
The two of them looked at each other for less than a breath of time. Luo Zheng's face revealed a smile, a smile that faintly contained a trace of contempt.
"What are you laughing at?" Seeing that trace of contempt, Maha was even more displeased. In any case, they had already shed all pretense of cordiality. Maha guessed that these Empyreans wouldn't dare to make a move here, so he directly asked Luo Zheng.
This Maha had come prepared today. If negotiations failed, he would rely on the intimidation of a True Divinity to show off his might!
"I'm laughing at you. You're just a mere Realm Master. You can't wait to use the might of a True Divinity behind you to intimidate others. You still seem to be so proud of yourself?" Luo Zheng calmly asked.
Maha's expression immediately darkened. He had long had the opportunity to shoulder the Mandate of Heaven. In the past, there was even an extremely strong Mandate of Heaven that could have helped him become an upper Empyrean. But he had chosen to give it up. After shouldering the Mandate of Heaven, he was bound by the Heavenly Dao and was unable to cross the Heavenly Dao to become a True Divinity.

Chapter 1464
As a member of the saint race, Maha understood the Divine Realm far better than the people of this universe.
Just like the higher realms and the lower realms.
To the Divine Realm, the universe was a lower plane!
The rules that were cultivated in the universe were nothing more than rules established by the Saints.
In the eyes of the people of the Divine Realm, the life forms of the universe were nothing more than ordinary life forms!
Even a Dao Child would have to painstakingly work for tens of millions of years, shed an endless amount of sweat and blood, and consume a massive amount of resources in order to have the qualifications to stand on equal footing with the geniuses of the Divine Realm!
This was the inherent gap between them.
Maha was the son of a True Divinity. With his status, he could laugh proudly in the saint race.
But as long as one was a life form, how could they be satisfied with the status quo?
Maha also wanted to go to the Divine Realm and become a True Divinity. He had once gone to the Forbidden Land of Divine Refinement and gained many harvests there. But in the end, he wasn't able to become a True Divinity!
But condensing a godhood … it really wasn't an easy matter.
Luo Zheng's words poked at Maha's sore spot. A dangerous light gradually appeared in his bewitching eyes.
The saint race's True Divinity, his father, was unable to personally come to this universe for certain reasons.
This was the reason why the saint race chose to negotiate.
However, this group of old bastards weren't so easily deceived. They refused his request.
If so, why not borrow the remaining might of a True Divinity to kill this person?
The moment this thought appeared in his mind, he was stunned. It seemed that he subconsciously dreaded Luo Zheng!
"Although I am only a World Lord, if I want to exterminate you, it will be a piece of cake …"
Maha's thoughts were decisive. Once he made a decision, he would carry it out. He had always believed in his own intuition and judgment. Since Luo Zheng gave him a feeling of threat, he absolutely wouldn't allow him to grow!
The nine Empyreans also seemed to understand His Highness' temperament. Although Maha's temperament seemed exaggerated and cruel, he always kept his word!
Just like Maha had said, once he became a True Divinity, he would kill Empyrean Cloudfox. In the future, he would definitely accomplish this.
As soon as Maha finished speaking, old man Feng waved his hand gently and an invisible force spread out, forming a ten thousand zhang tall wall.
This Maha might not be worth mentioning, but they had no choice but to let go of that giant hand. In the end, it was the hand of a True Divinity. If they were caught off guard and attacked, they might not be able to withstand it.
At this moment, many Empyreans were conflicted. They didn't want to provoke a powerful True God.
But if he didn't make a move, he couldn't allow Maha to throw his weight around and display his insufferably arrogance.
"I admit that I'm not your match now." Luo Zheng maintained a faint smile on his face. "But when I reach the Sector Lord realm, I'll only need a single finger to kill you!"
After saying this, Luo Zheng also extended a finger towards Maha!
Maha was an overlord of the saint race, and his father was a True Divinity that surpassed the universe. His talent was unprecedented in the saint race, and he had always been the one to say such things to others. What qualifications did others have to say such things to him?
He also didn't expect that this Luo Zheng, who made him feel a trace of danger, would be even more arrogant than him at this moment!
Unknowingly, the confrontation between the two races gradually focused on Luo Zheng and Maha.
The Empyreans gathered in the Demi-Immortal Tribe didn't make a move, and the Empyreans behind Maha, as well as the True Divinity hand, didn't make a move.
In the end, there was still a taboo between the two sides, and they weren't willing to shed all pretense of cordiality at this moment.
But with Maha's personality, he was destined not to care about this balance. In the end, he was someone who had never suffered a loss. Hearing Luo Zheng's words, Maha coldly smiled. A thought flashed through his mind, and the giant hand behind him suddenly moved, ruthlessly slapping towards Luo Zheng.
The old patriarch, Empyrean Oracle, Empyrean Original Sin, and Empyrean Thunder Punishment all moved at almost the same time!
The giant hand rapidly slapped towards Luo Zheng. Even the old patriarch's invisible strength was instantly torn apart, unable to block it in the slightest!
"You really think we're afraid of a True Divinity?"
Empyrean Oracle's expression was cold, and the short sword in her hand turned into a sharp stream of light in the air!
The streak of light split into multiple layers, transforming into a series of long serrated hooks that hooked onto the giant hand.
Empyrean Oracle had already seen the power of this giant hand, so she naturally didn't dare to underestimate it. She just wanted to stop this giant hand!
As for Empyrean Thunder Punishment, he directly transformed into a Dharma Idol Vajra. He took a step forward and stood in front of Luo Zheng, determined to block this giant hand.
Empyrean Original Sin's expression was cold, and he disappeared from where he stood, his whereabouts unknown!
As for the other Empyreans, they weren't mentally prepared. Were they going to start a war with the Sacred Race now?
If the owner of this giant hand directly came out, how could these Empyreans resist?
The Empyreans' worries were completely dispelled in the next instant!
At this moment, an old laughter suddenly rang in everyone's ears. A shadow slowly condensed beside Luo Zheng. It was a thin figure. The moment this person appeared, he stretched out his withered hand and gently tapped the palm of the giant hand with a finger!
The two did not come into contact, but the big hand seemed to have grown eyes. It seemed to have sensed the power of the finger and changed its move in mid-air. The finger on the palm straightened and turned into a palm knife.
The old man's expression didn't change, but the fingers on one of his hands changed. His thumb and little finger pinched together, and then he tapped the wrist of the giant hand.
The hand gestures between the two constantly changed, like two drunkards playing a drinking game. There wasn't a single real exchange!
Every time the giant hand changed its stance, the old man's hand would change accordingly, as if he could always restrain the giant hand.
Finally …
The giant hand finally came to a stop.
A cold voice sounded out from the center of the nine Empyreans. "I didn't think that a dignified disciple of a Saint would be hiding in the universe. You really didn't die."
"I will never die." The old man faintly smiled and responded.
"You will never be too far away." The voice said again.
The old man chuckled. "You should worry about yourself first. Your son's life … we will spare him."
As the old man spoke, he didn't even look at Maha. It was as if Maha was a character that couldn't be brought to the table, not worthy of the old man's attention.
The moment the old man's voice fell, Empyrean Original Sin's figure appeared beside Maha. A black spear had appeared in Empyrean Original Sin's hands and he pressed it against Maha's head.
Maha was the most promising Dao Child of the Sacred Race to break into the True Divinity realm. He was also the son of a True Divinity. If he could be killed, it would be of great benefit to the universe.
The other side was silent for a long time. Then, the voice sounded out once more. "If you dare to kill my son, I will abandon the Sacred Race and kill you. I will flatten this universe. I will do as I say!"
A sly smile appeared on the old man's face. "You really can't leave. I wonder which universe has the courage to invade the Sacred Race's universe? Or … could it be that two Saints from the Realm of the Gods have started a war? "
Old man Feng had already speculated that if the Sacred Race had absolute strength, they wouldn't have sent Maha to negotiate at all. Instead, they would have directly pushed their entire race into the universe. At this moment, this old man had taken this speculation one step further.
Within the Sacred Race's universe, there was someone holding down this True Divinity. He had to guard that universe, thus he couldn't personally come here!
As these words were spoken, the other Empyreans were suddenly enlightened!
The old man didn't really want to kill Maha. At this stage, it wasn't wise to fight to the death. His threat to kill Maha was only a probe!
"You don't have the qualifications to know." The hand gradually drew back, no longer revealing any other information. It tossed Maha and the other nine Empyreans here.
The master of the hand had already said that if anyone dared to kill Maha, he would first flatten the universe. After saying these words, he chose to leave. This showed that he had absolute confidence in his own strength. It also showed that the master of the hand didn't place the old man in his eyes.

Chapter 1465
The appearance of this old man from the Demi-Immortal Clan left many Empyreans pleasantly surprised.
They never thought that the Demi-Immortal Clan would also have a True Divinity. This would be a great help to them.
But seeing the saint True Divinity calmly leave and leave his son here, it proved that he didn't place the True Divinity of the Demi-Immortal Clan in his eyes.
As for Empyrean Original Sin, when he heard Maha's words, he smiled and said, "Your father just didn't want to kill you. Get out of my way."
With that, Empyrean Original Sin flicked his spear. Maha felt his vision blur as if a star had smashed into his chest and exploded. The massive impact sent him flying away!
As Maha was sent flying away by the spear, the other nine Empyreans didn't say a word. They all rose into the air and followed after their Highness …
"Are we really going to let them go like this?" Empyrean Original Sin asked as he held his spear in his hands.
The old man looked in the direction they left and lightly said, "Let them go. If we kill a saint Dao Child, we will lose all opportunities. It isn't worth it."
After the saint Dao Child left, Old Man Feng introduced this person to the Empyreans present.
It turned out that this old man was the third disciple of the Saint. His name was Startail.
Because of the conflict between the Saints, the Saint's disciples had all died. Startail had escaped into the Saint's inner world. It was only in this universe that he managed to escape this calamity. But, he had fallen into a long slumber.
Many years ago, Startail had only awakened once. At the same time, he had activated the bloodline of the Demi-Immortal Clan.
The Demi-Immortal Clan was arranged by the disciples. Once it was activated at a specific time, the Demi-Immortal Clan would begin to operate according to the predetermined path …
The Old Patriarch was one of the links, the True Dragon was another, Gods Without Thought was another, and Luo Zheng was the most important link.
But from the very beginning, the Saint's disciples had planned to use Luo Zheng as a hidden chess piece. They hoped that he would grow in the dark while Xuanyuan Chenfeng would be a chess piece in the light. The other Dao Children would support Xuanyuan Chenfeng.
But in terms of operation, there were still many mistakes. No matter how skilled the disciples were, it was impossible for them to not make mistakes.
As a result, Luo Zheng obtained first place, and in the end, Luo Xi Xuan could only become this hidden chess piece.
However, the plan was progressing so quickly that Sir Xing Wei and the Old Patriarch couldn't think too much about it. They had to send Luo Zheng and the others into the Forbidden Land of God Refinement first.
Otherwise, no matter how many arrangements he made, it would still be in vain.
The chaos caused by the saints caused the many Empyreans to feel an even greater sense of crisis. In particular, the four Empyreans – Gods Without Thought – were the leaders. The Xuanyuan, Lie, and Ji Families were all disciples and descendants. If the saints were to occupy the universe, they might be able to leave behind other influences, but they would absolutely exterminate the four great families.
Several days later, the Daoists left the universe and were transported to the Forbidden Land of God Refinement.
According to the old patriarch, the Forbidden Land of God Refinement was an extremely complicated place.
First of all, this world was vast and boundless. It did not belong to the universe, but was also the inner world of a Saint.
However, in this inner world, there were martial artists from the Divine Realm as well as the inner worlds of the various Saints. There were all sorts of different races …
In the Forbidden Land of God Refinement, it was also an excellent place for adventuring.
The ten Daoists were transmitted at the same time. Moreover, this transmission wasn't a transmission channel in the ordinary sense!
A transmission channel only used the Space Laws to draw extremely far away space closer, folding it in half. In truth, the distance still existed …
But in this transmission, Luo Zheng arrived in a strange world in the blink of an eye. He knew that this was no longer the universe.
"Cave, cave?"
Luo Zheng looked up at the skies. He discovered that he seemed to be in a massive cave!
The reason he said 'massive' was because Luo Zheng was unable to describe just how massive this cave was. The dome of this cave seemed to extend into an infinite distance. From the ground to the top of the cave, the height was probably equal to the height between a great world and a planet!
This massive cave could no longer be called a cave, but a world of its own.
There seemed to be countless tiny motes of light growing at the top of the cave. These motes of light were constantly alternating between dimness and brilliance. Thus, from time to time, waves of light would rise up from the top of the cave, dazzling the eyes.
It was also these motes of light that brought light to this massive cave. These motes of light were the sun of this cave!
Because the distance was too great, Luo Zheng couldn't clearly see what these motes of light were. However, Luo Zheng speculated that they should be some sort of life form that was attached to the top of the cave. Moreover, if they could emit such a radiance from such a great distance, this life form wouldn't be small. Perhaps each mote of light was as large as a planet.
These motes of light weren't the destination of Luo Zheng's exploration this time.
Entering the Forbidden Land of God Refinement this time was to increase his cultivation at the fastest speed possible!
That Maha was also a Daoist and was only a step away from being conferred the title of True Divinity. If he could become a True Divinity, then he would have the qualifications to save the universe …
As for who was right and who was wrong, it no longer mattered!
The location Luo Zheng was transmitted to was extremely desolate. It seemed to be a flat desert. However, below this desert, there were countless divine runes drawn.
What made Luo Zheng surprised was that the attainments of these divine runes were so high that he had never heard of them before!
In Luo Zheng's opinion, the divine runes that Qinglong had imparted to him were already very impressive. However, with a casual glance, he discovered that the divine runes that Qinglong had recorded weren't even 1% of the divine runes here!
"The Dao of Divine Runes in the Forbidden Land of God Refinement is abnormally developed," Luo Zheng came to this conclusion.
However, he didn't dwell on this. Instead, he quickly left along the desert.
Before entering the Forbidden Land of God Refinement, the old patriarch had told him in detail about the things to pay attention to in the Forbidden Land of God Refinement.
There were countless transmission points in the Forbidden Land of God Refinement. They could appear at any place. However, no matter where they appeared, the first thing to do was to hide themselves.
The Forbidden Land of God Refinement was a rather cruel world. At the same time, there was no way to be reborn after death. Therefore, if one wanted to accept the trial, the first step was to survive …
Fortunately, the desert was extremely desolate. Luo Zheng spent an hour to completely pass through it. What appeared in front of Luo Zheng's eyes was a gray forest. When Luo Zheng carefully sized it up, he discovered that this forest was actually made of stone.
Luo Zheng approached one of the trees and lightly leaped. When he landed on the ground, he had already broken a branch in his hand.
No matter how carefully he looked, from the bark to the trunk, and then to the leaves that were covered in veined patterns, they were all stone!
In fact, it wasn't rare for trees to turn into stone. For example, fossils were often formed by various creatures immersed in the long river of time. This forest might have encountered some accident and turned into stone.
However, Luo Zheng could feel the life force slowly flowing from this tree trunk. He could be sure that this stone forest was still growing. They were born stone.
"In the Forbidden Land of God Refinement, anything that goes against common sense can happen. Don't be too surprised. In fact, the inner world of every Saint is different …"
This was one of Old Man Feng's warnings.
After Luo Zheng carefully looked at it, he placed the branch where it was and quietly sneaked into the stone forest.
However, Luo Zheng didn't know that the moment he entered the forest, a huge monster somewhere in the forest had its eyes on him.
＝＝＝＝＝

Chapter 1466
In this stone forest, there was almost no movement.
Luo Zheng was also very vigilant when he entered.
This wasn't the universe, so Luo Zheng couldn't use his power to move. More importantly, there were natives of the Forbidden Land of God as well as martial artists from the Realm of the Gods here!
In this stone forest, whether it was the tall trees or the short weeds, they were all in the form of stones.
Luo Zheng would occasionally step on them, and those thin weeds would break...
Luo Zheng walked forward for a distance of about four to five hundred miles, spending about four hours. If he flew in the air, this distance would only take Luo Zheng a few breaths. But in an unfamiliar place, rashly exposing himself to the sky was an extremely dangerous thing to do.
As the sky gradually darkened, Luo Zheng indeed saw that the starlight above the dome that illuminated the cave was gradually dimming. Clearly, there was a difference between day and night in the Forbidden Land of God.
But this night came too quickly. In just a few breaths of time, the light turned into darkness. There was no change at all.
This darkness was unable to suppress Luo Zheng's perception range. His eyes flashed with a green light, and the blurry world of darkness around him gradually became clear...
"Continue forward."
At this moment, Luo Zheng wanted to meet a martial artist the most. He wanted to first understand the basic situation of this world.
But the instant darkness descended, Luo Zheng immediately felt the earth begin to tremble...
"Thump, thump, thump..."
Huge footsteps stepped on the ground, forming one pit after another.
Not far in front of Luo Zheng, there was a "mountain" slowly moving. This huge monster had a body that was several thousand feet tall, but its head was surprisingly small.
This huge monster walked for a distance, then stopped. It stretched out its small head towards the stone forest, then opened its mouth and began to reject the tree trunks and leaves in this stone forest!
"Even stones are eaten." Luo Zheng was only fifty to sixty feet away from this huge monster. This scene made Luo Zheng feel very strange.
But when he thought about it, these stones contained life force, and in his eyes, they were exactly the same as stones, hard and brittle. But in the eyes of the living beings in the Forbidden Land of God Refinement, they were their natural food!
In the process of evolution, this kind of creature forgot to evolve its intelligence. It only had a huge body, but it did not have any predatory nature and was very gentle.
However, in the natural world, gentle creatures were destined to become food for others!
Suddenly, a hoarse cry came from the darkness, and the whole stone forest became lively. At the same time, some strange words were transmitted. Luo Zheng couldn't understand those words, but Luo Zheng was sure that the creatures in the darkness were intelligent!
Although this mountain-like beast was simple-minded, it was not stupid. After hearing the noise, its huge body began to turn around.
It took a big step with its head. If it ran at full speed, its speed would naturally not be slow. But at this moment, it was slowly adjusting its direction. The shadows in the stone forest were getting closer and closer, and at the same time, they jumped onto the mountain-like beast!
Luo Zheng completely restrained his aura in the darkness, and he could clearly see the origin of these creatures. They seemed to be monkeys less than half a meter tall.
However, these monkeys were slightly different from the monkeys Luo Zheng had seen. At least, they didn't have the so-called "pointy mouth and monkey cheeks."
Many of these creatures jumped around Luo Zheng, and they divided the work clearly. The stronger ones tried to drive away the huge beast, and the faster ones directly climbed onto the huge body, and began to advance towards the neck of the huge creature...
Not long after, Luo Zheng heard the huge monster howl, and then it fell to the side, like a mountain collapsing. The moment it fell, the earth trembled.
Such a huge body, to those short and small creatures, was like a huge 'mountain of flesh'. These creatures dug out the thick flesh and began to cut off the oil and other things under the skin, appearing to be in an orderly manner.
Luo Zheng hid his figure, and after looking around, he prepared to leave.
These creatures were the natives of the Forbidden Land of God Refinement, but in a situation where there was a language barrier, Luo Zheng's rash appearance would only be met with a counterattack. Luo Zheng's goal was to find humanoid creatures, whether it was the Demon Night Clan, the Demon Race, or even the Giant Race...
Overall, this Forbidden Land of God Refinement was not as dangerous as he imagined.
Luo Zheng's figure quietly flashed, and he disappeared into the stone forest. He planned to continue his exploration.
However, the moment Luo Zheng hid in the stone forest, he suddenly heard a soft sound. Although the sound was soft, it seemed to be extremely fast.
It was also this small sound that attracted Luo Zheng's attention.
As his figure floated again, he moved through the jungle very agilely. After choosing a tree trunk, he suddenly stopped on this tree trunk.
"Swoosh swoosh …"
Two extremely soft sounds rang out again!
Luo Zheng raised his eyebrows, and with a light shake of his long sword, a sword light slashed out in an arc!
With Luo Zheng as the center, all the stone forest within thirty miles of him was cut into pieces!
"Swoosh swoosh swoosh …"
The thing that followed Luo Zheng no longer had a place to hide, and at this moment, it spread its wings and flew up high!
"Huge bats?" Luo Zheng was stunned.
This thing was completely black, and it had a pair of pitch-black fleshy wings. It moved silently in the night.
The wingspan of these bats was probably seventy to eighty feet!
Apart from the monkey-like creatures Luo Zheng saw before, the creatures hiding in the stone forest seemed to be exceptionally huge.
Could it be the world outside the Immortal Mansion?
In the huge forest outside the Immortal Mansion, there were incomparably huge creatures. However, the world outside the Immortal Mansion, together with the trees, was also unimaginably tall. Luo Zheng quickly rejected this idea.
If Luo Zheng's guess was correct, the world outside the Immortal Mansion should be somewhere in the God Domain, and this place was not the God Domain.
"Whoosh whoosh whoosh..."
Those bats streaked across the sky, and with a sudden flicker, they disappeared from Luo Zheng's field of vision. They circled around Luo Zheng at an extremely fast speed...
Although their speed was fast, they couldn't escape Luo Zheng's surveillance range. He held his long sword in his hand, and stood in place. If these giant bats dared to attack him, he would immediately kill them.
However, these giant bats seemed to have high intelligence. They also feared the long sword in Luo Zheng's hand, and weren't willing to swarm him like this.
After confronting them like this for a long time, Luo Zheng couldn't be bothered to deal with these bats. Instead, he increased his speed in the darkness, constantly moving forward.
As he moved forward, those giant bats did the same. They followed closely behind Luo Zheng, neither approaching nor retreating. From beginning to end, they maintained a safe distance from Luo Zheng!
"I don't know what these bats... are after." Luo Zheng was also puzzled.
After Luo Zheng moved forward another three to four hundred li, the stone forest finally came to an end. At this moment, the bats stopped moving forward. They still wanted to follow Luo Zheng, but they seemed to be afraid of something in front of them. They kept circling above Luo Zheng's head.
"What kind of powerful existence is in front of us?"
Vicious beasts were only defined in the universe. Luo Zheng also knew that the creatures in the Forbidden Land of God Refinement couldn't simply be classified as vicious beasts.
These creatures also had a strong sense of territory. If they didn't move forward, then there must be some other creature's territory in front of them.
Luo Zheng didn't hesitate and continued forward. Overall, the creatures he encountered in the Forbidden Land of God Refinement weren't too powerful. At the very least, there wasn't anything that could threaten Luo Zheng's life right now.
But just as this thought appeared in his mind, Luo Zheng immediately rejected it.
After passing through the stone forest, the surrounding forest seemed normal. Although it was filled with unknown trees and flowers, it still had a lush and verdant appearance. Compared to the stone forest, it was much better.
Luo Zheng had just stepped into the forest when he heard the sound of horse hooves.
Luo Zheng saw a white pony wildly running through the forest. This pony was extremely handsome and had a sharp horn on its head.
"A unicorn?"
Luo Zheng's eyes widened, his expression no longer as relaxed as before. This was because this pony was carrying a woman whose cultivation was at the peak of the Divine Transformation realm.
Besides this woman, the martial artists following behind her also had high cultivations. There were even World Lords amongst them.

Chapter 1467
The unicorn had a pure white mane that was incomparably smooth. If one looked carefully, one could see that the hooves of the horse were inlaid with perfect god runes.
Every time the unicorn took a step, these god runes would create an extremely small force field. Under these force fields, the unicorn would run wildly in the air …
"Eldest Princess, we've already patrolled to the edge of the grey forest. This time, the darkness will last for sixty-two hours. I'm afraid there will be danger if we're late!" A black-clothed man in a long robe among the horses advised.
In the eyes of an ordinary person, the darkness had already descended. Suddenly, no light could be seen.
However, in the eyes of Luo Zheng and the warriors in front of him, this darkness wasn't a hindrance at all.
Unless the darkness law enveloped them, they would be able to see clearly even in the darkness!
Because the opponent was a Sector Lord, Luo Zheng was now even more careful as he curled up in a corner. Hearing this conversation, Luo Zheng obtained a few more pieces of information.
Eldest Princess …
Did these people belong to the Forbidden Land of God Refinement or were they from the Realm of Gods? This woman's status was clearly very high.
"As long as sixty-two hours?" Luo Zheng quietly pondered. In other words, if the things at the top of the cave wanted to light up again, it would take sixty-two hours. Moreover, during these sixty-two hours, would there be any danger in this area?
"What's the rush? Those yellow birds were chased away last time. Even if they come out this time, there won't be so many of them." The woman indifferently replied. She urged her horse to charge into the grey forest.
"Even so, the place of birth in the Dazzling Gold Desert has been abandoned for thousands of years. The legend that Eldest Princess heard was just a joke. When it comes to fate, it can never be predicted!"
When it comes to fate, it can never be predicted?
Hearing this, Luo Zheng was momentarily stunned.
Ever since he stepped on the path of martial arts, there had been a huge disagreement about fate.
Luo Zheng had once believed that everything that happened to him was caused by the clouds. It was fate that had been decided by the heavens!
However, not long ago, Hua Tianming told him that fate was just a lie …
This person's words once again proved Hua Tianming's point of view!
"He Zuowei! How dare you eavesdrop on me! "The woman's voice turned cold.
"In order to protect Princess Royal, this is something that must be done. If Princess Royal has any dissatisfaction, you can report it to His Highness!" The black-robed warrior calmly said, "The Heavenly Soul race member who sold the flowers has already been captured by us. The story he fabricated was just to lure the Eldest Princess into the Gray Forest. According to what I know, there are at least a few extremely powerful demonic creatures deep underground that are interested in the Eldest Princess! Of course … I'm referring to the Grand Princess's flesh and blood. "
"You!"
The woman was clearly angered by the guard's words, and for a moment, she was unable to refute.
But she still rode the unicorn forward …
"Princess Royal, you're only 200 feet away from the Grey Forest!" He Zuowei's voice suddenly became louder.
The Eldest Princess didn't say anything else. She just slowly drove the unicorn forward, but she was getting closer and closer to the Grey Forest.
"Princess Royal, you are only ten feet away from the Grey Forest …" He Zuowei warned again.
At this moment, a crafty look appeared on the princess's face. She suddenly shook the reins and the unicorn shot towards the gray forest like an arrow leaving the bow!
"You can't!"
He Zuowei's expression changed. His hands suddenly stretched out and transformed into two giant hands. They instantly appeared behind the Princess Royal and grabbed her along with her horse. He Zuowei finally managed to stop the Princess Royal.
However, the unicorn was too fast. Even though He Zuowei stopped the Eldest Princess, the Eldest Princess and the horse were already half a body length away from entering the Grey Forest.
Luo Zheng quietly hid in the forest, but he noticed He Zuowei's expression.
This He Zuowei had the cultivation of a world overlord and had saved the eldest princess. Why was his expression getting uglier and uglier? Luo Zheng clearly saw He Zuowei's ashen face in the darkness!
Soon, Luo Zheng understood why He Zuowei's expression was so bad …
Just now, Luo Zheng had passed through the stone forest. It was deathly silent. After a long time, he saw a group of monkey like creatures hunting for food. At most, he was attacked by bats!
Overall, there weren't many trees in this forest. Naturally, there wasn't much danger!
However, the moment the woman entered the stone forest!
Luo Zheng felt a continuous tremor under his feet. It was like a continuous earthquake!
"Rumble …"
As the stone forest shook, it seemed to awaken. All the trees and branches waved in the darkness, as if they were the claws and fangs of a demon.
This wasn't much.
Ordinary warriors wouldn't be afraid of these natural phenomena. No matter how strong the earthquake was, it wouldn't kill a warrior who could fly …
However, a boundless and terrifying aura was suddenly transmitted from the ground!
The moment this strong aura was released, Luo Zheng almost lost control of himself. His upper and lower teeth trembled violently before he could regain control of himself!
After feeling this aura, Luo Zheng's instinctive fear caused Luo Zheng to lose control of his body!
However, this loss of control only lasted for a moment. If it was any longer, Luo Zheng would have already fled. However, now wasn't the time for Luo Zheng to expose himself.
What kind of thing was hidden under the ground?
Even if he was facing an enraged Empyrean, Luo Zheng wouldn't feel this terrifying.
As for the eldest princess sitting on the unicorn, her face was deathly pale at this moment!
"Quick, run! Otherwise, we will all die here! "He Zuowei's expression was extremely ugly.
He Zuowei was a Sector Lord. Now that he had lost control of himself, one could imagine how terrifying the thing hidden in the grey forest was.
Why wasn't there any reaction when I walked all the way here? Luo Zheng couldn't help but mutter in his heart. Could it be like what He Zuowei said? The thing underground was only interested in the princess's flesh?
He Zuowei suddenly pulled with both hands and pulled the princess out of the Grey Forest along with her horse!
"Go go go!"
"However, the darkness has lasted for so long. I'm afraid there's going to be trouble!"
"I can only let the obsessives come out!"
Luo Zheng was originally hesitating whether or not to hide here. However, when he turned his head, he only glanced at the grey forest and immediately fled!
At this moment, Luo Zheng squeezed out all of his potential and ran with extreme speed after the group of people!
From within the grey forest, a huge black shadow stood up! It was unknown how long that huge black shadow was, but its body was hundreds of zhang wide. It was probably the size of a true dragon or phoenix.
However, the top of this huge body was filled with huge barbs. All of the barbs were growing towards the black hole at the top.
And at the edge of the black hole, there were black and white spheres!
Although Luo Zheng couldn't understand what this monster was, he could almost instantly tell that these balls were the monster's eyeballs. All creatures were most sensitive to the 'eye' part, and Luo Zheng was no exception.
Seeing such a thing, Luo Zheng didn't hesitate at all. Why would he be afraid of exposing himself?
The Eldest Princess rode her unicorn at an extremely fast speed. She was already at the front. The others were in a row, following behind the Eldest Princess. They didn't choose to fly …
These people were very familiar with the Forbidden Land of God Refinement. Luo Zheng naturally wouldn't choose this step. He used his extremely agile body to run madly behind the group of warriors!
Under such a mad dash, Luo Zheng naturally couldn't hide himself. The Eldest Princess was indeed in a panic after disturbing such a huge monster. However, even in her panic, she still discovered Luo Zheng!
"He Zuowei! I found him, I found him! I already said … he will definitely appear! "The Eldest Princess loudly shouted.
As these people were running madly, they suddenly saw Luo Zheng jump out and join the fleeing group. They were also baffled! Especially He Zuowei. His eyes focused on Luo Zheng, and his face was full of surprise. "This is impossible. The Golden Desert's birth place must have been destroyed. How could someone appear there?"

Chapter 1468
The surface of this giant shadow was full of bumps and hollows. It was ferocious and terrifying. If one looked at it from afar, it would look like an earthworm crawling on the ground.
However, this earthworm was massive and the speed at which it moved was also incomparably strange!
The words of the two naturally reached Luo Zheng's ears.
Luo Zheng heard the general gist of their conversation from beginning to end. It seemed that the Eldest Princess had believed someone's words and then urged her horse to search for that person.
But this He Zuowei believed that this was impossible and in the end, the Eldest Princess had entered the Gray Forest.
However, the Eldest Princess didn't think that the consequences would be so severe.
She had only taken a small step into the Gray Forest and she had already attracted such an existence. Was the attraction of her flesh and blood really that strong?
Although she was riding her unicorn and madly running, she wasn't afraid at all. On the contrary, she felt that it was extremely exciting!
At this moment, her figure turned slightly on the horse's back, allowing the unicorn to gallop forward. Her pair of round eyes locked onto Luo Zheng!
"He has a Divine Limit realm cultivation … he should have just been born in the Forbidden Land of Divine Refinement and should have just left the Gray Forest …" The Eldest Princess softly analyzed.
"Eldest Princess, now isn't the time to discuss this." He Zuowei didn't know whether to laugh or cry. He simply didn't place Luo Zheng in his heart. This fellow had been hiding on the side just now and he had faintly sensed him. However, he didn't think that he was a human, but this wasn't important.
At this moment, he was thinking about how to block that monster behind him. Otherwise, everyone would be buried together!
"No." The Eldest Princess stared at Luo Zheng. Her small lips suddenly curled up. "Capture him for me! Capture him alive! "
Hearing this, Luo Zheng's expression suddenly sank.
In this group, besides He Zuowei who was a World Lord, there was another World Lord. Besides them, almost everyone else was a Divine Transformation realm martial artist.
As long as he could avoid these two World Lords, Luo Zheng wouldn't be afraid of those Divine Transformation realm martial artists!
These two Sector Lords wanted to capture Luo Zheng, and capture him alive. The key was that there was still a huge shadow chasing after them … Luo Zheng didn't think that the two Sector Lords would listen to the eldest princess, because that would be seeking death.
Sure enough, He Zuowei said bitterly, "Eldest Princess, it's more important to escape now. I don't have the confidence to capture the person behind us alive!"
"You're a Sector Lord, yet you can't even catch a little fellow at the Ultimate Divine Stage?" The Eldest Princess took the opportunity to ask.
He Zuowei could only curse in his heart.
If he was given enough time and space, no matter how good this Ultimate Divine stage little fellow was at running, he would still be able to easily capture him. However, what was the situation now? This was the time to run for their lives! The one chasing after him was the Formless Heavenly Centipede!
The mouth of the Formless Heavenly Centipede was said to be able to devour everything in the world. In the belly of the Formless Heavenly Centipede was a strange, narrow cave. Any forest that entered would be slowly melted and dismembered …
"Now is not the time to capture people. Besides, this little brother should be a newcomer as well. He wants to escape with us! Little brother … You won't run away, right? You've just been born in the Divine Refinement Secret Realm. If you get our help, you'll be able to get twice the result with half the effort! "He Zuowei couldn't help but say.
He Zuowei was too clear about the Eldest Princess' temperament. She had come all the way here and actually "coincidentally" found a descender. It was indeed a miraculous thing! She would definitely not let that guy go!
Looking at this guy's figure and speed, his strength was definitely not weak. Even if he was a Sector Lord, he wouldn't be able to control this guy in the blink of an eye. Therefore, it was best to use words to control him first!
"Yes, you won't run away, right?" The Eldest Princess had thought it through. Anyway, this guy had nowhere to go. As long as he followed them, there was no need to capture Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng, who was behind them, blinked expressionlessly. Then, he suddenly jumped to the other side. His body rolled twice in the air, forming an arc in the air before he quickly fell into the bushes!
This group of people didn't dare to fly high, so Luo Zheng naturally wouldn't fly either.
However, Luo Zheng already knew that the Non-Phase Sky Puppet seemed to be attracted by this "Eldest Princess". He naturally wouldn't foolishly follow them. Wouldn't that be courting death? Moreover, this woman had revealed obvious malice towards him. She wanted to capture him. If he obediently listened to her, he would become an idiot …
The Eldest Princess had just finished asking her question when the other party parted ways. She was really angry in her heart. "Go!"
At this moment, she suddenly shook the reins and made a ninety degree turn, chasing after Luo Zheng!
When He Zuowei and the rest saw this, their mouths were filled with a bitter taste. They didn't expect things to develop to this point. Was their lives more important, or was that insignificant fellow more important?
However, the Eldest Palace Master's personality had always been unreliable!
"Eldest Princess! Don't chase! "
"That Non-Phase Sky Puppet has already caught up. Its target is you, Eldest Princess!"
If one were to talk about depression, the most depressed person right now was probably Luo Zheng …
Previously, he had been hiding his body and moving forward peacefully.
Who would have thought that just as he was running halfway, he would encounter such a strange woman!
Because the Eldest Princess had changed her direction while riding on the unicorn, the enormous Non-Phase Sky Puppet had also twisted its enormous body. As it continued to shrink and shrink on the ground, it continued to chase after the Eldest Princess. Naturally, it was also chasing after Luo Zheng!
If one looked down from high up in the sky, a huge ravine would appear in this endless forest. Wherever the Non-Phase Heavenly Centipede passed, it would be a complete mess!
However, this kind of thing was too common in the Forbidden Land of God Refinement!
Because the Saint had intentionally created this Forbidden Land of God Refinement, the benefits of cultivating in it were indeed great, and there were indeed martial artists who had condensed Divine Deity within it!
But at the same time, the Forbidden Land of God Refinement was extremely dangerous! More importantly, it was a great taboo to move around at night!
In the eyes of the natives of the Forbidden Land of God Refinement, the light in the dome was called "divine light". Under the illumination of the divine light, the entire Forbidden Land of God Refinement was safe, and all demons and monsters had to retreat!
However, once the divine light retreated, it would be another world.
Ordinary martial artists didn't dare to move around at all, and only World Lords dared to move around in groups!
The Eldest Princess relied on the fact that she had people protecting her and insisted on coming to the Grey Forest to find a descender.
Luo Zheng had indeed been born in the Dazzling Gold Desert …
However, that place was an abandoned birthplace, so Luo Zheng was alone there.
Therefore, the Eldest Princess took the call and wanted to bring Luo Zheng back!
The unicorn's speed was incomparably fast. Every time the horse's hooves stomped, there would be a faint light flickering under the horse's hooves. While maintaining its high speed, it was also extremely nimble, and it kept chasing after Luo Zheng all the way!
"Hey, wait a moment. Don't run so fast!" The Eldest Princess shouted from behind.
The group of people behind the Eldest Princess all had helpless expressions. There was a huge beast chasing after them, and you kept saying that you wanted to bring him back. You expect him to wait for you? This isn't a dream, right?
As expected, Luo Zheng didn't have any reaction and continued to run forward. At this moment, he didn't even choose his path. When he encountered some bushes and even some huge trees, he would directly crash into them. In any case, these forests couldn't stop Luo Zheng's figure, let alone injure him.
Hearing the Eldest Princess shouting from behind, Luo Zheng wanted to attack several times, wanting to kill this woman!
However, thinking about the two World Lords following behind this woman, Luo Zheng could only endure. It was better to think of a way to get rid of her!
"He Zuowei! Help me stop that guy. Otherwise, I won't be polite to you! " After chasing for a while, the distance between the two sides didn't close. The Eldest Princess finally got angry and used her trump card. She gave the order!
Hearing this, Luo Zheng had already grasped the Thunder Wind Ghost Sword in his hand!
In any case, the Formless Sky Centipede was chasing this woman. If this World Lord really dared to make a move to stop him, he would definitely kill this woman first!
"Hu hu …"
As he ran, Luo Zheng didn't stop his killing intent from spreading outwards.
Although this killing intent was very shallow, He Zuowei could clearly feel it. This killing intent that was purposely spread out was a clear warning.
He Zuowei didn't plan on listening to the Eldest Princess's orders. In any case, this wasn't the first time he didn't listen to her orders. However, the killing intent that Luo Zheng suddenly released left He Zuowei surprised!
This person's killing intent was to remind him that if He Zuowei attacked, this person would kill the Eldest Princess?
This fellow clearly knew that they had two World Lords in their group and that the Eldest Princess was a peak Divine Transformation realm martial artist. Even so, he still dared to release such a warning killing intent …
Was he that confident in his own strength? He Zuowei instantly came to this conclusion.
＝＝＝＝＝

Chapter 1469
He Zuowei's mentality was firm.
His only goal was to protect the Eldest Princess!
If Luo Zheng had so brazenly released his killing intent to intimidate him, then normally he would have already killed Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng was only a descender, and from the looks of it, he was a descender from the Realm of the Gods. This sort of low life form had no value in He Zuowei's eyes.
But under the pursuit of the Formless Heavenly Puppet, He Zuowei didn't dare to recklessly attack.
At this time, Luo Zheng was still maintaining his calm.
The higher the benefits, the higher the risks. This was a very basic principle.
Although the Forbidden Land of God Refinement was a completely independent world, for a martial artist to obtain such great benefits and even condense a godhead here and become a True Divinity … the risks were also great!
In this situation, Luo Zheng might perish if he wasn't careful.
He would either be devoured by the behemoth or killed by the two Realm Masters. These were genuine Realm Masters. That day, when they fought against the Great Shaman in the Great Thousand World, several Daoists took turns to fight, but it was still so strenuous. Now that Luo Zheng was facing two of them alone, it was impossible for him to win!
"He Zuowei, you dare not listen to me!?" The Eldest Princess was enraged. In her eyes, as long as He Zuowei attacked, that fellow in front of her wouldn't be able to escape!
He Zuowei rolled his eyes. He pinched a divine rune in his hand. The Formless Heavenly Puppet behind him was getting closer and closer. He wanted to use the Royal Divine Rune to block it …
But before He Zuowei could move, the Eldest Princess finally couldn't bear it any longer. "Humph, then I'll do it myself!"
The Eldest Princess had a peak Divine Transformation realm cultivation and could be considered a half-step World Lord. Facing the Divine Limit realm Luo Zheng, she was confident she could take him down!
Woosh!
The Eldest Princess suddenly leapt up, an umbrella appearing in her hands!
This was an oil paper umbrella, the Eldest Princess' weapon.
Umbrella type weapons were relatively rare, but the one in the Eldest Princess' hand was especially fierce!
The Eldest Princess' exquisite figure gently flipped on the horse's back and she stood firmly on it. Then, she grabbed the oil paper umbrella with her slender hands and shook it!
"Boom!"
The ribs of the umbrella suddenly opened up!
"Heavenly Spirit Shield!"
The moment the Grand Princess opened her umbrella, an umbrella-shaped light suddenly appeared above Luo Zheng's head, covering Luo Zheng's head!
A sharp light flashed in Luo Zheng's eyes. The killing intent in his heart was obvious!
He didn't want to get into trouble here, but this woman was chasing after him for no reason. If she wanted to die, she couldn't blame him.
Before the Heavenly Spirit Shield fell, Luo Zheng rolled in the air, and a sharp sword light slashed towards the Heavenly Spirit Shield!
"Yi Shen's Sword! Wind Kill! "
After comprehending the complete Yi God's Sword, Luo Zheng's understanding of the way of the sword had reached another level!
Previously, Luo Zheng had comprehended the Origin Returning Yi Divine Sword, which was based on the Metal Elemental Law and mixed with the Chaotic Qi!
However, the Metal Elemental Law wasn't the only Law in this world. Luo Zheng tried to integrate many kinds of Law into the Chaotic Qi, trying to stimulate the Chaotic Qi to become stronger. Unfortunately, Luo Zheng failed in the process of integration.
He couldn't integrate more than two kinds of Law with the Chaotic Qi. The result was that the two kinds of Law were directly swallowed by the Chaotic Qi. As for why this happened, Luo Zheng didn't know.
However, after some research, Luo Zheng discovered that the Yi God's Sword had its own effect when combined with other kinds of Law!
For example, with the Wind Elemental Law as the foundation, the sword light could instantly expand infinitely. Moreover, whether it was the speed or the angle of cutting, it was unpredictable.
Whoosh whoosh whoosh …
The Heavenly Spirit Shield was almost instantly destroyed by Luo Zheng's sword.
"Eh?"
The Eldest Princess, who was standing on the horse's back, was stunned. Her Heavenly Spirit Shield was destroyed so easily?
This umbrella was given to her by her father. How could it be so easy …
This umbrella was called the Lone Star Umbrella, and it was a Primordial Treasure!
With the Eldest Princess's cultivation, the Heavenly Spirit Shield released, other than the Realm Lord, almost every one of them would be hit!
The Eldest Princess was in a daze, but the others were naturally not.
Especially He Zuowei, he didn't stop the Eldest Princess when he saw her attack. Perhaps the young man in front of him had some ability, but his cultivation was still inferior to the Eldest Princess by a large margin, not to mention the weapon in the Eldest Princess's hand was a Primordial Treasure!
Therefore, he was constantly forming seals with his hands. He wanted to form the Imperial God Rune to block the Non-Phase Heavenly Centipede first. Only then would they have a chance to escape.
However, it was fine if the Non-Phase Heavenly Centipede didn't come out. Now that it had chased them out, it would definitely plunder this place. If they shook off the Non-Phase Heavenly Centipede, who knew which city would suffer a calamity!
However, before he could form the Imperial God Rune, the young man in front of him easily cut through the Eldest Princess's Heavenly Spirit Shield.
"This speed … Oh no!"
He had already formed half of the Imperial God Rune, but at this moment, he didn't hesitate at all and instantly gave up.
It must be known that the Imperial God Rune consumed a lot of his energy, and giving up halfway was a considerable burden for him. However, He Zuowei's consciousness changed at a speed countless times faster than an ordinary person's. Since he saw the young man's terrifying sword technique, he knew that if he didn't make a move, the Eldest Princess would probably die at the young man's hands!
"Pah!"
He Zuowei suddenly clapped his hands together, and a divine rune spread out!
An identical divine rune spread out in front of the Eldest Princess, forming a black vortex!
"Pfft!"
At the same time, Luo Zheng's second slash also floated over and crashed into the black vortex.
He Zuowei's attack had annihilated Luo Zheng's second slash!
The Eldest Princess was stunned for a while before she realized what had happened. A silver spiral appeared in her eyes, and her expression became angry. This guy actually wanted to kill her!
He Zuowei's face also darkened. If it wasn't for his quick reaction, the Eldest Princess would already be a dead person!
"Old Five, take down that kid. I will continue to form the array!"
Before this, He Zuowei didn't have any killing intent towards Luo Zheng. He only treated Luo Zheng as a person he didn't want to imprison!
The Forbidden Land of God Refinement was very cruel. Some races would kill creatures of other races without mercy, but He Zuowei's group came from the Divine Realm, and their mentality was high and mighty. They didn't have much interest in killing these weak creatures in the universe.
However, since this person really dared to make a move on the Eldest Princess, it was a capital offense.
After the middle-aged man called Old Five heard He Zuowei's order, he also chose to make a move. With his Realm Lord cultivation, it wouldn't be too difficult to take down Luo Zheng.
However, at this moment, Luo Zheng's figure shifted in the air and changed direction again!
He was actually rushing towards the Eldest Princess!
Luo Zheng was very clear about the current situation. Whether it was the enormous Formless Heavenly Puppet behind him or this group of experts, Luo Zheng wasn't their opponent.
Now, Luo Zheng could only choose to borrow power to be qualified to get rid of this group of people … However, at this moment, he was sighing in his heart. Although the Forbidden Land of God Refinement was very dangerous, he had attracted this trouble for no reason!
The Eldest Princess didn't expect Luo Zheng to rush back. She stood on the back of the unicorn and madly rushed forward at an extremely fast speed!
Under the relative speed, she only saw a figure flash in her vision. The next thought was that she wanted to open the Lone Star Umbrella. This Lone Star Umbrella would protect its owner … Unfortunately, her reaction wasn't that fast.
"Bang!"
Luo Zheng stretched out his hand and grabbed the Eldest Princess tightly!
Under the restraint of the ocean-like power, even with her peak Divine Realm cultivation, she couldn't break free from Luo Zheng's hands!
Moreover, the Eldest Princess was already out of her wits and didn't even try to break free …
"Oh no!"
The two Sector Lords, He Zuowei and Old Five, had a huge change in expression!
This youth was actually holding the Eldest Princess hostage and rushing towards the Formless Heavenly Puppet!
The moment Luo Zheng made his move, he tried to analyze the situation in his heart.
First, he understood that the Eldest Princess on the back of the unicorn definitely couldn't be killed. Not to mention the difficulty of killing a peak Divine Realm expert, even if Luo Zheng could do it, the two Sector Lords wouldn't let him go if he killed her.
In the Forbidden Land of God Refinement, Luo Zheng couldn't escape. Therefore, killing the Eldest Princess was definitely a path to death!
The previous two swords Luo Zheng used were just probing.
Just as Luo Zheng had guessed, when he used the second sword to threaten the Eldest Princess's life, the other Sector Lord had already planned to take his life. The other Sector Lord was also prepared to make a move against him.

Chapter 1470
Luo Zheng knew that he couldn't kill this woman, but it was impossible for her to know what Luo Zheng was thinking.
The biggest surprise in this step was this woman's strength!
But from Luo Zheng's first and second sword attacks, not only did he understand the situation, but he also figured out this woman's strength!
Just like Luo Zheng's initial judgment, this woman was the same as Xi Youqin. She was a martial artist who only had cultivation but didn't have any actual combat experience. This kind of martial artist's cultivation and strength were often unequal … but what Luo Zheng didn't know was that his guess was only half correct!
This was because Luo Zheng's thought process was still that of the universe. He didn't know that the Realm of the Gods was far beyond Luo Zheng's imagination.
This woman wasn't as simple as he thought.

Just like before, when Luo Zheng wanted to kill him, he gave up on forming seals and went to stop Luo Zheng's attack.
At this moment, he was going to go all out. The Eldest Princess didn't treasure her own life, but her own was the most important!
"I make a great wish! I wish for the Divine Path Immeasurable Law Sovereign to descend upon the world, create a supreme space, and seal away all evil … "
When He Zuowei made such a grand wish, his entire being emitted a strong and powerful aura!
Luo Zheng found it hard to stabilize himself under the impact of this aura. At the same time, he was extremely shocked. This Sector Lord's aura was comparable to an Empyrean's!
What the hell was that Great Ambition? To be able to raise his strength to such a level?
After he made this great wish, blue crystals gradually floated out and accumulated in front of He Zuowei, forming a huge space!
The huge Non-Phase Heavenly Centipede was less than three hundred meters away from them. With their speed, they could cross this distance in the blink of an eye. However, under the layers of sealing space, the Non-Phase Heavenly Centipede was actually forcefully sealed within.
Seeing the motionless Non-Phase Heavenly Centipede, Luo Zheng was also extremely surprised.
This Formless Heavenly Centipede was probably a fierce beast that could contend against an Empyrean. It was actually sealed by a Realm Lord?
At this moment, Luo Zheng's view on cultivation had changed again.
Or rather, Luo Zheng's understanding of the division of realms was only from the universe. He wasn't clear about the situation in the other universes, nor was he clear about the situation in the Divine Realm.
This was because in the universe, it represented that a certain Realm Lord was in charge of an extremely special destiny. However, in the Divine Realm, there was no such thing as an Empyrean. Above the Realm Lord was the True God!
The Realm Lords in the Divine Realm were unable to bear the perfect laws of the universe, but they could cultivate great wishes. Of course, this great wish technique wasn't something that only the Realm Lords could use.
Although this great wish technique was powerful, it also had a considerable price to pay.
It actually represented an extremely powerful obsession!
For example, if someone wanted to complete their revenge and wanted to kill the current emperor, then this person could make a great wish. The great wish was that one day, he would definitely kill the emperor!
When the great wish technique was successfully used, this person would receive an additional boost. Especially when he was planning to kill the emperor, his cultivation, his arrangements, and his thoughts would all be greatly improved!
Similarly, if the plan was carried out halfway, because of himself or external factors, or even if the emperor suddenly died, then the great wish wouldn't be fulfilled!
If the great wish wasn't fulfilled, it would ultimately transform into a heart demon. It would appear when the person was at his weakest and give him a fatal blow! In the Divine Realm, there were many people who understood the great wish technique! After all, the great wish technique was considered an absolute unifying path in the Divine Realm.
There were many people who understood it, but those who were willing to make great wishes were few and far between. After all, it was something that would harm one's future.
Although this He Zuowei made this great wish, his heart was filled with regret.
The area near the Gray Forest was actually a relatively safe area. As long as one didn't pass through the Dazzling Gold Gobi and didn't continue to advance into the desert, the danger wouldn't be too great!
As for the several colossi below the forest, they naturally couldn't be provoked.
Unfortunately, the Eldest Princess made such a move in the end, immediately giving He Zuowei a difficult life and death challenge.
If this great wish was made, then in the future, he would have to kill this Non-Phase Heavenly Centipede. Only then could he continue to break through …
Although Luo Zheng was curious about this Realm Lord's methods, he didn't stop at all. He madly rushed forward!
"Stop him, Old Five!"
He Zuowei used the great wish to bind this Non-Phase Heavenly Centipede not to let Luo Zheng escape. At this moment, he had to support his great wish, so it wasn't convenient for him to chase after Luo Zheng.
But just as he froze this Non-Phase Heavenly Centipede, this Non-Phase Heavenly Centipede could still sense the Eldest Princess' existence. Its massive body crazily shrank and contracted, wanting to take a step forward!
Crackle …
Even the space created by the great wish was still creaking at this moment, as if it would shatter at any moment!

"Let me go!"
This Eldest Princess finally opened her mouth to speak!
Just now, Luo Zheng had seized her and madly rushed back. They were only 300 feet away from that Non-Phase Heavenly Centipede. That massive monster's head was covered in a circle of skin and flesh, a pile of horrifying eyeballs.
When the Eldest Princess saw this scene, it could be said that she was scared out of her wits. Even without Luo Zheng's coercion, she still wouldn't dare to move.

"Humph!"
Luo Zheng coldly snorted. Since he had already tied up the Eldest Princess here, how could he let her go?

But at the same time, Luo Zheng also became vigilant. After all, there were two Realm Masters in this Eldest Princess's team!
Luo Zheng's thoughts were constantly spinning, but his feet didn't stop for a moment. He controlled the Eldest Princess and continued to fly forward!

Many Divine Transformation realm guards shouted out at this time, all of them desperately threatening Luo Zheng.
What they didn't know was that such threats would only cause Luo Zheng to run even faster … no matter what the situation was, Luo Zheng had already formed a grudge with these people. However, before the Soul-Chasing Spikes could reach him, Luo Zheng swung the "weight" in his hand behind him, blocking the Soul-Chasing Spikes.

Chapter 1471
Luo Zheng was originally disdainful of using a woman as a shield.
However, this wasn't the universe he came from!
Ever since Luo Zheng stepped onto the martial path, he never lacked experts behind him.

However, in the Forbidden Land of God Refinement, Luo Zheng could only rely on himself.
