Seeing that his threat had no effect, Luo Zheng frowned.
The opponent had a very accurate grasp of his mentality. He only wanted to escape and didn't have the determination to harm the life of the woman in his hand!

The Eldest Princess was like a dragonfly skimming the surface of the water. Her feet constantly tapped the air. The Eldest Princess's supernatural power was extremely special. Since she was a child, she hadn't been able to completely control it. At this time, her eyes revealed a panicked expression.

Chapter 1472
As she grew up, there were many people who died in vain by her side!
The clan regarded her divine ability as an extremely powerful talent, but she regarded this flame as a demon. At least, ordinary people didn't dare to approach her, afraid that they would be burned to death by her if they weren't careful.
Some of the servants who served her were also trembling in fear.
Although those servants had received special training and tried their best to suppress their fear when facing the Eldest Princess, the fear of death couldn't be completely eliminated. Some extremely small movements would cause the servants to overreact. Perhaps because of orders, the servants didn't dare to leave her side, but the Eldest Princess could keenly sense their stiff reactions. They treated her as a monster.
When she was thirteen years old …
The Eldest Princess burned a playmate of hers to death, causing her to sink into endless sorrow. She was like a hedgehog that couldn't restrain its poisonous thorns. Anyone who approached her would be in danger.
This matter had become a shadow in her heart, and it hadn't dissipated until now.
However, the stronger her blue flame was, the happier her brother was, and the happier her father was …
They had done a lot of research on this flame, but they had never told her about it!
However, after coming to the Forbidden Land of God Refinement, her mood became better!
Almost all the warriors in the Forbidden Land of God Refinement lived a life of bloodshed. There might be great danger by her side, but this kind of danger wasn't worth mentioning in the eyes of these people.
Of course, this was related to her cultivation getting higher and higher, and her strength getting stronger and stronger. After reaching the Third Transformation of the Divine Transformation, the warriors' reactions were extraordinarily fast. Occasionally, when she couldn't control the blue flame and let it explode, others could easily avoid it.
However, the shadow in her heart hadn't dissipated … In theory, she wasn't willing to use this flame unless it was a matter of life and death.
Moreover, the Eldest Princess didn't plan to kill Luo Zheng at all.
In panic, the Eldest Princess retreated like a dragonfly, but how could she avoid Luo Zheng?
"Don't!"
The moment Luo Zheng pounced on her, she screamed.

As for the martial artists behind them, they were all dumbfounded at this moment.

The flames on Princess Royal's body quickly spread to Luo Zheng's body. The two of them instantly turned into a large fireball, burning in the air!
Countless golden vortexes once again appeared on the surface of Luo Zheng's body, rapidly absorbing the Princess Royal's flames …
Many years ago, her childhood playmate had been burned to death just like that. She had grabbed her playmate's hand and watched in despair as her friend was burned inch by inch. She had wanted to hold on to her friend tightly, but in the end, all she could grab was a pile of ashes!
At this moment, the Eldest Princess originally thought that this kind of thing would happen again.
However, when she raised her head to look at Luo Zheng, she saw that Luo Zheng's sturdy body was actually absorbing her Deviant Flame. This person was not afraid of her flames!
"That prophecy … is true?" The Eldest Princess's eyebrows jumped wildly.
Under the descent of darkness, she still wanted to leave the Immortal Town. Her goal was to find the one who had descended into the Glittering Gold Desert!
A few days ago, a Heavenly Soul Race member had come to the Immortal Town to sell Falling Crystal Flowers.
The origins of the Heavenly Soul Race were extremely mysterious. They existed widely in the Forbidden Land of God Refinement, but they did not know that the natives of the Forbidden Land of God Refinement did not seem to come from the various universes. This race did not exist in the Divine Realm either.
Some people classified them as part of the Soul Race, but there was a fundamental difference between the Soul Race and the Heavenly Soul Race!

Other than the Eldest Princess, no one else believed this rumor.
However, she still came without hesitation and finally found this young man in front of her!
At this moment, the Eldest Princess was still not sure if this person could really bring her to the Soul Subduing Cliff. However, when she discovered that this young man could actually resist her blue Deviant Flame, she was 80% convinced!
Although she could not find the origin of her flames, it was still a Deviant Flame that even her father was afraid of ….
How did this fellow do it?
How could Luo Zheng know that this woman had so many thoughts in her mind? He continued to hold this woman and run!
And at this moment, a loud sound suddenly came from behind him. It was like the rumbling of thunder!

The Non-Phase Heavenly Puppet moved again!
The two Realm Lords had failed.
The Non-Phase Heavenly Puppet was not something that the Realm Lords could resist. Even if the two Realm Lords took the initiative to use their great aspirations to seal the Non-Phase Heavenly Puppet, it was still able to break free. As a Realm Lord, to be able to seal the Non-Phase Heavenly Puppet for such a short period of time was enough to be proud of!
However, Luo Zheng's expression was not good. He saw that the huge shadow in the distance suddenly crawl on the ground. The ground began to tremble again. The Non-Phase Heavenly Puppet seemed to be chasing over here!
The target of this Non-Phase Heavenly Puppet was not Luo Zheng, nor was it the two Realm Lords. Its only target was the woman in Luo Zheng's hands!
The Grand Princess did not understand at first, but she quickly realized something and her face turned pale.
"Sorry," Luo Zheng was about to release his hand and throw the woman out.
All the trouble was caused by this woman, and it had nothing to do with Luo Zheng.
If someone was in danger, Luo Zheng would not leave them to die. He was not a cold-blooded person, but firstly, this woman wanted to capture him, so strictly speaking, she was his enemy and not his friend. Secondly, Luo Zheng had just arrived at the Forbidden Land of Divine Refinement, so he did not have the ability to fight against the Non-Phase Heavenly Puppet. Luo Zheng did not care about this at all. He focused on rushing forward. The Grand Princess gritted her teeth and followed behind Luo Zheng!
"To think that I was afraid of burning you to death! Now, it seems that you deserve to be burned to death! "the Grand Princess said angrily as she followed Luo Zheng.
"I am not afraid of your flames, so it naturally cannot burn me to death. How can you say that I deserve to be burned to death … Don't follow me," Luo Zheng replied impatiently.
"I will follow you!"
The Grand Princess braced herself and rushed forward. He Zuowei and Old Five were unable to stop the Non-Phase Heavenly Puppet, so she did not know what to do next. Although this young man was a stranger, because of the legend of the Heavenly Soul Race and the fact that Luo Zheng had devoured her Heavenly Flame, she placed all her hopes on Luo Zheng.
Extraordinary people would always have extraordinary methods. This was what her father had taught her, even if this fellow was only at the Ultimate Divine Realm …
Who knew that as soon as she finished speaking, Luo Zheng would flip over and suddenly swing the sword in his hand!
"Whoosh!"
The sword light was sharp and incisive. All of the trees within a ten mile radius were cut down. The sword light drew an arc in the air as it cut towards the Grand Princess!
If the Grand Princess wanted to dodge, she would have to lower her head or fly too high to avoid this strike. But, Luo Zheng had deliberately controlled the speed of his sword so that she would have enough time to dodge!
But, she had no thoughts of dodging. She only maintained her height as she followed behind Luo Zheng.
When the sword light cut towards the Grand Princess's ear and her head was about to fall to the ground, a series of cries of alarm rose up from a thousand feet behind her!
The Grand Princess's followers were also frightened. As these Divine Transformation realm martial artists saw the Non-Phase Heavenly Puppet break through the control of two World Masters, all they could think of was to run for their lives. But, they were well aware that the result of running for their lives would be even worse than being eaten by the Non-Phase Heavenly Puppet. They could only helplessly follow behind.
However, Luo Zheng's sword light finally stopped by the Grand Princess's ear!
A section of the long hair above her left ear was cut off neatly, and the sword light even cut a thin wound on her ear, from which beads of blood seeped out.

Chapter 1473
Luo Zheng's face was as gloomy as water.
In the end, he couldn't bring himself to kill this woman …
Seeing the Formless Heavenly Centipede's massive body wriggling towards them at an outrageous speed, Luo Zheng snorted. Invisible wind blades spread out from beneath his feet as he turned around and continued to dash forward!
A sly smile appeared on the eldest princess's face. She tapped the air with the tip of her foot and also followed behind.
Her strength might be average, but with her cultivation, her speed wasn't any slower than Luo Zheng's. In fact, she was even slightly faster than Luo Zheng. Luo Zheng opened a path in front of her and she followed Luo Zheng's path.
The group of Divine Transformation Realm martial artists also followed suit and continued to follow behind the Eldest Princess!
The Formless Heavenly Centipede was less than a hundred miles away from them. Its massive body shrunk and then bounced off the ground, instantly traversing dozens of miles. After its body shrunk twice, everyone heard a rumbling sound in their ears. Along with the trembling of the earth, the Formless Heavenly Centipede's head was lifted up high and then smashed down from tens of thousands of feet in the air!
Rumble rumble rumble …
The Formless Heavenly Centipede's head was like a large cylindrical mountain as it fell from the sky!
Whether it was Luo Zheng or the Eldest Princess, their faces were filled with panic. The Divine Transformation Realm martial artists' faces were all deathly pale!
"Run!"
"Ignite your blood essence!"
"We can't escape …"
In the blink of an eye, the massive head came crashing down from the sky.
This was like a giant pot lid coming down on their heads. If they wanted to live, they could only escape the range of this pot lid …
The Eldest Princess's breathing suddenly became incomparably intense. Her chest heaved up and down. The oil paper umbrella in her hand suddenly spun, emitting a faint luster. At this moment, it was pulling her forward, its speed actually 50% faster than Luo Zheng's!
Luo Zheng's face was completely black at this moment.
"Another four thousand feet …" He raised his head and judged the height of the Formless Heavenly Centipede's head. Then, he judged that there was still at least twenty miles to go!
Twenty miles was just a blink of an eye for Luo Zheng at full speed! But this Formless Heavenly Centipede came crashing down from four thousand feet. Luo Zheng couldn't escape this distance!
Was he going to die in the Forbidden Land of God Refinement?
At this moment, Luo Zheng wasn't willing ….
Before entering the Forbidden Land of God Refinement, Luo Zheng had prepared.
For example, he considered the immortal's mansion's shelter!
When he met with real trouble or danger, he could use the Gen Token to enter the immortal estate and everything would disappear!
But the Old Patriarch told Luo Zheng that the Forbidden Land of God Refinement could not be separated!
But to the Forbidden Land of the Forbidden Land of's, and the Forbidden Land of God Refinement Forbidden land, anytime and anywhere.....
However, the Forbidden Land of God Refinement forbade all methods to leave. This was one of the basic rules of the Forbidden Land of God Refinement.
Luo Zheng didn't understand this rule, so the Old Patriarch explained it to Luo Zheng. Building a spatial node and then hiding in it might be rare in the universe, but it was a very common method in the Forbidden Land of the Gods.
The Forbidden Land of God Refinement accepted many geniuses in the universe and the Forbidden Land of God Refinement. Although this place wasn't big, it had its own set of rules.
If everyone in the Forbidden Land of God Refinement brought a spatial treasure into the Forbidden Land of God Refinement, and if they encountered danger, they would enter the spatial explosion. Then, the Forbidden Land of God Refinement would lose its meaning …
"Whoosh —"
At this moment, Luo Zheng used all his strength. His speed was extremely fast, but the Formless Heavenly Centipede's head had already descended to a height of three hundred feet!
At this moment, his thoughts were also spinning!
"Just six to seven miles away …."
If he hadn't turned around and slashed at the Eldest Princess, delaying him for a moment, he would definitely be able to rush out.
This Formless Heavenly Centipede looked like a hollow bamboo tube. Spiraling circles of inverted spikes grew from its huge mouth, spiraling all the way to the depths of its abdomen, as if it was a staircase leading to hell.
At such a close distance, Luo Zheng could smell the aura of death coming from the Formless Heavenly Centipede's abdomen. That kind of smell carried a strong stimulation that made people want to vomit …
From the head to the top, all the eyes were staring at the Eldest Princess.
At this distance, Luo Zheng could even see the reflection of this woman in all those eyes, as if there were over a hundred mirrors hanging on top of them!
However, at this moment, a jade-like arm grabbed onto him. The Grand Princess held an umbrella in one hand and grabbed onto Luo Zheng with the other. Under her full strength, Luo Zheng's speed had more than doubled!
"Whoosh whoosh …"
As the oil paper umbrella spun, there was an extremely strange power that seemed to be able to completely eliminate the obstructive force in space, pulling the two of them forward at high speed!
Rumble rumble rumble …
The Formless Heavenly Centipede finally drilled into the ground. This rumbling sound came from behind Luo Zheng and the Eldest Princess. They had escaped from the Formless Heavenly Centipede's mouth!
As the massive body drilled into the ground, the surrounding earth was squeezed apart. Chunks of earth shot up like a mountain, crazily growing upwards.
The massive chunks of earth struck Luo Zheng and the Eldest Princess, pushing them forwards. After dozens of miles, the chunks of earth lost their strength and gradually fell to the ground. As for Luo Zheng and the Eldest Princess, they passed through the chunks of earth and continued forwards!
Their complexions weren't too good.
The group of Divine Transformation realm followers behind the Eldest Princess had already vanished without a trace. They simply didn't have a chance to escape before they were completely swallowed up into the Formless Heavenly Centipede's body.
The ground continued to shake and rumbling sounds continued to echo out, as if countless bolts of thunder were exploding underground!
The Formless Heavenly Centipede's massive body drilled into the ground, stealthily moving underground.
"Continue forwards!"
Although they had escaped disaster, the two of them hadn't yet escaped danger!
The two of them were only separated from the Formless Heavenly Centipede by a thin layer of earth.
Now that the Formless Heavenly Centipede was hiding in the darkness, it was like a crocodile swimming beneath the surface of the water, ready to attack at any moment. They were in an even more passive position.
But this Formless Heavenly Centipede's speed still surpassed their imaginations …
Just as Luo Zheng's voice fell, the ground beneath them suddenly caved in, forming a small basin that was thousands of feet wide. Then, the small basin and the trees above it vanished, replaced by the Formless Heavenly Centipede's giant mouth. This time, the Formless Heavenly Centipede directly rushed upwards, wanting to swallow the Eldest Princess!
The two of them were directly above the giant mouth. At this time, they really couldn't avoid it. They couldn't use the Teleportation Token, they couldn't use the Gen Token, and Luo Zheng didn't have any other means of escaping!
Facing such a colossus, a normal martial artist simply couldn't resist. Perhaps only an Empyrean would be able to temporarily suppress this Formless Heavenly Centipede.
The two of them felt their hearts sink once more.
"I'm sorry, I've caused you to die …"
At this moment, the Eldest Princess held the oil paper umbrella and said with a calm expression.
Looking at her apologetic appearance, it was very sincere. Her pair of beautiful eyes stared at Luo Zheng with sparkling eyes.
Luo Zheng wanted to curse in his heart. At this point, what was the use of apologizing now! If you want to apologize, don't harm others in the beginning!
He wanted to fight with his life, but with his strength, it wasn't difficult to fight against the Formless Heavenly Centipede. That was wishful thinking …
The Formless Heavenly Centipede's head rapidly rose, wanting to swallow the two of them from below. The two of them also desperately tried to increase their height, but in the end, they were still much slower than the Formless Heavenly Centipede's giant mouth. It was inevitable that they would be swallowed!
But after the Formless Heavenly Centipede rose to a certain height, it suddenly stopped. Its cylindrical body stretched as straight as a pencil!
Luo Zheng and the Eldest Princess were both stunned. They looked up and discovered that He Zuowei and Old Five, the two Sector Lords, had once again used Great Ambition! They had sealed the tail of the Formless Heavenly Centipede, which was also the other head of the Formless Heavenly Centipede!
The Formless Heavenly Centipede had one head and one tail each. They had sealed the tail, which was equivalent to imprisoning the Formless Heavenly Centipede on the other side and pulling its tail. The head beneath Luo Zheng and the Eldest Princess's feet was unable to continue upwards!
"Let's go!"
Luo Zheng had never had such a strong desire to survive. This time, he pulled the Eldest Princess forward and once again jumped over the Formless Heavenly Centipede's giant mouth, escaping from this giant mouth that led to the abyss!
But just as they escaped, a violent vibration sounded out once again. The tail of the Formless Heavenly Centipede was fiercely struggling.

Chapter 1474
No matter how loud the commotion behind them was, the two of them did not stop at all.
The intelligence of this Non-Phase Heavenly Centipede was not high, and it only locked onto the Grand Princess. Luo Zheng did not know the reason behind this.
The two of them ran forward, and in the time it took for an incense stick to burn, they had traveled over a thousand miles!
Perhaps there were some other unknown beasts ahead, but the two of them could not care about anything else. They could only try their best to distance themselves from the Non-Phase Heavenly Centipede.
But not long after, the rumbling sound came from behind them again. That huge creature was like a ghost that did not disperse and chased after them once again!
It seemed like this Non-Phase Heavenly Centipede would not stop until it had devoured the woman beside it …
He did not know how He Zuowei and Ol 'Five were doing, so the two of them could only run as fast as they could.
This lush forest stretched for over a thousand miles, and as they continued forward, huge lakes appeared one after another. The terrain went from low to high, and from plains to hills.
As they sped forward, the distance between them and the Non-Phase Heavenly Centipede was rapidly shrinking!
"Ah!" "In front …"
The Grand Princess suddenly cried out, and the oil paper umbrella in her hand suddenly stopped in the air. Her mouth was slightly open as she looked ahead.
"What's wrong?" Luo Zheng frowned.
Since he could not kill this woman and she was following him, he did not have a good way to deal with her.
At this moment, they could be considered to have "shared trials and tribulations" together. It was just that this trial and tribulations came out of nowhere!
"Ahead is the territory of the Mountain Collapsing Race, one of the natives of the Forbidden Land of God Refinement," the Grand Princess replied.
"Natives? Is it dangerous? " Since this woman called the Mountain Collapsing Race a native, Luo Zheng knew that this Grand Princess should be from the Realm of the Gods without a doubt.
The Grand Princess curled her lips, "It's hard to say. The natives of the Forbidden Land of God Refinement are very hostile to outsiders, but it's not like there's no chance …"
Halfway through her words, Luo Zheng had already rushed out, muttering in his head that this woman did not know how to judge.
Staying here would mean certain death, and there was still a chance of survival if he walked forward. This choice was too simple.
However, Luo Zheng had neglected the Princess Royal's previous actions. The Princess Royal had taken a huge risk to search for a "Descender" like Luo Zheng after the darkness had descended. To be able to cause the Princess Royal to hesitate, the Mountain Collapsing Tribe would not be so simple.
The Eldest Princess floated in the air and hesitated for a while before following Luo Zheng. Indeed, she had no other choice.
According to this situation, even if the Grand Princess had the chance to enter the safe zone, the Non-Phase Heavenly Centipede would still follow her. At that time, who knew how many people would die unjustly …
But now that he had entered the territory of the Collapsing Mountain Race, who knew when he would be able to come out!
"Rumble …"
A huge rumbling sound came from behind, and a huge black shadow drilled out of a lake in the distance, and plunged into another lake.
"Ugh …"
The woman puffed up her cheeks and followed after Luo Zheng.
After passing through this hilly area, Luo Zheng also discovered the strangeness of this place.
He had been flying at a height of seventy to eighty meters above the ground, but at this moment, there were a few small stones scattered around him, slowly floating in the air.
Luo Zheng casually grabbed a small stone and carefully sized it up in his hand, only to discover that it was just an ordinary stone, and there was nothing special about it. Then, Luo Zheng casually threw the stone out, and the stone formed a parabola as it rapidly plummeted downwards …
This small stone was not a floating stone, and did not have the ability to float in the air.
Seeing Luo Zheng silently exploring, the Grand Princess said, "It's a force field, the force field of the Collapsing Mountain Race is very special, it's almost in a chaotic state …"
Luo Zheng nodded slightly.
Luo Zheng already had a complete concept of the inner world and the structure of the universe.
The more mature the inner world, the stricter the laws within it!
Just like the universe Luo Zheng was in, it belonged to the inner world of a true Sage. Although it was like what Hua Tianming said, that Sage was on the verge of death, from the lower world — the Boundless World, to the Upper Realm, and then to the stars in the sky, it was all a complete structure, and the laws were almost the same, only the stages were different.
However, the Forbidden Land of God Refinement was not a complete universe, and it could also be that the Sage who built the Forbidden Land of God Refinement intentionally did it, so the laws within were strange and strange, resulting in such a strange scene.
The further they went, the more stones were floating in the air, and those stones were getting bigger and bigger. Finally, they became fist-sized stones, and then millstone-sized stones, slowly floating in the air as if they were weightless.
Luo Zheng also felt that chaotic force field, but at this moment, the force field was very weak, and it did not affect Luo Zheng and the woman much.
… …
The huge body of the Non-Phase Heavenly Centipede drilled in and out of the lake. Every time it drilled into a lake, its body, which was thousands of zhang wide, could almost occupy the entire lake. The water in the entire lake would be discharged by it, forming a small tide that surged to the other lakes.
However, it continued to drill in and out of these lakes, and did not follow Luo Zheng and the others into them!
This huge strange beast was also hesitating!
"Huala … Huala …"
After rolling like this for seven or eight times, the lakes also collapsed, connecting with each other to form a huge lake …
The Non-Phase Heavenly Centipede seemed to be unable to resist the temptation of the woman, and finally made up its mind, choosing to advance, directly drilling into the bottom of a mountain!
"Rumble …"
As the mountains and rivers trembled, the Non-Phase Heavenly Centipede drilled out a giant cave that was thousands of zhang tall!
… …
"It's coming, let's speed up." Luo Zheng did not even turn his head. Feeling the tremors of the earth, he knew that the Non-Phase Heavenly Centipede was getting closer and closer.
The two of them had just entered this area, and their speed had slowed down slightly. In an unfamiliar environment, the necessary exploration was the way of survival for a martial artist. However, the danger behind them made Luo Zheng understand that they did not have the time to pay attention to the strange phenomenon here. Compared to the threat of that fellow behind them, the other threats were not worth mentioning!
But not long after, Luo Zheng discovered that he was wrong, and very wrong at that!
Because there were many floating stones in the air, they chose to avoid them at first, but as the rumbling sound grew closer and closer, Luo Zheng simply opened up a path in front, completely smashing apart these stones along the way!
Previously, he had absorbed the flames from this woman's body, and his body had been tempered, but he had not advanced.
This flame was very effective against Luo Zheng, but the amount he absorbed seemed to be a bit too little.
It seemed that this woman was still somewhat useful. If he could escape danger, he would ask her for this strange flame to burn himself.
"Sou sou …"
Moving forward at high speed, Luo Zheng smashed apart boulder after boulder.
At first, the stones he encountered were yellowish-brown sand, but gradually, the color of the stones floating in the air changed, and the surface of these stones became blood red.
These stones were rich in iron ore, and were slightly stronger than the sand from before. However, to Luo Zheng, they were no different, and he still smashed them to pieces.
But as he continued forward, the red stones gradually disappeared, and began to appear cyan stones. These stones formed pillars, and were twice as hard as the iron ore.
From cyan to brown, then to blue, and finally to sparkling and translucent crystal stones.
Finally, a type of black stone appeared, and Luo Zheng could no longer smash it. Under the impact, he could not break these stones, and could only go around them, but his speed had slowed down!
"I'll go in front." The Eldest Princess raised the oil paper umbrella in her hand, and the umbrella frame bent forward, looking like a long spear.
Luo Zheng saw the changes in the umbrella, and his face revealed a trace of interest. He always felt that this umbrella used by this woman was very mysterious!
After the Eldest Princess aimed the umbrella frame forward, it began to spin again!
Following this, a thin blue spiral gradually condensed. This spiral was only as thin as an arm, and its tip was incomparably sharp. It was like a thin snake that kept twisting. When the tip of the spiral touched the black stone, the stone instantly shattered, and its power was unexpectedly strong!
At this moment, it was the Eldest Princess's turn to open a path in front!
When they broke through the black floating boulder, another type of silver floating boulder appeared in front of them!
The Eldest Princess charged forward as usual, wanting to break through the silver boulder, but the two of them had just flown out seventy to eighty feet when they suddenly felt a strong suction force, quickly pulling them over.
This force was so strong that it was impossible to resist!
The two of them only felt dizzy, and then they fell down heavily. They didn't fall to the ground, but rather to the silver boulder!
When the two of them came back to their senses, they found that they were lying on the silver boulder. Their hands and feet couldn't move at all, as if they were glued to the silver boulder.
