Chapter 1475
Like this, the two of them were stuck to the silver boulder. Even turning around had become extremely difficult.
Under the chaotic force field, this silver boulder emitted an irresistible attractive force!
Luo Zheng tried to lift his arm, but as he tried, it was as if there was an invisible hand pressing down on every part of his body.
"You can't move either?" The Eldest Princess stared at Luo Zheng and asked.
After falling onto the boulder, the two of them were extremely close to each other. They just happened to be looking at each other, as if they were a couple lying face to face.
"It's a bit difficult …" Luo Zheng tried to shake his head, but found that it was extremely difficult to even move his neck. It could be imagined just how strong this attractive force was.
"The Mountain Shattering Clan has boundless strength. It's said that if an ordinary martial artist enters, they won't be able to move at all. They will only become lambs waiting to be slaughtered." The Eldest Princess blinked her eyes and smiled at Luo Zheng.
"…"
Luo Zheng stared at this woman, speechless. "You already knew about the situation here?"
"This is the place where the source of strength is located. Of course I've heard about it …" The Eldest Princess replied.
"Then why did you bring me in?" Luo Zheng was a bit annoyed.
"But we don't have any other path to take …" The Eldest Princess had a natural expression.
Luo Zheng choked once more. This woman wasn't wrong. No matter where they fled, they wouldn't be able to shake off the Non-Phase Heavenly Centipede. It seemed that they could only enter this place to try their luck.
"I bet that the Non-Phase Heavenly Centipede won't dare enter the territory of the Mountain Shattering Clan." This woman wasn't brainless; she also had her own judgment.
The Forbidden Land of God Refinement was originally a training ground. Here, one could obtain the fastest growth. Even the Realm of the Gods couldn't compare with this place. However, the risks were also infinitely magnified. This place was very dangerous.
The Forbidden Land of God Refinement was only the size of two or three great worlds. There were many training grounds scattered around, and the Mountain Shattering Clan was the guardian of the source of strength. This was the holy land of body refiners …
"Unfortunately … you made the wrong bet." Luo Zheng bitterly smiled.
That familiar rumbling sound rang out once more. Although Luo Zheng and the Eldest Princess were sucked onto the silver boulder, they could still see the earth shaking below.
"Rumble …"
As if a huge earthquake had occurred, the ground began to ripple like the surface of the water. Clearly, there was a huge monster moving through it.
Luo Zheng took a deep breath. At this moment, he wanted to stand up from the boulder and get rid of the bindings of this boulder!
Luo Zheng couldn't do it by himself, so he had no choice but to borrow the power of the dragon scale.
After the Nine True Dragons woke up, they had a conflict with Luo Zheng. So far, they had not communicated with each other, but Luo Zheng was still able to borrow Qinglong's power …
After pouring all the power into his body, Luo Zheng's body suddenly shook!
"What are you doing?" When the Eldest Princess saw Luo Zheng's appearance, her eyes revealed a trace of surprise.
"Get up!" Luo Zheng gritted his teeth and answered.
The Eldest Princess smiled when she heard this …
Although the Collapsing Mountain Race was a race that was hard to communicate with, they wouldn't go so far as to kill the innocent. However, many warriors wouldn't enter if they weren't fully prepared. The key reason was this, the first step to get up!
Many warriors who entered were like meat patties stuck to the ground, unable to move at all! In this state, they couldn't cultivate at all, let alone enter the source of power!
"Impossible, unless you have a Realm Lord's cultivation … cultivation …" The Eldest Princess was still talking, but she couldn't finish her sentence because she saw Luo Zheng climbing up …
This huge gravitational force was like a huge hand that couldn't be resisted, pressing Luo Zheng firmly onto the boulder, causing him to be unable to move!
However, Luo Zheng, who had poured the power of the dragon scale, still defied this force and slowly raised his hands and feet! Then his waist, and finally he bent his legs and actually squatted!
"How did you do it? You borrowed external force." The Eldest Princess was still very knowledgeable. She instantly saw where Luo Zheng's strength was. This wasn't Luo Zheng's own strength.
Luo Zheng nodded. He didn't deny it.
His strength would eventually grow, and he couldn't always rely on the power of the dragon scale.
Since the Collapsing Mountain Race was a sacred place for body refinement, how could Luo Zheng miss this opportunity?
At this moment, Luo Zheng suddenly gritted his teeth, straightened his back, and began to slowly stand up!
It was just a simple action of standing up, but he felt like he was carrying dozens of mountains on his back. It was extremely difficult!
"Ka ka ka …"
With every stretch of his spine, an ear-piercing sound could be heard. Even a divine artifact body couldn't bear it.
Luo Zheng couldn't understand. The Collapsing Mountain Race had lived here for generations, so how could the babies born here bear this force?
Warriors of the third transformation like them could barely bear this force. If ordinary creatures were to enter this place, they would probably be crushed into meat patties in an instant!
Under the Eldest Princess's gaze, Luo Zheng still stood up!
"So powerful." The Eldest Princess smiled. Under the flower seller's prophecy, she had a preconceived notion that Luo Zheng was destined to be extraordinary.
However, extraordinary was extraordinary, but the Eldest Princess came from the divine realm. She thought that Luo Zheng was extraordinary because he could bring her into the Soul Suppressing Cliff. That was why she was so unreasonable and wanted to forcibly bring Luo Zheng back.
Only now did the Eldest Princess realize that she seemed to have underestimated this Divine Realm youth.
"It's a pity that it's useless." Luo Zheng stood up and looked ahead, a bitter smile on his face.
Luo Zheng had fallen horizontally onto the silver boulder. After standing up, he was parallel to the ground and could see the abnormality below!
Below these boulders, the ground had sunk down several thousand feet, forming a small basin!
Luo Zheng had seen this scene before. It was the precursor to the attack of the Formless Sky Puppet.
"Boom!"
The basin only existed for a breath of time before it disappeared in front of Luo Zheng. What replaced it was the Formless Sky Puppet's giant mouth. It suddenly rose upwards, wanting to swallow the Eldest Princess and Luo Zheng!
The silver boulders floating in the air were naturally huge for Luo Zheng, but compared to the ridiculously huge Formless Sky Puppet, they were just small stones.
As for the strong gravitational force emitted by the silver boulders, it didn't have any effect on the Formless Sky Puppet.
Luo Zheng had just arrived at the Forbidden Land of Divine Refinement and had already fallen into danger three times. He was also very depressed. This was purely bad luck!
This time, without the grand ambitions of the two Realm Masters, there was no chance of escaping. He lowered his head slightly, his gaze once again landing on the woman beside him. Seeing that the Eldest Princess was about to speak, Luo Zheng spoke first, "No need to apologize to me. I'm just unlucky!"
The Eldest Princess didn't have a trace of sadness on her face. Instead, she smiled. "Who said I was apologizing? Look over there … "
The Eldest Princess wasn't a body refiner. Under the effect of the gravitational force, she couldn't even turn her head and could only look straight ahead.
Luo Zheng followed her gaze and saw a few figures rapidly approaching from not too far away.
"Warriors?" Luo Zheng's gaze focused.
"Mountain Shattering Race!" The Eldest Princess smiled as if she had seen her saviors.
Those warriors were all bare-armed, revealing their bronze muscles. They seemed to be the Mountain Shattering Race that the Eldest Princess was talking about?
Just from the outside, the Mountain Shattering Race didn't seem to be anything special. They were similar to ordinary humans, and only a few details were slightly different. However, what surprised Luo Zheng was that these warriors were actually crazily jumping on these silver boulders. Their speed was shockingly fast!
They weren't affected by the gravitational force at all. Perhaps they had some secret method to escape the influence of the gravitational force.
The mouth of the Non-Phase Heavenly Centipede had only risen halfway when the Mountain Shattering Clan members arrived …
The next scene immediately corrected Luo Zheng's judgement. This group of Mountain Shattering Race people didn't escape from the gravitational force.
It was just that their strength was boundless. The gravitational force on the silver boulders had already become a habit for them!
"Bang, bang, bang!"
The three strongest Mountain Shattering Race people rushed to the front and simultaneously punched the side of the Formless Sky Puppet!
The slowly rising Formless Sky Puppet was like a rapidly growing bamboo. However, it was only halfway there when it was smashed down by the three punches.

Chapter 1476
The huge body of the Non-Phase Heavenly Centipede smashed to the other side, creating a deep ravine in the ground, causing Luo Zheng to be extremely shocked!
He did not expect this scene at all.
The natives of the Forbidden Land of God Refinement were actually so powerful?
The Non-Phase Heavenly Centipede was probably on the same level as a complete True Dragon!
The power that these three people unleashed was enough to directly knock down the Non-Phase Heavenly Centipede?
"Wuuu …"
The Non-Phase Heavenly Centipede's large circular mouth that could not be closed let out a ghost-like cry for the first time. After twisting on the ground twice, it seemed to want to move its head back to its original position. This woman was its easy prey, it did not want to give up just like that!
But just as it was about to get up, the three members of the Landslide Clan rushed forward and once again collided with the seven inches of the Non-Phase Heavenly Centipede!
The Landslide Clan was relatively tall and sturdy, and their average height was close to three meters. They were about the same as the Demon Clan, but compared to the Non-Phase Heavenly Centipede, they were simply a grain of sand!
"Wu!"
"Rumble …"
Under the impact of these three grains of sand, the Non-Phase Heavenly Centipede was once again smashed to the ground.
The Non-Phase Heavenly Centipede twisted its body unwillingly, and no longer dared to disobey the Landslide Clan. It directly drilled into the ground, and with a series of rumbling sounds, it drilled into the ground and quickly left!
However, before it left, the Non-Phase Heavenly Centipede was still extremely unwilling. Before its tail was retracted into the cave, the head on the tail suddenly roared at the Grand Princess in the sky. "Wuuuu —"
Seeing that the three members of the Landslide Clan were about to rush up again, the head on the tail impatiently drilled into the ground and escaped without a trace!
"Hey," Luo Zheng looked at the huge cave that the Non-Phase Heavenly Centipede had drilled out from and asked, "That thing seems to have a deep grudge against you. Why does it have to eat you? How did you offend it? "
From the beginning to the end, this thing had been chasing after the woman beside it, but he did not know if this woman's flesh was more fragrant than ordinary people's? Why was the attraction to the Non-Phase Heavenly Centipede so great?
"You're the one who offended it!" The Grand Princess said unhappily.
"You didn't offend it, so why does it want to swallow you?" Luo Zheng asked curiously.
The Eldest Princess curled her lips. She naturally wasn't willing to tell Luo Zheng the truth. Luo Zheng saw that she was being secretive and didn't pursue the matter any further. However, those members of the Mountain-Crushing Tribe slowly drew closer.
"Whoosh whoosh whoosh …"
There weren't many Mountain Crushing Clansmen in front of him, only about thirteen or fourteen of them.
As they continued to jump in the air, they each stood on a different silver boulder. Some people were directly on top of the boulder, while others were upside down under the boulder. It was as if they were hanging on the boulder with their feet, but they were actually attracted by the gravitational force of the boulder.
One of the three Landslide Clan members who had just repelled the Non-Phase Heavenly Puppet was standing on the silver boulder where Luo Zheng and the Grand Princess were.
This Landslide Tribesman sized up Luo Zheng. After noticing Luo Zheng's cultivation, his eyes revealed a hint of surprise.
To be able to stand up on his own with the power of the Ultimate Divine Realm was already quite impressive.
This Landslide Tribesman quickly said, "Hand over the Trial Talisman!"
"What Trial Talisman …" Facing such an unfathomable expert, Luo Zheng still felt pressured. As for the Trial Talisman, he had never heard of it before.
"Without the Trial Talisman, those who are proficient in this place will die." After speaking, that Landslide Tribesman had already clenched his fist.
Just now, the strength that this Landslide Tribesman had exploded with was still vivid in his mind. If he was hit by this fist, even with the strength of his physical body, Luo Zheng would probably be smashed into smithereens!
Thus, Luo Zheng immediately cast his gaze towards the Grand Princess.
This Grand Princess definitely understood the Forbidden Land of God Refinement better than Luo Zheng. Previously, when she barged into this place, she only said that the Landslide Tribesmen were very troublesome. She didn't say anything about the Trial Talisman. Now that they would die without the Trial Talisman, this was a serious problem …
"I have, I have a lot!" The Grand Princess smiled slightly.
Luo Zheng felt his heartbeat instantly stabilized. Thinking about it, with her status, she didn't lack the Trial Talisman. Thus, he directly ignored this problem.
At this moment, Luo Zheng finally felt a trace of luck. At least this woman wasn't purely bringing trouble to him.
"But I didn't bring it with me." The Grand Princess curled her lips and smiled apologetically at Luo Zheng.
"… …"
Luo Zheng's face became ugly once again. The feeling from before instantly disappeared!
"Without the Trial Talisman, die! Taishan, kill them. "This member of the Landslide Clan leapt up and left without even turning his head.
"Wait, wait!" At this moment, the Grand Princess suddenly shouted, "I am Mu Tu's blood sister!"
When this Landslide Tribesman heard this, he circled in the air and heavily landed on the silver boulder. He stared at the Grand Princess with a strange expression. For a moment, he even stretched his head to look at this woman.
It seemed that he really felt that this woman was similar to the Mu Tu she spoke of. He immediately asked, "Evidence?"
"Evidence … …" The Grand Princess thought for a moment before saying, "The Soul Refining Eagle Horn that my brother killed in the Landslide Tribe! It's in my hairpin! "
The Landslide Tribesman bent down and took out the Grand Princess' hairpin.
The hairpin was multicolored and rippled with light. It faintly emitted the Law of Space. It was clearly a Sumeru Space.
However, the Law of Space contained an aura that Luo Zheng couldn't describe. It seemed to be more perfect than the Law of Space that Luo Zheng had seen before.
The Landslide Tribesman reached out and lightly flicked the hairpin. With a flash of light, a pitch-black eagle horn appeared in his hand. The Landslide Tribesman carefully examined it. "It's indeed the one that Mu Tu killed in the past. Even so, you still have to hand over the Trial Talisman."
The Grand Princess smiled and said, "For my brother's sake, I'll owe you this time, okay? Or I can have my tribesmen send it over! "
Her brother had made outstanding contributions in the Landslide Tribe. He had even received the title of "Wang Lian" and became the leader of the Landslide Tribe!
The Landslide Tribesman revealed a hesitant expression. He then turned around and communicated with the other two Landslide Tribesmen. At this moment, they were using the Landslide Tribe's language.
After communicating for a while, the Landslide Tribesman said, "I'll give you 20 days. This time, I'll bring you into my tribe."
After saying that, he swaggered off.
The Landslide Tribesmen were very old-fashioned and not easy to talk to. Now that they were willing to give them a way out, it was enough to see the Grand Princess's brother's position in the Landslide Tribe.
Then, a member of the Mountain Crumble Clan jumped over and grabbed Luo Zheng and the Eldest Princess. He threw them on his back as if they were sacks and left with the other members of the Mountain Crumble Clan!
Luo Zheng originally thought that once he left the silver boulder, he would regain his freedom and wouldn't be affected by the gravitational force.
At the beginning, it was like that. However, as the Landslide Tribesman flew through the air, the gravitational force disappeared!
There was an invisible force field here. This force field didn't cover a large area. As long as one entered that area, they would be firmly sucked in. However, if one left that area, there wouldn't be any effect.
However, as the Landslide Tribesmen continued to go deeper, more and more silver boulders appeared. In the end, Luo Zheng saw that the silver boulder directly became the foundation of the ground, extending all the way to the end of his vision.
Naturally, this force field was everywhere. Furthermore, as they continued to go deeper, the gravitational force field brought about by the force field became stronger and stronger!
The two of them crawled on the Landslide Tribesman's back and flew forward. They could only stare at each other!
"That's right, I haven't asked for your name." The Grand Princess stared at Luo Zheng and smiled.
Actually, as the gravitational force increased, she also felt very uncomfortable. Especially when she was lying on the Landslide Tribesman's shoulder like a sack. It was very difficult to maintain this position.
"Luo Zheng." He replied truthfully.
This was the first time Luo Zheng had come to the Forbidden Land of God Refinement. There was no need for him to conceal his identity.
"Han Liusu." She smiled.
At this moment, Luo Zheng wasn't in the mood to talk. He was more concerned about what he was going to face next.
This Han Liusu was talking non-stop. She asked all sorts of questions. She asked why Luo Zheng could absorb her flames and not die. She also asked which universe Luo Zheng came from.
"I'm not sure which universe it is." Luo Zheng shook his head.
The living beings in the universe couldn't cross the Heavenly Dao's restrictions. Luo Zheng also couldn't obtain enough information.

Chapter 1477
These Mountain Shattering Tribesmen carried the two of them forward, and finally entered a huge tribe. Along the way, the two of them chatted, and Luo Zheng and Han Liusu had a preliminary understanding of each other.
The buildings in this tribe looked very primitive, but all of them were built with that kind of silver stone. The surface of the buildings were painted with other patterns. Although it looked rough, it gave people a kind of dignified feeling. After all, this was the accumulation of a kind of culture.
After the Mountain Shattering Tribesmen carried Luo Zheng and Han Liusu into the tribe, they found a corner and casually threw them down, and then left immediately.
From the Mountain Shattering Tribesmen's shoulders to the ground, the height was less than one zhang, but when they hit the ground, the two of them let out a muffled groan!
Under this kind of strong gravitational force, the power contained in such a fall from such a height could not be underestimated.
Luo Zheng was still fine. He only felt that his five viscera and six bowels were surging, and quickly returned to normal.
Although this Han Liusu's physique was not bad, she was after all a woman. Previously, she did not pay much attention to refining her body, and that Mountain Shattering Tribesman also did not have the slightest intention of showing mercy to a woman. After being thrown down, she suffered internal injuries, and a trace of blood hung from the side of her mouth!
"Are you okay?" Luo Zheng asked.
"En …" Han Liusu was also depressed. This kind of injury was nothing, but when had she received such treatment?
After the two of them were thrown to the ground, the two of them could not move, and could only lie on the ground motionlessly. This huge gravitational force was practically everywhere, and at any time, there were dozens of mountains pressing down on their bodies. How difficult was it to climb up?
Luo Zheng could only activate the power of the dragon scale again. The gravitational force here was much stronger than that of the silver stone. Although he borrowed the power of the dragon scale, Luo Zheng still could not stand up. In the end, he turned over and sat up!
Lying on the ground, he could not see anything.
After sitting up with difficulty, he leaned against the wall beside him. Only then did he notice that there were many martial artists on the other side of his body!
"This …"
These martial artists came in all sorts of shapes and sizes. Some had green skin, some looked like dwarves, and some were very handsome. However, all of these martial artists were very similar to humans!
Why were most intelligent creatures similar in shape to humans?
Luo Zheng had thought about this question a long time ago. Whether it was the Sacred Clan, the Human Clan, the Demon Clan, or even the Demon Clan, each race had their own culture and power. Their common characteristic was that they walked upright. Although there were certain differences in body shape and appearance, the internal structure of their bodies was almost the same. For example, the Demon Clan only had pointy ears, while the Demon Clan only had a ferocious appearance.
Even strange creatures like the Ancient Shamans did not look too different from humans!
Not to mention that many Super Divine Beasts still used humans as the standard when they finally transformed … Even after crossing over to another universe, the Mountain Shattering Clan in the Forbidden Land of God Refinement was the same. This was naturally not a coincidence.
These martial artists were the same as Han Liusu; nearly all of them had a peak Divine Transformation realm cultivation. But, they were all lying on the ground, motionless.
Now that they saw that Luo Zheng was actually able to sit up, the expressions on the warriors' faces were extremely interesting. They had already noticed that Luo Zheng was actually only at the Extreme Divine Realm.
In truth, after the vast majority of martial artists were sent to the Forbidden Land of God Refinement, they would cultivate in the nearby cities. A place like the Source of Strength was not a place that a Divine Realm martial artist could come to …
Luo Zheng could be considered an exception. The place where he was born had already been abandoned for countless years. In these years, only Luo Zheng had been born here. Then, he encountered Han Liusu and was chased by the Formless Heavenly Centipede into the Mountain Shattering Clan's territory.
For a Divine Realm martial artist to run into the Mountain Shattering Clan's territory, this wasn't a problem at all! The problem was that this fellow was actually able to sit up!
"Friend, not bad. You just arrived and you're already able to sit up. You have inborn divine strength!" A martial artist said to Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng leaned against the wall. As he heard this he faintly smiled, "I was just lucky."
"Lucky? Hehe … I've been lying here for three years and I've never heard of anyone being lucky enough to sit up! "The martial artist said.
Three years …
Hearing this, Luo Zheng was somewhat speechless.
Under such a strong gravitational force, even lying here was a form of cultivation!
If one could stand up one day, then one could truly accept the Mountain Shattering Clan's trial. If one could obtain the Source of Strength, it would be a qualitative leap for a body refinement martial artist!
"You've only been here for three years. I've already been here for nine years! If I crawl up before next year, I'm afraid I'll be chased out! "Another martial artist said with a sullen face.
"Isn't it good to leave?" Luo Zheng asked curiously.
Lying here for ten years was also a painful thing. Without the help of the Mountain Shattering Clan, even crawling out would be impossible. He didn't know why this martial artist was so sad.
"Good my ass … I wasted a Trial Talisman for nothing. For this Trial Talisman, I almost lost my life!" The martial artist said gloomily.
"…"
Luo Zheng was prejudiced by first impressions. Because this Han Liusu said she had many Trial Talismans, Luo Zheng naturally thought that these Trial Talismans were very easy to obtain.
What he didn't know was that for the Trial Talismans, many martial artists had sacrificed their lives in this Forbidden Land of God Refinement.
Some martial artists had painstakingly obtained a Trial Talisman and traveled thousands of miles to enter the Six Great Cultivation Holy Lands. This Mountain Shattering Clan was a holy land for body refinement martial artists. Many martial artists brought their Trial Talismans without hesitation.
But the final result was that they were thrown into the Mountain Shattering Clan's tribe, lying on the ground without moving.
This would often last for several years!
Some martial artists with innate divine strength could stand up in a few months and gradually get used to the gravitational force here. Then, they would join the Mountain Shattering Clan and begin to accept the trial. Finally, after completing the trial, their strength and body would receive a terrifying increase!
But even more martial artists would lie here for seven to eight years before they could gradually stand up.
To a martial artist with three transformations, this amount of time wasn't a problem. But if they couldn't stand up in ten years, they would be expelled from the Mountain Shattering Clan. That was the most tragic thing.
So when that Eldest Princess Han Liusu realized that this was the Mountain Shattering Clan's territory, she hesitated. Her goal was to enter the Soul Suppressing Cliff and she didn't need to refine her body. If she was brought into the Mountain Shattering Clan's territory, within ten years, even if she wanted to leave, she would have to rely on her own strength to stand up and walk out. If she couldn't do it, it would be a waste of her time and effort.
Her ten years was naturally much more valuable than an ordinary martial artist.
After several hours …
Those spots of light at the top of this huge cave regained their luster one by one, releasing a faint light. Under the illumination of countless rays of light, the night of the Forbidden Land of God Refinement passed and the day finally arrived.
The martial artists in front of Luo Zheng were still lying on the ground like corpses, not moving at all.
Maintaining this position for ten years without moving was also an unbearable thing. Luo Zheng couldn't help but sigh in his heart.
At this moment, a roar suddenly came from the Mountain Shattering Clan's tribe!
"Roar!"
A Mountain Shattering Clan member stood at the highest point and began to roar towards the sky!
At the same time, all the Mountain Shattering Clan members began to respond to that person's roar and began to sing at the top of their lungs!
Luo Zheng couldn't understand this song at all. It was sung in the language of the Mountain Shattering Clan and didn't follow the music theory. But under the rough and orderly voices, there was an extraordinary strength that had a different aura to it!
"What are they singing?" Luo Zheng asked.
Han Liusu curled her lips. "Listen carefully, it's good for you … if you can stand up, you can carry me out in the end!"
She didn't plan to refine her body, but she also didn't want to lie here for ten years. She had only been lying here for a few hours and she was already depressed. If she really had to lie here without moving and waste ten years, she wouldn't be willing even if you beat her to death!
So in these few hours, Han Liusu could only place her hopes on Luo Zheng!
This fellow had just come in and was already able to borrow strength to help her sit up. She naturally hoped that Luo Zheng could quickly adapt to the gravity here …
"Benefits? What benefits? "Luo Zheng's face revealed a puzzled expression, but soon his body felt a trace of abnormality!
These voices contained a strange vibration. Under the constant roar, Luo Zheng's body produced a very strange resonance.
Under this resonance, Luo Zheng's blood flow suddenly increased, and the speed of his heartbeat also began to increase. When he looked at the other martial artists, their faces were all flushed!
This Mountain Shattering Clan was very unique. Just their singing could refine the body?
＝＝＝＝＝

Chapter 1478
The Mountaincrasher Clan seemed to be an extremely primitive race. Even their social structure was maintained in an ancient clan state.
But, there were many mysteries hidden within!
It could even be said that their every movement was a form of cultivation!
For instance, the Mountaincrasher Clan's women would be pregnant for 30 years. During these 30 years, their babies would be baptized by the singing of the tribe every day. Before they were even born, they would passively undergo body refining!
During this body refining process, these babies would gradually adapt to the gravity of this land. After they were born, they would possess infinite strength.
Thus, the Mountaincrasher Clan's babies would be able to crawl all over the ground as soon as they were born …
As for the Divine Transformation realm martial artists present, they couldn't even move their fingers. They were inferior to even the babies of the Mountaincrasher Clan.
This was also the reason why the Mountaincrasher Clan only treated these martial artists as trash after receiving the trial talismans. The Mountaincrasher Clan worshipped powerhouses, but these fellows couldn't even compare to the babies of their clan. Then, they could only obediently lie down here! When they could stand up one day, they would come back and accept the Mountaincrasher Clan's smelting trial!
The Mountaincrasher Clan's singing continued for two hours!
Every day after dawn, they would shout for two hours. This Mountaincrasher Clan actually had the strength to do so.
The Forbidden Land of God Refinement was filled with all sorts of strange things. Singing was originally a method of body refining, but this was also normal.
This strange song produced a subtle vibration. This vibration was like countless small hammers that constantly hammered down on Luo Zheng's body. This hammering came from all directions, from the outside to the inside, slowly sinking into him!
Luo Zheng finally understood. This method of tempering the mortal body was similar to the principles of Luo Zheng's Most High Refining Method!
However, the Most High Refining Method turned a person into a weapon and tempered them. As for the Mountaincrasher Clan's method, it directly hammered down on the body …
If it weren't for Luo Zheng's body being turned into a weapon, he wouldn't have been able to resist this sort of attack. His injuries would have become increasingly severe. Naturally, an ordinary person wouldn't use such a method.
But, the vibrating strength of this song was relatively gentle. Under the resonance of this song and combined with the powerful gravity of this land, the benefits to a body refining martial artist were obvious.
Listening to this Body Tempering Ballad for a day might not be a big deal. It would only cause one's Qi and blood to surge and one's body to tighten. However, if one continued to listen to it for a year, five years, or even ten years, the effect would naturally be extraordinary.
Therefore, even after ten years, he still did not have the qualifications to climb up and join the Mountain Crushing Tribe. Once he left this place, his physical body and strength would increase by leaps and bounds. That was why the Mountain Crushing Tribe was seen as a holy land for cultivation!
After the song dissipated, all the members of the Mountain Crushing Tribe set off in an orderly manner, beginning their day of hunting.
The members of the Crumbling Mountain Race weren't like the other martial artists in the universe. Once they cultivated to a certain level, they would be able to abstain from eating and consume the energy of heaven and earth as food. Even though their strength was comparable to World Overlords and Empyreans, they still had to consume energy to replenish themselves. Also, because their fleshly bodies were extremely strong, they needed to consume large amounts of food to replenish themselves.
If the warriors who entered the Collapsing Mountain Clan to cultivate could stand up one day and gradually adapt to the gravity of this place, they would also need to hunt with the Collapsing Mountain Clan.
However, Luo Zheng and many other warriors could only watch from the sidelines …
After the members of the Collapsing Mountain Clan left, the entire tribe became much quieter. However, not long after, many children emerged from the tribe!
The members of the Collapsing Mountain Clan had large physiques. Naturally, these children weren't small either. Some of the children were already seven to eight feet tall. This was considered burly among the human race. However, from their outer appearance, they still maintained the appearance of children.
After these children appeared, they started to play around in the tribe. Among them, there were even the scolding voices of the Collapsing Mountain Clan women. This was a normal day in the Collapsing Mountain Clan.
Not long after, a few children rushed over here!
"Uncle Toad!"
"Uncle Toad! My wooden cow is broken! "
"How should I fix it …"
The Uncle Toad that these children were talking about was also a warrior who came to accept the trial.
This warrior was the same as Luo Zheng. He belonged to the category of being able to barely adapt to the gravity. However, he was still unable to completely stand up. He was on all fours, lying on the ground like a toad, motionless. It was probably because of this reason that these children gave this person such a nickname …
Seeing these children dashing around freely in the tribe, Luo Zheng sighed inwardly. They were really inferior to the children of the Collapsing Mountain Clan. It was no wonder that those three Collapsing Mountain Clan members could beat up the Non-Phase Heavenly Centipede. This was the terrifying strength and talent of the Collapsing Mountain Clan that created such a unique life form!
When the children gathered around that "Uncle Toad", the other warriors revealed looks of envy on their faces.
Luo Zheng didn't understand at first, but he quickly understood. This "Uncle Toad" had used his own skill to create a good opportunity for himself!
These children were all leading a small wooden cow behind them. This wooden cow was very exquisitely made, and its appearance was very lifelike. It was obvious that this was a very unique puppet technique.
This puppet technique didn't exist in the Collapsing Mountain Clan. It was imparted to these children by this Uncle Toad, allowing them to create it themselves. The only thing that Luo Zheng couldn't figure out was that this wooden cow, as a puppet, was actually able to escape the powerful gravity of the Collapsing Mountain Clan. Perhaps it was because of the special materials used.
"Uncle Toad, my wooden cow's leg is broken. It walks like a cripple, and it's very ugly!"
"Hmph, who told you to fight with Ah-Tu? Serves you right!"
"It seems like you didn't win!"
Just as these children were arguing, the Uncle Toad chuckled and said, "Uncle, I can teach you how to fix the wooden cow's leg, but according to the usual practice …"
Before this Uncle Toad could finish speaking, one of the children had already taken out a large black object and placed it in front of Uncle Toad. "Uncle Toad, Ah-Han has already prepared a piece of meat! It's what I saved yesterday! "
That piece of black meat looked ordinary, but under the illumination of the light, it was suffused with a unique phosphorescence. It was unknown what kind of creature it was cut from, but in Luo Zheng's opinion, it was definitely not an ordinary item!
"Hahaha, then Uncle Toad will kindly accept it. Please help me stuff this piece of meat into …" Before this Uncle Toad could finish speaking, the child had already used a small knife to cut the meat and personally fed it to him!
This warrior not only imparted the puppet technique to them, but also taught them how to use puppets to fight, which was what these children called a bull fight.
Although the wooden cow's material was indeed extraordinary, the wooden cow would eventually break down after fighting with each other. Although this Uncle Toad taught them how to make the wooden cow, he didn't tell them how to repair it. After the wooden cow was broken, they naturally had to find this Uncle Toad to ask for the method to repair it.
And this Uncle Toad's condition for the exchange was naturally those pieces of meat!
In this tribe, only the warriors who participated in the hunt with the Mountain Collapsing Tribe had the qualifications to eat meat. The warriors lying here didn't have the qualifications. The benefits of these pieces of meat to the warriors were obvious, so naturally, there would be people who would use their brains.
In fact, this Uncle Toad had only entered this tribe for a very short period of time. It was because he ate these pieces of meat that this Uncle Toad could barely get up. It could be seen that these pieces of meat contained a miraculous ability.
Or it could be said that this person had already planned this idea before he entered the Mountain Collapsing Tribe!
"Talent …" Luo Zheng couldn't help but sigh.
Those children took out their own pieces of meat and cut them piece by piece to feed this Uncle Toad. Then, this Uncle Toad commented on them one by one and imparted the method to repair the wooden cow to these children. Only then did the children leave contentedly.
Before those children left, there were also other warriors lying on the ground who tried to "steal business".
"Ah-Han, I also know how to repair the wooden cow …"
"Uncle will tell you a story, okay?"
"I'll teach you how to make a flying wooden bird. It's so fun!"
However, the children's interests were all concentrated on the wooden cow. The other warriors' 'seduction' did not have much effect. The children very quickly ran away without a trace. This group of people also had no way to get up and chase after them. They could only look at the backs of the children leaving with a helpless expression.
Luo Zheng did not participate in this.
Since he had unintentionally entered this Mountain Collapsing Tribe, it could be said that it was fated. According to Han Liusu's explanation, this cultivation talisman was so hard to obtain. He could be said to have followed Han Liusu in for free. He naturally had to seize this opportunity to refine his body!
The day passed very quickly. When night fell, those Mountain Collapsing Tribesmen also returned to the tribe.
And on their backs, there were huge beasts. That was the result of their hard work for a day.
There was a one-legged bull the size of a small mountain, a scarlet blood eagle with wings hundreds of feet long, and a mutated porcupine with golden spikes all over its body.

Chapter 1479
When these beasts were alive, they were all overlords of a region, at the top of the food chain. Some of them could even transform.
But here, they could only become food for the Collapsing Mountain Tribe …
In the center of the tribe that was several thousand zhang wide, the Mountain Crushing Tribesmen carried out a bloody disassembly!
The Collapsing Mountain Tribe members in charge of dissecting the beasts were very experienced. Perfect beast skins were peeled off from the surface of the beasts, then the meat, bones, tendons …
These beasts were comparable to sector lords. Every part of their body was very precious. Not long later, all the beasts were dismembered!
A thick smell of blood rushed into the sky, pervading the entire tribe.
Whether it was the adult fighters or the children, they were all used to it. But the fighters beside Luo Zheng were very uncomfortable!
Most of these fighters were lying on the ground, unable to move. Naturally, they couldn't see the entire process. Luo Zheng leaned against the wall, but he could see the entire process.
Previously, he noticed that there were grooves in the central plaza of the tribe. He thought that it was a huge array, but now he understood that the Mountain Crumbling Tribe didn't even need to use any array. Those grooves were only used to divert the blood of those ferocious beasts. They were blood grooves.
These ferocious beasts were comparable to world overlords. The essence blood in their bodies was incomparably vigorous. If they sliced open a ferocious beast, the blood would flow out like a river. If they didn't use these blood troughs to reverse the flow, the blood of a few ferocious beasts would be enough to drown the entire tribe. It was just that no one knew where they had collected the blood.
After the slaughter was completed, bonfires were lit one after another!
The wood they used was a special kind of wood, and when it was burned, it emitted a unique green wood fragrance.
Even though Luo Zheng had abstained from eating for many years, when he smelled that unique wood fragrance, his appetite was aroused. That fragrance could arouse a human's most instinctive appetite!
"Gu gu gu …"
Luo Zheng's surroundings were instantly filled with cries!
Including Han Liusu, it was the same …
The fighters present had cultivated for much longer than Luo Zheng. Their years of abstaining from eating were counted in tens of thousands of years. At this moment, their appetite was aroused.
"Ai, it's here again …"
"Every time night comes, it's the most difficult time!"
"I really want to eat a piece of meat!"
"Wu wu …"
Someone actually started to cry!
The smell of the burning wood was like a kind of hunger poison.
Warriors who had reached the Third Transformation of the Demigod Realm naturally had much more perseverance than ordinary people. Even if these warriors who were lying on the ground were tortured even more severely, they might not even shed a single tear. Those who could reach this level, unless they were the inheritors of great forces or had amazing talents themselves, had all endured great pain.
But at this moment, these martial artists became incomparably frail.
"It can't be …" Seeing the reactions of the warriors, Luo Zheng was somewhat surprised.
Han Liusu was lying on the ground. After hearing Luo Zheng's words, she said, "That's the Avoiding Calamity Wood. It emits the Avoiding Calamity Fragrance. It can arouse the most primitive desires of living beings. It's a specialty of the Forbidden Land of God Refinement."
To the north of the Mountain Crushing Tribe, there was a sea of trees called the Woeful Forest. Most of the trees there were these towering trees that were thousands of feet tall.
The Du 'e Tree was constantly emitting a faint, exotic fragrance.
In this Woeful Forest, the desires of all living beings were infinitely magnified countless times. The instincts of these living beings were also stimulated to the limit.
From small insects and ants to large birds, from low level Vicious Beasts to high level Vicious Beasts, their strongest desires were stimulated at any time. They crazily devoured each other, killed each other, and at the same time, crazily copulated to produce offspring …
This was hell, but it was also a paradise for Vicious Beasts.
Even if a warrior entered this place, after staying for a long time, he would not be able to resist the most basic desires in his heart. A person's desires would also be stimulated to the limit. Appetite, lust, possessiveness … they would all be magnified countless times. They would also become naked beasts.
"However, they lit these bonfires here and would eat here every day. Those people have stayed here for a long time and know that it's time for the Mountain Crushing Tribe to eat. That's why their appetites are so stimulated," Han Liusu said naturally.
Luo Zheng nodded, "No wonder I saw that when they saw that piece of meat earlier, their eyes were all fixed on it. It's probably because they had been holding it in for so many years …"
If a person was tied up and could only see fish and meat beside them every day while they couldn't eat a single bite of food, it would be unbearable after a long time. A warrior's heart was firm, but their desires were lost in the Avoiding Calamity Fragrance in the air. Naturally, it would be incomparably painful.
As Luo Zheng said this, his gaze landed on Han Liusu.
Before this, Luo Zheng had not paid much attention to this girl. At this moment, he could not help but notice her.
This Han Liusu was also a rarely seen fairy-like figure in the mortal world. Under her neat and tidy hair was an elegant and refined face. She was wearing a dark embroidered silk corset and a ruyi pattern skirt. Although she was lying on the ground, her exquisite and undulating figure was clearly outlined … ….
"Don't look at me!"
Yun Liusu glared at Luo Zheng and could not help but remind him.
Hearing this, Luo Zheng was suddenly startled. Then, with great difficulty, he slowly moved his head to the other side.
Although the Avoiding Calamity Fragrance could magnify a person's appetite, it could also magnify other desires. Generally, warriors who cultivated their bodies were male. Previously, these warriors did not have such desires between them. Naturally, they could not be aroused by the Avoiding Calamity Fragrance. However, now that Yun Liusu had arrived, the situation was naturally different.
Yun Liusu also knew what kind of reaction Luo Zheng had. Her face turned red and her neck turned red. However, in her heart, she silently chanted the Clear Heart Mantra to calm herself down.
When the bonfire started burning, the most difficult moment had already arrived!
Those huge pieces of meat were being roasted on the raging fire. As the fat in the meat exploded, a meat fragrance that made people despair quickly spread!
The meat fragrance was like a devil, teasing everyone's most primitive nerves!
"Ah ah ah …"
"I can't take it anymore!"
"I'm starving to death!"
The warriors lying on the ground seemed to be enduring great torture. Howls rose and fell one after another.
The members of the Collapsing Mountain Tribe heard the howls and seemed to be used to it. One of them walked over and said coldly, "Don't make a fuss! If you have the ability, get up. There are large pieces of meat here and good wine! If you don't have the ability, lie down properly! "
Get up and eat meat …
This was blatant seduction!
To martial artists, this was also a kind of cruel trial.
It would arouse your desires and then use a huge gravitational force to suppress you. If you wanted to satisfy your desires, then you had to resist this terrifying gravitational force. If you succeeded, it would be a huge breakthrough for warriors who cultivated their bodies.
Luo Zheng had just arrived and was able to resist the temptation of food. However, Yun Liusu's lovely figure just now kept replaying in his mind.
"How troublesome." Luo Zheng tried to stifle the desire in his heart and tried to distract himself.
At this moment, he saw the members of the Collapsing Mountain Tribe starting to gorge themselves!
It was no wonder that the Collapsing Mountain Tribe was incomparably strong. Even the strength of those children was much stronger than that of ordinary warriors.
He saw with his own eyes that those children wolfed down food that was several times their weight. Being able to eat was also a kind of talent! That Overcoming Adversity Incense was effective on Luo Zheng and the others, but it was also effective on the Collapsing Mountain Tribe!
As for the adults of the Collapsing Mountain Tribe, it was even more exaggerated. Each person could swallow a piece of meat the size of a house!
"I, I want to eat …"
Just as Luo Zheng was letting his imagination run wild, he saw a warrior roar and suddenly struggle up from the ground. Then, he stood up!
But in an instant, two crisp sounds were heard!
"Pa, pa!"
Luo Zheng saw that the warrior's legs were twisted at an unbelievable angle. They were actually fractured!
When this warrior used all his strength, his strength might be able to temporarily break free from this strong gravitational force. However, his body could not withstand it. His strength and the gravitational force overlapped each other, and finally, the bones in his legs broke!
At this moment, Luo Zheng sighed. There was no free lunch in this world. Raising one's strength in this Forbidden Land of God Refinement might be fast, but one had to experience the most cruel oppression!

Chapter 1480
Beneath the dual enticement of the Avoiding Calamity Incense and food, many martial artists began to tremble.
One could see that they were desperately trying to crawl up from the ground!
Once a person had a goal, they would stimulate an intense instinct and display their strength beyond their normal limits.
These martial artists had been lying here for months and years. Every day, they would be baptized by the Mountainshattering Clan's songs. Every day, their strength would increase by a little. Every day, they would suffer such torment. Their instincts would drive them to cultivate so that they could crawl up and eat a mouthful of delicious food!
This unique method of cultivation was a barbaric destruction, but it was also an effective form of whipping!
"I also want …"
Another martial artist struggled up from the ground. This martial artist was a peak Divine Transformation realm martial artist and also walked the path of body transformation. His strength far surpassed Luo Zheng's because he had relied on his own strength to stand up!
Although this martial artist crawled up, he was still barely able to do so. His feet seemed to be glued to the ground and even moving a single step was difficult. His toes dragged along the ground as he staggered like a drunkard. But in the end, he still managed to walk out!
As the many martial artists lying on the ground looked at this fellow that stood up, complex looks appeared in their eyes. Not only was there envy in their eyes, but there was also pure jealousy.
In this sort of situation, it was hard for anyone to maintain their original heart.
Even Luo Zheng felt his stomach rumble with hunger at this moment. His hunger was unbearable.
Jugs of wine that were as tall as a person were opened up and a rich fragrance of wine wafted out.
Ordinary wine had no allure to a martial artist. But, the Mountainshattering Clan's strong wine was concocted from the bones of all sorts of vicious beasts. This sort of precious beast bone wine contained a primal and wild fragrance; its allure was no small matter.
The fragrance of barbecued meat and wine mixed with the fragrance of the Avoiding Calamity Incense; just how deadly was that?
"I … I also want to try!"
Luo Zheng erupted with the power of the dragon scales once more.
Now, Luo Zheng felt a bit of regret. When he returned to the Demi-Immortal Clan a second time, the clan should have already gathered a considerable number of weapons, enough for Luo Zheng to light up many dragon scales. But in the end, he decided to accumulate enough Heaven's Divination essence to help the nine dragons forge a Heaven's Divination Body.
If it weren't for this, Luo Zheng would have been able to borrow far more power from the dragon scales!
With all his strength, Luo Zheng climbed up with great difficulty!
Although he could resist the gravitational force for the time being, his strength was being consumed very quickly!
The power contained in the dragon scales was not limitless, but the power contained in them was much more vigorous than Luo Zheng's. The dragon scales had not yet dimmed, but Luo Zheng's own power had been exhausted in a few breaths!
"Plop!"
Just as he stood up, he suddenly fell to the ground!
Hearing the voice coming from behind, the warrior in front turned his head and glanced at Luo Zheng. He laughed dryly and walked toward the huge bonfires in the square without looking back.
The meaning behind that laughter was very obvious …
He was a newcomer and had only been here for a day. Plus, he was only at the Extreme Divine Realm. It was impossible for him to get up and eat meat.
Luo Zheng's face heavily hit the ground. If it wasn't for his head being as hard as a divine tool, he would have been injured. Even so, he still felt dizzy!
"I want to rely on my own strength!"
At this moment, a trace of understanding suddenly appeared in Luo Zheng's heart.
In this world, there were some strengths that did not belong to him, including Qinglong's strength! What he borrowed would always be borrowed, and these nine true dragons would leave him in the end!
The trial in front of him was indeed a kind of crazy torture, but in Luo Zheng's eyes, it was the best trial. If he could leave the Mountain Shattering Clan's territory easily one day, his strength and body would be raised to the extreme!
As for tonight, he was destined to not be able to eat meat.
The length of the night in the Forbidden Land of God Refinement varied. The first night lasted for about sixty-two hours, but tonight's night only lasted for fourteen hours.
The Mountain Shattering Clan members seemed to not know fatigue. They ate meat and drank wine under the bonfires. Just eating alone took them ten hours. The torture for Luo Zheng and Han Liusu also lasted for ten hours!
After they finished eating, all the martial artists, including Luo Zheng, were sweating profusely. It was as though they were about to collapse from exhaustion!
Many martial artists' souls were strong and did not need to sleep. However, in the Mountain Shattering Clan, they were constantly subjected to this strong gravitational force. Previously, they were also tortured by the Calamity Avoiding Incense. At this moment, they actually fell into a deep sleep!
Luo Zheng was also one of those who had fallen asleep!
They had just slept for four hours, and the sky was bright again!
Thus, accompanied by the Mountain Shattering Clan's singing, a new day began …
On the second day, Luo Zheng saw that "Uncle Toad" seducing the children to share the meat. Only then did he have some thoughts. No wonder the others were so envious of this "Uncle Toad" on the first day. Even Luo Zheng himself could not help but want to cheat some meat from the children to eat!
However, in the end, Luo Zheng still gave up.
This method was too strenuous. Moreover, the efficiency was not high. If he could stand up, he would naturally have a lot of meat to eat and a lot of wine to drink.
Luo Zheng was not in a hurry, but Han Liusu was anxious.
"Luo Zheng, you better think of a way to stand up quickly. I don't want to waste any more time here," Han Liusu said.
"This place … isn't it good?" Luo Zheng smiled and said, "If I could stand up, or even cultivate the terrifying strength of the Mountain Shattering Clan, it would be a huge improvement for me!"
Who knew that Han Liusu would snort coldly, "I don't need to cultivate my body. If I really needed pure physical strength, I wouldn't have come here!"
With her condition, there were many ways for her to cultivate. Being tortured here was probably the worst choice …
"But I need to cultivate my body," Luo Zheng replied.
Even if Luo Zheng could stand up, he would not choose to leave quickly!
"You …" Han Liusu suddenly became anxious. If she really wasted several years here, even if she was beaten to death, she would not be willing.
"You can find someone else to bring you out. With your status, you should be able to do it, right?" Luo Zheng said again. It was not that he was unwilling to help Han Liusu. Time may be precious to this woman, but it was also very precious to Luo Zheng!
According to what Luo Zheng said, she could indeed do it. In this tribe, other than the Mountain Crushing Tribe, there were many other tribes mixed in. Some of them even came from the Divine Realm. They also went out to hunt with the Mountain Crushing Tribe.
Han Liusu's status was special. If she entrusted someone else, they could naturally bring her out.
"But only you can bring me to the Soul Suppressing Cliff!" Han Liusu said with a pout.
She had chased after Luo Zheng all this time not because she had nothing to do but because of the prophecy of the Tianhun Clan. It was meaningless for her to leave the Mountain Shattering Clan alone. The most important thing was Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng shook his head slightly, "You've found the wrong person. I don't know what the Soul Suppressing Cliff is, and it's even more impossible for me to bring you there."
After hearing this, Han Liusu only glared at Luo Zheng resentfully and did not say anything else.
After thinking about it, she was not so anxious. This guy did not know the meaning of going to the Soul Suppressing Cliff. If He Zuowei and the others did not die, they would find the Mountain Shattering Clan sooner or later. At that time, she would have to think of other ways to persuade Luo Zheng. She might have to take this guy away by force.
The next day passed in a flash. At night, the same process was repeated. This was another round of torture.
The gravity here was very beneficial to Luo Zheng.
The gravity was constantly exerted on Luo Zheng's body, and it would continuously form Primordial Astral Winds. The next day, under the baptism of the Mountain Shattering Clan's battle song, the Primordial Astral Winds would flow evenly through his body again!
The only problem was that Luo Zheng's physical body had not been upgraded. It had not broken through its current divine artifact body.
Last time, he had swallowed Han Liusu's flame. Perhaps the flame he had swallowed was not enough, or the Primordial Astral Winds in his body was not enough. It could be both.
"You … can you condense that flame again?"
A week later, Luo Zheng felt that the Primordial Astral Winds had been accumulated enough. In the end, he still made this request to Han Liusu.
If his physical body was upgraded again, it would be greatly beneficial to him.
Previously, Han Liusu had already noticed the uniqueness of Luo Zheng's body. Not only was this guy not afraid of the strange flames on his body, but the countless tiny runes on his body were also continuously absorbing the strange flames. This guy must have cultivated a cultivation technique that required him to swallow strange flames.
In the end, he still had a favor to ask of her!
Her eyes narrowed, and her smile turned into a pair of small crescent moons. She laughed and said, "It would be my pleasure!"

Chapter 1481
"But I still have the same condition. Take me to the Soul Suppressing Cliff!"
Han Liusu had never thought that there would be a day when she would need to exchange conditions with others.
Moreover, the target of her exchange was a secondary life form — this was what the people of the God Domain called it.
Although the world created by the Saints was huge and could produce True Gods, these life forms relied on the rules set by the Saints to exist.
Once they left the Saints' inner world, they would return to True Essence and dissipate.
The so-called secondary life form was a lower life form. This wasn't a derogatory term but a description based on facts.
There were also weaker life forms and martial artists in the God Domain. They might not be a match for the secondary life form, but at least the life forms in the God Domain wouldn't return to normal and dissipate …
"What kind of place is the Soul Suppressing Cliff? Why is it so important to you?" Luo Zheng asked.
Han Liusu also knew that Luo Zheng didn't know much about the Forbidden Land of God Refinement. She could only slowly explain to Luo Zheng.
The entire Forbidden Land of God Refinement was a huge training ground!
The people of the God Domain and the elites of the universe all had the qualifications to be sent here to cultivate.
Outside the many birth grounds were all kinds of training grounds …
This time, after being sent to the Forbidden Land of God Refinement, whether it was Xi Youqin or Hua Tianming, they were still in their birth grounds. The training grounds around them were enough for them to cultivate.
As their strength gradually increased, the range of activities of martial artists would further increase and they would be able to enter the six sacred training grounds!
The Collapsing Mountain Clan was a body refining sacred ground.
Other than the Collapsing Mountain Clan, there was also the Myriad Elephant Clan's Annihilation Valley, the Yuan He Clan's Heart Refining Pagoda, the Sky Dome Clan's Beginning and End True Essence, etc.
No matter which training ground one entered, one would need the highest grade of training talisman. In the Forbidden Land of God Refinement, there were millions of martial artists working hard for these talismans …
"Since you have the talisman, why don't you go to the Soul Suppressing Cliff?" Luo Zheng asked curiously.
Han Liusu then said indifferently, "The Soul Suppressing Cliff … is not a training ground. It is the core of the Forbidden Land of God Refinement. It is above the six sacred training grounds."
The six sacred training grounds of the Forbidden Land of God Refinement were places that millions of martial artists yearned for. It was also the final destination of many martial artists!
Just like cultivating in the Landslide Clan, one could easily open the eighth level of Dao Pillars, and even open the Spiritual Pedestals … This was the best place to cultivate in the eyes of body refiners!
However, in the eyes of a secondary life form like Luo Zheng, these cultivation holy lands were indeed good places that were hard to come by. Currently, Luo Zheng had already made up his mind to become a member of the Collapsing Mountain Clan and cultivate the terrifying power of the Collapsing Mountain Clan here.
But to Han Liusu, a life form from the Realm of the Gods, the Mountain Crumbling Clan was nothing more than this.
In the Divine Domain, the place people yearned for the most was the depths of the Forbidden Land of God Refinement. There was the Soul Suppressing Cliff, the Paramita Sand, the Sunflower Water Ancestral Land …
In the eyes of the people in the Realm of the Gods, that was the real Forbidden Land of God Refinement!
Hearing this, Luo Zheng had a rough outline of the Forbidden Land of God Refinement. He started to plan.
The old patriarch had sent the ten Dao Children into the Forbidden Land of God Refinement. Their final destination was probably the same as the majority of the martial artists here, which was to enter the Six Great Trial Grounds to cultivate.
If one succeeded in cultivating in the Collapsing Mountain Race, they would at least be able to raise their strength to the Sector Lord level. If they were comparable to the leaders of the Collapsing Mountain Race, their strength would most likely be comparable to a Heavenly Venerable!
Then what about the Soul Suppressing Cliff? What benefits could one obtain there?
"What's in the Soul Suppressing Cliff?" Luo Zheng opened his mouth and asked. If the Soul Suppressing Cliff was beneficial to him, he didn't mind taking a trip there.
Han Liusu lay down on the ground and glanced at Luo Zheng. She then said, "That place can allow a life form to transform from a cocoon into a butterfly!"
"What do you mean?" Luo Zheng asked.
"If one day you can enter that place, you will naturally understand." Han Liusu's smile was quite mysterious.
Luo Zheng stared at Han Liusu in silence for several breaths of time. Finally, he nodded his head and said, "Deal!"
Using the strange fire on Han Liusu to refine his body was extremely urgent for Luo Zheng. However, there were many spies in the Collapsing Mountain Race. This was a problem …
The problem was that Luo Zheng's own movement was a problem. It was even more impossible for him to bring Han Liusu to another location.
After thinking about it for a while, he could only make do with it.
Even Han Liusu couldn't see the origin of Luo Zheng's weapon refining technique. Normal people shouldn't be able to see it either. Furthermore, the Collapsing Mountain Race was a native of the Forbidden Land of God Refinement. They didn't seem to be interested in things like cultivation techniques from the outside world …
After making his decision, Luo Zheng activated all the energy in his body!
Under the strong gravitational force, the amount of Primordial Astral Nova in Luo Zheng's body was extremely shocking.
Strictly speaking, the Primordial Astral Nova should also be a type of energy.
Just like when a weapon refiner was refining a weapon, the energy would seep into the weapon through hammering. Most of the energy would spread out with the weapon, but a small amount of energy would remain.
At this moment, Luo Zheng wanted to borrow Han Liusu's strange fire to refine his body!
Under the continuous flow of the dragon scale energy, Luo Zheng slowly stood up against the strong gravitational force and crawled towards Han Liusu …
After reaching Han Liusu's side, Luo Zheng was a bit troubled.
Han Liusu turned her face and saw Luo Zheng's conflicted expression. She guessed what Luo Zheng was conflicted about. "You … just lie down with me and hold my hand."
"En."
Luo Zheng had only crawled for less than five feet and had already used up most of his energy. Turning over like this, he had squeezed out the last bit of energy in his body!
With a "putong" sound, he fell heavily beside Han Liusu.
At this moment, Luo Zheng had used up all of his energy. He couldn't even move his fingers. After lying down on the ground and resting for a while, Luo Zheng felt that his energy had recovered slightly. He used his strength to slowly move his arm and finally grabbed onto Han Liusu's cold and smooth jade hand.
Being grabbed by Luo Zheng like this, Han Liusu's heart slightly jumped and she couldn't help but shrink back.
In this week, the Adversity Avoiding Incense had confused everyone's nerves every day. What it stimulated was the hunger in their hearts. However, another rather primitive desire was also aroused. It was just that it was suppressed by the powerful hunger. Suddenly being grabbed by Luo Zheng, she was also flustered.
However, retracting her hand was just her subconscious reaction. The reality was that she didn't have the strength to resist the gravitational force and move her hand.
"Are you ready?" Han Liusu blinked her eyes and asked.
Luo Zheng nodded his head.
"Let's begin!" Han Liusu smiled slightly.
Every time this strange strange fire ignited on her body, it was the beginning of a nightmare for her. She didn't expect that someone would eat her strange fire!
"Hua!"
The blue flame swept over and suddenly spread towards Luo Zheng's body!
This flame continued to sweep over Luo Zheng's body without stopping for a moment.
"What's going on?"
"What's wrong with that kid?"
Some cultivators were attracted by the commotion and had different expressions at this moment.
Luo Zheng's body emitted golden runes, and under the constant rotation, began to greedily absorb the flame.
The two people's clasped hands were like a bridge that transmitted the flame. The blue flame burned along the bridge and then was constantly absorbed by the rotating golden runes.
After reaching adulthood, Han Liusu still couldn't freely control this strange strange fire. She could release this strange fire at any time, but she couldn't stop it at any time.
Her pair of beautiful eyes, under the reflection of the blue strange fire, were suffused with a faint blue light.
"Luo Zheng … are you okay?" Han Liusu asked. At this moment, Luo Zheng was motionless, so she couldn't help but be a little worried.
"I'm fine," Luo Zheng, who was already a ball of fire, replied.
"Can you absorb more?"
"Yes!"
"Hualala …"
Fierce flames soared into the sky, and the tongue of the flame rushed several feet into the sky!
This fierce fire quickly attracted the attention of the children in the tribe. The people of the Collapsing Mountain Tribe were originally strong, brave, and fearless. They were a tribe that was born to fight, but this didn't mean that they were reckless. Otherwise, the Collapsing Mountain Tribe wouldn't have become one of the six great cultivation holy lands in this harsh environment.
Although these children were curious, they also looked at the scene in front of them from afar and didn't dare to approach. Their eyes revealed fear.

Chapter 1482
The life of the Collapsing Mountain Clan was to work as the sun rose and rest as the sun set …
At this time, most of the adult males had already gone out to hunt, but there were also some elders and adult males who stayed behind to guard the village.
The child of the Collapsing Mountain Clan called over an elder of the clan.
This elder was as thin as firewood, and the white beard at the side of his mouth drooped down. It was more than ten feet long and looked like a bamboo pole.
Even so, this elder of the Collapsing Mountain Clan walked as if he was flying under this powerful gravitational force, not showing any signs of aging.
Although Luo Zheng was absorbing the Strange Flame, his mind was still very clear. When he saw this elder of the Collapsing Mountain Clan arrive, he was also secretly on guard.
This Collapsing Mountain Clan didn't seem to have any reason. If he offended the other party, it would be bad.
The old man was slightly startled when he saw this flame. A trace of fear was revealed in his eyes. It was as if he was extremely afraid of this flame. However, when he saw Luo Zheng crazily absorbing the Heavenly Flame, a strange expression was revealed in his old eyes!
"Kaji, Sorado …"
It was unknown what the elder said, but he quickly left.
Soon, a large group of old men suddenly appeared in the Collapsing Mountain Clan …
Before this, Luo Zheng hadn't discovered that there were so many elders in the Collapsing Mountain Clan. But now, there were 50 to 60 elders surrounding Luo Zheng!
A large group of old men, who were as tall as bamboo poles, gathered in front of Luo Zheng, mumbling something that Luo Zheng could not understand.
Some of the more mischievous children watched for a while. They probably felt that the flame on Luo Zheng's body wasn't that big of a threat, so they took a stick and wanted to start the fire. But soon, they were grabbed by the elders and thrown away.
"Crack …"
"Haruoyi smash!"
"Keke!"
As they spoke, the voices of these elders became louder and louder. When the Collapsing Mountain Clan members roared, it was truly earth-shattering.
The ears of the Divine Transformation Realm martial artists lying on the ground were also ringing. If these elders roared at a mountain like this, they might even cause the mountain to collapse.
Within the flames, Han Liusu and Luo Zheng, who were lying on the ground while holding hands, exchanged glances with each other. Their faces revealed an inexplicable expression. They were completely unable to understand the language of the Collapsing Mountain clan. However, they did not know why they were arguing endlessly!
"Crack crack!"
At this moment, the elder at the front of the group suddenly waved the walking stick in his hand.
This elder had a dignified expression. He seemed to have a high status in the Collapsing Mountain Clan. With a wave of his walking stick, all sounds instantly disappeared, and the scene fell into silence!
The old man walked over with his walking stick and asked Luo Zheng sternly, "What's your name?"
At this moment, Luo Zheng's Body Tempering was nearing its end, and his body's thirst for fire had basically been satisfied. However, Han Liusu was still releasing a steady stream of Deviant Fire. She still couldn't control it as she wished …
Hearing the old man's words, Luo Zheng's heart skipped a beat. It seemed like the old man was not friendly. Had he really broken some taboo? At this moment, he could only answer truthfully, "I am Luo Zheng."
"Luo Zheng? Hehe, what a good name … "Contrary to Luo Zheng's expectations, the elder's expression and tone took a 180-degree turn, suddenly becoming incomparably amiable.
Even Han Liusu was baffled. Logically speaking, shouldn't he be more interested in her Deviant Flame? Why did this elder only pay attention to Luo Zheng?
However, this elder only remembered Luo Zheng's name. Then, he bid his farewells and led the group of elders away …
It was unknown what they were planning. In any case, Luo Zheng and Han Liusu discussed for a moment but couldn't come up with an answer.
Two hours later, Han Liusu's Strange Strange Flame was finally finished. The flame continued to burn without being replenished. The flames on Luo Zheng's body began to extinguish!
After the flame was extinguished, not even a speck of dust was left on Luo Zheng's clothes.
Han Liusu let out an "ah" sound and then closed her eyes … Her clothes were sewn from the Realm of the Gods' extremely precious Water Thread. This Water Thread wasn't a material, but a special kind of light. However, this kind of light had a sense of touch and could be used to sew clothes. It was extremely popular in the Realm of the Gods!
Of course, the production cost was also extremely high. This was because this Water Thread was created by changing part of the rules of "light". Those who understood this method weren't ordinary people.
Clothes made from Water Thread wouldn't be stained with dust or filth. Even after countless years, they would still be as shiny as new!
As a girl, every time the Strange Flame flared up, all of her clothes would be burned away. She would be completely naked. That was indeed indecent. In order to help her get this set of clothes, her brother had spent a lot of effort …
But how could Luo Zheng have this kind of thing?
Every time he was burned by the Strange Flame, his entire body would be burned away. It was indeed indecent. Luo Zheng also felt very embarrassed.
Previously, Luo Zheng had been burned by the Strange Flame once, but he was still able to quickly change into a new set of clothes.
Moreover, at this moment, it was difficult for him to move. Wearing clothes while he was unable to move was a difficult matter. However, with a slight movement of his finger, he activated the spatial ring on his finger. From it, he took out a blanket. Then, he used his true essence to transform this blanket over his body …
The two of them laid down like this. In this awkward atmosphere, there was nothing to say. However, Luo Zheng actually carefully withdrew his hand. Holding onto a girl's hand like this really wasn't appropriate.
However, Han Liusu didn't know why, but there was a slight sense of loss in her heart.
After this Strange Flame Body Tempering, Luo Zheng's body transmitted a very unique feeling!
Previously, every time he tempered his body, Luo Zheng felt that the strength of his body had increased several times. It was as if he had been reborn and obtained a brand new body.
However, this time, Luo Zheng had a strange feeling. It was as if every muscle and every bone in his body was under his control … Human muscles were driven by nerves, and nerves gathered in the spine. Then, following the spine, they gathered in the brain and were controlled by one's soul!
However, there were always some tissues in the body that didn't have nerves. Therefore, no matter how he tempered his body, it was impossible to control every muscle. For example, a person couldn't control their heart to sway left and right. It was also impossible to make their stomach freely expand and contract.
However, Luo Zheng discovered that he seemed to be able to do it!
This strange ability made Luo Zheng feel very strange. He also felt that he was developing more and more in the direction of a monster …
However, because he couldn't move, Luo Zheng couldn't fully verify it. As for how strong his body had become after absorbing these Strange Flames, Luo Zheng couldn't determine it himself. He could only find an opportunity in the future.
Fortunately, after this Body Tempering, Luo Zheng clearly felt that his body's strength had increased greatly. Even his strength had increased by a lot. With the help of the dragon scales, Luo Zheng should be getting closer and closer to the day when he could crawl back up completely.
After nightfall, it was the most difficult time of the day.
As the Adversity Avoiding Fragrance filled the air, the roasted meat of the Mountain-Crushing Tribe also began to emit a delicious aroma.
Luo Zheng was prepared to tense up and face this cruel test. However, at this moment, two Mountain-Crushing Tribe members walked up to Luo Zheng.
After these two Mountain-Crushing Tribe members muttered a few words, they pulled Luo Zheng up from the ground!
"What are you doing?" Luo Zheng asked curiously.
The two Mountain-Crushing Tribe members didn't answer. They just lifted him up and walked towards the tribe's huge plaza.
Luo Zheng swayed left and right in the air. His gaze constantly swept around. Only then did he realize that many Mountain-Crushing Tribe members were looking at him with strange gazes!
He couldn't help but swallow his saliva. He realized that he was getting closer and closer to those huge bonfires. His heart was resisting. They wouldn't dismember him and eat him right?
The Mountain-Crushing Tribe's diet was too rich. All sorts of powerful Sector Lord level beasts and even all sorts of rare poisonous bugs were eaten by them. It wouldn't be strange if they ate him …
Just as he was thinking this, the two Mountain-Crushing Tribe members carried him in front of a bonfire.
The flames of the Adversity Avoiding Wood were also very fierce. Normal flames couldn't cook the meat of a Sector Lord level beast. When he was still a hundred feet away from the bonfire, Luo Zheng could already feel the scorching heat waves!
When he reached the bonfire, Luo Zheng felt two huge forces shake him. His entire body fell into the bonfire just like that!

Chapter 1483
The fire from the Avoiding Calamity Wood was powerful, but it couldn't cause any harm to Luo Zheng's body.

"Pi li pa la …"
His body pressed down like this, the dry tree trunk below continued to collapse, finally Luo Zheng was covered by the burning red charcoal!
Many Mountain Crumbling Tribe members gathered together, observing Luo Zheng's movements under the bonfire!
Obviously, these Mountain Crumbling Tribe members already knew that Luo Zheng had the ability to resist the strange fire, and now they were using Luo Zheng as an experiment.
After about half an incense's time, someone removed the charcoal from Luo Zheng's body, and after seeing that Luo Zheng was unharmed, they pulled him out and threw him to the side!

At the same time, this huge plaza seemed to be divided into levels.
All the bonfires were set up according to the distribution of the blood grooves, the Mountain Crumbling Tribe members were all gathered in the innermost layer, and beside the dozens of bonfires outside, there were many warriors from other races.
