As for Luo Zheng, he was thrown into the huge bonfire in the center of the plaza.

A muscular Mountain Crumbling Tribe member said to Luo Zheng.
Under such gravity, sitting down was still a test for Luo Zheng.
However, Luo Zheng had just succeeded in forging his body, and his physical strength and strength had increased by a lot. With the help of the dragon scales, he struggled to sit up, but Luo Zheng didn't dare to move at all. Sitting like this, he was already on the verge of collapse, and it seemed that he would fall down again if he moved even a little bit.
That burly warrior from the Mountain Shattering Tribe had thick hair on his chest. On his shoulder was a golden shoulder guard, and on the shoulder guard was an exquisite carving of a wolf's head! There were three sharp thorns on the wolf's head, but Luo Zheng couldn't tell what kind of wolf it was.

"Pa pa!"
With a heavy clap of his hands, a member of the Collapsing Mountain Clan brought over a huge plate. On this plate were two huge hearts. This heart was even taller than the seated Luo Zheng, but it was unknown what kind of strange beast it was taken from.

What was more amazing was that this heart was still beating slightly. Luo Zheng could even see light green blood flowing out from the wound on the heart!
What kind of beast's heart was this? After being dismembered and roasted, the heart was still beating?
"Clang dang!"
The Mountain Crumbling Race's clansman took the plate and smashed it heavily in front of Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng was originally a little unsteady, and the air current created by the plate immediately pushed him back!
"Plop!"

Another burst of laughter came from around the bonfire. In the distance, there were some children from the Mountain Crumbling Race laughing. They probably felt that Luo Zheng's action was very funny.
However, the Mountain Crumbling Race's clansman raised his hand and made a "shushing" gesture. "Outsider, you can stand up at the Divine Realm. You're the first!"
Hearing this, everyone stopped mocking him.
The Mountain Crumbling Race's clansmen had a special physique. While they were in the stomach, they had to withstand the abnormal gravitational pull. Thus, they were able to move freely here.
As for the martial artists that entered the Forbidden Land of Divine Refinement, those that obtained a top smelting talisman were all outstanding talents. Nearly all of them had a Divine Transformation realm cultivation. Reaching the Divine Realm was extremely rare, and no one had ever been able to crawl up from the Divine Realm. After all, there were a considerable number of Divine Transformation realm martial artists that couldn't even crawl up.

The Mountain Crumbling Race's clansman grabbed one of the hearts and threw it at Luo Zheng …
"Evil dragon heart, eat it!" The person said.

The scorching hot Sin Dragon's heart smashed towards Luo Zheng!

As the Mountain Crumbling Race's clansman saw Luo Zheng use such a method to resist the impact, a light flashed in his eyes, revealing a trace of appreciation.
Luo Zheng held the heart in his arms. Without caring about anything else, or whether it tasted good or not, he buried his head in the heart and opened his mouth wide as he began to chew on it!
The Crossing Calamity Incense had been tossing and turning for a long time. Luo Zheng's hunger was like a sharp knife, tormenting him.
At this moment, he took a bite and wolfed it down, trying to fill himself up in the shortest time possible.
This Evil Dragon's heart looked a little scary. Lying on top of this huge heart, Luo Zheng could still feel its violent pulsation. After swallowing the heart, he suddenly felt a burning sensation in his stomach. This strange feeling was instantly transmitted to Luo Zheng's body, filling every inch of his body with strength!
"This Evil Dragon's heart actually has such an effect!"
Luo Zheng didn't stop. No matter what he was chewing, he just closed his eyes and swallowed it!
The Mountain Crumbling Tribe's blade technique was extremely powerful. Even though the heart was cut off, the blood within was not completely drained. The green blood splattered out and Luo Zheng forcefully swallowed it! Translator: Atlas Studios Editor: Atlas Studios The Mountain Crumbling Tribe's blade technique was extremely powerful.
The Mountain Crumbling Race's people lived a very pure life. In their opinion, the potential of a warrior depended on whether he could eat or not.
For the warriors of the outside world, it didn't matter whether they could eat or not. After cultivating to a certain level, they could directly abstain from eating and didn't need to eat. However, in the Mountain Crumbling Race, whether they could eat or not was almost equivalent to their talent!
After a while, Luo Zheng had eaten the huge Evil Dragon's heart clean!
"Hehe, good appetite!"
The Mountain Crumbling Race's man opposite him smiled slightly. He grabbed a wine bag next to him and threw it to Luo Zheng!

On the contrary, the Mountain Crumbling Race's man opposite him and the other people of the Mountain Crumbling Race were quietly staring at Luo Zheng, quietly staring at the wine bag that was getting smaller and smaller …
The Mountain Crumbling Race's man sitting opposite Luo Zheng was the new patriarch of the Mountain Crumbling Race, Lei Toad!
The Mountain Crumbling Race's people loved to drink. With their physiques, ordinary strong wine was like clear water. Thus, the warriors who had stayed in the Forbidden Land of God Refinement for a long time knew that if they had a good method of brewing wine, they could exchange it for a Trial Talisman from the Mountain Crumbling Race.
These years, the Mountain Crumbling Race had indeed mastered many strange methods of brewing wine. For example, the Frost Wine made from the Nine Nether Cold Spring. The wine was like white frost, and when it was poured, the surrounding area would be frozen. There was also the Pure Flame Wine made from the Fire Snake Fruit. Once it was lit, it could last for ten years!
However, the most precious thing was the wine bag next to the patriarch of the Mountain Crumbling Race. It was called the Heart Severing Wine. It was said that a person from the God Domain had given the Mountain Crumbling Race the method of brewing wine for free. It was made from the essence blood of various powerful beasts.
The strength of this wine surpassed one's imagination!
In the eyes of others, this wasn't wine at all, but the most potent poison.
Even if it was a Divine Transformation realm martial artist, they would need to use all of their strength to refine this wine for their own use. If they were even the slightest bit careless, they would die from being pierced through.
And this was only a small sip!
Even Lei Toad himself only dared to drink a single mouthful of this wine every day.
Firstly, with the strength of this wine, even he couldn't withstand it. Secondly, this wine was brewed from the essence blood of a hundred beasts. Even within the Mountain Crumbling Race, it was extremely precious. Even the members of the Mountain Crumbling Race rarely had the chance to drink it.
Thus, at this time, Lei Toad had an extremely conflicted expression. Luo Zheng's drinking method truly made his heart ache.

Chapter 1484
The members of the Mountain Crumbling Tribe were known for their generosity. Lei Tou was distressed, but he couldn't say anything.

As for the other members of the Mountain Crumbling Tribe, they looked as if they wanted to swallow Luo Zheng!
At this moment, Luo Zheng's eyes were red. He felt as if there were countless nails in his body that were crazily moving around, stimulating every nerve in his body.
Under this stimulation, his body also underwent a miraculous transformation.
In fact, entering the Mountain Crumbling Tribe under the high pressure of the gravity was a kind of cultivation!
Therefore, some warriors would choose to cultivate under a big waterfall when they first cultivated their body. Under the constant pressure of the waterfall, the body would undergo a transformation in the process of resisting this pressure.
After entering the Mountain Crumbling Tribe, this trial had already begun.
Although the rare birds and beasts that the Mountain Crumbling Tribe ate were not refined into elixirs, the effects were not to be underestimated.
In particular, the Evil Dragon's Heart was very rare. Ordinary members of the Mountain Crumbling Tribe didn't have the fortune to enjoy it. At this moment, after Luo Zheng swallowed it, it was also very beneficial to him!

Luo Zheng wasn't a fool. How could he not see that ordinary people couldn't get such treatment?

According to Luo Zheng's judgment, the problem must be with his body. It might even be related to his body's resistance to fire. Otherwise, they wouldn't have thrown him into the bonfire.

Their Collapsing Mountain Clan did not reject outsiders, but it was not so easy for ordinary foreign martial artists to obtain the protection of the Collapsing Mountain Clan. Most of the martial artists here relied on their own strength to fight for a piece of the pie in the Collapsing Mountain Clan.
If they put all their efforts into nurturing Luo Zheng, not to mention that Luo Zheng's talent was originally very strong, even if Luo Zheng's talent was ordinary, they were confident that they could help Luo Zheng quickly improve!
Luo Zheng looked calm on the surface, but inside, he was extremely excited.

If Luo Zheng was willing to help them, if he helped them, they would do everything in their power to repay this favor!

That night, Luo Zheng's appetite was also opened, and he crazily devoured all kinds of meat …
Every piece of meat contained a different kind of power. Those strange beasts were at least comparable to Sector Lords, and some were even comparable to Heavenly Venerables. However, they were still hunted down and killed by the Mountain Crushing Race.

The Mountain Crushing Race naturally understood this logic, but they did not lack people who hunted strange beasts, and this Forbidden Land of God Refinement did not lack strange beasts either. They did not care about this kind of waste, and their ancestors had used this kind of method to cultivate. This had already become their tradition, and it had created such an extremely valiant race.

What they did not know was that Luo Zheng had only been in the Forbidden Land of God Refinement for less than half a month …

Luo Zheng leaned against the wall, and at this moment, his body was still burning with pain. After meeting Han Liusu's dissatisfied gaze, he smiled, and with a light flick of his finger, a piece of meat rolled to Han Liusu's side.

While he was eating and drinking, he had also secretly hidden some food in his spatial ring.

Chapter 1485

Han Liusu used Strange Fire to help him refine his body. Luo Zheng also agreed to her request. If there was a chance, he would go to the Soul Suppressing Cliff in her place. Even though he didn't know where that place was.

Night fell and the bonfire rose.
Luo Zheng once again went to the plaza to eat heartily! The Mountain Shattering Race's cultivation seemed to be this simple and casual. The more they ate, the faster their strength grew.
Almost every day, Luo Zheng would eat three times his own weight in food!
This kind of appetite was already very shocking for ordinary martial artists. After all, the capacity of the stomach was limited. In a limited amount of time, they had to use their own strength to digest as much food as possible. This was an indispensable ability for the Mountain Shattering Race.
Compared to other martial artists, Luo Zheng's appetite was shocking. However, compared to the Mountain Shattering Race, he was somewhat lacking.
The amount of meat that a child of the Mountain Shattering Race could eat was almost five times that of Luo Zheng. As for the adult Mountain Shattering Race, the amount of meat that they could eat in one meal was almost equivalent to a small mountain …
Such a terrifying digestive ability created the Mountain Shattering Race's terrifying corporeal body and unimaginable strength. It was also the foundation of this race.
On the other side, He Zuowei saw that Luo Zheng was actually qualified to eat in front of the largest bonfire in the plaza. They looked at each other in dismay. If they didn't remember wrongly, only Han Cangyan was qualified to eat here. That was where the leader of the Mountain Shattering Race ate.
From this, it seemed that the Mountain Shattering Race wasn't lying. This Luo Zheng seemed to be really important to the Mountain Shattering Race.
He Zuowei and the others were all thinking about Han Liusu. They naturally knew that the Calamity Avoiding Incense was very torturous. Right now, they needed to satisfy the Eldest Princess's appetite.
Actually, in their opinion, although these foods contained the meat of mutated beasts, they still weren't qualified to enter Han Liusu's mouth. However, they were currently living under someone else's roof. They had no way to find better food for Han Liusu. They could only make do …
"I'm not eating!"
"Get away from me!"
"Scram far away!"
Unexpectedly, when He Zuowei and the others brought several relatively delicate pieces of meat to Han Liusu, Han Liusu directly drove them away.
"But Eldest Princess, this Calamity Avoiding Incense can stimulate your appetite a hundred times. Do you really not want to eat it?" He Zuowei had a strange expression. He didn't believe that Han Liusu could resist the hunger in her stomach under the effects of the Calamity Avoiding Incense. Not to mention the Eldest Princess, even He Zuowei couldn't do it.
"I'm not eating! You just need to take care of yourselves! "Han Liusu coldly said.
Not too far away, the martial artists lying on the ground suddenly burst into tears as they heard Han Liusu's words. Even in their dreams, they wanted to take a bite. How could this woman waste such a heavenly treasure!
He Zuowei was very puzzled, but since the Eldest Princess insisted on not eating, they could only withdraw. In their hearts, they felt more and more strange. Could it be that the Eldest Princess could resist the effects of the Calamity Avoiding Incense?
However, not long after, He Zuowei saw Luo Zheng slowly walk over step by step. He took out pieces of meat and tore them into pieces before feeding them to the eldest princess. He Zuowei immediately understood what was going on.

Chapter 1486
He Zuowei had a heavy responsibility to bring the Eldest Princess into the Forbidden Land of God Refinement.
Due to the uniqueness of the Forbidden Land of God Refinement, even people from the Divine Realm would enter to cultivate.
All the famous sects in the Divine Realm had branches in the Forbidden Land of God Refinement. Some of the big cities were built by people from the Divine Realm.
Han Jia was no exception.
The "Han City" that was 150 million miles away from the Mountain Shattering Race occupied a place of birth. It was a big city in the Forbidden Land of God Refinement that could not be ignored.
However, the Saint Emperor was still worried. Therefore, he sent He Zuowei and the others to ensure the Eldest Princess' safety.
When they encountered the Formless Sky Puppet, He Zuowei was scared half to death.
In order to save Han Liusu, they were going all out.
The price of using the Great Ambition was terrifying!
Once one made a great wish, one would have to fulfill it in this lifetime. If one was unable to fulfill it, not only would a huge hole appear in one's heart of martial arts, one's cultivation in this lifetime would be directly crippled at a critical moment!
Unless it was a life and death enemy, they would not use the Great Ambition.
Now that they made a Great Ambition to the Formless Sky Puppet and were unable to fulfill it, how big of an impact would it have on He Zuowei? However, He Zuowei was clear that if the Eldest Princess really died, they would also die. Although the Formless Sky Puppet was terrifying, there would still be a chance to fulfill it in the future...
Fortunately, the Eldest Princess was still unharmed.
But now, He Zuowei saw some bad signs. It could even be said that it was a very dangerous sign.
The Eldest Princess seemed to have a good impression of that secondary life form?
"He Wen, what do you think?" He Zuowei asked.
"What do you mean?" He Wen was confused. He did not seem to understand He Zuowei's words.
"You..."
He Zuowei also knew that these fellows were trying to distance themselves from him.
They knew how important the Eldest Princess was to the Saint Emperor. The key was that the Eldest Princess was engaged...
He Zuowei was at a loss. He wanted to go up and persuade them, but He Wen stopped him. "Boss He, some things will turn into ashes in the blink of an eye if they get involved."
"But..." He Zuowei was hesitant.
"There are some things that the Eldest Princess can't decide. That brat is just a second-level creature and is nothing to worry about. Naturally, there will be people to deal with him. As servants, we can just pretend we don't know anything," He Wen said indifferently.
In God's Domain, people like He Zuowei and He Wen were nothing more than small fries. There were some things that they simply couldn't manage. Staying out of it was the best choice. The only thing they could do was to ensure the absolute safety of the Eldest Princess. As for the rest, they could only act as if they didn't see anything.
This was the most basic form of cultivation, and it was a daily cycle.
Luo Zheng's strength was also growing day by day. In the beginning, Luo Zheng had a hard time moving around in the Mountain Crumbling Tribe, but as time passed, Luo Zheng felt that the gravitational force was getting weaker and weaker. This meant that his strength was getting stronger and stronger!
Actually, when ordinary martial artists were in the Collapsing Mountain Clan, as long as they crawled back up, their improvement would be relatively fast.
Moreover, Luo Zheng was receiving the best treatment. In order to allow Luo Zheng to quickly grow, the Collapsing Mountain Clan gave Luo Zheng much more than ordinary martial artists.
Every day at dawn, he would receive the baptism of battle songs. At sunset, he would eat all kinds of powerful vicious beasts. This was the most primitive and most effective way of training.
Three months later, Luo Zheng was already able to resist the intense gravitational force. He could walk freely in the Collapsing Mountain Clan. If he clenched his teeth, he could even walk as if he was flying. Unfortunately, the gravitational force in the Collapsing Mountain Clan was too strong. Although Luo Zheng knew that his strength had increased by a lot, he was unable to verify just how strong this strength was...
Besides Luo Zheng, the other Dao Children in the universe were also diligently cultivating in various parts of the Forbidden Land of God Refinement.
In the Reverent King City that was 15 million miles away from the Collapsing Mountain Clan.
Xuanyuan Chenfeng defeated his last opponent, and another red Trial Talisman appeared in his hand.
In these few months, he had already obtained 36 red Trial Talismans...
There were three trial grounds around the Reverent King City, and one could enter them by paying the red Trial Talisman. However, to Hua Tianming, such trial grounds were meaningless.
"I still need 64 more … At this stage, my improvement should be the fastest, right? I wonder how Luo Zheng is doing now? "
When he accumulated 100 talismans, he could find someone to exchange for a purple Trial Talisman. At that time, he would be able to enter the Destruction Wind Valley to cultivate. It was said that the Destruction Wind Valley was one of the six great trial grounds, and that was the final destination of his trip to the Forbidden Land of God Refinement.
After keeping the red Trial Talisman, he stepped onto the stage again and looked at the many opponents below the stage.
"Who else is willing to challenge me!?"
…
200 million miles away, Emerald Forest.
This was a beautiful and enchanting forest. Whether it was the trees or the weeds, they all looked as if they were carved out of emerald.
However, Hua Tianming was not in the mood to appreciate such a scenery.
His figure was like a shuttle, shuttling back and forth rapidly. He was as agile as a ray of light, and it was hard to capture his figure with one's eyes.
"Sou!"
With a flash of sword light, a grey spirit musk fell to the ground.
As his figure floated, he took the spirit musk into his Sumeru Ring.
This spirit musk was an extremely agile mutated beast. Whether it was its fur or its body, it was very valuable. It was worth at least 15 green Trial Talismans. But the most valuable part was the spirit musk's eyes. When this kind of creature fled in panic, its eyes would turn a beautiful deep green. This was a reaction caused by fear.
The greener the spirit musk's eyes, the more valuable they were.
Therefore, when Hua Tianming found a spirit musk, he would not kill it. He would let it run for a long time before killing it with a single sword strike!
This method was very inhumane, but he had come to the Forbidden Land of God Refinement to improve. In order to obtain a purple Trial Talisman and obtain the qualifications to enter the Heart Refining Pagoda, he had no choice but to do this.
"Still a bit off, but it's faster than I expected." Hua Tianming stood under a jade tree.
"Roar!"
Suddenly, a roar sounded, causing Hua Tianming's heart to tremble. He quickly hid. This Emerald Forest did not only have spirit musk. There were many powerful existences that Hua Tianming could not fight against.
…
Eternal City.
Xi Youqin was bored to death as she strolled around the streets.
The Forbidden Land of God Refinement was not a good place to travel. The key was that Luo Zheng was not by her side, so she had no confidence in finding Luo Zheng.
It was said that when night fell, the outside of the city would become extremely dangerous …
"I wonder where Luo Zheng is," Xi Youqin muttered.
"Little girl!"
At this moment, a voice suddenly floated from the side.
Xi Youqin turned her head and saw a black-clothed and masked martial artist. His appearance was very strange, but from his appearance, he was at least in human form. During this time, Xi Youqin had seen many strange creatures …
"What are you doing?" Xi Youqin asked.
"Coincidentally, I feel a unique aura from your body, hehe." The masked martial artist gave a dry laugh.
Xi Youqin glared at the man, a vigilant look on her face. Aside from the Illusory Battlefield, Xi Youqin had never trained alone. Facing this kind of situation, she did not know how to deal with it. However, she was not stupid. It was best not to provoke this guy of unknown origin.
Just as Xi Youqin was about to leave, the masked martial artist flashed in front of her and blocked her path.
"What are you doing?" Xi Youqin coldly scolded. She did not believe that this guy would dare to make a move in Eternal City.
"Hehe, little girl is very vigilant. But do you recognize this?" On the back of the masked martial artist, four sword shadows slowly appeared. Then, the four swords suddenly floated and formed a sword array in a unique way.
Seeing the four swords and the way they were arranged, Xi Youqin's expression changed greatly. Although this was just a starting position, Xi Youqin was very familiar with it.
"God Slaying Array, who are you?"
…
Luo Xi Xuan, Ku Deng, Ji Luoxue …
All the Dao Children were constantly cultivating in the Forbidden Land of God Refinement, striving to break through as quickly as possible. They all had their own opportunities and dangers.
In the blink of an eye, half a year had passed.
Now, Luo Zheng had completely adapted to the gravity of the Mountain Shattering Clan as if it did not exist.
"When Big Brother entered the Mountain Shattering Clan, it also took him a year and a half to be able to move freely …"
Han Liusu also felt that this was somewhat inconceivable. Not only her, but He Zuowei and the others were also very surprised. They found it hard to believe that a second-level creature could completely adapt to the gravity in just half a year without relying on external forces.
＝＝＝＝＝

Chapter 1487
For Luo Zheng to be able to improve so quickly, the Mountain Crushing Tribe's nurturing had contributed greatly.
However, the most important factor was the strength of Luo Zheng's physical body.
Although Luo Zheng had never tested how strong his physical body was, according to his experience of leveling up, his physical body had already reached the level of a supreme artifact.
Ordinary martial artists who entered the Mountain Crushing Tribe to refine their bodies were divided into two parts.
One part was to refine their bodies, and the other part was to refine their powers.
The Mountain Crushing Tribe was the source of their powers. Staying here, one's powers would increase at a relatively fast speed.
But many martial artists would reach a stagnant state when they reached a certain level.
Because their powers were too strong, their physical bodies couldn't bear it.
Back then, when Luo Zheng used Qinglong's powers, he also encountered such a bottleneck. Hence, even though he had a strong power, he couldn't fully utilize it.
Now, Luo Zheng didn't have such a bottleneck. Naturally, he would improve rapidly.
After half a year of contact, Luo Zheng had a rough understanding of the Mountain Crushing Tribe. He even had a rough understanding of the living beings in the God Refinement Sacred Ground.
The biggest dream of ordinary martial artists in the universe was to cross the universe, escape the Heaven Dao's reincarnation, and enter the Deity Domain. If they could bear the Heaven's Will and become a Heavenly Lord, they wouldn't have lived in vain. These were probably the two greatest dreams that all martial artists in the universe pursued.
It was also these dreams that urged them to reach the peak step by step.
And the structure of the universe was very stable. From the Lower Realm to the Upper Realm, from the Xiantian Realm to the Heavenly Lord, step by step, it whipped martial artists from one level to another.
But the Forbidden Land of God Refinement was completely different.
The Forbidden Land of God Refinement was very different from the universe constructed by ordinary Sages!
Firstly, there was no Heaven Dao here. All kinds of powers were very chaotic here. It could even be said to be simple and violent …
Just like body refinement, it was very difficult to cultivate such a strong power in the universe. For example, there were at least 13 people in the Mountain Crushing Tribe who were stronger than a Heavenly Lord. As for the Thunder Toad's power, it was bottomless.
Other than that, the entire Mountain Crushing Tribe's power was comparable to a Sector Lord, including the newborn babies of this tribe! At least these infants didn't feel much pressure from the gravity. However, ordinary sector lords couldn't move freely in the face of such strong gravity.
This was the miraculous effect of the Source of Power. The Forbidden Land of God Refinement compressed all the treasures of the entire universe into this huge cave and then used a crude method to distribute them to the living beings inside.
But what was the goal of the Forbidden Land of God Refinement?
Just like the Collapsing Mountain Clan, the Sky Dome Clan, and the Myriad Elephant Clan, what was the purpose of their existence? Could it be that he was just going to work for others and nurture martial artists from other universes or the Realm of the Gods?
The answer to these questions could only be asked by the owner of the Forbidden Land of God Refinement. What was his purpose for constructing the Forbidden Land of God Refinement?
But who was their master?
So far, there was no answer.
The Collapsing Mountain Clan, the Sky Dome Clan, the Myriad Elephant Clan, and the Yuanhe Clan were all searching for this answer.
From the information revealed by the Thunder Toad, the place they wanted Luo Zheng to enter was called the Smelting Flame Lair. The thing they wanted him to take out was related to the master of the Forbidden Land of God Refinement.
Today, Luo Zheng sat across from the Collapsing Mountain Clan's Thunder Toad as usual. They ate meat and drank wine …
"Bang!"
A huge silver plate smashed in front of Luo Zheng.
"Eat this," the Thunder Toad said indifferently.
The thing on the silver plate was a kind of golden pupa. This pupa had already been roasted, but it was unknown what kind of insect this pupa belonged to. The strange thing was that there were strange divine runes drawn on the pupa. These divine runes didn't seem to be carved, but formed naturally when the pupa formed.
Insects also knew how to carve divine runes on their pupa? Luo Zheng could only sigh at the mystical nature of the Forbidden Land of God Refinement …
During this period of time, Luo Zheng had eaten too much of all kinds of strange things.
Under the influence of the Avoiding Adversity Incense, Luo Zheng wasn't picky about his food.
The feeling of eating those pieces of meat was still fine, but there were some strange things that gave Luo Zheng all kinds of terrifying experiences!
Compared to the Nightmare Beast's brain matter or the Sulfur Ghost's head, these pupae in front of him were too cute.
Luo Zheng casually grabbed a handful and stuffed it into his mouth.
"Crunch!"
A sweet fragrance instantly spread in Luo Zheng's mouth. It was like chewing peanuts, and the taste was unexpectedly good.
"This taste is not bad," Luo Zheng said with a smile as he drank a mouthful of strong wine.
At this moment, Luo Zheng noticed that the entire plaza was extremely quiet. No matter if it was the martial artists from the other clans or the Collapsing Mountain Clan, they were all staring at him.
Luo Zheng's gaze froze slightly. "Why are you all staring at me?"
"This is a Barbaric Bee pupa. It's a type of bee that lives beside the source of power. It's very precious," the Thunder Toad said with a faint smile.
Luo Zheng was slightly stunned. Looking at the Thunder Toad's expression, his heart was like a mirror. This Thunder Toad said it casually, but looking at the expressions of the Collapsing Mountain Clan members …
"This Barbaric Bee pupa is probably not ordinarily precious, right?" Luo Zheng asked.
Luo Zheng realized that he had underestimated his usefulness to the Collapsing Mountain Clan. It seemed that the thing that the Collapsing Mountain Clan wanted him to take away was indeed very important.
"More or less," the Thunder Toad's expression suddenly became solemn. "The source of power will open in three days. I hope you can do your best."
The source of power …
When Luo Zheng entered the Collapsing Mountain Clan, he knew that the source of power was the foundation of the Collapsing Mountain Clan.
Adapting to the gravity of the Collapsing Mountain Clan was only the first step. It only allowed him to stand on the starting line of the ordinary members of the Collapsing Mountain Clan. If he wanted to undergo another transformation, the most important thing was the source of power!
After swallowing the Barbaric Bee pupa, Luo Zheng felt a steady aura being transmitted from his stomach. This kind of aura not only permeated Luo Zheng's body, but also poured into Luo Zheng's dantian and escaped into Luo Zheng's inner world.
In this world, there were always some divine objects that could directly affect the inner world in the dantian, and the Barbaric Bee pupa was one of them.
In this half a year, Luo Zheng's inner world was also developing rapidly.
After the first island appeared, a layer of green was gradually spread over it. The first batch of living creatures were born in the short grass, such as voles, lizards, snakes and other small creatures.
These living creatures even formed a food chain, feeding on each other and reproducing.
On the other hand, the World Tree grew even more luxuriant. On the huge World Tree, another type of living creature was unfurled. All kinds of strange insects were born on it. Everything seemed to be in order. Luo Zheng was looking forward to it. When would the first human be born in this inner world?
In the next three days, Luo Zheng felt as if there was an extra heater in his body, and the effect of the Barbaric Bee pupa was gradually released in his body.
Three days later …
Luo Zheng and the members of the Collapsing Mountain Clan, as well as many warriors from other clans, appeared in front of the mountain to the north of the Collapsing Mountain Clan.
This mountain was completely silvery white, as if it was a mountain made of silver.
Lei Toad brought everyone to a stop at the foot of the mountain. "This is the Sacred Spring Mountain, the source of our Collapsing Mountain Clan's power. How much of the Spring of Power you can obtain will depend on your understanding and luck of power!"
The source of power flowed all year round, but even the Collapsing Mountain Clan only had one chance to enter it every year. The warriors from other clans who entered the Collapsing Mountain Clan to cultivate were all aiming for this source of power!
At this moment, all the warriors were rubbing their palms, and their eyes were filled with greed. They knew clearly how precious this source of power was, as it gathered the source of power of the entire Forbidden Land of God Refinement!
This kind of source of power existed in other universes, but it was extremely rare. Even if it was just a drop of the source of power, it would cause a great conflict. Moreover, the conditions for its birth were extremely harsh.
The more perfect the inner world was, the more evenly distributed the source of power would be. All kinds of source of power were evenly distributed in the vast space of the universe, and it was almost impossible to rely on human strength to collect them.
However, in the Forbidden Land of God Refinement, the source of power was abnormally concentrated. In other words, the master who built the Forbidden Land of God Refinement didn't plan to build a complete universe. Although he was a Saint, he just scattered all the source of power casually, which created the Forbidden Land of God Refinement today …

Chapter 1488
Each of the Six Cultivation Sanctuaries had a different source of power.
For example, the Thousand Elephant Clan controlled the Source Law, the Yuan He Clan controlled the Soul Origin, and the Sky Dome Clan controlled the Vitality Origin.
Below the Sacred Spring Mountain, there was a crack that extended all the way, forming a triangular gap. That was the entrance to the Source of Power.
The Thunder Toad and some of the elders from the Mountain Crushing Clan stopped there.
How much of the Source of Power could be obtained depended on many things, such as luck, power, knowledge, everything was possible. To put it bluntly, everything depended on oneself.
According to the usual practice, the people from the Mountain Crushing Clan would be the first to enter, followed by the warriors from other clans.
However, there was an exception this time.
This time, Luo Zheng was the first to enter, followed by the people from the Mountain Crushing Clan, and finally, the warriors from other clans.
Luo Zheng did not hesitate at all. He accidentally entered the Mountain Crushing Clan and received their full support. To Luo Zheng, this was an extremely precious opportunity.
He knew that if it was not for this, he would still be working hard near the place of his birth. If he wanted to enter any of the Six Cultivation Sanctuaries, he would have to pay a high price. Among them, there was a price that Luo Zheng could not afford, which was time …
The Sacred Clan had already crossed the Wall of Sighs.
Although he was not sure why their Real God did not appear, the entire universe was in a state of collapse. He had to seize every opportunity to improve himself.
Before Luo Zheng got close to the huge cave, he could already feel the strong wind that blew out from within.
The speed of the strong wind was not fast, at least it was far from the point of producing wind blades. To warriors, this kind of wind could only be considered as a breeze, and it was not a threat at all.
However, the moment Luo Zheng came into contact with this gust of cold wind, his entire body instantly retreated backwards!
This seemingly light cold wind actually pushed him out of the cave by a few dozen feet!
"There is a strong power hidden in this wind …"
Seeing Luo Zheng retreat from the cave, the Thunder Toad could not help but remind him, "Luo Zheng, the wind in the cave is flowing with the Source of Power. It is a weak wind formed by the mixture of the Source of Power. With your power, you will not be able to withstand it. However, this wind is intermittent."
Luo Zheng nodded at the Thunder Toad. "Thanks for the reminder."
Even if the Thunder Toad didn't say anything, Luo Zheng figured out the problem.
The second time he entered, Luo Zheng was much more careful. He quietly approached the huge cave from the other side. His whole body was attached to the mountain wall, trying his best not to be blown by the weak wind.
After approaching the edge of the cave, Luo Zheng quietly stretched out an arm.
At this moment, Luo Zheng didn't feel any wind blowing out. He didn't rush in, but suppressed his impatience and waited on the side. Since he knew that the weak wind was intermittent, Luo Zheng would first estimate the duration of the weak wind.
"Huff huff huff …"
A slight breeze blew out and brushed against Luo Zheng's arm. Luo Zheng immediately felt a huge force pushing his arm back!
Luo Zheng only used one hand to sense it. With a twist of his arm, his whole body spun in a circle, and his whole body was ruthlessly smashed against the side of the mountain wall.
Fortunately, Luo Zheng's body was much stronger than before. Otherwise, he would have been seriously injured.
Luo Zheng crawled up from the ground and was secretly shocked. The source of power contained in this source of power was actually so terrifying?
After that, Luo Zheng didn't dare to use his hand to test the weak wind. Instead, he gently pulled a strip of cloth from his waist and threw it towards the cave entrance!
"Puchi!"
Although this strip of cloth was as soft as air and could have been blown away by the wind, because of the source of power contained in the wind, this strip of cloth was instantly torn to shreds.
As a result, Luo Zheng simply tore a few more strips of cloth and continuously stretched them towards the cave entrance to test it.
After tearing about ten strips of cloth, Luo Zheng once again stretched his hand towards the cave entrance. There was no response from the cave entrance …
"Now!"
Luo Zheng didn't stop at all. His whole body turned into a stream of light and instantly drilled into the cave.
"Whoosh …"
He didn't know the terrain of this cave. Under such circumstances, Luo Zheng didn't have time to observe. He was almost madly moving forward by relying on his senses and instincts.
There were several times when Luo Zheng directly hit the silver walls of the cave. Accompanied by a muffled sound, he bounced to the other side and moved forward again!
In the blink of an eye, Luo Zheng had entered the cave for about two to three hundred feet. At this moment, Luo Zheng found that the cave entrance was still very far away. However, there was a big black pit beside it!
He almost subconsciously drilled into that big pit!
The moment he drilled in, Luo Zheng heard a whistling sound behind him. It was the sound of the weak wind moving forward in the cave and passing through some stone clocks.
"That was close. If I hadn't hidden in this pit, I'm afraid I would have been blown out again!"
After staying in this big pit for a while and confirming that the weak wind had stopped, Luo Zheng shrank his body and continued on his way!
On the way forward, Luo Zheng found that there were many big pits on both sides of this long cave. These pits were about ten feet high and the people of the Mountain Crumbling Tribe could also enter them.
It seemed that these big pits were originally dug by the people of the Mountain Crumbling Tribe so that the later generations could temporarily hide in them.
Luo Zheng kept shuttling back and forth. Then, he kept dodging back and forth in the big pits and moving forward in a roundabout way …
This Sacred Spring Mountain didn't look very big, but this cave was actually so deep. This made Luo Zheng very surprised. Perhaps he had already passed through the Sacred Spring Mountain and was moving towards the underground.
"Whoosh whoosh whoosh …"
After an hour, the sound of the wind suddenly became louder. At this moment, Luo Zheng didn't need to probe to know that the wind had stopped and the wind had risen again.
"I'm already close to the wind gap?"
If he passed through the wind gap, he wouldn't be blown out directly.
However, Luo Zheng noticed that there was a considerable distance between him and the wind gap.
The closer he was to the wind gap, the shorter the interval between the wind gap. With Luo Zheng's speed, it would be quite difficult for him to cross this distance during the interval between the wind gap and the wind gap.
No matter how difficult it was, Luo Zheng wouldn't give up now. However, after weighing the pros and cons, Luo Zheng still didn't have much confidence.
"Whoosh whoosh whoosh …"
While waiting, Luo Zheng had already passed through three periods of rest.
At this moment, Luo Zheng's eyes suddenly flashed. His body was as light as a bat. His legs suddenly stomped on the ground and his whole body started to shoot forward like a shuttle.
Luo Zheng had never set a limit for himself. Even if he couldn't do it, he would still try to do it first!
Sprinting like this, Luo Zheng rushed towards the wind gap at an extremely fast speed!
However, he had only rushed out two-thirds of the way when a sharp sound of the wind suddenly rang out.
"Whoosh …"
The closer he was to the wind gap, the more concentrated the wind was, and the stronger the source of the wind was.
This weak gust of wind was like a hammer, smashing directly on Luo Zheng's chest. The huge force immediately pushed him back!
"I failed …"
Luo Zheng's face suddenly became very ugly. At the same time, there was a trace of doubt in his heart.
His speed was already not slow. If he couldn't rush into the wind gap, then the others wouldn't be able to enter either …
The moment he was "hit" by the weak wind, Luo Zheng suddenly raised his head.
He tried his best, but he still couldn't rush into the wind gap. The problem definitely didn't come from him. He must have overlooked something!
"Above!"
When he raised his head, Luo Zheng saw that there was another cave above this cave.
It turned out that the wind was divided into two. One wind blew horizontally towards the cave that Luo Zheng came from, and the other wind blew towards the cave above!
In other words, Luo Zheng's destination wasn't the wind gap, but the cave above!
The moment he came to this conclusion, Luo Zheng suddenly stomped his feet on the ground. With the help of the wind that "hit" him, Luo Zheng forcibly crashed into the cave above …
"Bang! Bang … "
Under the push of the wind, Luo Zheng was like a paper kite, crashing everywhere in the cave. In the end, he still rushed up, but the remaining force didn't diminish.
Luo Zheng didn't know how many times he hit the ground. When the wind stopped, Luo Zheng landed on the ground of the cave.
Such a violent crash made Luo Zheng dizzy.

Chapter 1489
After this source of power gushed out, it slowly flowed along a small path in the cave and finally seeped into a crack at the side.
"This is the source of power. This is the power of a Sage?"
Things like power had no shape. For example, when Luo Zheng used his power to lift a rock, his power would be used on the rock. In this case, the power was invisible.
However, this source of power was the source of the Forbidden Land of God Refinement.
After a Saint constructed a world, his soul, perception, strength, and True Yuan would fuse into every corner of the universe in the form of the origin.
However, the Sage who constructed the Forbidden Land of God Refinement had thrown his source of power here.
This source of power had a great attraction to Luo Zheng. This seven-colored spring water also had a fatal attraction to Luo Zheng! Not to mention him, any living creature would not be able to remain calm in the face of such temptation!
Although Luo Zheng was excited, he did not let it get to his head...
According to Lei Toad, the Mountain Shattering Tribe members who entered this place were already very lucky to be able to swallow a mouthful of the source of power. Most of them returned empty-handed and did not gain anything.
Luo Zheng looked at the flowing source of power. How could it only be a mouthful? Even if a large bucket was placed at the mouth of the spring water, it would not take long for the bucket to be filled to the brim.
What would happen if he swallowed such a large amount of the source of power? Luo Zheng did not know. At least, Lei Toad told Luo Zheng that such a situation had never happened before. Even Lei Toad only drank a dozen mouthfuls of the spring water, which was enough for him to become the leader of the Mountain Shattering Tribe.
Luo Zheng stood in place and hesitated for a moment. Then, he carefully moved between the rocks.
Luo Zheng was about a hundred zhang away from the source of power. As he slowly moved closer, Luo Zheng could hear the whistling of the wind from time to time.
Those weak winds brushed past Luo Zheng's head. Luo Zheng moved forward while crawling. He was afraid that he would be swept into those weak winds. These caves were connected to each other. If he was swept into those weak winds, who knew where he would be blown to...
As he slowly moved forward, Luo Zheng was getting closer and closer to the seven-colored spring water.
At this moment, Luo Zheng could smell a faint fragrance.
"This spring water has a fragrance?"
Luo Zheng could not resist this fragrance. The desire in his heart became stronger and stronger. He wanted to drink a mouthful of the seven-colored spring water and be satisfied.
What Luo Zheng did not know was that the source of power did not have a smell. However, the source of power was the most instinctive desire of all living creatures.
........................... Luo Zheng..................................................
...........
.........
.............
.....................
But at this moment, he discovered that there was a fault in the ground in front of him. There was actually another huge cave passing through. Below it was a dark and bottomless mountain stream, and this mountain stream extended all the way up to an extremely far distance!
"You have to jump over." Luo Zheng raised his eyebrows slightly.
. This mountain stream was only about three zhang away...
. If it was any other place, Luo Zheng could easily cross it with a single step. However, the gravity in the Sacred Spring Mountain was still there. Moreover, because of the weak wind, the power here was even more chaotic.
After thinking for a moment, Luo Zheng lay on the edge of the mountain stream. He tried again to see if there was any weak wind in the mountain stream. The result made Luo Zheng feel relieved. There was no weak wind here.
Luo Zheng entered the Mountain Breaker Tribe and adapted to the terrifying gravity here. He could move freely. However, his jumping ability was severely limited. With his current strength, it was almost impossible for him to fly by relying on Zhenyuan. He could only rely on his own strength to sprint and jump.
After confirming the position in front of him, Luo Zheng slowly retreated a few steps. Then he took a deep breath.
"Go!"
Everything was possible in the Sacred Spring Mountain. The source of power was not something that could be easily obtained. There were some risks that he had to take.
"Whoosh!"
He suddenly sped up. His feet stepped on the edge of the mountain stream. His whole body instantly soared into the air.
It was only a distance of three zhang. As long as there were no accidents, he could easily cross it.
However, the moment Luo Zheng floated in the air, his face suddenly sank. He could not help but curse in his heart. Then he felt an irresistible gravitational force pulling him down.
He jumped up like a stone that was thrown out. He fell rapidly in a parabola. There was no room for him to resist.
Falling into the darkness below, Luo Zheng tried to fly in the air and change his falling trajectory. However, the gravitational force was too overbearing. Luo Zheng was like a giant's hand tightly grasping him. He could not break free.
After falling for a considerable distance in this thin mountain stream, he suddenly found that the gravitational force began to weaken rapidly. At the same time, he felt that there was a gravitational force around him from all directions.
When the gravitational forces from all directions reached a state of equilibrium, Luo Zheng fell to a place where the gravitational forces were balanced. His whole body floated in the air.
This kind of floating was not because Luo Zheng was floating in the air. It was because the gravitational forces were pulling at each other.
These gravitational forces were like ropes that bound Luo Zheng's limbs, forming a force that pulled at each other. At this moment, Luo Zheng was neither falling nor rising. He was only swaying gently in the air.
This situation was similar to the silver boulders that Luo Zheng saw in the Mountain Collapsing Tribe. Because of the chaotic forces, those boulders eventually floated in a place where the forces were balanced. They floated in the air like that for thousands of years.
"You won't let me float here forever, will you?"
Luo Zheng struggled for a while, but soon gave up. The gravitational forces from all directions firmly bound his body. He could not move at all. He was like a small insect in a spider's web.
"Drip, drip, drip..."
The sound of flowing water came from beside Luo Zheng's ears. The moment he heard this sound, Luo Zheng's nose was filled with a fresh fragrance.
There was also a source of power here?
Luo Zheng's eyes slightly blinked. Dots of green light spread out from his pupils. Soon after, he saw cracks on the mountain wall not far away. The seven-colored spring water was slowly flowing along these cracks.
The water flowed downwards. What was strange was that this source of power was like a little spirit. It jumped up from the cracks drop by drop and actually flowed upwards.
Luo Zheng curled his lips. Many attempts had been subverted in the Forbidden Land of God Refinement. Although this scene was surprising, it was not enough to make Luo Zheng too surprised.
The most important thing was that he could see this source of power and it was not far from him. However, he did not have the opportunity to drink a mouthful of it.
Luo Zheng floated in the air. He used several methods to try to break free from the restraints of the gravitational forces. He also tried to use some methods to get some of the source of power from the opposite side.
However, after trying several methods, they all failed.
Just as Luo Zheng was gritting his teeth, he suddenly realized that the seven-colored spring water on the mountain wall had suddenly changed direction. It started to flow downwards!
"The gravitational forces have changed?"
Just as this thought appeared in his mind, he suddenly felt his head become lighter. The gravitational forces from downwind had disappeared.
When the gravitational forces lost their balance, Luo Zheng was fiercely pulled upwards by the gravitational forces above his head. His entire body rose up at an extremely fast speed!
"Whoosh!"
As he flew upwards, Luo Zheng once again returned to the place where he had fallen. Once again, he saw the source of power that had converged into a small stream not far away.
However, Luo Zheng still could not change his direction. His entire body continued to be pulled upwards along this vertical cave.
"Bang!"
In the end, Luo Zheng fiercely hit the ceiling of the cave. His entire body tumbled and fell into an unfamiliar cave.
At this moment, in front of Luo Zheng, there was a protruding rock. And right below this rock, there was a drop of the source of power hanging above it.
To be precise, this drop of source of power was not hanging below the rock. It seemed to be affected by the gravitational forces and was "falling" upwards. However, this drop of source of power happened to be blocked by this rock. Therefore, it was stuck below the rock.
Seeing this drop of source of power, Luo Zheng's face revealed a happy expression. Suddenly, he realized something. He immediately raised his head and was stunned when he saw the ceiling of the cave.
He saw a pool of source of power gathered at the ceiling of the cave. It was emitting a charming color and a faint fragrance.

Chapter 1490
The pool of rainbow-colored spring water lay quietly at the top of the cave. It was only about ten zhang above Luo Zheng's head.
Luo Zheng sized up the surrounding environment. After circling around, he tried to climb up from the surrounding walls. However, the walls of the cave were as smooth as a mirror. There was nowhere for him to place his hands …
"Bang!"
Luo Zheng punched the wall of the cave. The wall only produced a muffled sound. He wanted to smash a step out of the wall. However, even with his current strength, Luo Zheng was still unable to smash open the silver rock.
"Phew …"
After exhaling, Luo Zheng could only restrain his desire. The pool of spring water was hanging upside down above his head. This thing seemed to completely ignore the law of water flowing downwards. It flowed down wherever it wanted …
Luo Zheng frowned and pondered for a moment. His eyes flashed slightly, and his face revealed the light of hope.
Just now, when he fell into the mountain stream, the spring water in the crack of the mountain stream was also in a state of reverse flow. Then, it seemed to have changed its direction and started flowing downwards! The direction of its flow was extremely strange.
Could it be that all the spring water in the Sacred Spring Mountain would be reversed after a period of time?
Luo Zheng's gaze once again fell on the protruding rock to the side. There was still a drop of spring water left below the rock. This meant that the spring water here had indeed been reversed.
Not long ago, the spring water should have been below Luo Zheng's feet. After it suddenly reversed, all the spring water rushed to the top of the cave and converged into a small pool.
"If it reverses again …"
Luo Zheng's mind moved slightly. Under the change of gravity, the spring water in the small pool would fall down again!
According to what Luo Zheng saw, this inference should be correct.
He carefully sized up the ground below his feet. This incomparably solid silver rock was covered with potholes. It seemed to have been eroded by water for a long time. Most importantly, there was still a drop of spring water left here. This was irrefutable evidence.
At this moment, Luo Zheng focused his attention on this drop of spring water. Although it was only a drop, it did not hinder Luo Zheng's interest in it.
Luo Zheng reached out his hand and touched the drop of power source.
The small water droplet kept changing into a magnificent color, as if a small rainbow was compressed into this water droplet. It was like the most beautiful gem in the world.
Luo Zheng held the source of power in his hand and turned his palm downwards. The water droplet was pressed against Luo Zheng's palm. If Luo Zheng flipped the water droplet, the source of power would escape upwards.
Naturally, he wouldn't give this little droplet a chance to escape. With a flip of his palm, Luo Zheng swallowed this droplet of Source of Strength!
A fiery power spread out from Luo Zheng's throat. He felt his body instantly absorb this power source!
This kind of origin energy could not be pure without any impurities. It did not even need to be absorbed because the origin energy was originally a part of this world.
After swallowing it, it naturally merged with Luo Zheng.
That cool feeling quickly spread throughout Luo Zheng's body. Although it was only a drop, it made every muscle in Luo Zheng's body become active!
"This feeling is similar to swallowing the meat of those strange beasts! It's just that the effect is faster! "
The strange beasts that the Mountain-Crushing Tribe hunted and killed all grew up around the Mountain-Crushing Tribe. They were also attracted by the source of power. This source of power was endlessly attractive to martial artists, and it was the same for other creatures.
Although these strange beasts had never swallowed the source of power before, the existence of this source of power still affected them! Among them, the Mountain-Crushing Tribe was affected the most. This was also one of the reasons why the Mountain-Crushing Tribe was born with great strength.
As the heat in his body became stronger and stronger, Luo Zheng also felt his strength increase rapidly.
Luo Zheng could not measure the growth of his strength in the Mountain-Crushing Tribe. As for how much his strength had increased, he would have to wait until he left the Mountain-Crushing Tribe to see!
"Just a drop of the source of power has such an effect." Luo Zheng stared at the pool of source of power above his head. "If I swallow all of this, what effect will there be?"
With this thought in mind, Luo Zheng's eyes became even more fiery.
The problem now was that Luo Zheng couldn't climb up, and he couldn't take down this source of power!
Luo Zheng spread out his Divine Sense, then he sneaked into the Sumeru Ring and started searching for tools. For all these years, Luo Zheng had stuffed all sorts of things into his Sumeru Ring, and there were quite a lot of them.
After searching for a while, Luo Zheng took out a fine steel cable. This fine steel cable was something Luo Zheng had taken from a Divine Core warrior when he was still in the Lower Realm.
This fine steel cable could be thrown out for over 30 meters, and the distance was just right. The only flaw was that this fine steel cable was only a Low Grade Immortal Item, and its grade was too low. Therefore, Luo Zheng had no choice but to put up with it.
After taking out the fine steel cable, Luo Zheng took out a small container and tied it tightly to the end of the fine steel cable. With that, the preparation was complete.
All he needed to do was throw this thing up, and use the small container to scoop down the source of power …
But, even though Luo Zheng had prepared for a long time, and just when he was about to throw the fine steel cable into the air, he heard a cracking sound. The fine steel cable was broken by Luo Zheng, and the other part of the cable was sent flying into the air, smashing into a rock wall and shattering into pieces.
An Immortal Item like this couldn't withstand the gravitational force in this place. Under the powerful gravitational force, a Low Grade Immortal Item like this would be as fragile as porcelain …
"This …"
After curling his lips, Luo Zheng took out his Sumeru Ring once again and started searching for a suitable tool. But, he still couldn't find a suitable tool.
Feeling helpless, Luo Zheng could only wait. He was waiting for the gravitational force to reverse, and the source of power would fall down by itself.
With regards to his own actions of waiting for the rabbit to appear, Luo Zheng also felt extremely helpless. It was just that the previous encounter had already made Luo Zheng understand that this Forbidden Land of God Refinement was not a reasonable place. The rules of this world were not strict, otherwise, there would not be such a miraculous thing like the source of power.
If he wanted to obtain more power here, he needed patience and luck.
Sitting here for two hours, the source of power still didn't show any signs of falling.
Now, Luo Zheng couldn't sit still anymore.
He had enough patience to wait here, but time didn't allow him to. The Thunder Toad let him in in advance, hoping that he could seize the opportunity and obtain more power.
If he stayed here for too long, he might lose other opportunities.
The forks in the Sacred Spring Mountain were as numerous as a strand of hair. Every path had a different opportunity, and the amount of power one could obtain was also different. However, if one was one step ahead, they would still be able to gain an advantage.
Taking a deep look at the small pool above his head, Luo Zheng could only firmly remember the position of this place. Perhaps he had misunderstood, and the gravitational force of the source of power didn't reverse naturally, but under someone's control?
After thinking, Luo Zheng continued to explore the depths of the cave.
The source of power in the Sacred Spring Mountain was very widely distributed. Along the way, he could see the source of power everywhere. However, his luck didn't seem to be too good. It was so close, but he couldn't obtain a single drop!
As he continued to move forward, the cave continued to extend downwards.
At this time, Luo Zheng didn't encounter the damned weak wind and chaotic gravitational force. Similarly, he didn't see any trace of the source of power.
After moving down for about a few hundred feet, the terrain suddenly became flat.
However, the light became even dimmer. Luo Zheng had no choice but to use his Clear Eyes Spirit Eyes. However, when he saw the scene in front of him clearly, he discovered that there was a huge sphere not far away.
Under careful observation, Luo Zheng saw that there were fist-sized things constantly moving in and out of this huge sphere. Although they weren't big, when they flapped their wings, there was a powerful roar.
"Hive, this is the nest of those Barbaric Bees," Luo Zheng frowned slightly.
The Thunder Toad had told him that not only was there a source of power in the Sacred Spring Mountain, there were also a considerable number of accompanying creatures!
These creatures grew under the nourishment of the source of power, and their strength was also very terrifying.
The Barbaric Bees were a relatively common strange beast in the source of power. Although they were only the size of a fist, they definitely couldn't be ignored!

Chapter 1491
The passageway extended all the way and was blocked by the hive.
If he wanted to move forward, he would have to pass by the hive. This was undoubtedly very risky for Luo Zheng.
If he disturbed these Barbaric Bees, the situation would become very troublesome.
Perhaps Luo Zheng didn't know the strength of these Barbaric Bees, but they were definitely not to be trifled with.
However, since Luo Zheng was already here, he couldn't turn back …
After making up his mind, Luo Zheng took a deep breath and began to restrain his aura. He followed the corner and slowly moved forward. Both his movements and footsteps were extremely light!
In Luo Zheng's opinion, Barbaric Bees weren't particularly aggressive. Generally, they wouldn't attack unless provoked. As long as they weren't disturbed, they wouldn't take the initiative to attack.
There was a huge difference between Barbaric Bees and Barbaric Bees, but now it seemed that their habits were similar. At the very least, Barbaric Bees had built their own territory, "Hive". As long as Luo Zheng didn't invade their territory, there shouldn't be any problems.
As he slowly approached the hive, Luo Zheng's observation became clearer.
There were countless hexagonal openings above the hive, and Barbaric Bees the size of a fist were entering and exiting from these openings. After careful observation, Luo Zheng discovered that these Barbaric Bees had a ball of fine hair under their abdomens. And this ball of fine hair was stained with a drop of Rainbow Spring Water!
These Barbaric Bees were actually harvesting the Rainbow Spring Water as nectar!
It was no wonder that Barbaric Bees could fly. Even Luo Zheng couldn't fly now. After all, he couldn't escape the powerful gravitational force. These Barbaric Bees fed on the source of energy all year round. By flapping their wings, they could escape the gravitational force of this place.
The closer he got to the hive, the more cautious Luo Zheng became.
When he was about seven or eight zhang away from the hive, Luo Zheng suddenly had a bad feeling. It seemed like something was spying on him at close range!
As soon as this feeling appeared, Luo Zheng suddenly became alert.
Luo Zheng had received the best treatment in the Mountain Collapsing Tribe, but this did not mean that there was no danger here. In this lawless and unreasonable place, Luo Zheng would never let his guard down.
After stopping, Luo Zheng looked around carefully. There was nothing in the surroundings except for a cave that led to the top. However, the cave was empty.
After looking at it again, Luo Zheng finally found the problem. There were two cracks in the center of the giant hive, and there was a pair of eyes hidden in the cracks.
The eye was made up of countless compound eyes, and Luo Zheng could see his own reflection in each of the tiny grids. In each of the grids, there was a reflection of himself!
He was being watched by this pair of eyes!
This feeling made Luo Zheng's hair stand on end. What the hell was in this hive?
This pair of eyes was dozens of times bigger than an ordinary Barbaric Bee. It was definitely not an ordinary Barbaric Bee … Could it be a Barbaric Bee King?
When bee-type creatures lived in groups, there would usually be a Barbaric Bee King that would do its best to reproduce. At the same time, this Barbaric Bee King would also take 99% of the food. All the worker bees served the Barbaric Bee King. The problem was that the only food in the Sacred Spring Mountain was the source of power.
Thinking of a Barbaric Bee King that could devour a large amount of the source of power, Luo Zheng's heart started to beat faster. He was already mentally prepared to encounter some weird things in this damned place, but the thing in front of him still made Luo Zheng's scalp tingle.
"Try to restrain your aura as much as possible!"
At this moment, Luo Zheng wanted to close his eyes and try not to let his emotions fluctuate. It would be best if he could turn into a log and pass through.
Just as Luo Zheng was about to move, he suddenly heard a rumbling sound.
"Dong dong dong …"
Luo Zheng was shocked. He saw a tall and sturdy figure falling from the cave on the side!
It was a member of the Mountain Crumbling Tribe!
This member of the Mountain Crumbling Tribe had a huge body, and the walls of the cave were smooth all the way to the top. Sliding down from the top, there was a huge inertia and impact force!
At this moment, Luo Zheng could not care less about restraining his aura. He quickly retreated backward!
"Boom!"
Just as he retreated, the tall and sturdy figure rolled down and crashed into the mountain wall on the right side of Luo Zheng, creating a shocking sound!
Fortunately, the members of the Collapsing Mountain Clan had thick skin and flesh, so they were not seriously injured from the collision. This member of the Collapsing Mountain Clan had just gotten up and cursed in the language of the Collapsing Mountain Clan when he saw Luo Zheng at the side.
"Luo Zheng?"
The Mountain Crumbling Tribe member glanced at him indifferently and called out Luo Zheng's name. However, whether it was his eyes or his tone, he was not very friendly.
After the Mountain Crumbling Tribe members reached adulthood, they would go through a rite of passage. The rite of passage was to enter the Sacred Spring Mountain and accept the test. Then, they would absorb the source of power and complete a transformation!
As for the foreign martial artists, they could only follow behind the Mountain Crumbling Tribe and take advantage of the situation. Therefore, no matter how outstanding the foreign martial artists were, it was difficult for them to surpass the Mountain Crumbling Tribe members.
Of course, there were exceptions to everything. For example, Han Liusu's brother, Han Cangyan, had also created many miracles.
This time, the situation was different.
The Mountain Crumbling Tribe placed Luo Zheng first. Not only did they let Luo Zheng be the first to enter the Sacred Spring Mountain, but Lei Toad also instructed the other martial artists of the Mountain Crumbling Tribe to help Luo Zheng as much as possible if they met him.
They did not dare to disobey Lei Toad's orders, but they were naturally disdainful in their hearts!
This kid only had the ability to be immune to fire. What right did he have to be treated so well by the Mountain Crumbling Tribe?
This group of Mountain Crumbling Tribe members had been dissatisfied for a long time. Now that they saw Luo Zheng, how could they be friendly? It was even more impossible for them to help Luo Zheng.
If there was a chance, he even wanted to fight with Luo Zheng.
However, Luo Zheng only pointed behind him. When the Mountain Crumbling Tribe member turned around, he was instantly dumbfounded. He stood on the spot and did not dare to move. He did not even dare to breathe loudly.
"Buzz buzz buzz buzz …"
Just now, when the Mountain Crumbling Tribe member smashed down, it caused such a huge commotion. The Barbaric Bees in the hive swarmed out in large numbers, constantly flying and circling in the air …
"Barbaric, the nest of the Barbaric Bees …"
Although this was the first time that the Mountain Crumbling Tribe member entered the Sacred Spring Mountain, he understood the terror of the Barbaric Bees better than Luo Zheng.
The Barbaric Bees waved their hands in circles in the air, which was a kind of warning.
If they came any closer, they would attack!
After the time it took for a stick of incense to burn, Luo Zheng and the Mountain Crumbling Tribe member were frozen in place. They were like stone statues, standing motionless in place!
Those Barbaric Bees did not chase after them. After circling around the hive once, they returned to the hive in an orderly manner. This place regained its peace.
"Hmph, which path is not easy to take? You actually chose this path. You're courting death!" The Mountain Crumbling Tribe member coldly glared at Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng did not care about the attitude of the Mountain Crumbling Tribe member in front of him. He indifferently replied, "The path behind is a dead end."
"A dead end?" The Mountain Crumbling Tribe member was stunned.
He had fallen down from above, but he was not that nervous. The paths in the Sacred Spring Mountain were tangled and complicated. It was formed by the erosion of the source of power.
Therefore, there was no real dead end here. Everything was connected and would eventually converge on the same path!
The problem was that the path in front of them was blocked by the hive.
It was impossible for him to climb up the cave he had fallen into. Luo Zheng also said that the path behind them was a dead end. Therefore, they were only left with the path in front of them.
However, there was a huge Barbaric Beehive blocking this path.
"Then what should we do?" The Mountain Crumbling Tribe member blurted out.
Luo Zheng pursed his lips. This guy was full of hostility towards him just now. Now, he was acting like this. This Mountain Crumbling Tribe member's character was really bad …
After staying here for half a year, Luo Zheng also knew that the Mountain Crumbling Tribe members would not fake or even lie. As long as they had a reason to hate him, they would definitely show it on their faces.
If they needed help, they would directly ask for help. Luo Zheng was already used to this way of doing things. He did not argue with this worker and only indifferently said, "We can only go over carefully. As long as we don't disturb these Barbaric Bees, there shouldn't be a big problem!"
Even with such a huge commotion, these Barbaric Bees only circled around the hive and did not attack Luo Zheng and the Mountain Crumbling Tribe member. This meant that the Barbaric Bees were not particularly aggressive.
"The only problem is the Queen Bee in this hive," Luo Zheng added.
"What! There's a Queen Bee in here! " The Mountain Crumbling Tribe member's voice suddenly rose by 80%. Then, he covered his mouth, but his eyes revealed fear.
Luo Zheng became curious. "Isn't it normal to have a Queen Bee in a hive?"
The survival of a hive depended on the Queen Bee to continuously nurture its offspring. These Barbaric Bees should not be able to escape this rule, right?
"No! It's not like that. It's definitely not normal to have a Queen Bee! "The Mountain Crumbling Tribe member said with a face full of fear.

Chapter 1492
"Why is it abnormal if there is a Wasp King?" Luo Zheng asked puzzledly.
The Mountain Crushing Race clansman curled his lips and said, "The clansman said that there is only one Wasp King in the Sacred Spring Mountain and it is in the deepest part of the Sacred Spring Mountain!"
"If there is only one Wasp King, then what is going on with this hive?"
The Mountain Crushing Race clansman obviously noticed the two big eyes on the hive. Even though he was Rao Yong, his voice was trembling. "If there is a Wasp King here, then the Wasp King in the deepest part of the mountain must be dead! There must be a huge problem. We should leave the Sacred Spring Mountain! "
Luo Zheng didn't know much about the Sacred Spring Mountain. After all, there were many secrets in the Mountain Crushing Race that weren't open to the public. For example, he had never heard of the Mountain Crushing Race's clansman. He only knew that the current leader of the Mountain Crushing Race was Lei Toad.
Luo Zheng wasn't willing to leave now. If he left now, he might have to stay in the Mountain Crushing Race for another year.
"The problem is, how are we going to leave?" Luo Zheng suddenly asked.
The Mountain Crushing Race clansman patted his head. He had forgotten that this was the only path they could take.
If they were stronger and could break free from the gravity of this place and fly, they could return the way they came. However, it wasn't that easy to leave now...
The two of them stood on the spot and discussed for a while. It seemed like they couldn't find any other way out. In the end, they could only grit their teeth and move forward.
The Mountain Crushing Race clansman realized his situation and didn't have much hostility towards Luo Zheng. At this moment, he told Luo Zheng his name. This person's name was Lie Yu and he had just turned 30 years old this year.
However, this kind of age calculation wasn't accurate. After all, the time of a day in the Forbidden Land of God Refinement wasn't fixed. Sometimes, the day would be as long as dozens of hours. Sometimes, the night would be as short as six to eight hours. Moreover, the calendar in the Forbidden Land of God Refinement was different from the universe Luo Zheng was in.
Lie Yu walked in front. Although he was tall, the Mountain Crushing Race clansmen had extraordinary control over their strength. He could contract every muscle on his body at will. When he slowly walked forward, he was as light as a cat.
Luo Zheng once again concealed his aura and stared at the scattered Wasp King in the hive while quietly following behind Lie Yu.
"Buzz buzz buzz …"
A few Barbaric Bees were flying around the hive. As for the pair of large eyes in the hive, they were still staring at the two's every move. The Wasp King should be the biggest threat. At this moment, it even had the power to decide the life and death of the two. If it got angry, all the Barbaric Bees would attack them without hesitation.
Not long after, they arrived at the edge of the hive. Its large compound eyes stared at Luo Zheng and Lie Yu through the cracks of the hive, giving them an invisible pressure.
At this moment, Lie Yu suddenly stopped. His huge body blocked Luo Zheng's attempt to take it away. Perhaps he was too nervous, but he stood there and panted anxiously!
"Go!"
Luo Zheng pushed him from behind and frowned.
However, at this moment, the two suddenly heard a crisp sound coming from the hive. The cracks on the surface of the hive widened, and a pair of large, orange-yellow compound eyes were completely exposed to their field of vision. The two could even see a part of the Wasp King's body. Its erect hairs were like iron nails that were inserted into the Wasp King's body.
"Hu …"
Lie Yu suddenly took a deep breath, and the sound was like a whistle.
"Buzz buzz …"
A few Barbaric Bees might have been startled by the sound. They circled the hive twice and then went straight for Luo Zheng and Lie Yu.
The two stood in place, not daring to move. In this situation, they could only resign themselves to fate. If they chose to fight back, all the Barbaric Bees in the hive would come out in full force. At that time, no one would be able to save them.
"Buzz buzz buzz …"
One of the Barbaric Bees circled in front of Luo Zheng. This Barbaric Bee's tail was embedded with a huge black spike, and the tip of the spike had two black barbs. It was like an anchor. Being stung by this thing was not a fun experience.
At this moment, Luo Zheng's muscles were tense. If this Barbaric Bee attacked, he could only choose to fight back and then escape!
Fortunately, these Barbaric Bees only circled the two a few times, then slowly circled back to the hive.
"Not bad," Luo Zheng breathed a sigh of relief.
Lie Yu's originally nervous expression also relaxed.
But just as the two relaxed a little and were about to continue moving forward, they heard a rumbling sound coming from behind!
Someone else had fallen!
The two looked at each other, and they already knew the answer.
"Run!"
The two of them had just started to accelerate forward when a loud bang came from behind them. They didn't know who had fallen. In short, such a violent commotion would definitely alarm these Barbaric Bees. The two of them were on the side of the hive, so they would definitely be the first to receive the most concentrated attacks!
After realizing this, the two of them immediately rushed out!
Although their movements were not small, it was still incomparable to the sound of the cultivator falling from behind!
"Buzz buzz buzz buzz …"
At this moment, countless Barbaric Bees swarmed out of the hive.
"What the hell is this!"
A cursing voice came from behind Luo Zheng. It seemed that the cultivator who had fallen this time was not from the Mountain Shattering Tribe. However, Luo Zheng had no time to care about that person!
The Barbaric Bees in the hive split into two groups. One group rushed towards the cultivator, while the other group chased after Luo Zheng and Lie Yu!
The two of them no longer cared about the distance between them. They only covered their heads and ran madly!
"Buzz buzz!"
In the end, their running speed could not compare to the flying speed of the Barbaric Bees. That group of fist-sized Barbaric Bees quickly rushed towards Luo Zheng!
"Sou!"
Luo Zheng heard an extremely soft sound breaking through the air!
He almost subconsciously lowered his head and rolled on the ground. He saw a tiny Wasp Tail Needle fly past his head and nailed itself to the ground in front of him!
Then, a strange scene appeared.
According to Luo Zheng's judgment, the strength of this Wasp Tail Needle should not be very strong. It also contained a highly toxic poison, so he shouldn't be too afraid. However, when this needle shot out, it actually nailed itself to the ground!
One had to know that the entire Sacred Spring Mountain was made of silver rock. Even Luo Zheng was unable to destroy this silver rock. However, this light Wasp Tail Needle had directly nailed itself to the ground.
"Hualala!"
