The originally solid ground of the cave suddenly began to boil like boiling water. The Wasp Tail Needle had actually melted the silver rock and completely corroded it!
"What a strong corrosive power. This isn't an ordinary poison …"
At this moment, Luo Zheng was secretly alarmed. If he were to be nailed by this Wasp Tail Needle, wouldn't a large piece of him also be corroded?
However, there were countless Barbaric Bees behind them. They had more than one Wasp Tail Needle!
After the two realized the difficult situation they were in, they began to run even harder. At the same time, they heard a piercing scream!
Amidst the chaos, Luo Zheng turned his head and saw a martial artist in the distance whose body was already blurred. That martial artist had probably been hit by many Wasp Tail Needles. His entire body was like a candle being roasted by a raging fire. His entire body was constantly melting and shrinking. In a few breaths of time, he had turned into a pool of blood …
Just as he turned his head, Luo Zheng saw dozens of black Wasp Tail Needles shooting straight towards him.
Luo Zheng stepped on the wall of the cave and rolled, barely avoiding the Wasp Tail Needles. The Wasp Tail Needles nailed to the wall and quickly corroded dozens of deep holes. The incomparably solid silver rock was as soft as tofu under the Wasp Tail Needles!
If he hadn't dodged, his fate would have been the same as that corroded martial artist.
"Die for me!"
At this moment, Luo Zheng's heart was filled with anger. Just as he steadied himself, the long sword in his hand suddenly slashed out.
Although he didn't know if his sword art would be effective against these Barbaric Bees, he couldn't just sit there and wait for death …
"Shua!"
The effect of this sword strike was completely out of Luo Zheng's expectations.
Those Barbaric Bees were actually cut in half by his sword strike. These Barbaric Bees weren't as strong as he had imagined!
Previously, Luo Zheng had been chased by the Formless Heavenly Centipede all the way. Coupled with the strange gravitational force of the Mountain Collapsing Race, he had been overly nervous about the creatures in the Forbidden Land of God Refinement. It could be said that he had overestimated the strength of these Barbaric Bees.
Although their Wasp Tail Needles were terrifying, they were still bee-type creatures. The characteristics of this kind of creature were their large numbers, speed, agility, and venom … However, their defensive power was only average.
Luo Zheng was pleasantly surprised. The sword light in his hand became even sharper, instantly killing dozens of Barbaric Bees!
"Help me!" Luo Zheng reminded Lie Yu.
He didn't expect Lie Yu to just charge forward. As he ran, he said, "I haven't cultivated any martial skills. How can I help you!"
Originally, the only thing the Mountain Collapsing Race relied on was their terrifying strength. However, when it came to the use of strength, they were in an extremely primitive state. This Lie Yu really couldn't help Luo Zheng!
He could only rely on himself!
The sword light in Luo Zheng's hand was like a net. As he dodged the Wasp Tail Needles, he quickly killed the Barbaric Bees. At the same time, he madly ran forward.
"Buzz buzz buzz buzz buzz!"
Swaths of Barbaric Bees were cut down by Luo Zheng. Along the way, the corpses of the Barbaric Bees that had been cut in half scattered all over the ground. Many Barbaric Bees were afraid of the sword in Luo Zheng's hand. They flapped their wings and cried even louder!
"Whoosh!"
At this moment, a sharp whistling sound was heard. It was a black light that went straight for Luo Zheng's face.
Seeing that the black light was about to hit Luo Zheng, he suddenly held the Lei Feng You Divine Sword horizontally. The black light directly nailed onto Luo Zheng's sword!
This First Grade Divine Weapon that had accompanied Luo Zheng for many years was corroding at a visible speed. When Luo Zheng threw it out, it had already turned into a pool of molten iron before it hit the ground.
Luo Zheng's face suddenly darkened. He didn't feel sorry for the sword. Instead, he saw that the hive in the distance was completely destroyed. A black object was nailed to the wall of the cave. On top of its head was a pair of huge compound eyes that emitted a faint light. It was the Queen Bee!
The black light that went straight for Luo Zheng's face was shot out by the Queen Bee.
＝＝＝＝＝

Chapter 1493
The Barbaric Bee King did not have wings and looked like a large worm. Its tail curled up like a scorpion, and there was a cluster of black thorns at the tip of its tail!
Although the Barbaric Bee King looked very bloated and could not fly, its movement speed was not slow at all. After its black beam was blocked by Luo Zheng, the twelve pairs of legs under its abdomen began to move rapidly, and it rushed toward Luo Zheng at an extremely fast speed.
Seeing this, Luo Zheng turned and ran!
When he turned his head, he saw that Lie Yu from the Mountain-Crushing Clan had already run a distance of 300 meters. Luo Zheng could not help but twitch his mouth. It seemed that the Mountain-Crushing Clan was not as brave as they appeared to be.
However, Lie Yu could not be blamed for this. All courage was built on the foundation of being able to fight.
When facing the Barbaric Bee King, running away was not something to be laughed at.
"Whoosh whoosh whoosh!"
Luo Zheng's expression was also very focused at this moment. Every step he took was extremely precise. When he used his footwork, his speed was even faster than Lie Yu's!
Lie Yu had grown up in the Mountain-Crushing Clan and was one of the best of the younger generation. In terms of pure strength, Luo Zheng might lose to him.
However, the Mountain Crasher Race only used their strength to the extreme. They had almost no research on techniques, techniques, and the like. In this kind of ecological environment, they did not need to research. This was because every race faced different environments, and the path of evolution they took was also different.
Lie Yu ran wildly in front and occasionally looked back. When he saw the Barbaric Bee King behind Luo Zheng, he looked as if he had eaten something bad. His expression was as ugly as it could be. Therefore, he ran even faster.
Generally speaking, the trial of the Sacred Spring Mountain was not very dangerous. However, there was still a certain mortality rate. Every year during the coming of age ceremony, the Mountain-Crushing Clan would lose three or four people. The number of foreign warriors who died in the Sacred Spring Mountain was even higher.
However, dying at the hands of the Barbaric Bee King was more unjust. After all, this thing should not have existed here in the first place …
Lie Yu ran wildly as his thoughts ran wild. As he was distracted, he missed a step!
After being stunned for a moment, he rolled down again. He was just curious. There should not be a big hole here!
Luo Zheng, who was behind him, saw Lie Yu's figure disappear in an instant and understood that he must have fallen down.
Seeing this, a glimmer of hope appeared on Luo Zheng's face.
If this path continued, he would be caught by the Barbaric Bee King sooner or later. At that time, he would definitely die! If there was a passage leading down ahead, that would be the best.
"Whoosh!"
Along with the sound of the air being torn apart, Luo Zheng was suddenly shocked in his heart. His entire body suddenly leaped up and rolled in the air!
The faintly discernible black light passed through Luo Zheng's clothes and nailed itself to the rock wall beside him. Immediately, it corroded a large hole in the rock wall.
Luo Zheng used the momentum of his fall to step into the place where Lie Yu had disappeared. According to Luo Zheng's thoughts, this must be a hole leading down.
However, as he fell, his expression immediately changed. He saw Lie Yu crawling up from the hole below. As he fell, he stepped on Lie Yu's shoulder.
"You!"
Lie Yu was just about to open his mouth to speak when Luo Zheng stomped him into the pit again …
Luo Zheng used Lie Yu's shoulder to jump again. He somersaulted out of the hole and hovered up. His expression immediately turned ugly. The place where Lie Yu had fallen was just a big hole. It was not a passage leading down!
Lie Yu had been stomped into the pit by Luo Zheng, but he quickly crawled back up. He did not dare to argue with Luo Zheng, as escaping was more important.
The two of them continued to run forward. The Barbaric Bee King was clearly faster than them. It was getting closer and closer to them!
Not long after, the two of them finally reached the end of the path. Their hearts sank at the same time. The end of the path did not connect to the other caves. This was a dead end …
The Barbaric Bee King was still chasing after them. The two of them had already retreated to the end of the path. The silver stone wall behind them was incomparably sturdy. It was impossible to destroy it.
Seeing the Barbaric Bee King rapidly approaching them, the anxiety on their faces intensified. How could they fight the Barbaric Bee King with their strength? There seemed to be no other choice!
"Perhaps the Barbaric Bee King is the same as the Barbaric Bees. Its defense is not …"
Luo Zheng was halfway through his sentence when he suddenly heard a pitter-patter sound. He looked up and saw the Mountain Breaker Tribesman, who was more than three meters tall, crying. "What should we do? Wuwuwuwu …"
Luo Zheng was originally very nervous. However, when he saw the Mountain Breaker Tribesman crying, he immediately rolled his eyes. He had wanted to cooperate with this guy, but he did not expect that this guy would immediately collapse.
He could only rely on himself. Luo Zheng's gaze swept over and a longbow appeared in his hand.
He pulled the bowstring and released the arrow!
"Pa pa!"
After the second acceleration, the arrow shot towards the Barbaric Bee King.
However, under the enormous gravitational force, the power of the arrow was greatly reduced. Before it could reach the Barbaric Bee King, its power was exhausted. The Barbaric Bee King used its thin front legs to lightly push the arrow away.
Luo Zheng's resistance probably angered the Barbaric Bee King. Perhaps it realized that the two prey in front of it still had the intention to resist, so it raised its tail and shot out three stingers!
"Dodge!"
Luo Zheng lowered his body and saw that the majestic man beside him was still crying. He swept his leg and knocked him to the ground!
"Pop pop pop!"
The three sharp needles nailed into the rock wall behind them and the rock wall began to melt.
Luo Zheng lay on the ground and took the opportunity to nock another arrow. With a shake of the bowstring, the sharp arrow flew out again!
There was still no effect. However, Luo Zheng knew that if he did not counterattack, the Barbaric Bee King would probably directly pounce on him.
As expected.
Under Luo Zheng's continuous attacks, the Barbaric Bee King stopped moving forward. After pushing away Luo Zheng's arrows, the Barbaric Bee King raised its tail again!
"Sou sou sou!"
Another three sharp needles flew over!
Although Lie Yu was extremely depressed, he did not want to die. He crawled up at the same time as Luo Zheng and began to dodge the sharp needles!
"You want to use up the sharp needles on the Barbaric Bee King's tail?" Lie Yu seemed to have seen a glimmer of hope.
The sharp needles on the Barbaric Bee King's tail were like a bush of grass. There were about fifty to sixty of them. While they were chasing, they had already shot out a portion of them. Now, they seemed to have lessened a lot.
If they could really use up the sharp needles on the Barbaric Bee King's tail, they might have a chance to survive.
However, just as Lie Yu finished speaking, he saw the Barbaric Bee King shake its tail. Long Barbaric Bee Needles grew out from its tail. They seemed to be even denser than before …
"Of, of course I didn't say anything …" The glimmer of hope that had just been ignited in Lie Yu's eyes was immediately extinguished.
Luo Zheng also frowned. He did have this idea, but it seemed that this idea was too naive. However, at this moment, Luo Zheng suddenly felt a chill behind his back. When he turned around to look, his face immediately revealed a strange expression!
"Quickly look!" He reminded Lie Yu.
Lie Yu turned around and looked. His face immediately revealed an expression of great joy.
The rock wall behind them was the end of the path. Just now, the Barbaric Bee King had shot three needles at it. The silver rock wall had been corroded quite a bit. At this moment, a fist-sized hole had been corroded!
This hole was so deep that one could not see the bottom. There was even a slight breeze blowing in …
"So this rock wall is so thin!"
Lie Yu was pleasantly surprised. He smashed his fist into another part of the rock wall, hoping to rely on his brute force to break through the rock wall.
However, this silver rock was too hard. Even if it was just a thin piece, it was not something that they could break. If it were the Thunder Toad, it would be more likely!
Luo Zheng shook his head. He could only sigh that this guy was too stupid. Then, he silently nocked an arrow on his longbow.
"Get ready to dodge." Luo Zheng's voice fell and he released the bowstring in his hand.
Naturally, he didn't expect the arrow to kill the Barbaric Bee King. What he needed to do now was to lure the Barbaric Bee King to shoot out more stingers. If the stingers could melt the rock wall behind them, they would have a chance!
Fortunately, Lie Yu's brain was not very good. The Barbaric Bee King was even worse. Once it was attacked, it would immediately counterattack. This was almost an instinct of the Barbaric Bee King!

Chapter 1494
The Barbaric Bee King maintained a certain distance from Luo Zheng.
If the Barbaric Bee King got any closer, it would be difficult for Luo Zheng to avoid it …
"Pop pop pop!"
Three more Barbaric Bee Needles shot into the rock wall behind them.
This unimaginably hard silver rock was boiling and flowing like water. One could imagine how strong the corrosive power of the Barbaric Bee Needles was.
Previously, there was already a fist-sized hole in the rock wall. As the corrosion continued, the hole grew bigger and bigger!
Soon, a hole the size of a human head appeared. Of course, this was measured in terms of the size of a human head.
"I can go over now." Luo Zheng's mind moved slightly.
Generally speaking, as long as the head could fit through the gap, there would be no problem with the body. However, the big guy next to him might have a problem. This guy's head was almost twice the size of Luo Zheng's.
Lie Yu probably thought of the same thing as Luo Zheng. His face trembled slightly. He was naturally afraid that Luo Zheng would leave him here alone to face the Barbaric Bee King.
In the end, Luo Zheng didn't go in alone. Instead, he continued to lure the Barbaric Bee King to shoot its needles!
The Barbaric Bee King shook out the Barbaric Bee Sting on its tail as it crawled towards the two people. The two people deliberately moved back and forth near the small hole, luring the Barbaric Bee Sting to shoot into the stone walls around the small hole.
Now, it was a matter of luck. After all, they couldn't control the Barbaric Bee King's accuracy. Sometimes, the needles were too accurate and directly entered the small hole.
However, as the needles shot over, more and more of the rock wall behind them melted. In the end, a large hole was melted!
At this moment, the Barbaric Bee King increased its speed and rushed over. The speed of the needles on its tail also increased. The two of them found it more and more difficult to dodge. Lie Yu was especially huge. There were a few times when the Barbaric Bee Needles almost brushed past his body. He was lucky to be able to hold on for so long.
At this moment, the two of them didn't hesitate and went into the large hole …
Previously, when the Barbaric Bee Needles shot into the hole, they didn't hear the slightest echo. The two of them knew that the back of the hole was definitely empty. The moment they entered, the two of them began to fall rapidly. As they fell, Luo Zheng could even see two Barbaric Bee Needles flashing above his head.
"That Savage Bee King won't chase after us, right?"
Luo Zheng looked back worriedly, only to see the Barbaric Bee King stick out its huge eyes from the hole. In the end, the Barbaric Bee King shrank back!
Under such a strong gravitational force, if the distance was too long, even if it was Luo Zheng, he would most likely be smashed into pieces.
Realizing this problem, Luo Zheng turned his body and observed the distance below.
But as soon as he turned his body, he saw a black dot constantly flying below. Although he didn't know what it was, Luo Zheng subconsciously wanted to avoid it.
Unfortunately, as he was unable to fly, Luo Zheng had no way of dodging. In the end, both he and Lie Yu crashed into it, passing through the cluster of black dots. Only now did he realize that the black dots were actually bugs the size of fingertips. However, they did not pose much of a threat.
It was pitch-black below them, and they couldn't see the bottom …
Fortunately, they didn't fall for long before the two of them landed on a slope. They rolled down the slope, and the force of their fall became weaker and weaker. The slope became gentler and gentler, and the speed at which the two of them rolled became slower and slower.
After rolling to the bottom, the two finally stabilized their bodies.
However, at this moment, Lie Yu was extremely excited. He jumped up from the ground and began to check his body.
"What's wrong?" Luo Zheng's face revealed a strange expression.
"It's a Horseshoe Beetle! Bad luck! We just bumped into a Horseshoe Beetle! "Lie Yu cried out.
"Horseshoe Beetle? What will happen? "Luo Zheng asked again.
The panic on Lie Yu's face became even more obvious. He was even more frightened than when he encountered the Barbaric Bee King. "This thing will lay eggs on the warrior's skin, then burrow into the body to hatch!"
Hearing this, Luo Zheng immediately frowned. After raising his arm, he saw some small black dots on his arm. These black dots were only the size of sesame seeds. They were the eggs of the Horseshoe Beetle, but they had already burrowed into his skin.
Being burrowed into the body by insects was not a pleasant thing.
However, these small insects should be very weak. How could they burrow into the body? Luo Zheng did not believe it. He suddenly circulated his True Essence and was about to force these small black dots out.
Unexpectedly, it would have been better if he didn't circulate his True Essence. As soon as he did, the small black dots began to burrow into his body!
Luo Zheng suddenly jumped in fright and hurriedly dispersed his true essence.
"What should we do?" Luo Zheng asked.
Although Lie Yu was not very smart, he was still a member of the Collapsing Mountain Clan. Since he recognized the Horseshoe Beetle, the Collapsing Mountain Clan should have a solution.
"We can only use poison," Lie Yu replied.
This kind of Horseshoe Beetle was not rare in the Sacred Spring Mountain. It even existed in the territory of the Collapsing Mountain Clan. After being bitten by this Horseshoe Beetle, its eggs would be attached to the body. It was indeed very troublesome.
In the beginning, the people of the Mountain Crushing Race could only wait for the little insects to hatch and then pick them out bit by bit. However, this method would leave them half dead even if they didn't die. Even if they recovered, they would be covered in scars.
Later, the people of the Collapsing Mountain Clan discovered that this Horseshoe Beetle was afraid of a kind of poison. If this poison was taken, it could completely kill the Horseshoe Beetle eggs under the skin!
However, this poison was extremely toxic. Lie Yu took out a small purple bottle from the belt around his waist. "This is the poison of the Horseshoe Beetle. It can drive away these Horseshoe Beetle eggs."
After saying this, he opened the small bottle and took a small sip. Then he glanced at Luo Zheng. This guy had saved his life.
Now that he was not so hostile towards Luo Zheng, he continued, "This poison of the Horseshoe Beetle is very powerful. The body of an ordinary ancient martial art practitioner would not be able to withstand it … I don't dare to drink too much. I can only suppress these small insects. How much you can drink is up to you."
If it was an adult member of the Collapsing Mountain Clan, he could drink this poison of the Horseshoe Beetle in one gulp and directly force the Horseshoe Beetle eggs out of his body. However, Lie Yu had just become an adult. Although the physique of a member of the Collapsing Mountain Clan was much stronger than that of an ordinary ancient martial art practitioner, he did not dare to drink so much in one gulp.
This small sip could only suppress these Horseshoe Beetle eggs. He would think of a solution when he returned to the Collapsing Mountain Clan.
In his opinion, as a human, Luo Zheng would not be able to withstand this poison of the Horseshoe Beetle. Even a small sip would be enough to take his life.
Seeing Lie Yu's worried expression, Luo Zheng smiled and directly reached out to take the small bottle from Lie Yu's hand. This small bottle was very small in the thick hands of a member of the Collapsing Mountain Clan, but in Luo Zheng's hands, it was like a wine jug.
He took the jug of poison and gulped it down. Perhaps Luo Zheng's strength was not outstanding now, and his strength was not particularly outstanding in the Forbidden Land of God Refinement, but he was not afraid of this kind of poison.
"You, you will die …" Lie Yu's mouth was wide open when he saw Luo Zheng drink so much in one gulp. "Actually, these Horseshoe Beetles are not fatal. You can just use a knife to pick them out …"
Luo Zheng wiped the corner of his mouth and threw the small bottle to Lie Yu. He smiled and said, "This little bit of poison won't be much of a problem!"
As soon as he finished speaking, the Horseshoe Beetle's poison began to spread along Luo Zheng's meridians. The Horseshoe Beetle's eggs that had entered Luo Zheng's flesh seemed to be inexplicably afraid of Luo Zheng, and they kept burrowing out from the bottom of his skin …
However, after most of the Horseshoe Beetle's eggs crawled out, they fell down like grains of rice. The Horseshoe Beetle's poison was the bane of these Horseshoe Beetle's eggs. Just a little bit of it would cause them to die.
From beginning to end, Lie Yu stared at Luo Zheng without moving. After realizing that Luo Zheng was not afraid of the Horseshoe Beetle's poison, he believed Luo Zheng's words. It seemed that there would not be much of a problem.
Therefore, in Lie Yu's impression, besides not being afraid of fire, Luo Zheng was also immune to poison …
After cleaning up the Horseshoe Beetle's eggs, the two of them began to size up their surroundings.
As they raised their heads, Luo Zheng's eyes suddenly lit up. He saw that there was a little bit of Rainbow Spring Water hidden in the strange rocks on the mountain wall. There were actually quite a few sources of power there!
Thinking about the characteristics of the source of power that kept flowing in reverse, Luo Zheng guessed that the source of power must have been "caught" by the strange rocks after it was reversed, so it did not fall to the ceiling.
Then, above them …
Luo Zheng subconsciously raised his head to look, and his entire body suddenly froze!
Lie Yu also noticed Luo Zheng's strange behavior and also raised his head to look. His tall and sturdy body suddenly trembled.

Chapter 1495
"I … I've never heard of a river at the bottom of the Sacred Spring Mountain!"
Lie Yu muttered.
As far as the outside world knew, the Mountain Crumbling Tribe was known as a holy land for cultivation because of its source of power.
The rare source of power was described as a spring because there was not much of it to begin with. It was just that there was a source of power gushing out rare source of power.
Hence, this mountain was called the Sacred Spring Mountain because of its source of power.
After entering the Sacred Spring Mountain, all sorts of caves extended downwards like capillaries. If one searched carefully, they would eventually find something.
After so many years, the Mountain Crumbling Tribe had almost finished researching the internal structure of the Sacred Spring Mountain. Although the paths here were complicated, they still had a map. However, other than the elders of the tribe, it was not open to the members of the Mountain Crumbling Tribe who were participating in the coming of age ceremony, let alone outsiders.
In fact, Lei Toad had wanted to give a portion of the map to Luo Zheng. After all, there were several places marked on the map that could allow Luo Zheng to find more source of power.
However, this plan was rejected by the elders of the tribe. If the map was leaked, it would be a hidden danger to the Mountain Crumbling Tribe. How much source of power Luo Zheng could obtain would still depend on his own abilities.
The Mountain Crumbling Tribe knew about the Sacred Spring Mountain but they did not know everything about it. Up until now, they only knew that there was a spring mouth underground and all the source of power should be from there!
The source of power had a very interesting characteristic. It looked like flowing water and would flow downwards. However, it would often flow in the opposite direction, completely ignoring the existence of gravity and "climbing" upwards. Hence, the source of power that was supposed to be deep underground would appear at the top of the Sacred Spring Mountain. Occasionally, it would even overflow from the top of the mountain.
In fact, the chaotic energy around the Mountain Crumbling Tribe had a lot to do with the source of power.
Before today, no one knew that there was a river inside the Sacred Spring Mountain! If news of this were to spread, the Sacred Spring Mountain would probably have to change its name to the "Sacred River Mountain".
Even Luo Zheng was unusually excited when he saw this scene. Previously, when he was being chased by the Barbaric Bee King, he had actually used the Barbaric Bee King to open up a new path!
The two of them stared blankly at the seven-colored river above them. Only after the time it took for an incense stick to burn did they finally calm down.
The biggest problem had appeared. Just like before, Luo Zheng had discovered quite a number of energy essences along the way. However, in the end, Luo Zheng had only obtained one drop of energy essences. The others could be seen but not touched.
How many sources of energy were there in this rainbow river? However, the source of the energy was still hanging high up in the air. Looking at the distance, it was probably two to three thousand feet. Without being able to fly, they would definitely not be able to climb up!
At this time, Lie Yu was more clear-headed than Luo Zheng. He could not get the energy source above his head, but there was still a lot around him …
Thus, he quietly walked to the side and began to devour the energy source in the cracks of the stones.
However, Lie Yu had just swallowed a few drops when he saw Luo Zheng look over and awkwardly smiled, "There's still a lot here. Let's share it first."
The amount of energy source in the cracks of the stones around them was not a small amount. As long as they could devour it, it would be enough to match the harvest of this trip to the Sacred Spring Mountain. In fact, many cultivators who entered this place would only obtain a few dozen drops of energy source. If they were unlucky, they might only obtain a few drops. It was even possible for them to return empty-handed.
Luo Zheng naturally knew that there was a lot of energy source in the cracks of the stones, but since he had come here and seen this river, he had a vague idea in his heart.
What he needed to devour was not just a few hundred drops of energy source. His goal on this trip to the Sacred Spring Mountain was to obtain this river of energy source!
However, every time he swallowed a drop of this energy source, his strength would increase by a bit. Luo Zheng did not mind devouring the energy source here …
Thus, just like Lie Yu, Luo Zheng began to collect the energy source in the cracks of the stones.
These energy sources were hanging upside down in the cracks of the stones. Luo Zheng reached out his hand and pulled them out drop by drop, swallowing them as if he was swallowing dew.
The extremely pure energy source almost instantly fused with Luo Zheng's body, causing his strength to increase at an extremely fast rate …
Luo Zheng and Lie Yu took one side each, picking the energy source along the cracks of the stones.
The crack was at least two to three miles long, and the energy essence scattered within was naturally devoured by the two of them without leaving a single drop behind.
As they continued forward, the stone walls on both sides became smooth. Naturally, they could no longer hide any energy source.
At this moment, Lie Yu stretched his body. As his bones crisscrossed, they emitted a series of crackling sounds like firecrackers. This was a sign that energy was surging within his body.
The physical body could not bear an unlimited amount of energy. Devouring too much energy source was not necessarily a good thing. After devouring so much energy source, Lie Yu was nearing the limit of his physical body.
Luo Zheng also moved his body slightly, but he did not have such a reaction. His strength might be about the same as Lie Yu's, or even slightly weaker, but the hardness of his physical body was far beyond Lie Yu's. He could still devour even more energy source.
"Should we continue forward?" Lie Yu asked.
He was lucky to have followed Luo Zheng. The harvest this time had far exceeded his expectations.
"Do we still have a way back?" Luo Zheng asked in return.
Lie Yu stared blankly for a moment before placing his thick hand on his forehead. The joy that had been brought about by the growth of his strength had completely disappeared …
If they wanted to go back, they had to climb back to the hole they had fallen into. Not to mention that they did not have the ability to climb back up, the Barbaric Bee King was still guarding the hole. If they went back the way they came, it would not bode well for them.
"Then what should we do?" Lie Yu asked.
Luo Zheng said indifferently, "We can only go forward."
According to Luo Zheng's guess, their current situation was not optimistic.
Because they had broken through a cave, they had discovered a brand new path. This path had never been explored before.
Although there were many caves in the Sacred Spring Mountain, they were all connected to each other. They had not found this path after so many years, nor had they found the river that was the source of the energy source. This could only mean one thing: this path, or rather this cave, was not connected to any other path!
The only place that was connected was the hole created by the Barbaric Bee King's corrosion …
Luo Zheng could not think of a way to leave for the time being. The only thing they could do now was to keep moving forward.
As they moved forward along the flat path, the river above their heads became wider and wider. At first, the edges of the river were very irregular. It should have been formed naturally, but as they walked, something was wrong.
The edges of the river above their heads gradually formed a straight line, like a man-made canal. Although nature was uncanny, it was impossible for it to form such a river. It must have been deliberately created by an intelligent life.
In addition to the regular river above their heads, Luo Zheng's feet also became regular. The silver mountain walls on both sides became smoother and smoother, almost identical to the shape of the river above their heads!
"The intelligent life that created this river knew that the source of the energy source would reverse, so they created two canals above and below the cave … I wonder what the purpose of this is?"
Luo Zheng analyzed as he walked.
Not long after, the path ahead suddenly opened up. A huge circular pit appeared below their feet. As for the seven-colored river above their heads, it formed a waterfall.
Ordinary waterfalls flowed straight down from the top, but the waterfall above their heads flowed straight up from the bottom. It was the complete opposite.
This scene was not out of Luo Zheng's expectations. Luo Zheng had seen this completely reversed characteristic several times, but the waterfall that flowed straight up converged in a circular lake at the top of the cave!
In front of Luo Zheng and Lie Yu, there was naturally an identical circular lake, but the lake below was empty. After all, only when the source of the energy reversed would the source of the energy in the lake above their heads fall into the lake below …
The two of them stood in the middle of the empty lake and observed it for a while. However, they discovered something gray that was constantly jumping around in the middle of the lake. As for what it was exactly, the two of them could not see it clearly. However, the things that appeared in this damned place were too bizarre. Neither Luo Zheng nor Lie Yu had any intention of entering the lake. Curiosity was important, but their lives were more important.
＝＝＝＝＝

Chapter 1496
At this point, the surrounding scenery had undergone a fundamental change.
The first part of the journey was still a primitive cave, which was completely formed naturally. Now, whether it was the lake in front of them or the flat mountain wall, it was obvious that they were man-made.
In the empty lake below, there was a gray thing that kept circling around.
Vigilant, Luo Zheng and Lie Yu moved forward along the edge. In any case, there was no way back. He wanted to see what was at the end of the lake.
This man-made lake was not large, only about two or three li in length. At the end of the lake, the flat mountain wall began to shrink, finally forming a slender passageway.
After moving across the edge of the lake, the two of them discovered that the passageway was bottomless. It was actually a dead end!
Luo Zheng and Lie Yu looked at each other. Lie Yu took a brown wooden stick from his body and gently waved it in the air. The end of the wooden stick burst into flames.
Then, Lie Yu threw the wooden stick down.
The two of them watched as the flames in their field of vision became smaller and smaller, only disappearing after a long time.
Lie Yu's face revealed a gloomy expression. "It's too deep down."
Previously, they had fallen from above and were close to this man-made lake, so they were already very deep under the Sacred Spring Mountain. If they continued to fall, who knew where they would fall? Moreover, with such a strong gravitational force, it would be difficult to keep their lives.
"It seems that this is also a dead end." Lie Yu sighed. The thing he had been worried about had finally come true. He was afraid that they would be trapped here until they died.
"There's a way," Luo Zheng suddenly said.
"Where's the way?" Lie Yu hurriedly asked.
Luo Zheng raised his head and pointed upwards.
The passageway in front of them was a fault, but there was another passageway above it. However, this passageway was embedded in the ceiling.
"This …" Lie Yu also discovered that the other passageway was indeed connected to the one below them. The problem was that they could not walk on this path.
If they could fly, they would not have to waste so much effort.
Luo Zheng's eyes narrowed into slits. In the end, he turned around and looked at the lake behind them.
Reversal …
After Luo Zheng entered the Sacred Spring Mountain, he discovered that the source of power had undergone a reversal.
Furthermore, this reversal should have been carried out by all the energy sources at the same time. Thus, there was clearly some mechanism within the Sacred Spring Mountain that could control the energy sources to reverse this process.
At this moment, Luo Zheng saw that the road in front of him was actually hanging directly from the ceiling. He was more confident in his judgment!
This inexplicable road in front of them was definitely designed for people to pass through. The gravity here was so great that if a martial artist couldn't fly, they could only rely on the reversal of gravity to pass through.
Then the mechanism should be here?
There was nothing in the artificial lake behind him except for the gray thing. Was this thing the key to the gravitational reversal?
Lie Yu leaned against the wall. He had already lost his temper. He had gotten lucky this time and had obtained quite a bit of the Origin of Power. With his gains, it would not be difficult for him to rank first in this coming of age ceremony.
The problem was that he had to return alive. Otherwise, everything would be for naught.
When he saw that Luo Zheng was still trying to think of a way, he smiled faintly. This human fellow was quite resilient. He did not seem to be discouraged at all.
"It's useless. The terrain here is not complicated. We have not seen any forks along the way. Unless you want to jump down … If there is no other way, we will jump down and try our luck!" Although Lie Yu said this, he knew in his heart that jumping into that bottomless abyss would not bode well for them.
Luo Zheng did not answer Lie Yu's words. Instead, he walked towards the artificial lake.
"What are you doing?" Lie Yu was shocked. Only the center of the artificial lake had that gray thing. He could not tell what it was. In any case, no one in the Mountain-Crushing Tribe had mentioned it. Perhaps even the Prophet did not know what it was …
However, the source of power in the Sacred Spring Mountain had nurtured many terrifying creatures. The Mountain-Crushing Tribe's people had been warned not to provoke any of the strange beasts in the Sacred Spring Mountain!
"You will be sending yourself to death if you go down!" Lie Yu continued. He was afraid that if Luo Zheng died, wouldn't he be in an even worse situation?
Luo Zheng did not pay attention to Lie Yu's words.
If there was a way out, he would take it step by step and look for the greatest opportunity. If there was no other way out, he could only choose to take risks. This was Luo Zheng's way of doing things.
"Pu!"
With a leap, Luo Zheng jumped into the artificial lake.
The artificial lake was only three meters deep. After jumping in, Luo Zheng walked directly towards the gray thing. The closer he got, the clearer the gray thing became.
Not long after, Luo Zheng discovered that the gray thing was many thin wisps of smoke. The wisps of smoke were constantly floating and rotating around a sphere.
As he got closer, Luo Zheng could barely make out that the center of the smoke was actually a round Eight Trigrams Sphere!
At this moment, Lie Yu had also jumped into the artificial lake. He could not really watch Luo Zheng die … However, as Luo Zheng got closer and closer, the gray thing did not attack Luo Zheng. This gave Lie Yu a glimmer of hope. Could the problem really be here?
However, with Lie Yu's brain, he really could not think of a way out of here.
The closer he got to the Eight Trigrams Sphere, the slower Luo Zheng's footsteps became, and the more cautious his movements became.
If there was some powerful mechanism here, Luo Zheng would not have much room to resist!
Ten meters …
Fifty meters …
Thirty meters …
Ten meters …
Finally, Luo Zheng walked into the gray mist.
Before entering the mist, Luo Zheng had already held his breath. Although he was not afraid of poison, he was still afraid of accidents. He could only be fully prepared.
"It's okay." Luo Zheng's eyes became more and more excited. Perhaps his guess was correct. This thing should be related to the reversal of gravity!
Then, Luo Zheng grabbed the Eight Trigrams Sphere in the center!
This Eight Trigrams Sphere was like a piece of fired porcelain. Its surface was very smooth, and it was heavy in his hand.
Luo Zheng held it with one hand and carefully examined the Eight Trigrams Sphere. There were two holes on the sphere, one black and one white, and there was a gap in the center following the pattern of the Eight Trigrams.
After observing it for a while, Luo Zheng still could not figure out what was going on.
He tried to use his blood to recognize it as its master, and then used his perception to immerse the Eight Trigrams Sphere, but there was no reaction.
After trying several methods, Luo Zheng suddenly grabbed the top of the Eight Trigrams Sphere and twisted it.
"Crack!"
He actually twisted the Eight Trigrams Sphere, and the black part and the white part were reversed.
At this moment, Luo Zheng suddenly heard a roar in his ears. Then, he saw the power of the lake above his head directly pour down!
At this moment, Lie Yu also walked into the gray fog. He wanted to warn Luo Zheng not to touch that thing casually. If he accidentally triggered something, they would really be finished.
However, before he could say anything, Luo Zheng had already twisted it open …
"Hualala …"
Even if a basin of water was poured down, it would cause a great disturbance, let alone a lake of power of the lake.
After changing the gravitational force, the power of the lake directly fell from the artificial lake above to the artificial lake below, instantly drowning Luo Zheng and Lie Yu!
Lie Yu was really stunned at this moment. This was the power of the lake, and it was the power of the entire lake! He got it so easily?
The power of the lake fell from the sky and kept surging in the artificial lake below. After a while, Luo Zheng and Lie Yu floated up from the bottom of the lake.
"I … I'm going to absorb all of this power of the lake! Hahaha! " Lie Yu was so happy that he was about to go crazy. Not to mention absorbing the power of the lake, just absorbing a small part of it would make him, Lie Yu, qualified to be the leader of the Mountain-Crushing Clan in the future!
In the past, Lie Yu had never thought that he would have such a day, but now he was almost certain.
The only thing that made Lie Yu depressed was that after absorbing a small part of the power of the lake, he could no longer absorb it …
Although Lie Yu's physical body was far stronger than the warriors of other clans, there was still an upper limit. Under the crazy absorption of the power of the lake, his physical body quickly reached a state of saturation.

Chapter 1497
Luo Zheng's body was much stronger than other cultivators of the same level, but there was a limit to how much it could hold.
Luo Zheng continued to absorb this power source, filling his bones, muscles, organs, and spine.
This increase in power gave Luo Zheng a marvelous experience. His entire body was stretched taut, as if he had an inexhaustible supply of power. An intense feeling of abundance washed over him.
As he continued to absorb, this feeling of abundance disappeared, replaced by a feeling of fullness!
This feeling meant that the absorption of the Origin Energy had almost reached its limit.
When Luo Zheng had borrowed the power of the dragon scales, he'd encountered the same problem. His body hadn't been strong enough to withstand this power.
If he forcibly absorbed this power source, Luo Zheng's body would easily disintegrate!
Luo Zheng hadn't even absorbed a ten-thousandth of the power source in the lake before him. He was extremely depressed. He was like a person who'd entered a mountain of treasures empty-handed, only to find that there were so many treasures that he could only take a small portion of them.
"This power source is enough to break through to the eighth level of Dao Platform. Don't waste such a good opportunity …"
A faint voice transmitted from the depths of Luo Zheng's mind.
It was Qinglong's voice.
After the nine true dragons had been awakened, they'd remained silent in his mind, not even responding. But now, Qinglong had spoken.
Perhaps they really didn't want him to waste this opportunity …
Luo Zheng hadn't gone far on the path of body refinement. Back then, Luo Zheng had only forged a single Dao Platform, and he'd used the jade bones of a Cloud Chasing Lion to do so.
Since Qinglong had spoken, Luo Zheng didn't hesitate in the slightest.
Perhaps the nine true dragons had other plans, but in the end, Luo Zheng chose to believe in them.
When this power was channeled into Luo Zheng's glabella, the Dao Platform forged from the Cloud Chasing Lion's jade bones was unexpectedly washed away in an instant …
"What's going on?" Luo Zheng was taken aback. He hadn't expected this to happen.
While he was hesitating, a forthright smile appeared in the depths of his mind. It was the crimson dragon's voice. "Since Big Brother has spoken, as the seventh brother, I will naturally take action! To Luo Zheng, this is indeed an extraordinary opportunity. If we miss it, we'll miss out on too much! "
After the Red Dragon's voice was heard, a mental power was transmitted from Luo Zheng's mind. It was a complicated veined pattern.
"Luo Zheng brat, use this to guide the energy source. This energy source is extremely pure, without any impurities. Naturally, it will be countless times better than the jade bones of the Cloud Chasing Lion! The Dao Platform forged from this is not something that the Cloud Chasing Lion can compare to … "
Luo Zheng closed his eyes and immediately did as the crimson dragon said.
Soon, a vortex appeared on Luo Zheng's hands, feet, head, chest, back, and dantian!
After the vortex appeared, it began to madly absorb the energy source!
Previously, Luo Zheng had absorbed tens of thousands of drops of energy source. But at this moment, the energy source absorbed by the eight vortexes was almost equal to Luo Zheng's body!
But the amount of energy source around Luo Zheng was still too much. Such a large lake contained more than tens of millions of drops of energy source. Hundreds of millions of drops?
He had absorbed hundreds of thousands of drops of energy source, but it had no effect on this place …
Just as Luo Zheng was absorbing, within the eight vortexes, Dao Platforms flashed with a seven-colored luster and began to appear.
The eight Dao Platforms of Body Cultivators were often of different shapes. But most of them were milky white in color. This was because most Body Cultivators forged their Dao Platforms with various rare jade bones as the foundation.
This kind of energy source could not possibly exist in the ordinary universe. Even if it did, it would not be in such an astonishing amount.
Even if it were a Body Cultivator from another race that entered the Mountain Collapsing Race, it would be impossible for them to find such an enormous amount of energy source. For example, Han Liusu's older brother had obtained a considerable amount of energy source. But compared to Luo Zheng, it was on a completely different level!
To put it bluntly, Luo Zheng was desperately squandering energy. He was using the energy source to forge the eight Dao Platforms, creating the most solid foundation.
This sort of treatment … was extremely rare in the Realm of the Gods.
Perhaps only the direct descendants of Saints would be able to make a Saint extract the energy source from the universe they had created.
Moreover, to a Saint, energy source was an extremely precious existence. To extract the energy from a Saint's body and make a Saint make such a decision was not an easy matter.
As for why the master of the Forbidden Land of Divine Refinement was willing to take out his own energy source and share it with so many other living beings, this had always been a mystery.
Dazzling seven-colored Dao Platforms were completed. In the time it took for an incense stick to burn, Luo Zheng had walked the path that many Body Cultivators would take in their entire lives. Moreover, his Dao Platforms were the purest of Dao Platforms. They were in a perfect state, without the slightest crack or flaw!
Finally …
Eight Dao Platforms were forged.
Those vortexes also gradually dissipated, stopping absorbing the energy source.
"The eighth layer of Dao Platforms has been forged. Now you can break through to nine stars of the Spiritual Pedestal Tier!" The crimson dragon's voice was heard again.
Luo Zheng's eyes were still tightly shut.
Following the patterns transmitted by the crimson dragon, Luo Zheng continued to absorb the energy source.
After this energy source was absorbed, it began to circulate towards Luo Zheng's Dantian.
Nine stars of the Spiritual Pedestal Tier was the ultimate path for Body Cultivators.
To construct nine stars in one's own inner world …
The inner world constructed by ordinary martial artists did not have stars because they were not Saints. The inner world was not complete!
Body Cultivators, on the other hand, constructed nine stars in their Dantian. Under the chain of nine stars, energy could naturally multiply endlessly.
"Isn't it too early for Luo Zheng to construct nine stars of the Spiritual Pedestal Tier now?" a green True Dragon asked in his mind.
Since ancient times, cultivation had always been focused on one path!
No matter if it was the lower realm, the higher realm, or the Realm of the Gods, dual cultivators of energy and body were extremely rare.
Furthermore, dual cultivators were destined to not go far on the path of martial arts.
However, this was only for ordinary martial artists!
In reality, if the numerous True Gods in the Realm of the Gods wanted to pursue the path of Saints, they would not only dual cultivate, but also triple cultivate!
These triple cultivation were divided into Essence, Qi, and Spirit.
Spirit was for refining the soul, Essence was for cultivation, and Qi was for refining the body!
Only martial artists who cultivated Essence, Qi, and Spirit to the extreme would have the qualifications to construct a true inner world, a universe placed at the edge of the Realm of the Gods, a real world.
Luo Zheng was now only an ultimate Divine Tier martial artist. Strictly speaking, the inner world of an ultimate Divine Tier martial artist could not bear nine stars!
The instant the energy source surged into the inner world, it would cause Luo Zheng's weak inner world to explode, and the outcome would be very tragic!
This was also the problem that the fourth Green Dragon was worried about.
"No … Luo Zheng's inner world is admittedly weak, but it is no longer an inner world in the ordinary sense. It is a true inner world!" The Five-Clawed Golden Dragon opened his mouth to speak.
They had a panoramic view of Luo Zheng's every move. Previously, Luo Zheng had taken out a small, lively fish from his inner world. This was still vivid in their minds.
At that time, the nine True Dragons' faces were brimming with astonishment.
At that time, they had already understood that Luo Zheng's inner world already had the same characteristics as a Saint. His inner world was constructed from chaotic Qi, and this had already surpassed the scope of the True Dragons' understanding.
Strictly speaking, Luo Zheng was now a "Minor Saint", and he was even more powerful than a Saint in one aspect.
The inner worlds constructed by Saints were formed from True Essence, and were limited by the Heavenly Dao. If a creature within wanted to transcend, they had to comprehend the Divine Dao. Only then would they have the qualifications to cross the Heavenly Dao and enter the Realm of the Gods.
Luo Zheng's inner world was of an even higher level. It was formed from chaotic Qi, and it was not limited by the Heavenly Dao. All creatures could directly leave his inner world!
From this point of view, this was not an inner world in the ordinary sense, nor was it a universe in the ordinary sense. This was a Realm of the Gods, a miniature Realm of the Gods!
The nine True Dragons had their own reasons for choosing Luo Zheng. They hoped to return to their original positions, and they hoped to guide Luo Zheng to find the truth. They hoped that Luo Zheng would help them take revenge.
This was why the Exalted Tool Refining Method was used on Luo Zheng, and it created his unique body.

Chapter 1499
"He's finally stopped …"
The Mountain Crushing Clan's Lie Yu stared at Luo Zheng in a daze. He really couldn't figure out where Luo Zheng had managed to absorb so much of the energy source.
A quarter of the energy source in such a large lake had been absorbed by Luo Zheng!
After stopping his absorption, Luo Zheng finally opened his eyes. A faint smile appeared on his face as he swam out of the lake.
"You, how did you absorb so much of the energy source …" Lie Yu asked in a daze.
Luo Zheng blinked, "I drank it all."
"Isn't that nonsense …" Lie Yu whispered. The problem was that he had to be able to drink it all!
As Luo Zheng reversed the energy source, the energy source in the countless caves within the Sacred Spring Mountain also began to reverse. The energy source that was originally out of reach began to reverse.
As for the characteristics of the energy source, the Mountain Crushing Clan had guarded it for so many years and had already done considerable research on it.
In the eyes of the Mountain Crushing Clan, this energy source seemed to have a life of its own. After a period of time, it would reverse. The energy source that was originally accumulated in the various caves would flow upwards not long after the Sacred Spring Mountain opened, once again accumulating at the top of the cave. It would even continue to flow upwards along the top of the cave.
Therefore, the majority of martial artists that entered the Sacred Spring Mountain were able to obtain a relatively small amount of energy source.
This reversal would often occur after a martial artist had entered the cave … there were a few martial artists that were able to grab a portion of the energy source before it reversed.
This time, because Luo Zheng had accidentally opened up an undiscovered path, he had found the key item that could reverse the energy source, which was the Eight Trigrams Ball in Luo Zheng's hand.
Therefore, at this moment, in every corner of the Sacred Spring Mountain, the vast majority of martial artists had expressions of wild joy on their faces!
In a slender cave, the energy source had formed a stream that slowly flowed above. Three foreign martial artists were walking together, but they could only stare at the energy source above their heads, unable to touch it.
They paid more attention to the stone walls around the cave, hoping to find a few drops of energy source embedded in them.
At this moment, the gravitational force of the energy source suddenly reversed. The energy source at the top of the cave suddenly fell down like raindrops, dripping down, almost driving the three foreign martial artists crazy.
This scene played out in almost the entire interior of the Sacred Spring Temple. Other than Luo Zheng and Lie Yu, no one knew why this was happening. This year was destined to be a bountiful year for the Mountain Crushing Clan.
Luo Zheng left the lake with the Eight Trigrams Ball. Looking at the energy source in front of him, he was somewhat reluctant to part with it.
Now that his Inner World and body had reached their limits, they could no longer bear this Source of Strength, but in the future, he still had a great need for it …
However, Luo Zheng immediately put an end to this thought.
To be able to obtain such an opportunity to increase one's own strength was already the fortune of three lifetimes.
If this energy source really was the Saint's own strength, taking it all away would be too greedy.
Many times, one had to know when to stop. Excessive greed would often bring about devastating consequences. This Forbidden Land of Divine Refinement was so strange. The Saint who had constructed this Forbidden Land of Divine Refinement was so generous that he had even given his own energy source to all living beings. This was something that did not conform to logic.
"But … how do we leave?"
After absorbing the Power Origin, Lie Yu looked at his surroundings and returned to his original question.
Luo Zheng did not pay attention to Lie Yu's words. He held the Eight Trigrams Ball in his hand and sized it up.
Previously, he had twisted the white portion downwards, causing all the energy source to overturn and pour down. Now, he grabbed the black portion and twisted it in another direction.
"Kacha!"
The moment the Eight Trigrams Ball twisted, Luo Zheng felt the gravitational force of the entire Sacred Spring Mountain reverse.
However, it was not the energy source that had reversed, but all the living beings in the Sacred Spring Mountain!
"F * ck!"
Lie Yu cursed. He felt the world spin around him as his entire body fell towards the ceiling. His reaction was extremely quick. The moment he 'fell' onto the ceiling, he suddenly twisted his body so that his entire body hit the ceiling horizontally. For better or worse, he did not let his head hit the ceiling.
Luo Zheng seemed to have been prepared for this. His body twisted in midair and his feet stepped on the ceiling …
At this moment, the two of them were like bats hanging upside down on the ceiling. However, for them, they were standing upright because their own gravitational force had been changed.
The two of them were still in a good situation …
At the same time, all the martial artists in the Sacred Spring Mountain had reversed. There were also many powerful beings that lived in the Sacred Spring Mountain. They were all hanging from the ceiling of the various caves.
"What happened!?"
"The gravitational force of this place suddenly reversed!"
"F * ck, I almost fell to my death!"
These martial artists were able to obtain a large amount of energy source. After all, it was Luo Zheng's kindness. However, at this moment, they had suffered a great loss at the hands of Luo Zheng.
"Now do you know how to leave?" Luo Zheng said to Lie Yu with a smile.
Since the Eight Trigrams Ball could change the gravitational force, and the narrow passage ahead was constructed in such a strange manner, Luo Zheng had already made some guesses. Only by changing their own gravitational force could they pass through that passage.
"Get ready." Luo Zheng's voice fell as he twisted the Eight Trigrams Ball in his hand again. As a result, he and Lie Yu fell from the ceiling again.
This time, Luo Zheng landed safely. However, because Luo Zheng had acted so suddenly, Lie Yu once again tragically fell from the top to the bottom. Fortunately, he had thick skin. Otherwise, under such a strong gravitational force, falling from such a height was no laughing matter.
"Can you not be so sudden?!" Lie Yu angrily shouted behind Luo Zheng.
"I'll remind you." Luo Zheng walked forward with the Eight Trigrams Ball in his hand.
"You …"
Under Luo Zheng's guidance, the two quickly arrived at the narrow passage again. The world beyond the end of the passage was absolute darkness, except for the passage hanging upside down above their heads.
"Get ready."
As soon as Luo Zheng's voice fell, Lie Yu's whole body tensed up, as if he was facing a great enemy.
Seeing Lie Yu's appearance, Luo Zheng smiled slightly. "Don't be so nervous. It's very dangerous here. I'll be careful …"
Lie Yu had already done a test before. This place was bottomless. Who knew where they would fall?
When the two of them were as close to the edge of the passage as possible, Luo Zheng twisted the Eight Trigrams Ball in his hand again.
"Kacha!"
The gravitational force reversed.
Luo Zheng and Lie Yu took a step forward at the same time. In the void, the two of them did not fall downwards. Instead, they fell into the narrow passage above.
This time, the two of them landed safely and walked upside down on the passage.
After walking for about one or two li along the passage, the passage once again formed a fault, but another passage appeared below the two of them.
"Again!"
Luo Zheng changed the gravitational force again. The two of them fell from the passage above and stepped on the passage below …
This strange passage was like this. They had to rely on the Eight Trigrams Ball to constantly change the gravitational force, sometimes below, sometimes above.
After getting used to the changes in the gravitational force, the two of them were very relaxed.
But the other cultivators inside Sacred Spring Mountain were in a tragic situation …
Just as they realized what was going on, the gravitational force on their bodies suddenly changed direction and smashed into the ceiling of the cave. Not long after, the gravitational force changed direction again and smashed into the ceiling again. The constant up and down had completely confused them. Had the Sacred Spring Mountain gone mad?
All kinds of curses rose one after another, but they were powerless to change the direction of the gravitational force. They could only try their best to endure it.
However, there were a few cultivators who were smarter. After falling twice, they realized that this situation would repeat itself. They found a stone pillar and hugged it tightly, or stuck themselves in a crack. At least this way, they could avoid being thrown around like a shuttlecock.

Chapter 1500
Based on the path Luo Zheng had taken, they should be in the depths of the Sacred Spring Mountain.
According to Lie Yu, although the paths in the Sacred Spring Mountain were extremely complicated, most of them converged on the main path at the very bottom. If one followed the main path upwards, they would be able to leave the Sacred Spring Mountain and return to the Mountaincrumbling clan.
However, the cave that they had discovered was in a sealed environment, a place that no one had ever entered before.
The door in front of them was the only way out. Would they be able to leave this place?
However, Luo Zheng had already made up his mind. If this place was a dead end, he would return the way he had come!
He had absorbed so much energy source for no reason. The most powerful part of the Sacred Spring Mountain was the sturdy silver rocks. These rocks were extremely smooth and difficult to climb.
However, the situation was different now that Luo Zheng's strength had skyrocketed.
When they reached the stone door, Lie Yu took the lead and pressed his hands against the sides of the door.
"Ha!"
With a sudden burst of strength, the muscles all over his body bulged.
This energy source had transformed Lie Yu, and his strength had increased exponentially. However, the stone door in front of him still did not budge.
"Heh heh, I can't push it open." Lie Yu had pushed his strength to the limit. Seeing this, he could only give up. His gaze fell on Luo Zheng. Perhaps the situation was different now that Luo Zheng had absorbed so much energy source. A member of the Mountain-Crushing Tribe was always proud of his strength. Now that he was relying on an outsider, he could not help but feel embarrassed.
Luo Zheng's gaze was indifferent as he slowly approached the stone door.
After being tempered by the Mystical Fire in the Mountain-Crushing Tribe and absorbing the energy source, he had simultaneously created eight Dao Pillars and even opened the Nine Stars of the Spiritual Platform. To Luo Zheng, this was an earth-shattering transformation.
He slowly stretched out his hands and pressed them against the two stone doors.
This was different from borrowing the strength of a True Dragon …
This strength was more real. This strength was hidden in every part of his body. When he made a move, this strength would naturally erupt.
After all, other people's things were borrowed.
Only the things that belonged to him could be used with great ease!
"Open for me!"
He suddenly exerted strength in his arms!
This power suddenly dissipated, and a gentle breeze blew past!
"Shua!"
Lie Yu, who was about ten feet tall, immediately felt an intense pressure. He could not help but take two steps back!
At this moment, Luo Zheng had already used all the strength in his body, but the door still didn't move.
Luo Zheng's physical body might contain several times more power than Lie Yu's, but this power was still not enough.
"Eight-level Dao Platform!"
The eight Dao Pillars on Luo Zheng's body began to flicker with colorful light!
That power was crazily emitted from the eight Dao Platforms, causing Luo Zheng's strength to double!
Luo Zheng's strength was already unimaginable when it burst out, but on top of that powerful foundation, it doubled …
"Rumble …"
The stone door in front of him made a dull rumbling sound, and a small crack appeared. In the end, it was pushed open by Luo Zheng.
As Luo Zheng continued to push, the stone door continued to rumble. After pushing it to a certain limit, Luo Zheng withdrew his arms. The crack on the stone door was large enough for two people to pass through.
"Amazing!"
Lie Yu's eyes flashed. The Mountain Crumbling Tribe had always respected those with great strength. The relationship between this tribe was very simple. Aside from the prophet who was rarely seen, the entire Mountain Crumbling Tribe was based on who had the greatest strength and who had the highest status. Even if an outsider came in, they would also have the opportunity to become the leader!
The world on the other side of the door was still dark, but Luo Zheng didn't hesitate and went in through the opened stone door.
However, the moment Luo Zheng entered the stone door, a strange force suddenly stuck to the eight trigrams ball beside Luo Zheng. The eight trigrams ball immediately flew out of his hand and flew in the direction the two of them had come from. After passing through this distance, it fell into the lake of the source of power with a "dong" sound.
Then, the eight trigrams ball twisted on its own. The power source in the lake floated up and "fell" into the lake at the top. The power source's gravitational force was once again reversed.
Clearly, someone did not want Luo Zheng to take away the eight trigrams ball.
Luo Zheng was secretly shocked. Fortunately, he did not use the spatial ring to take away the power source.
There must be a powerful being watching this place …
What he did not know was that even the hole that the Barbaric Queen Bee had corroded had been completely repaired.
Then, the stone door behind them began to slowly close. The two were once again immersed in darkness.
The left and right were still thick silver rocks. There was only one path in front of them. Lie Yu and Luo Zheng had the same thought. There were all kinds of strange things in this place. They should have been arranged by someone. That person might even be right beside them.
The two did not speak and continued forward. Unexpectedly, they had only taken a few steps when they felt a flash of light in front of them. It was as if they had passed through a tunnel and entered an extremely wide cave.
Behind them, there was a mountain of silver boulders.
They had been sent out by someone. The two came to this conclusion at the same time.
In contrast, Luo Zheng was even more shocked. He was originally sensitive to the Laws of Space, but when the light flashed just now, he could not see any fluctuations of the power of Laws.
This meant that the person who sent them out was very powerful. It was far beyond his imagination!
"Perhaps this is the master who constructed the Forbidden Land of God Refinement." Luo Zheng made such a guess for the time being.
He was even more curious about the master of the Forbidden Land of God Refinement. What was the purpose of this master so generously giving away their source strength?
The six great cultivation Holy Lands all contained this master's source strength. If the life forms of the Forbidden Land of God Refinement swallowed this source strength, they would have to return it to the Forbidden Land of God Refinement. It was just like how a martial artist from the universe could not cross the universe no matter how strong they were. In the end, if a martial artist was unable to cross the universe, they would return to dust, dust to dust. All good fortune and everything they obtained would be returned to the universe.
But when a foreign martial artist entered the Forbidden Land of God Refinement and took away their source strength, it was a true loss. This master of the Forbidden Land of God Refinement was just too kind.
As he thought about this, Lie Yu looked around and revealed a happy expression, "This is the path at the bottom of Sacred Spring Mountain. This path extends from the deepest part of the underground to the exit!"
Just as he finished speaking, Lie Yu's expression turned ugly again, "But … our position seems to be too deep …" Finished speaking, he pointed ahead.
There was a path at the bottom of Sacred Spring Mountain that led directly to the exit, but the source strength here was extremely scarce. Most of the source strength gathered at the top of Sacred Spring Mountain, and there were even occasional sources of energy that overflowed from the peak of the mountain.
The reason for this situation was that the source of energy had its gravity reversed all year round. These sources of energy often flowed towards the top.
Generally speaking, the densely packed caves that led down from Sacred Spring Mountain would all converge at the middle of the path they were on, and they could continue upwards. However, Luo Zheng and Lie Yu had appeared at the end of this path, which was the very bottom of Sacred Spring Mountain.
There were many powerful mutated beasts here. The growth of these mutated beasts was more or less related to the source of energy.
"Those are Great Demon Badgers," Lie Yu reminded.
An ordinary badger was about the size of a kitten, but these Great Demon Badgers that were slowly approaching were comparable in size to a bull. Their large bodies did not hinder their agility. These creatures were not something Lie Yu could hunt right now. Generally, only adult members of the Collapsing Mountain Tribe would form a team to hunt these Great Demon Badgers.
Luo Zheng nodded. He had been worrying about where he could use his strength, so he was not afraid of these Great Demon Badgers. With a sudden stomp, he charged forward at an extremely fast speed!
The Great Demon Badgers had already regarded Luo Zheng and Lie Yu as prey, so they worked together to completely surround the two of them.
However, they did not expect that Luo Zheng would not have the slightest intention of escaping, and instead take the lead to charge towards them.
"Roar!"
One of the Great Demon Badgers let out a warning roar. Immediately, three of the Great Demon Badgers split into three groups, charging towards Luo Zheng.
These Great Demon Badgers were not stupid and knew how to work together to defend against the enemy.
If they encountered an ordinary cultivator, the three Great Demon Badgers would have a high chance of victory with their extraordinary strength and agility.
